### [1] [Detect Early, Escalate Rarely: Anytime Detection of AI-Generated Video from the Compressed Bitstream](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.19476)
*Mert Onur Cakiroglu,Mehmet Dalkilic,Hasan Kurban*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 将AI生成视频检测重新定义为流式感知任务：直接从码流的运动场进行解析（而非像素域解码评分），利用单调聚合性质实现任意时间有效的单阈值校准，在GenVidBench上以像素CNN五阶量级的计算量达到0.64 AUC，在CPU上运行；通过15%的延迟决策将准确率从0.75提升至0.78且节省7倍计算量。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有AI视频检测器在离线评估中逐帧解码像素后进行评分（越来越多地依赖大视觉语言模型），但实际部署是在线流式场景。这种离线-在线不匹配导致计算开销巨大且无法在流式环境中做出数据自适应的即时决策。作者希望将检测重构成流式感知问题，利用编解码器已写入码流的运动场信息，实现极低计算量的在线检测。

Method: (1) 将任务重构成流式感知：直接从码流中解析运动场（而非解码像素），利用编解码器已有的运动信息；(2) 利用运行累计量的单调性，采用端校准的单阈值（即在完整序列上校准的阈值）在数据自适应的决策时刻具有任意时间有效性（anytime-valid），而每个前缀重新校准则不具备此性质；(3) 升级代价（escalation）以闭式定价；(4) 计算预算映射到延迟窗口，在延迟条件成立的单调前沿上；(5) 引入门控机制在保持停止时间假阳性率的同时实现延迟决策。

Result: (1) 在GenVidBench上，码流阶段以像素CNN五阶量级（10^5倍）更少的计算量达到全长度AUC 0.64（在CPU上）；(2) 门控机制在真实数据匹配校准设置时保持停止时间假阳性率在目标水平，在分布漂移下会漂移超出；(3) 延迟15%的片段将准确率从0.75提升至0.78，同时节省7倍计算量（配对McNemar检验p<10^{-6}）；(4) Stage-1排序在AIGVDBench上可复现。

Conclusion: 本文没有提出新的检测器，核心贡献在于：(1) 将AI视频检测重新定义为流式感知问题的理论框架；(2) 两个理论保证（单调聚合的任意时间有效性、闭式升级定价）；(3) 实测的计算-延迟-准确率前沿。证明了通过利用码流中的运动信息并结合流式统计推断，可以在极小计算量下实现有效的AI生成视频检测，且通过延迟决策机制可以进一步提升性能。

Abstract: Detectors for AI-generated video are evaluated offline. A clip is decoded to pixels and scored once, increasingly by a large vision-language model. Detection, however, is deployed online. We recast the task as streaming perception and score the motion field the codec already wrote into the bitstream. Reading that field is a parse, not a pixel-domain forward pass. Because the running aggregate is monotone, one end-calibrated threshold is anytime-valid at the data-dependent decision time. Recalibrating at each prefix is not. Escalation is priced in closed form. A compute budget maps to a deferral window, on a frontier monotone exactly where the deferral condition holds. On matched GenVidBench the codec stage reaches full-length AUC 0.64 at five orders of magnitude less compute than a pixel CNN, on CPU. Its gate holds the stopping-time false-positive rate at target while the real data match its calibration, and drifts above it under distribution shift. Deferring 15% of clips lifts accuracy from 0.75 to 0.78 at $7\times$ less compute (paired: McNemar $p<10^{-6}$). The stage-1 ordering replicates on AIGVDBench. We introduce no new detector. The contribution is the reframing, two guarantees, and the measured frontiers. Code, configurations, and evaluation splits: https://github.com/KurbanIntelligenceLab/streamdet.

</details>


### [2] [Crowd4D: Scene-Aware Monocular 4D Crowd Reconstruction](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.19517)
*Hongbo Kang,Tianyi Zhou,Qingyang Yang,Hongwei Wen,Jing Huang,Yu-Kun Lai,Kun Li*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 提出Crowd4D，首个从单目RGB视频中联合优化人群与场景的大规模4D人群重建框架，通过多阶段优化策略、人-场景交互代理（HSIP）和人群结构相干性正则化（CSCR），在复杂大尺度场景中实现鲁棒的单目4D人群重建。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有单目人群重建方法通常依赖单平面假设，在复杂地形下会导致不可靠的度量尺度和空间漂移；人和场景重建通常是解耦的，缺乏准确的人-场景对齐。

Method: 提出Crowd4D框架：(1) 多阶段优化策略联合优化人群和场景；(2) Human-Scene Interaction Proxy (HSIP)作为中间表征，包含Scene Interaction Point Clouds (SIPC)和Scene Interaction Surface (SIS)，编码显式的场景感知几何先验；(3) Crowd Structural Coherence Regularization (CSCR)利用HSIP空间先验对局部人群邻域内的成对相对位移和方向施加软时间一致性约束。

Result: 在复杂大规模真实场景中，Crowd4D持续优于现有最先进方法，实现了鲁棒的单目4D人群重建。

Conclusion: Crowd4D通过显式融入场景几何并确保图像空间和场景空间的一致性，有效解决了大规模场景中单目4D人群重建的深度模糊和空间漂移问题。

Abstract: Recovering scene-consistent 4D crowd motion from monocular video in large-scale scenes remains challenging due to severe depth ambiguity and complex scene geometry. Existing monocular crowd reconstruction methods typically rely on single-plane assumptions, leading to unreliable metric scale and spatial drift under complex terrain. We propose Crowd4D, the first scene-aware 4D crowd reconstruction framework that jointly optimizes the crowd and scene from a monocular RGB video in large-scale scenes. Crowd4D explicitly incorporates scene geometry and ensures consistency across image and scene spaces via a multi-stage optimization strategy. A key bottleneck of this task lies in accurate human-scene alignment, particularly in scale and position. However, human and scene reconstructions are typically decoupled. To address this, we introduce the Human-Scene Interaction Proxy, abbreviated as HSIP, as an intermediate representation derived from Scene Interaction Point Clouds and a Scene Interaction Surface, abbreviated as SIPC and SIS. These representations encode explicit scene-aware geometric priors and redefine the optimization space for large-scale monocular 4D crowd reconstruction. To further improve temporal stability under occlusions, we introduce Crowd Structural Coherence Regularization, abbreviated as CSCR, which leverages HSIP-based spatial priors to impose soft temporal consistency on pairwise relative displacements and directions within local crowd neighborhoods. Extensive experiments demonstrate that Crowd4D consistently outperforms existing state-of-the-art methods and enables robust monocular 4D crowd reconstruction in complex, large-scale real-world scenes.

</details>


### [3] [D3VL: Understanding Driving Scenes from 3D Time Series Data and Video with Language Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.19528)
*Heesang Han,A. Lynn Abbott,Abhijit Sarkar*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文提出 D3VL，一种集成了 2D 和 3D 时序数据的多模态大语言模型（MLLM）框架，用于自动驾驶场景理解和安全问题问答。该模型在 KITTI QA 数据集上相比基线方法提升了 11%，并引入了 Waymo QA 数据集扩展以评估模型在多样化驾驶条件下的 3D 时序数据处理能力。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有 MLLM 在自动驾驶中主要关注 2D 图像和视频，忽略了 3D 传感器（如 LiDAR 和立体相机）的有效性。LiDAR 数据稀疏且缺乏网格结构，导致其在 MLLM 中的集成以及相机- LiDAR 融合面临挑战。然而，大多数自动驾驶系统依赖 LiDAR 感知，且 3D 数据在传统场景理解任务中已被证明能提升性能。因此，本文旨在探索将 2D 和 3D 时序数据有效集成到单一 MLLM 框架中，以改善交通场景理解与安全问答。

Method: 提出 D3VL 框架，以简洁的架构整合 2D 和 3D 时序数据，利用 LiDAR 和立体相机的多模态输入，通过统一模型处理时间序列信息，并基于此进行场景理解与安全相关的问答任务。

Result: 在 KITTI QA 数据集上，D3VL 相比基线方法在 2D 和 3D 时序数据处理上取得了 11% 的提升。此外，论文扩展了 Waymo QA 数据集，用于评估模型在多种驾驶条件下的 3D 时序数据处理能力。

Conclusion: D3VL 证明了将 2D 和 3D 时序数据集成到 MLLM 框架中的可行性，并显著提升了自动驾驶场景问答性能。Waymo QA 扩展数据集的引入为未来研究提供了更丰富的评估基准。代码和数据集已开源。

Abstract: Recent advances in Multimodal Large Language Models (MLLMs) have triggered the development of end-to-end MLLMs for autonomous driving. However, the main emphasis to date has been for MLLMs using 2D images and videos. In contrast, this paper considers MLLM effectiveness using 3D sensors, particularly LiDAR and stereo cameras. LiDAR presents unique challenges to integration within an MLLM, largely because of data sparsity and lack of a grid structure for the data. For similar reasons, fusion of camera and LiDAR data within an MLLM pipeline is also uncommon. However, most autonomous systems rely on LiDAR-based sensing, and incorporating 3D data has been proven to improve performance in traditional 3D scene perception tasks. This paper presents D3VL, a novel MLLM framework that integrates 2D and 3D time-series data in a single but simple architecture. The model aims to answer questions involving traffic scene understanding and safety. D3VL shows an 11% improvement in the KITTI Question-Answering (QA) dataset compared to baseline methods in processing 2D and 3D time-series data. This paper further introduces the Waymo QA dataset extension, which assesses models' capabilities in processing 3D and time-series data under diverse driving conditions. D3VL implementation code and WaymoQA extension can be found on our supplemental website: https://automotivesafety-lvlm.github.io

</details>


### [4] [Synthetic and Derived Training Images for Campus Waste Detection: A Multi-Seed Evaluation with YOLOv8n](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.19535)
*Ali Behbahani,Newsha Javanmardi,Shahriar Ahmed,Ling Chen,Phouvadeth Vathana*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文评估了合成图像和衍生图像对提升YOLOv8n检测器在校园回收流污染监控中性能的效果，发现所有增强配置均未超越仅使用真实图像的基线模型（mAP@0.5=0.691），且31张测试集过小导致结论强度有限。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 不当的废弃物处理会污染校园回收流，而安装在垃圾桶上的摄像头可在丢弃时提供反馈。研究旨在探索合成图像和衍生图像是否能提升基于该视角的YOLOv8n检测器性能，以弥补真实数据不足的问题。

Method: 使用148张校园照片（86训练/31验证/31测试），通过12种联合训练配置（变动添加图像的数量和来源）进行实验。7种主要设置使用4个匹配种子重复，并计算Bootstrap百分位区间。测试了背景替换、隔离物体图像、完整增强池以及手和前臂复合图像等增强策略。

Result: 纯真实模型mAP@0.5为0.691[0.665,0.722]；背景替换降至0.560[0.499,0.619]；隔离物体图像为0.680[0.644,0.724]；完整增强池为0.487[0.438,0.537]。手部复合实验校正后差异为+0.034[-0.063,0.199]，无可靠效果。所有配置均未超越纯真实基线。种子间变异显著，31张测试集不足以得出强类别级结论。

Conclusion: 当前实验条件下，合成与衍生图像未能提升YOLOv8n在校园回收流污染检测中的性能；未来需扩大真实测试集规模并探索更有效的合成数据生成策略。

Abstract: Incorrect disposal can contaminate campus recycling streams, and a bin-mounted camera could provide feedback as an item is discarded. We evaluated whether synthetic and derived images improve a YOLOv8n detector for this view. The real dataset contained 148 campus photographs: 86 for training, 31 for validation, and 31 for testing. Twelve joint-training configurations varied the amount and source of added images. We repeated seven principal settings with four matched seeds and computed bootstrap percentile intervals over those seeds. The real-only model reached a mean mAP@0.5 of 0.691 [0.665, 0.722]. Background replacement reduced the mean to 0.560 [0.499, 0.619], isolated-object images gave 0.680 [0.644, 0.724], and the full augmentation pool gave 0.487 [0.438, 0.537]. We also tested hand-and-forearm composites because every real photo showed a held object. Two cutouts in the initial composite set came from test photographs, so we discarded that experiment, rebuilt the set with training-split cutouts, and reran all four seeds. The corrected paired difference was +0.034 [-0.063, 0.199], which does not support a reliable hand-composite effect. Single-seed transfer experiments produced source-dependent rankings between joint mixing and sequential pretraining. None of the evaluated configurations exceeded the real-only baseline. The reported intervals quantify seed variation; the 31-photo test set remains too small for strong class-specific conclusions.

</details>


### [5] [ChronoStitch: Training-Free Composition of Visual KV Memories for Long-Horizon Temporal Reasoning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.19547)
*Santiram Tiwari,Nishant Sinha,Kunal Kislay*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文提出ChronoStitch，一种无需训练的方法，用于组合独立存储的视觉KV缓存以进行长视频问答。该方法通过全局三轴多模态RoPE坐标系统重新定位键，并选择性重新计算少量高偏差标记，在TempCompass数据集上优于简单组合和仅位置变体，速度比完整联合预填充快3.3倍。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 长视频问答需要模型随时间保留视觉证据而不重复处理整个视频。一种实用方法是存储每个视频块的视觉-语言模型内部KV缓存，并在查询时检索。然而，独立缓存的视频块不能正确组合：每个块从局部旋转位置零开始预填充，简单拼接会导致时间相位冲突并丢失全局顺序，无法回答关于事件顺序、频率或变化的问题。

Method: ChronoStitch方法首先将存储的后旋转键重新基于全局三轴多模态RoPE坐标系，该坐标系保留时间、高度和宽度结构。然后通过选择性地重新计算少量高偏差的后块视觉标记，允许它们关注组合缓存，从而解决位置修复后残留的内容差距。

Result: 在Qwen2.5-VL-3B和TempCompass的时间分割上，ChronoStitch优于简单组合和仅位置变体，提高了事件排序准确性，同时运行速度比完整联合重新预填充快3.3倍。

Conclusion: ChronoStitch是一种无需训练的有效方法，用于组合独立存储的视觉KV记忆，解决了长视频问答中的位置对齐和内容一致性问题，实现了更高的准确性和效率。

Abstract: Long-video question answering requires a model to preserve visual evidence over time without repeatedly reprocessing the same video. A practical approach is to store the vision-language model's internal key-value (KV) cache for each video chunk and retrieve that state at query time. However, independently cached video chunks do not compose correctly: every chunk is prefilled from local rotary position zero, so naive concatenation collides temporal phases and removes the global order required for questions about what happened first, how often events occurred, or what changed across the video. This paper presents ChronoStitch, a training-free method for composing independently stored visual KV memories. The method first re-bases stored post-rotary keys onto a global three-axis multimodal RoPE coordinate system that preserves time, height, and width structure. We show why a one-dimensional scalar re-indexing is geometrically inconsistent for visual tokens because it turns spatial order within a frame into false temporal displacement. We then address the residual content gap left by positional repair: later chunks were originally encoded without attending to earlier chunks. ChronoStitch therefore selectively recomputes a small fraction of high-deviation later-chunk visual tokens while allowing them to attend over the composed cache. On Qwen2.5-VL-3B and the temporal split of TempCompass, ChronoStitch outperforms naive composition and position-only variants, improving event-ordering accuracy while running 3.3x faster than full joint re-prefilling.

</details>


### [6] [VQ-Transplant: Efficient VQ-Module Integration for Pre-trained Visual Tokenizers](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.19575)
*Xianghong Fang,Yuan Yuan,Dehan Kong,Tim G. J. Rudner*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 提出 VQ-Transplant 框架，通过替换预训练 tokenizer 中的量化模块并冻结编码器-解码器，实现新 VQ 技术的即插即用，仅需轻量解码器适配即可接近 SOTA 重建 fidelity，同时降低 95% 训练成本。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 训练 SOTA VQ 模型的量化模块需要大量计算资源，限制了资源受限条件下新型 VQ 技术的开发。

Method: VQ-Transplant 将新的 VQ 模块直接替换到冻结的预训练 tokenizer 中，保持编码器-解码器参数不变，并引入轻量级解码器适配策略（ImageNet-1k 上仅训练 5 个 epoch）以缓解量化空间不匹配问题。

Result: 在 VAR 等工业级模型上取得接近 SOTA 的重建 fidelity，同时减少 95% 的训练成本。

Conclusion: 该框架民主化量化研究，使资源受限的场景也能高效集成新型 VQ 技术，并达到与工业级模型相当的重建性能。

Abstract: Vector Quantization (VQ) underpins modern discrete visual tokenization. However, training quantization modules for state-of-the-art VQ-based models requires significant computational resources which, in practice, all but prevents the development of novel, cutting-edge VQ techniques under resource constraints. To address this limitation, we propose {\bf VQ-Transplant}, a simple framework that enables plug-and-play integration of new VQ modules into frozen, pre-trained tokenizers by replacing their native VQ modules. Crucially, the proposed transplantation process preserves all encoder-decoder parameters, obviating the need for costly end-to-end retraining when modifying the quantization method. To mitigate decoder-quantization mismatch, we introduce a lightweight decoder adaptation strategy (trained for only 5 epochs on ImageNet-1k) to align feature priors with the new quantization space. In our empirical evaluation, we find that VQ-Transplant allows obtaining near state-of-the-art reconstruction fidelity for industry-level models like VAR while reducing the training cost by 95\%. VQ-Transplant democratizes quantization research by enabling resource-efficient integration of novel VQ techniques while matching industry-level reconstruction performance.

</details>


### [7] [EGRNet: A Lightweight Semantic Segmentation Network with Edge-Gated Refinement and Adversarial Sensing](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.19617)
*Bareera Qaseem,Mohsin Kamal,Muhammad Naveed Aman*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文提出EGRNet（边缘门控精化网络），一种用于城市场景实时语义分割的轻量级高效深度学习模型。仅0.46M参数，在Cityscapes数据集上达到65.28% mIoU，兼具低计算开销、高精度与对抗鲁棒性，适合边缘设备部署。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 随着自主系统和智慧城市的发展，对高效、鲁棒的场景理解需求日益迫切。语义分割是自动驾驶理解复杂城市环境的关键，但如何在保证高精度的同时最小化计算成本仍是重大挑战。

Method: 模型采用深度可分离卷积降低计算复杂度，使用空洞残差块捕获多尺度上下文信息；提出新颖的边缘门控精化（EGR）模块，通过可学习门控机制自适应融合原始特征与精化特征，增强边界保持；引入Squeeze-and-Excitation（SE）注意力提升特征表示；并设计轻量级对抗攻击检测策略保证鲁棒性。

Result: EGRNet仅0.46M参数，在Cityscapes数据集上达到65.28% mIoU，以极低资源消耗实现了先进的语义分割性能，同时保持实时推理速度。

Conclusion: EGRNet通过结合效率、精度与鲁棒性，非常适合部署于安全关键的实时应用中的边缘设备。

Abstract: As autonomous systems and smart cities continue to evolve, the demand for efficient and robust scene understanding becomes increasingly critical. Semantic segmentation plays a key role in enabling autonomous vehicles to comprehend complex urban environments. However, achieving high accuracy with minimal computational cost remains a significant challenge. In this paper, we present Edge-Gated Refinement Network (EGRNet), a lightweight and efficient deep learning model designed for real-time semantic segmentation in urban scenarios. The model incorporates depthwise separable convolutions to reduce computational complexity and dilated residual blocks for capturing rich multi-scale contextual information. Additionally, we introduce a novel Edge-Gated Refinement (EGR) module, which adaptively fuses original and refined features through a learnable gating mechanism, enhancing boundary preservation and edge-sensitive regions. To further improve feature representation, Squeeze-and-Excitation (SE) attention is applied across the network. With only 0.46M parameters, EGRNet achieves state-of-the-art performance while maintaining low computational overhead. When evaluated on the Cityscapes dataset, the model attains a mean Intersection over Union (mIoU) of 65.28%, demonstrating strong accuracy with minimal resource consumption. Moreover, we introduce a lightweight adversarial attack detection strategy, ensuring robustness against adversarial inputs without compromising real-time performance. By combining efficiency, accuracy, and resilience, EGRNet is well-suited for deployment on edge devices in safety-critical real-time applications.

</details>


### [8] [Pathologist Attention-Aligned Report Generation for Prostate Histopathology](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.19624)
*Ruoyu Xue,Suryakant Singh,Souradeep Chakraborty,Pierre Marza,Oksana Yaskiv,Constantin Friedman,Natallia Sheuka,Paul Friedman,Bharat Ramlal,Beatrice Knudsen,Rajarsi Gupta,Joel Saltz,Prateek Prasanna,Gregory Zelinsky,Dimitris Samaras*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文引入病理学家视觉注意力来训练病理报告生成模型，通过收集包含121例前列腺全切片图像的多模态注意力数据集（含多尺度视口轨迹、口头描述和光标移动），使用注意力对齐损失微调两个报告生成模型，使模型注意力分布匹配病理学家注意力。在前列腺癌报告生成和视觉问答任务上，NLP指标平均提升10.9%，准确率提升19.3%，且推理时模型注意力图与病理学家注意力更对齐。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 病理学家在癌症诊断中的视觉注意力高度选择性，能帮助医学影像任务（如分类、分割），并为报告生成提供诊断信息区域的强语义线索。现有报告生成模型未充分利用这种人类注意力，因此作者希望将人类注意力引入模型训练。

Method: 收集了121例前列腺全切片图像的多模态人类注意力数据集，包含病理学家多尺度视口轨迹、口头描述和光标移动，标注了五个临床相关成分（如Gleason模式）。使用注意力对齐损失（attention-alignment loss）微调两个报告生成模型，该损失正则化模型对图像块的注意力分布以匹配病理学家注意力分布。

Result: 在前列腺癌报告生成和视觉问答任务上，两个不同内部注意力机制的模型均取得提升：NLP指标平均提升10.9%，五个临床报告成分准确率提升19.3%。推理时模型注意力图与病理学家注意力更对齐，且计算开销极小，为生成报告提供了更强的视觉支持。

Conclusion: 将病理学家视觉注意力作为监督信号引入报告生成模型训练，能显著提升报告质量和临床相关成分的准确性，同时使模型注意力更可解释、与专家注意力一致，为临床报告生成提供更可靠的视觉依据。

Abstract: The allocation of visual attention by pathologists during cancer diagnosis is a highly selective process that critically shapes the information extracted from whole-slide images (WSIs). Human attention helps medical imaging tasks such as classification and segmentation, and becomes a strong semantic cue for identifying diagnostically informative regions for report generation. In this paper, we introduce human attention into the training of pathologist report generation models. To this end, we collected a multimodal human-attention dataset of 121 prostate WSIs annotated with pathologists' multi-scale viewport trajectories synchronized with the pathologists' verbal descriptions and cursor movements for five clinically relevant components (e.g., Gleason patterns). Using this dataset, we finetune two report generation models with an attention-alignment loss that regularizes the model attention over image patches to match the distribution of pathologist attention. We evaluate our approach on prostate cancer report generation and visual question answering using two models with different internal attention mechanisms (i.e., how image tokens are integrated into the language decoder). Experiments show average gains of 10.9% on NLP-based metrics and 19.3% in accuracy across five clinically relevant report components. Further, model attention maps extracted at inference time, with minimal computational overhead, align more closely with pathologist attention, providing stronger visual support for the generated reports by highlighting the regions that most influence the output.

</details>


### [9] [Domain Shift in Echocardiography: Interpretable Quantification and Prediction of Cross-Dataset Left Ventricular Segmentation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.19643)
*Soroush Elyasi,Nasim Dadashi Serej,Julie Wall,Massoud Zolgharni*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文研究了超声心动图左心室分割任务中跨数据集泛化的问题，发现域偏移主要来源于视野和构图不一致而非声学差异，几何感知预处理可显著改善迁移性能；通过多种特征表示（手工超声描述符、VAE潜在特征、分割潜变量）可有效预测分割性能下降（R²=0.612），且无需分割标签即可实现迁移风险监测（无LV特征时仍保持约70%解释力）。不同表示下最有效的差异度量不同：手工特征用CMD（r~0.86），VAE空间用log-Wasserstein（r~-0.90），分割潜变量用log-MMD（r~-0.92）。厂商效应与数据集混杂。域偏移是结构化的、可测量的，可通过几何预处理和表示特定的迁移风险估计来缓解。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 跨数据集泛化是超声心动图左心室分割临床部署的主要障碍，但域偏移的来源很少被解耦。本文旨在厘清域偏移的结构，并探索能否在部署前估计迁移性能下降。

Method: 使用六种超声心动图数据集，采用手工超声描述符、VAE潜在特征和分割潜变量三种表示，结合几何感知预处理和强度z归一化，分析域偏移；通过CMD、log-Wasserstein、log-MMD等差异度量预测绝对Dice下降，并评估无LV及扇形特征的变体。

Result: 几何感知预处理显著改善多个迁移不良案例；强度z归一化对数据集可分离性影响极小（<0.005）；预测绝对Dice下降的R²=0.612，MAE=0.082，Spearman ρ=0.681；无LV特征变体保留约70%解释力；不同表示下最优差异度量不同：手工特征CMD（r~0.86，R²~0.70），VAE空间log-Wasserstein（r~-0.90，R²~0.81），分割潜变量log-MMD（r~-0.92，R²~0.84）；厂商效应与数据集混杂。

Conclusion: 超声心动图域偏移是结构化且可测量的，几何感知预处理可部分减少其分割影响，且可通过表示特定的迁移风险估计提前预测性能下降。

Abstract: Cross-dataset generalisation remains a major barrier to clinical deployment of echocardiographic left ventricular segmentation, yet the sources of this shift are rarely disentangled. We examined whether transfer degradation could be estimated before deployment using handcrafted ultrasound descriptors, VAE latent features, and segmentation-derived latent features across six echocardiographic datasets. Geometry-aware preprocessing substantially improved several poor transfer cases, suggesting that much of the apparent domain shift reflects field-of-view and framing inconsistencies rather than intrinsic acoustic differences alone. Intensity z-normalisation changed dataset separability by less than 0.005, indicating that brightness and contrast are not the dominant shift axis. Absolute Dice drop on held-out source-target pairs was predicted with an R-squared value of 0.612, an MAE of 0.082, and a Spearman rho of 0.681. The variant without LV and fan-shaped features retained approximately 70% of this explanatory power, supporting mask-free transfer-risk monitoring. The most informative discrepancy measure depended on the representation, with CMD strongest in z-normalised handcrafted features, with an absolute r of approximately 0.86 and an R-squared value of approximately 0.70; log-Wasserstein strongest in VAE space, with an r of approximately -0.90 and an R-squared value of approximately 0.81; and log-MMD strongest in LV-segmentation latent features, with an r of approximately -0.92 and an R-squared value of approximately 0.84. Apparent vendor effects were largely dataset-confounded. Echocardiographic domain shift is therefore structured and measurable, and its impact on segmentation can be partly reduced through geometry-aware preprocessing and anticipated using representation-specific transfer-risk estimation.

</details>


### [10] [A Unified Variational Framework for Deep Weakly Supervised Image Segmentation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.19669)
*Yin King Chu,Lingfeng Li,Sung Ha Kang,Jianping Zhang,Xue-Cheng Tai*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 提出一个统一的变分框架，用于在稀疏像素级监督下进行图像分割。该方法基于带光滑周长正则化的单纯形约束Potts模型，形成凸且光滑的能量泛函，可作为弱监督深度学习中的训练损失或通过迭代方法高效优化。通过再生核希尔伯特空间（RKHS）中的函数扩展问题构建模糊隶属度函数，从而将稀疏标签纳入数据保真项，有效捕获非均匀强度统计信息。导出的离散损失在训练标准网络时表现出鲁棒性，在无需真实分割图像的情况下，相比非训练基线和部分交叉熵（PCE）基线有一致改进，性能相当。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 在稀疏像素级监督（如弱监督学习）下进行图像分割时，如何有效利用有限的标签信息并捕获图像的非均匀强度统计特性是一个挑战。现有方法要么无法充分利用稀疏标签，要么需要完整的地真分割图像。本文旨在提出一个统一的、可微的变分框架，将稀疏标签集成到凸优化框架中，既可作为深度学习训练损失，也可直接通过迭代方法优化。

Method: 1. 建立基于单纯形约束Potts模型和光滑周长正则化的变分框架，能量泛函凸且光滑。2. 通过RKHS函数扩展问题构建模糊隶属度函数，将稀疏像素标签转化为连续函数，从而在数据保真项中有效利用稀疏监督信息，处理非均匀强度统计。3. 将该变分模型离散化，得到可微的损失函数，可用于训练标准深度神经网络（如CNN），或直接使用迭代优化算法（如梯度下降）求解。

Result: 实验表明，所提出的离散损失在训练标准网络时具有鲁棒性，在多个分割任务上相比非训练基线和部分交叉熵（PCE）基线有一致的性能提升。在无需完整地真分割图像的情况下，该方法能达到与使用完整监督信息的方法相当的性能。

Conclusion: 本文提出的统一变分框架为稀疏监督图像分割提供了一种有效且鲁棒的解决方案。通过将稀疏标签通过RKHS扩展融入凸变分模型，不仅能够处理非均匀强度统计，还使得该能量泛函既可作为深度学习损失函数（端到端训练），也可作为独立优化目标。该方法在不牺牲性能的前提下减少了对密集标注的依赖，具有实际应用价值。

Abstract: We propose a unified variational framework for image segmentation under sparse pixel-level supervision. Our method is based on a simplex-constrained Potts model with a smooth perimeter regularizer, yielding a convex, smooth energy functional that can be used as a training loss in weakly supervised deep learning paradigms or optimized efficiently using iterative methods. Sparse labels are incorporated into the data fidelity term by constructing a fuzzy membership function via a function extension problem in a Reproducing Kernel Hilbert Space (RKHS), which can effectively capture inhomogeneous intensity statistics. The derived discrete loss for training standard networks demonstrates robustness and consistent improvements over non-training and partial cross-entropy (PCE) baselines in experiments, achieving comparable performance without requiring ground-truth segmentation images.

</details>


### [11] [PhenSPINE: A Standardized Benchmark for Spine Pathology Diagnosis](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.19696)
*Duong Ngoc Vu,Hai Son Nguyen,Trong-Nghia Nguyen,Bien Tran Van,Trang Mai Xuan,Huan Vu,Thien Van Luong*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文提出 PhenSPINE，一个包含 16,813 张 MRI 图像（来自 250 名患者）的脊柱病理诊断数据集，并通过结合卷积骨干网络与位置编码机制建立诊断基准。实验表明，单序列 Sagittal T2-weighted 的 Macro F1-score 达 50.31%，优于多序列融合策略，为脊柱 MRI 分析中的序列选择提供了重要参考。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有自动化脊柱病理诊断系统缺乏多样、高质量的标准基准数据集，阻碍了深度学习在该领域的进展。

Method: 构建 PhenSPINE 数据集（16,813 张图像，250 名患者），提出结合最先进卷积骨干网络与位置编码机制的诊断基准，显式建模椎间盘的解剖位置信息，并在四种标准 MRI 序列上评估。

Result: Sagittal T2-weighted 序列在 Macro F1-score 上达到 50.31%，为最佳单序列结果；多序列融合策略因图像受到周围解剖区域噪声干扰而表现更差。

Conclusion: 该工作建立了脊柱病理诊断的稳健基线，并揭示了序列选择的关键影响：单序列（尤其是 Sagittal T2-weighted）优于多序列融合，为后续研究提供了重要指导。

Abstract: The accurate diagnosis of spinal pathologies depends heavily on radiological interpretation, yet automated systems are hindered by the lack of diverse, high-quality benchmarks. In this study, we present PhenSPINE, a Magnetic Resonance Imaging dataset comprising 16,813 images from 250 patients, curated to facilitate advanced deep learning research. We propose a robust diagnostic benchmark that integrates state-of-theart convolutional backbones with a Positional Encoding mechanism to explicitly model the anatomical context of intervertebral discs. Evaluating across four standard MRI sequences, our experiments demonstrate that the Sagittal T2-weighted sequence offers the most robust diagnostic value, achieving a superior Macro F1-score of 50.31%. We find that multisequence fusion strategies yield inferior performance compared to this single-sequence baseline, as the images across sequences in our dataset are significantly compromised by noise interference from surrounding anatomical regions. This work establishes a robust baseline and offers critical insights into sequence selection for spine analysis.

</details>


### [12] [SafeGen: Goal-Conditioned Video Diffusion of Safety-Critical Scenarios for VLM-Based Autonomous Driving](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.19701)
*Jiangfan Liu,Zexuan Cui,Tianyuan Zhang,Zonglei Jing,Zonghao Ying,Yaoyuan Zhang,Jiakai Wang,Xiaoqi Jiang,Aishan Liu,Xianglong Liu*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: SafeGen是一个面向VLM自动驾驶系统的安全关键场景生成框架，它将场景生成建模为目标条件扩散过程，利用预定义的灾难性终态作为监督信号，通过上下文接地终态推理和终态条件视频演化两个核心模块，生成具有视觉合理性和时序连贯性的高风险驾驶场景。实验表明，该方法在多个VLMAD基准上相比SOTA有显著提升，且微调后的VLMAD在真实场景中性能提升15.9%。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有安全关键场景生成方法严重依赖基于模拟器的流程，存在显著的sim-to-real差距，无法捕捉真实、多样且不可预见的人车交互动态。随着VLM被越来越多地部署在自动驾驶系统中，迫切需要一种能生成符合现实物理规律、具有视觉真实感的安全关键场景的评估方法。

Method: SafeGen包含两个核心模块：1) 上下文接地终态推理（Context Grounded End State Reasoning）：利用VLM分析良性驾驶上下文，推断人车交互中的潜在脆弱性，生成结构化的终态规格说明；2) 终态条件视频演化（End State Conditioned Video Evolution）：通过深度感知几何投影在场景中实例化高风险智能体，再通过边界条件扩散生成具有一致运动模式和时序连贯性的中间帧，将语义威胁转化为物理上合理的视觉动态。

Result: 在3个VLMAD基准上，SafeGen使Judge Overall Score（利用VLM评估VLMAD理解和决策能力的指标）相比SOTA基线平均提升24.25%。此外，使用生成场景微调VLMAD后，在真实驾驶场景中的性能平均提升15.9%。

Conclusion: SafeGen通过目标条件扩散框架有效弥合了sim-to-real差距，生成的安全关键场景既具有视觉真实感又符合物理规律，能够显著提升VLMAD的安全评估效果和模型性能。该方法为解决自动驾驶中长尾安全场景的评估与训练提供了新思路。

Abstract: VLMs are increasingly deployed in AD systems, creating an urgent need for rigorous safety evaluation under rare yet safety-critical scenarios. Among these, interactions with vulnerable road users represent a major source of real-world failures. However, existing safety-critical scenario generation methods predominantly rely on simulator-based pipelines, which suffer from a substantial sim-to-real gap and often fail to capture realistic, diverse, and unforeseen human-vehicle interaction dynamics. We present SafeGen, a goal-conditioned diffusion framework for safety-critical scenario generation in VLMADs. Our key insight is to formulate scenario generation as a goal-conditioned diffusion process, where a predefined catastrophic end-state serves as a strong supervisory signal, guiding the generation of temporally coherent video trajectories that naturally evolve toward safety-critical outcomes. Building on this formulation, we introduce Context Grounded End State Reasoning, which leverages VLMs to analyze benign driving contexts and infer latent vulnerabilities in human-vehicle interactions, producing structured end-state specifications that induce high-risk scenarios. Conditioned on these targets, we further propose End State Conditioned Video Evolution, which grounds semantic threats into physically plausible visual dynamics. Specifically, we instantiate high-risk agents within the scene via depth-aware geometric projection, followed by boundary-conditioned diffusion to generate intermediate frames with consistent motion patterns and temporal coherence. Extensive experiments across 3 VLMADs demonstrate that SafeGen increases the Judge Overall Score, a metric using a VLM judge to evaluate VLMADs' understanding and decision-making, by 24.25% on average compared to SoTA baselines. Furthermore, fine-tuning a VLMAD improves performance in real-world driving scenes by an average of 15.9%.

</details>


### [13] [Point-Selection Fine-Tuning Framework for Robust Point Cloud Classification](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.19711)
*Da Li,Chang Ma,Dongfu Yin*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: PSFT是一种点选择微调框架，通过自适应选择最小影响点来抑制异常值，并结合提示生成和特征过滤，在保持参数高效的同时提升点云识别模型对噪声和损坏的鲁棒性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 噪声和损坏点会严重降低点云识别性能，特别是在困难的损坏设置下。全量微调3D预训练模型会放大异常值的影响并覆盖预训练时学到的鲁棒性先验，而朴素参数高效微调方法仍然对损坏token敏感。因此需要一种既能保持参数高效又能提升鲁棒性的微调方法。

Method: PSFT框架包含三个关键组件：(1) 点选择模块：从预池化特征中逐点估计影响力，自适应保留最小影响点以抑制异常值；(2) 提示生成分支：基于所选子集预测逐层提示token，注入冻结的骨干网络进行轻量下游适配；(3) 特征过滤器：包含瓶颈MLP变换和Beta门控残差混合，进一步减轻选择后残留噪声，在预测前细化patch-token表示。

Result: 在ModelNet-C和ModelNet40-C数据集上，PSFT在所有测试的3D预训练骨干网络上一致降低了损坏误差；在ScanObjectNN-C上，使用ULIP-2和Uni3D-B作为骨干时，PSFT在评估的微调策略中取得了最强结果。

Conclusion: PSFT通过点选择机制有效抑制异常值，结合提示微调和特征过滤，在保持参数高效的同时显著提升了点云识别模型在各种损坏场景下的鲁棒性。

Abstract: Noisy and corrupted points can substantially degrade point cloud recognition performance, especially under challenging corruption settings. In particular, full fine-tuning of 3D pre-trained models may amplify the influence of outliers and overwrite robustness priors learned during pre-training, while naive parameter-efficient adaptation remains sensitive to corrupted tokens. To address this issue, we propose PSFT, a point-selection fine-tuning framework that improves robustness while remaining parameter-efficient. PSFT first estimates point-wise influence from pre-pooling features and adaptively retains minimally influential points to suppress outliers. Based on the selected subset, a prompt generation branch predicts layer-wise prompt tokens and injects them into a frozen backbone for lightweight downstream adaptation. To further mitigate residual noise after selection, we append a lightweight feature filter with bottleneck MLP transformation and Beta-gated residual blending to refine patch-token representations before prediction. Extensive experiments show that PSFT consistently reduces corruption error on ModelNet-C and ModelNet40-C across all tested 3D pre-trained backbones, while achieving the strongest ScanObjectNN-C results with ULIP-2 and Uni3D-B among the evaluated tuning strategies. Our implementation can be found at https://github.com/CVChMA/PSFT/tree/master.

</details>


### [14] [A Unified Tokenization Framework for Pain Recognition using Heterogeneous 3D Modalities](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.19716)
*Stefanos Gkikas,Christian Arzate Cruz,Valentina Becchetti,Muhammad Umar Khan,Alessandro Giuseppi,Raul Fernandez Rojas*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 提出一个统一的 tokenization 框架，用于处理异质 3D 模态（面部视频和 fNIRS）的疼痛识别，无需为每种模态设计独立架构，在 AI4Pain 基准上达到最先进性能并支持实时评估。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 疼痛是一种复杂且普遍的现象，准确评估对临床管理和干预至关重要。现有计算疼痛识别系统通常需要为每种模态设计独立的架构或手工设计的归纳偏置，缺乏统一的处理流程。

Method: 提出一个统一的 tokenization 框架，将异构 3D 模态（行为数据如面部视频、脑活动数据如 fNIRS）映射到共享的 token 空间，同时保留空间、时间和时频结构。该框架提供单一处理流水线，无需为每种模态设计独立架构或手工归纳偏置。

Result: 在 AI4Pain 基准数据集上达到最先进性能，同时保持高计算效率，在 GPU 和 CPU 上均可实现实时评估。

Conclusion: 所提出的统一 tokenization 框架能够有效处理多种 3D 模态的疼痛识别任务，无需独立架构，兼顾性能与效率。

Abstract: Pain is a complex and pervasive phenomenon affecting a large percentage of the population, and accurate assessment is essential for effective clinical management and intervention. Computational pain recognition systems enable continuous monitoring, support clinical decision-making, and help mitigate pain-related distress and functional decline. This study introduces a unified tokenization framework for heterogeneous 3D modalities in pain recognition that provides a single processing pipeline across behavioral and brain-activity 3D data, without requiring separate architectures for each modality or handcrafted inductive biases. The framework preserves spatial, temporal, and time--frequency structure while mapping diverse inputs into a shared token space. Extensive experiments show that the proposed approach effectively processes facial videos and fNIRS data in both raw-signal and spectrogram-based representations. On the AI4Pain benchmark dataset, the proposed framework achieves state-of-the-art performance while maintaining high computational efficiency and enabling real-time assessment on both GPU and CPU hardware.

</details>


### [15] [ReFace: Reorganizing Facial Spatiotemporal Representations for Improved Pain Assessment](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.19722)
*Stefanos Gkikas,Yu Fang,Christian Arzate Cruz,Muhammad Umar Khan,Raul Fernandez Rojas*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 提出ReFace，通过将面部图像划分为四个空间象限进行标记化，而非整体处理，在AI4Pain数据集上以仅视频输入达到56.00%准确率，创下该基准协议下最高纪录；四象限配置与全脸输入使用相同像素预算却获得更高精度，单个象限仅用四分之一像素仍具竞争力。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 自动疼痛评估中面部视频的疼痛相关线索存在空间异质性，传统整脸处理方法难以有效捕捉局部疼痛特征，亟需一种能利用面部空间结构信息的方案。

Method: 提出ReFace空间重组流程：将面部输入在标记化前划分为四个空间象限（左上、右上、左下、右下），分别处理后再整合，而非将整张脸作为单一区域处理。

Result: 在AI4Pain数据集上，仅使用视频的测试准确率达56.00%，为固定AI4Pain基准协议下对比方法中的最高值；四象限配置与全脸输入总像素数相同但准确率更高；单象限区域仅用四分之一像素仍保持竞争力，计算成本大幅降低。

Conclusion: 空间重组在特定标记化设计下能有效提升面部疼痛评估性能，表明合理划分空间区域比单纯增加像素预算更重要。

Abstract: Automatic pain assessment from facial video remains challenging due to the spatial heterogeneity of pain-related facial cues. This study proposes ReFace, a spatial reorganization pipeline that divides facial input into four spatial quadrants before tokenization, rather than processing the entire face as a single region. Evaluated on the AI4Pain dataset, the proposed approach achieves $56.00\%$ accuracy on the test set using video only, achieving the highest reported accuracy under the fixed AI4Pain benchmark protocol among the compared methods. Notably, the four-quadrant configuration processes the same total pixel budget as the full-face input, yet achieves higher accuracy, suggesting that spatial reorganization can improve performance under the proposed tokenization design. A single quadrant region, processing just one quarter of those pixels, remains competitive at a fraction of the computational cost.

</details>


### [16] [An Exploratory Analysis of Pain Localization via Explainable Computational Modeling](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.19726)
*Ioannis Kyprakis,Stefanos Gkikas,Eric Nichols,Yu Fang,Manolis Tsiknakis*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 该论文系统比较了传统特征工程与深度序列学习在基于外周生理信号的自动疼痛定位任务上的表现。使用AI4Pain 2026 Challenge数据集（包含4种可穿戴模态），发现极端随机树方法取得最高宏F1分数0.539，超越最佳深度学习模型7.4个百分点，EDA频谱特征是最具判别力的特征。同时发现疼痛检测与定位之间存在26个百分点的性能差距，表明外周自主神经通路解剖弥散性在10秒分辨率下构成了根本上限。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 自动疼痛定位对于非语言患者具有重要临床价值，但目前缺乏系统研究。现有方法多依赖患者自我报告，无法适用于无法交流的患者。本文旨在探索基于外周生理信号的客观疼痛定位方法，并比较传统特征工程与深度学习方法在该问题上的有效性。

Method: 使用AI4Pain 2026 Challenge数据集，包含65名参与者在TENS诱导疼痛下同步记录的四种可穿戴模态信号（EDA、BVP、呼吸、SpO2）。构建115维手工特征集（涵盖时域、频域、模态特定及跨模态描述符），与端到端深度架构进行对比。采用主体独立的三分类疼痛定位设置，评估指标为宏F1分数。

Result: 极端随机树（Extremely Randomized Trees）取得最高宏F1分数0.539，比最佳深度学习模型高出7.4个百分点。EDA频谱特征是最具判别力的特征。所有模型在疼痛检测（F1=0.815）与疼痛定位（F1=0.552）之间存在约26个百分点的性能差距。

Conclusion: 传统特征工程方法在当前疼痛定位任务上优于深度序列学习，但疼痛定位与检测之间的巨大性能差距表明存在根本性上限，该上限源于外周自主神经通路的解剖弥散性以及10秒时间分辨率的限制。未来需要更高时间分辨率或空间信息来突破这一瓶颈。

Abstract: Automatic pain localization, which involves identifying the anatomical origin of pain from peripheral physiological signals without patient self-report, is a clinically critical but largely unaddressed problem, particularly for non-verbal patients. This paper presents a systematic comparison of classical feature engineering and deep sequence learning for subject-independent three-class pain localization using the AI4Pain 2026 Challenge dataset, which comprises four synchronously recorded wearable modalities: electrodermal activity, blood volume pulse, respiration, and peripheral oxygen saturation recorded from 65 participants under controlled TENS-induced pain. A 115-dimensional hand-crafted feature set spanning time-domain, frequency-domain, modality-specific, and cross-modal descriptors is benchmarked against end-to-end deep architectures. Extremely Randomized Trees achieves the highest macro-F1 of 0.539, outperforming the best deep model by 7.4 percentage points, with EDA spectral features emerging as the dominant discriminators. A consistent 26-point gap between pain detection (F1\,=\,0.815) and localization (F1\,=\,0.552) across all models points to a fundamental ceiling imposed by the anatomical diffuseness of peripheral autonomic pathways at 10-second resolution.

</details>


### [17] [Efficient Tracking and Understanding Object Transformations](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.19743)
*Yihong Sun,Bharath Hariharan*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: FluxGraph 提出了一种轻量级的物体状态变换追踪方法，通过利用 SAM2 内部的多掩膜不一致性作为触发信号，避免了对每一帧的密集实体分割和全场景追踪，在 VOST 上达到约 3.3 倍加速（相比 TubeletGraph），并在多个数据集上保持或提升性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有方法（如 TubeletGraph）在追踪物体状态变换时计算成本高昂，无法实时部署。其开销源于：（1）对每一帧都进行密集的实体分割，不论是否发生状态变换；（2）追踪场景中所有实体，计算成本随场景复杂度而非感兴趣的状态变换数量增长。

Method: 提出 FluxGraph，一种反应式（reactive）变体：利用 SAM2 内部的多掩膜不一致性（multi-mask disagreement）作为轻量级触发信号来检测状态变换，仅在必要时进行计算；同时取消了对视频中所有实体的追踪需求，仅关注与状态变换相关的实体。

Result: FluxGraph 在 VOST 数据集上比 TubeletGraph 快约 3.3 倍，同时提升了追踪性能并保持了状态图质量。在 VSCOS、M³-VOS 和 DAVIS17 数据集上获得 3.7-10.7 倍的持续加速，同时保持性能。

Conclusion: FluxGraph 通过反应式触发机制显著降低了物体状态变换追踪的计算成本，使得实时或近实时部署成为可能，同时不牺牲性能。代码已开源。

Abstract: Tracking objects through state transformations is essential for understanding real-world dynamics. However, existing methods are computationally expensive. TubeletGraph recently showed impressive capabilities, but its inference cost (~$4.4$ seconds per object-frame on VOST) precludes any real-time deployment possibilities. We observe that TubeletGraph's overhead arises from building a spatiotemporal partition of the input video: (1) entity segmentation is computed densely for every frame regardless of whether a transformation occurs, and (2) every entity in the scene is tracked, scaling cost with scene complexity rather than the number of transformations of interest. To address both, we propose FluxGraph, a reactive variant that uses SAM2's internal multi-mask disagreement as a lightweight trigger for transformation detection, and removes the need for tracking all entities in the given video. FluxGraph is ~$3.3\times$ faster than TubeletGraph on VOST while improving tracking performance and preserving state graph quality. Furthermore, we also observe consistent speedups of $3.7-10.7\times$ across VSCOS, M$^3$-VOS, and DAVIS17 while maintaining performance. Code is publicly available at https://github.com/YihongSun/FluxGraph.

</details>


### [18] [Extending a Large View Synthesis Model for Multi-view Panoptic Segmentation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.19765)
*Kwonyoung Ryu,In-Jae Lee,Jonghyun Jin,Hyunjee Lee,Jongmin Lee,Jaesik Park*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文发现大规模视图合成模型在RGB监督下学习到的空间对应关系可以泛化到非真实感信号（如二元编码的全景标签），并提出首个将冻结的视图合成模型用于3D场景理解的方法：通过将输入视图的全景标签编码为二元通道表示并传入同一模型，无需3D重建或分割训练即可渲染目标视图的语义分割。在ScanNet上分割质量与需要显式3D重建的高斯方法相当，新视图合成PSNR高出7 dB以上，且零微调跨数据集迁移至Replica仍表现更优。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 大规模视图合成模型在RGB监督下学会了精确的空间对应关系，且该对应关系可泛化到非真实感信号（如二元编码的全景标签）。现有方法通常需要显式3D重建或针对分割任务进行特定训练，而本文旨在探索能否直接利用冻结的视图合成模型进行3D场景理解（如全景分割），无需3D重建或分割训练。

Method: 提出一种全景分割流程：固定一个预训练的视图合成模型（如大型视图合成网络），将输入视图的全景标签编码为二元通道表示（binary channel representations），然后通过同一个冻结的视图合成模型进行跨视图注意力传播，从而渲染出目标视图的分割结果。整个过程无需3D重建，也不需要对视图合成模型进行任何分割相关的微调。

Result: 在ScanNet数据集上，该方法的分割质量与需要显式3D重建的高斯方法（如3D Gaussian Splatting）相当，但在新视图合成任务上PSNR高出7 dB以上。此外，该方法无需微调即可跨数据集迁移至Replica，并超越对比方法。

Conclusion: 视图合成模型学到的空间对应关系不仅可以用于RGB外观渲染，还可以直接用于传播任意逐像素标签（如全景标签），从而将视图合成模型从外观渲染扩展到3D场景理解领域。这一发现为利用预训练视图合成模型进行无重建、无分割训练的3D感知任务提供了新思路。

Abstract: Large view synthesis models synthesize novel views through cross-view attention without explicit 3D representations, and recent studies have shown that they learn accurate spatial correspondence from RGB supervision alone. We observe that this correspondence generalizes beyond appearance. When non-photorealistic signals such as binary encoded panoptic labels are passed through the model, they are propagated to novel views with consistent spatial structure. These results indicate that the correspondence learned for RGB view synthesis can also propagate view-independent per-pixel labels. From this observation, we present the first work to extend large view synthesis models beyond appearance rendering to 3D scene understanding. We propose a panoptic segmentation pipeline that reuses a frozen view synthesis model to propagate panoptic labels from input views to novel views, without 3D reconstruction or any segmentation-specific training of the view synthesis model. Given panoptic labels on the input views, we encode them into binary channel representations and pass them through the same model to render target-view segmentation. On ScanNet, our method achieves segmentation quality on par with Gaussian based approaches requiring explicit 3D reconstruction, while outperforming them in novel view synthesis by more than 7 dB. The label propagation also transfers across datasets, surpassing these approaches on Replica without any fine-tuning.

</details>


### [19] [Global Building Area Estimation Products: How Accurate Are They?](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.19766)
*Saad Lahrichi,Doa'a Allabadi,Kyle Bradbury,Jordan Malof*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 该论文对四种全球建筑栅格数据集（GHSL、TEMPO、GBA、Overture）进行了独立、全面且公平的准确性评估，利用全球多样化的手动标注建筑足迹数据集ORBITaL-Net作为真值，发现GBA或TEMPO通常表现最佳，但所有产品在非洲和亚洲以及高密度城市区域的准确性显著下降。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 尽管存在多个全球建筑栅格数据集，但缺乏独立、全面且公平的准确性评估，因此需要对这些产品的精度进行系统比较。

Method: 以ORBITaL-Net（全球多样化的手动标注建筑足迹数据集）为真值，评估GHSL、TEMPO、GBA和Overture四种产品；在多种空间分辨率网格上使用多项常规性能指标进行公平评估，并按地理位置、人口密度和收入群体对结果进行分层分析。

Result: GBA或TEMPO通常达到最高总体准确度（取决于具体评价标准）；所有产品在非洲和亚洲准确性显著较低；多数产品在高密度城市区域准确性也显著下降。

Conclusion: 当前全球建筑足迹产品的准确性在不同区域和城市密度下存在显著差异，尤其在非洲、亚洲和城市密集区表现不佳，提示需要进一步改进这些区域的建筑足迹测绘。

Abstract: Geo-spatial rasters of building footprint area are useful for a variety of tasks, such as monitoring urbanization, improving energy efficiency, and tracking greenhouse gas emissions. There are now multiple global building raster datasets, however there lacks an independent, comprehensive, and fair assessment of their accuracy. In this work, we evaluate the accuracy of four major global building products: Global Human Settlement Layer (GHSL), Microsoft's TEMPO (TEMPO), The Global Building Atlas (GBA), and Overture. As ground truth for assessing their accuracy, we use ORBITaL-Net, a globally diverse dataset of manually labeled building footprints. To ensure fairness, we evaluate products on grids of multiple spatial resolutions, and several conventional performance metrics. Our results indicate that either GBA or TEMPO generally achieves the highest overall accuracy, depending upon the particular evaluation criteria. We also stratify the accuracy of each product by several factors: geographic location, population density, and income groups. The results reveal that product accuracy can sometimes vary significantly with respect to these factors. Notably, all products are significantly less accurate in Africa and Asia. Most products also suffer significant accuracy reduction in high-density urban areas.

</details>


### [20] [DRGBT-1K: A Large-scale High-quality Benchmark for Dynamic RGBT Tracking](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.19772)
*Zhaodong Ding,Chenglong Li,Zeyu Ding,Futian Wang,Jin Tang*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文提出了DRGBT-1K，一个大规模高质量的动态RGBT跟踪基准，包含1045个真实世界序列和795K RGBT帧对，覆盖多种传感器模态变化和跨平台视角转换，评估了20种多模态跟踪方法，并提供了在线评估平台和排行榜。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有的RGBT跟踪基准仅考虑固定模态输入和固定观测平台，无法评估真实场景中传感器模态动态变化和观测平台切换（如无人机与手持设备协同）下的跟踪器鲁棒性，缺乏系统性的动态RGBT跟踪评估基准。

Method: 1) 构建DRGBT-1K基准：从无人机和手持RGBT设备采集1045个真实世界序列、795K帧对，涵盖多样化场景、视角变化、模态变化和外观不连续性。2) 提供精细标注：密集边界框、目标类别、挑战属性、帧级模态和平台标签，覆盖24类目标、15+场景和15种挑战属性。3) 统一评估20种多模态跟踪方法（常规RGBT、模态缺失RGBT、DRGBT跟踪器）。4) 发布非对齐版本UGVT-1K支持非对齐多模态跟踪和无人机-地面协同跟踪。5) 开发在线评估平台和排行榜。

Result: 构建了包含1045个序列、795K帧对的DRGBT-1K基准，标注了24种目标类别、15+场景类型和15种挑战属性，评估了20种代表性多模态跟踪方法，并提供了在线评估平台和排行榜。

Conclusion: DRGBT-1K填补了动态多模态跟踪领域缺乏系统性评估基准的空白，更符合真实世界的协同感知场景，为未来动态RGBT跟踪研究提供了标准化评估平台和数据基础。

Abstract: Dynamic RGBT (DRGBT) tracking aims to continuously localize a target when the available sensing modalities and observation platforms vary over time. Compared with conventional RGBT tracking with fixed RGBT inputs and a fixed observation platform, DRGBT tracking is more consistent with real-world collaborative perception systems, where targets may be observed by heterogeneous sensors from different viewpoints. However, existing benchmarks are still insufficient for systematically evaluating tracker robustness under real dynamic modality variations and cross-platform transitions. To address this limitation, we make the following contributions. 1) We construct DRGBT-1K, a large-scale high-quality benchmark for DRGBT tracking. It contains 1,045 sequences captured entirely in real-world scenarios and 795K RGBT frame pairs collected using UAVs and handheld RGBT devices, encompassing diverse real-world scenes, pronounced viewpoint changes, modality variations, and target appearance discontinuities. 2) We provide comprehensive annotations for fine-grained evaluation, including dense bounding boxes, target category labels, challenge attributes, frame-level modality labels and platform labels. DRGBT-1K covers 24 target categories, more than 15 scene types and 15 challenge attributes. 3) We establish a comprehensive benchmark by evaluating 20 representative multimodal tracking methods, including conventional RGBT trackers, modality-missing RGBT trackers, and DRGBT trackers under a unified evaluation protocol. 4) We release an unaligned version of DRGBT-1K and derive UGVT-1K to support broader research on unaligned multimodal tracking and UAV-ground collaborative tracking. 5) We develop an online evaluation platform for DRGBT-1K and provide a leaderboard that collects all methods evaluated on this benchmark.

</details>


### [21] [Look Before You Edit: Attention-Guided Camera Placement and Multi-View Alignment for 3D Gaussian Splatting Editing](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.19777)
*Jaeyeon Park,Taeho Kang,Youngki Lee*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: LB-Edit 是一种基于 3D 高斯溅射的文本驱动 3D 场景编辑框架，通过注意力引导的编辑相机放置（ACP）和多视图注意力对齐（MAA）两个核心组件，解决了编辑相机放置位置和跨视图编辑一致性问题，在少量视图（5 个）下实现了高指令保真度、多视图一致性和编辑局部性，并将延迟降低至现有方法的 1/7。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有的文本驱动 3D 场景编辑方法通常使用固定的训练相机视角来应用 2D 扩散编辑器，这限制了编辑的空间覆盖范围以及用户针对复杂场景中特定对象的自由编辑能力。LB-Edit 旨在解决两个耦合问题：如何放置编辑相机以实现局部编辑，以及如何使各视图的编辑相互一致以确保微调后 3D 场景的一致性。

Method: LB-Edit 包含两个主要模块：(1) 注意力引导的编辑相机放置（ACP）：通过探测扩散模型在多个候选相机距离下的自注意力和交叉注意力，找到注意力集中于感兴趣区域的最优距离，然后在该距离上放置一组紧凑且几何多样性的编辑相机；(2) 多视图注意力对齐（MAA）：沿两个轴引导编辑器跨视图执行相同编辑——通过令牌级对应共享自注意力特征来对齐外观，通过将交叉注意力图提升到 3D 高斯上作为共享的 3D 注意力场来对齐空间位置，从而抑制外观漂移和空间漂移。

Result: 在多个多对象和单对象场景上的实验表明，该方法在指令保真度、多视图一致性和编辑局部性方面获得了最高的用户偏好，仅使用 5 个编辑视图，并将延迟相比现有方法降低最多 7 倍。

Conclusion: LB-Edit 通过注意力驱动的相机放置和跨视图注意力对齐机制，以极少的编辑视图实现了高效且一致的文本驱动 3D 场景局部编辑，显著提升了编辑质量和计算效率。

Abstract: Text-driven 3D scene editing with 3D Gaussian Splatting (3DGS) typically applies a 2D diffusion editor to views rendered from fixed training cameras, limiting both the spatial coverage of edits and the user's freedom to target specific objects in complex scenes. We present LB-Edit, a framework that addresses two coupled problems: where to place editing cameras for localized edits, and how to make per-view edits agree with one another so that the 3D scene remains consistent after fine-tuning. First, Attention-Guided Editing Camera Placement (ACP) probes the diffusion model's self- and cross-attention at multiple candidate camera distances to find where attention is well-contained in the region of interest, then places a compact, geometrically diverse editing camera set at that attention-optimal distance. Second, Multi-view Attention Alignment (MAA) steers the editor toward the same edit across views along two axes: it aligns appearance by sharing self-attention features via token-level correspondence, and aligns spatial location by lifting cross-attention maps onto the 3D Gaussians as a shared 3D attention field, suppressing both appearance and spatial drift. Experiments on multi-object and single-object scenes show that our method achieves the highest user preference in instruction fidelity, multi-view consistency, and editing locality, using as few as 5 editing views and reducing latency by up to 7x over existing methods.

</details>


### [22] [Frequency-Hierarchical Active k-Space Sampling for Diagnostic MRI](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.19779)
*Ruru Xu,Kian Anvari Hamedani,Zhikai Yang,Ilkay Oksuz*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 提出了 HieraSample，一种利用频率层次结构的任务驱动主动采样框架，通过余弦退火课程降低加速倍数、Mamba策略选择高频坐标、双分类器指导奖励，在 fastMRI+ 膝盖基准上实现了与全采样相当或更优的 ACL 诊断性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有主动采样方法要么对低频和高频区域采用相同策略，要么将动作空间限制为整条笛卡尔填充线，导致高加速倍数下性能不佳。需要一种能利用频率层次结构（低频携带解剖上下文，高频携带病理细节）的采样方法。

Method: 框架包含三个关键组件：(1) 余弦退火课程，在 80 个采集步骤中逐步降低加速倍数从 20x 到 4x，同时每一步都保持低频盘被完全采样；(2) 基于 Mamba 的策略网络，从双分类器（疾病分类器和严重程度分类器）提取的特征中挑选单个高频坐标；(3) 奖励函数定义为每次动作后类别加权交叉熵的逐样本减少量，正奖励直接对应更自信的正确预测。

Result: 在 fastMRI+ 膝盖基准上，HieraSample 在 4x 到 10x 加速倍数下与全采样 oracle 在 ACL 诊断上持平，在 ACL 严重程度评估上比最近的笛卡尔基线提高了 20.4 AUC 点。

Conclusion: HieraSample 通过显式建模频率层次结构，实现了高加速倍数下的高质量主动采样，证明任务驱动方法能有效利用低频与高频信息的差异，显著提升加速 MRI 的诊断性能。

Abstract: Active sampling for accelerated MRI must distribute a tight sampling budget across spatial frequencies that carry very different kinds of information. Low frequencies hold most of the anatomical context; high frequencies carry the fine details that drive pathology assessment. Existing active samplers either treat both regions identically or restrict the action space to entire Cartesian rows, which forces a poor compromise at high acceleration. We propose HieraSample, a task-driven framework built around this hierarchy. A cosine-annealed curriculum lowers the acceleration factor from 20x to 4x across 80 acquisition steps while keeping a fully-sampled low-frequency disk at every step; a Mamba-based policy then picks individual high-frequency coordinates from features extracted by dual disease and severity classifiers. The reward is the per-sample reduction in class-weighted cross-entropy after each action, so a positive reward corresponds directly to a more confident correct prediction. On the fastMRI+ knee benchmark, HieraSample matches the fully-sampled oracle on ACL diagnosis from 4x to 10x acceleration, and improves on a recent Cartesian baseline by as much as 20.4 AUC points on ACL severity.

</details>


### [23] [WASABI: Whole-graph Assignment-based Stabilizer for lAne topology By Inter-frame tracking](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.19781)
*Tetsuhiro Uchida,Myu Sasaki,Kensho Nakajima,Yasuhiro Shimada,Toru Saito*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: WASABI是一个实时后处理管线，通过将车道段及其连接性（LCLC）作为联合跟踪目标，在帧内和帧间稳定自动驾驶中的车道拓扑输出，在10 Hz实时约束下显著提升了检测精度和时序稳定性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 自动驾驶需要理解道路作为可行驶车道及其连接性的图结构，但神经感知模型输出存在结构不稳定性（漏检、连接错误、过检测、标签闪烁等），需要一种能够在实时约束下稳定车道拓扑输出的方法。

Method: WASABI将车道段和LCLC连接性作为联合跟踪目标，整合了段跟踪与连接性、噪声鲁棒的拓扑感知精化，以及资源受限的实时设计（10 Hz / 20 ms / 最多200输入车道）。

Result: 在内部验证数据上，LCLC检测F1从0.834提升到0.948（+13.6%），中心线横向误差从2.50m降至0.95m，检测假阳性减少24.6%；LCLC切换率降低63.3%，边界标签闪烁率降低30.2%。

Conclusion: WASABI通过联合跟踪车道段及其连接性，在满足车载实时约束的同时，显著提升了车道拓扑检测的帧内精度和帧间时序稳定性，有效解决了神经感知输出的结构不稳定性问题。

Abstract: Autonomous driving requires understanding the road as a graph of drivable lanes and their connectivity, beyond the ego lane alone, to follow routes through intersections and reason about cross- and merging-traffic. Recent perception models infer such lane topology, i.e., lane segments together with their inter-lane connectivity (LCLC), from onboard sensors over a 360-degree BEV view. Due to neural perception's imperfections, their outputs retain structural instabilities such as missed detections, lost or incorrect LCLC, over-detection, and label flicker. This paper presents WASABI, a real-time post-processing pipeline that stabilizes lane topology outputs both within and across frames by treating lane segments and their LCLC connectivity as joint tracking targets, under onboard real-time constraints (10 Hz / 20 ms / up to 200 input lanes). The pipeline integrates segment tracking with connectivity, noise-robust topology-aware refinement, and a resource-constrained real-time design. On internal validation data (16 sequences), WASABI improves LCLC detection F1 from 0.834 to 0.948 (+0.114, +13.6%) and reduces centerline lateral error from 2.50 m to 0.95 m, while reducing detection false-positives by 24.6%. Temporal-stability metrics on the same data show LCLC toggle rate reduced by 63.3% and boundary-label flicker rate by 30.2%, confirming across-frame stabilization beyond per-frame accuracy.

</details>


### [24] [Physics-Aware Complex-Valued State Space Model with Scattering-Prior Feature Modulation for PolSAR Image Classification](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.19787)
*Fangyan Zhang,Fan Zhang,Shiqi Zhou,Jun Ni,Carlos López-Martínez,Qiang Yin*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: CV-SSMNet is a physics-aware complex-valued state-space network for PolSAR image classification that uses a complex-valued state-space model (CV-SSM) to capture long-range spatial dependencies while preserving amplitude-phase coupling, and encodes seven scattering priors as FiLM-style modulation signals to guide feature evolution. It achieves competitive accuracy, improved regional consistency, and better boundary preservation on L-band and P-band datasets.


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: Existing complex-valued networks for PolSAR classification preserve amplitude-phase information but are limited in long-range spatial dependency modeling and typically only incorporate polarimetric priors at input or shallow levels, resulting in insufficient physical knowledge guiding deep feature evolution.

Method: CV-SSMNet builds a complex-valued state-space model (CV-SSM) in the complex domain to capture long-range spatial dependencies while preserving polarimetric amplitude-phase coupling. It encodes seven physically meaningful scattering priors as FiLM-style modulation signals to adaptively recalibrate complex-valued representations during feature evolution. The network also integrates multi-scale complex convolutions, branch-wise CV-SSM encoding, prior-guided recalibration, and lightweight global context aggregation for physically guided representation learning from local to global context.

Result: Experiments on three L-band benchmark datasets and an additional P-band BIOMASS evaluation demonstrate that CV-SSMNet achieves competitive accuracy, improved regional consistency, and better boundary preservation compared to existing methods.

Conclusion: The results support the effectiveness of embedding polarimetric scattering mechanisms into complex-valued long-range GeoAI representation learning for PolSAR image classification.

Abstract: Polarimetric synthetic aperture radar (PolSAR) image classification is a representative task for physics-aware GeoAI, where land-cover semantics are closely coupled with electromagnetic scattering mechanisms. Many existing complex-valued networks can preserve amplitude-phase information, but they are often limited in long-range spatial dependency modeling and usually incorporate polarimetric priors only as input-level or shallow auxiliary features. As a result, physical knowledge is insufficiently used to guide deep feature evolution. To address this issue, this paper proposes CV-SSMNet, a physics-aware complex-valued state-space network with scattering-aware feature modulation for PolSAR image classification. The proposed method builds a complex-valued state-space model (CV-SSM) in the original complex domain to capture long-range spatial dependencies while preserving polarimetric amplitude-phase coupling. Meanwhile, seven physically meaningful scattering priors, are encoded as FiLM-style modulation signals to adaptively recalibrate complex-valued representations during feature evolution. CV-SSMNet further integrates multi-scale complex convolutions, branch-wise CV-SSM encoding, prior-guided recalibration, and lightweight global context aggregation, enabling physically guided representation learning from local scattering structures to global spatial context. Experiments on three L-band benchmark datasets and an additional P-band BIOMASS evaluation demonstrate that CV-SSMNet achieves competitive accuracy, improved regional consistency, and better boundary preservation, supporting the effectiveness of embedding polarimetric scattering mechanisms into complex-valued long-range GeoAI representation learning.

</details>


### [25] [Trace: A Taxonomy-Guided Environment for Multidomain Visual Reasoning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.19790)
*Md Tanvirul Alam*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 提出了 Trace，一个基于分类学引导的多领域视觉推理环境，通过将任务构建分解为场景语法和可执行任务程序，实现了视觉推理数据的可验证性和可复现性。在 64,000 个 Trace 实例上进行 RLVR 训练，在 24 个外部基准上的宏平均提升了 3.51%（3B 模型）和 4.06%（7B 模型）。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有的 RLVR 方法主要改善语言模型的推理能力，但将其扩展到视觉语言模型时，缺乏同时具备广泛性、精确可验证性和可复现性的训练数据，这成为主要的瓶颈。

Method: Trace 采用分类学引导的方法，将视觉推理任务构建分解为两个独立部分：场景语法（scene grammar）负责视觉实现，可执行任务程序（executable task program）负责答案计算。两者共享一个语义状态，共同决定渲染图像、提示词、类型化答案、验证器状态和可重放的实例轨迹。环境包含 277 个场景语法、11 个视觉领域，共 1,000 个任务。

Result: 在 64,000 个 Trace 实例上进行 RLVR 训练后，Qwen2.5-VL-3B 在 24 个外部基准上的宏平均提升 3.51 个百分点，Qwen2.5-VL-7B 提升 4.06 个百分点，证明广泛的程序化训练能够迁移到生成的训练分布之外。

Conclusion: Trace 提供了一种可扩展的范式，能够生成广泛、可验证且可复现的视觉推理训练数据，有效弥合了 RLVR 在语言模型和视觉语言模型之间的差距，并且训练带来的提升可以泛化到未见过的任务分布中。

Abstract: Reinforcement learning with verifiable rewards (RLVR) has substantially improved language-model reasoning, yet its extension to vision-language models remains constrained by the lack of training data that are simultaneously broad, exactly verifiable, and reproducible. We introduce Trace, a taxonomy-guided environment for multidomain visual reasoning. Trace factorizes task construction into a scene grammar and an executable task program, separating visual realization from answer computation. A shared semantic state determines the rendered image, prompt, typed answer, verifier state, and replayable instance trace. The resulting environment comprises 1,000 tasks over 277 scene grammars and 11 visual domains, with controlled semantic and visual variation. RLVR on 64,000 Trace instances improves the macro-average across 24 external benchmarks by 3.51 percentage points for Qwen2.5-VL-3B and 4.06 points for Qwen2.5-VL-7B, providing evidence that broad procedural training can transfer beyond the generated task distributions. Project page: https://maveryn.github.io/trace/.

</details>


### [26] [Lean-SAM2: Target-Anchored Memory and Encoder Acceleration for SAM2](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.19811)
*Xudong Ouyang,Wenlun Zhang,Yimin Xu,Huazhong Liu,Yunshan Zhong*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: Lean-SAM2 is a lightweight framework for efficient temporal promptable segmentation that integrates three mechanisms (TAMP, TCIM, TARR) to eliminate computational redundancies while maintaining accuracy, achieving speedups of ~1.4x on SAM2.1 models with improved J&F scores.


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: SAM2 suffers from heavy memory cross-attention overhead and redundant full-frame visual feature extraction, and existing efficiency methods (heuristic memory pruning, window-based sparse routing) degrade performance in complex scenes with occlusions and distractors.

Method: Three collaborative mechanisms: (1) Target-Anchored Memory Pruning (TAMP) safeguards target tokens by modulating attention with semantic consistency against prompt-derived foreground anchors; (2) Temporal Condensation with Insurance Memory (TCIM) condenses historical context via visibility-gated fusion and conditionally archives high-confidence entries; (3) Target-Anchored Risk-Aware Routing (TARR) selectively activates image encoder for target-related windows, with fallback full-frame refreshes during volatile transitions.

Result: On LVOSv2 validation, Lean-SAM2 achieves 1.412× and 1.417× speedups on SAM2.1-Large and SAM2.1-Base+, respectively, outperforming Efficient-SAM2 while boosting J&F scores by 5.0% and 3.6%.

Conclusion: Lean-SAM2 establishes a superior balance between accuracy and efficiency for temporal promptable segmentation, addressing vulnerabilities in complex scenarios.

Abstract: The Segment Anything Model 2 (SAM2) has advanced temporal promptable segmentation, yet its deployment remains hindered by heavy memory cross-attention overhead and redundant full-frame visual feature extraction. While recent methods explore efficiency via heuristic memory pruning and window-based sparse routing, they typically suffer from catastrophic performance degradation in complex segmentation scenarios replete with occlusions and distractors. To resolve these limitations, we propose \textbf{Lean-SAM2}, a holistic lightweight framework designed to address the above vulnerabilities while systematically eliminating computational redundancies. Specifically, Lean-SAM2 integrates three collaborative mechanisms: (1) Target-Anchored Memory Pruning (TAMP) safeguards target tokens against deceptive attention by modulating raw attention significance with semantic consistency against prompt-derived foreground anchors; (2) Temporal Condensation with Insurance Memory (TCIM) condenses historical context via a visibility-gated fusion while conditionally archiving high-confidence entries in a parallel insurance bank; and (3) Target-Anchored Risk-Aware Routing (TARR) selectively activates the heavy image encoder for target-related windows based on anchor similarity, utilizing a risk-aware fallback policy to trigger full-frame refreshes during volatile transitions. Extensive evaluations across multiple challenging benchmarks demonstrate that Lean-SAM2 establishes a superior balance between accuracy and efficiency. For example, on the LVOSv2 validation dataset, Lean-SAM2 achieves overall inference speedups of $1.412\times$ and $1.417\times$ on the SAM2.1-Large and SAM2.1-Base+, respectively, significantly outperforming Efficient-SAM2 while boosting the corresponding $\mathcal{J}\&\mathcal{F}$ scores by $5.0\%$ and $3.6\%$. Code is available at https://github.com/DeawhaleQwQ/Lean-SAM2.

</details>


### [27] [MoAKE: Toward Unified All-in-One Action Quality Assessment via Mixture of Action Knowledge Experts](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.19826)
*Huangbiao Xu,Huanqi Wu,Xiao Ke,Jiaxin Cai,Junyi Wu,Jinglin Xu*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种面向异构动作的统一质量评估框架MoAKE，通过混合动作知识专家和自适应时序建模，在单个模型中实现了对多种动作类型的通用评估，并在全包含、零样本和少样本设定下均取得了最优性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有动作质量评估（AQA）方法采用“一对一”范式，即对每种动作类型单独训练模型，这限制了实际部署（需预先知道动作类型来选择对应模型）且难以泛化到多样化的动作。为解决这些问题，本文探索了“全包含”（all-in-one）AQA任务，旨在用单一统一模型评估异构动作。

Method: 提出混合动作知识专家（MoAKE）框架。该框架学习互补的专家以在共享语义空间中捕获多样化的动作模式，并通过动态聚合机制根据输入动作自适应地组合专家知识。每个专家配备段感知原型（segment-aware prototypes）以处理不同时间长度，并设计了自适应帧内与帧间关系建模（AIISRM）模块来建模多粒度时序动态。

Result: 在三个长时数据集的全包含设定下，MoAKE显著优于现有方法；在三个短时数据集的零样本/少样本评估下也取得了一致的泛化性能。代码已开源。

Conclusion: MoAKE通过专家混合与动态聚合有效缓解了异构动作间语义差异导致的负迁移问题，实现了统一模型下的高效动作质量评估，并展现出良好的泛化能力。

Abstract: Action Quality Assessment (AQA) aims to objectively evaluate performance quality from action videos. Most existing methods follow a ``one-by-one'' paradigm, training a separate model for each action type. This setting limits real-world deployment, as it requires prior action-type knowledge to select the corresponding model and suffers from poor generalization across diverse actions. To address these limitations, we study the challenging task of all-in-one AQA, which aims to assess heterogeneous actions within a single unified model. We propose a novel Mixture of Action Knowledge Experts (MoAKE) framework, designed to mitigate negative knowledge transfer caused by large semantic discrepancies among actions. MoAKE learns complementary experts that capture diverse action patterns within a shared semantic space and dynamically aggregates their knowledge to adapt the assessment to the input action. Each expert is tailored with segment-aware prototypes to handle varying temporal lengths, together with an Adaptive Intra- and Inter-Segment Relationship Modeling (AIISRM) module to model multi-granularity temporal dynamics. Furthermore, we establish comprehensive benchmarks for all-in-one as well as zero/few-shot AQA. Extensive experiments on three long-term datasets demonstrate that MoAKE significantly outperforms existing methods in the all-in-one setting, while also achieving consistent generalization on three short-term datasets under zero/few-shot evaluation. Code is available at https://github.com/XuHuangbiao/MoAKE.

</details>


### [28] [Memory-Augmented Multimodal Large Language Models for Small Object Understanding in Streaming Aerial Videos](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.19857)
*Penglei Sun,Yehua Huang,Zhuoli Tao,Xiang Li,Runwei Guan,Yaoxian Song,Kaiyong Zhao,Henghui Ding,Bo Han,Yang Yang,Xiaowen Chu*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文针对无人机在线流式感知中的微小目标理解问题，从数据和模型两个角度提出解决方案：构建了首个像素级、开放词汇的无人机航拍微小目标描述与指代分割数据集DroneEyes，并提出了SkyAnchor多模态大语言模型，通过语义感知Token路由器和分层记忆库解决微小目标细节丢失和流式上下文遗忘问题。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有MLLM应用于无人机在线流式感知时面临两大挑战：(1) 航拍目标通常极小，而MLLM的视觉压缩对所有区域一视同仁，丢失了微小目标的细节；(2) 连续视频流需要历史帧上下文，但边缘设备资源受限无法存储全部历史，丢弃历史则导致目标漂移或消失。目前缺乏针对微小航拍目标的像素级开放词汇数据集和适配流式感知的MLLM方法。

Method: (1) DroneEyes数据集：首个像素级、开放词汇的航拍微小目标指代分割数据集，包含2140个高清视频和176,623对标注，覆盖目标描述和指代表达两类任务，提供密集逐帧掩码。(2) SkyAnchor模型：包含两个核心设计——语义感知Token路由器（Semantics-Aware Token Router），在减少视觉Token预算的同时保留小目标细节；分层记忆库（Hierarchical Memory Bank），使模型能在流式视频中持续跟踪目标。

Result: 构建了DroneEyes数据集（2140个高清视频，176,623对标注，密集逐帧掩码）；提出了SkyAnchor模型，通过语义感知Token路由和分层记忆库分别解决微小目标细节保留和流式上下文理解问题。

Conclusion: 本研究从数据和模型两个层面填补了无人机在线流式感知中微小目标理解的技术空白，DroneEyes为后续研究提供了基准，SkyAnchor为资源受限的无人机部署提供了可行的实时感知方案。

Abstract: Language-guided aerial perception aims to understand user-specified tiny targets in complex unmanned aerial vehicle (UAV) scenes. In real UAV deployment, the UAV must respond while it flies, so such perception runs in an online streaming manner, where frames arrive sequentially and the model responds to each one without access to future frames. However, applying current Multimodal Large Language Models (MLLMs) to this setting raises two challenges. First, targets viewed from the air are often tiny, yet the visual compression in existing MLLMs treats all regions equally and discards their fine-grained details. Second, understanding a continuous stream requires past-frame context, yet retaining the entire history is infeasible on resource-constrained onboard hardware, whereas discarding it causes the target to drift or disappear. We address the tiny object and streaming challenges from both data and method perspectives. From the data perspective, we present \textbf{DroneEyes}, the \textbf{first} pixel-level and open-vocabulary referring-segmentation dataset for tiny aerial targets, comprising $2,140$ high-definition videos and $176,623$ pairs across Object Description and Referring Expression tasks, with dense per-frame masks. From the method perspective, we propose \textbf{SkyAnchor}, an MLLM with two designs to the above challenges: a Semantics-Aware Token Router that preserves small-target under a reduced visual-token budget, and a Hierarchical Memory Bank that keeps the target consistently understood on streams.

</details>


### [29] [Robust Activation Map Rectification for Weakly Supervised Volumetric Segmentation: Temporal Coherence as a Free Lunch](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.19877)
*Renshu Gu,Jialiang Chen,Fei Gao,Hang Su,Jun Qi,Jiamin Xu,Yicheng Shen,Jiayu Zhang,Jiaxi Pan,Caiming Zhang,Gang Xu*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文提出一种无需训练、无需原型的弱监督分割框架，通过利用体素数据的时序和结构一致性来修正不可靠的类激活图（CAM），核心包括方差缩减激活聚合（VRAA）和双向极值修正（BER），在多个公开数据集上显著提升性能（Dice提升20%，mIoU提升40%），推理速度提升5倍以上。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 弱监督分割中CAM常含噪声且易出现灾难性失败，现有方法通过额外训练阶段或原型学习来补救，但增加了计算开销并降低了鲁棒性。本文旨在不增加训练负担的前提下，利用体素数据中天然的时序和结构一致性作为隐式归纳偏置来稳定CAM。

Method: 提出一个无需训练和原型的框架，包含两个核心组件：1) 方差缩减激活聚合（VRAA），通过将CAM建模为高维随机向量，证明聚合可理论保证方差缩减，从而抑制噪声并增强一致语义信号；2) 双向极值修正（BER），通过双向极值检查检测并修正不合理激活，无需学习额外参数即可缓解极值失败。该方法与模型无关，可无缝集成到现有流程中。

Result: 在多个公开基准上大幅超越现有弱监督方法，Dice提升高达20%，mIoU提升高达40%，同时推理时间减少5倍以上。

Conclusion: 利用体素数据的一致性作为隐式归纳偏置，能够以原则性且高效的方式稳定弱监督体素分割，验证了无需额外训练即可有效修正CAM的思路。

Abstract: Weakly supervised segmentation relies heavily on class activation maps (CAMs) to initially localize target regions. However, CAMs are often noisy and prone to catastrophic failures. Existing remedies typically introduce additional training stages or prototype learning, increasing computational cost and reducing robustness. In this paper, we propose a training-free prototype-free framework that rectifies unreliable CAMs by exploiting temporal and structural coherence in volumetric data as a free lunch. Our approach is built on two key components. First, we introduce Variance-Reduced Activation Aggregation (VRAA) which suppresses noise and amplify coherent semantic signals. We provide a theoretical justification by modeling CAMs as high-dimensional random vectors and show that aggregation yields provable variance reduction. Second, we design a Bidirectional Extremity Rectification (BER) mechanism that detects and rectifies implausible activations through bidirectional extremity checks, effectively mitigating extreme-value failures without learning additional parameters. Our method is model-agnostic and can be seamlessly integrated with existing pipelines. Extensive experiments on multiple public benchmarks demonstrate substantial improvements over state-of-the-art weakly supervised methods, achieving up to 20% Dice and 40% mIoU gains while reducing inference time by more than 5 times. These results indicate that leveraging coherence as an implicit inductive bias yields a principled and efficient approach to stabilizing weakly supervised volumetric segmentation. Our code will be available.

</details>


### [30] [Current Injection Spiking Neural Network for Infrared and Visible Image Fusion](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.19879)
*Rui Zhao,Zhuoyuan Li,Wenrui Li,Yanchen Dong,Yajing Zheng,Giuseppe Valenzise,Weisi Lin*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 提出CIS-Fuse，一种基于脉冲神经网络（SNN）的红外与可见光图像融合方法，通过在膜电位层面进行跨模态融合，使用电流注入脉冲（CIS）算子实现，在保持与ANN方法相当融合质量的同时，大幅降低推理能耗（约一个数量级）。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有红外-可见光图像融合方法大多基于人工神经网络（ANN），计算密集且能耗高。脉冲神经网络（SNN）通过稀疏二进制脉冲通信，仅在脉冲发生时计算，具有更高能效。然而，二进制脉冲会丢弃低于触发阈值的互补信息，导致跨模态融合质量下降。本文发现膜电位在脉冲发放前保留了这些亚阈值响应，因此提出在膜电位层面直接进行跨模态融合，兼顾SNN的能效优势与融合质量。

Method: 提出CIS-Fuse，核心是电流注入脉冲（CIS）算子：将一种模态作为门控辅助电流注入另一种模态的驱动神经元，两者在脉冲发放前于膜电位层面整合，并引入逐通道可学习的注入强度以自适应调节调制幅度。基于CIS构建双向跨模态融合（BCMF）模块，并部署在具有非对称堆叠深度的双分支架构上，使两个分支形成明确的功能特化。

Result: 在四个IVIF基准数据集以及下游目标检测和分割任务上的实验表明，CIS-Fuse的融合质量与最先进的ANN方法相当，同时继承了脉冲计算的能效优势，推理能耗比同等规模的ANN方法DCEvo降低约一个数量级。

Conclusion: 本文证明通过在膜电位层面整合跨模态信息，可以克服SNN二进制脉冲丢失互补线索的缺陷，实现高效且高质量的图像融合，为SNN在多模态融合领域的应用提供了可行路径。

Abstract: Infrared and visible image fusion (IVIF) integrates the complementary information of two modalities into a single image with richer scene content. While existing methods are largely built on artificial neural networks (ANNs), which densely compute over all activations, spiking neural networks (SNNs) communicate through sparse binary spikes and compute only where and when a spike occurs, offering a route to more energy-efficient fusion. However, directly applying SNNs to IVIF creates a fundamental tension: cross-modal fusion relies on fine-grained responses from both modalities, whereas binary spikes can discard complementary cues that remain below the firing threshold. The membrane potential retains these subthreshold responses before firing, letting both modalities jointly shape the output when integrated at this stage. Building on this, we propose CIS-Fuse, a spiking network that performs cross-modal fusion directly at the membrane-potential level. At its core is the current injection spiking (CIS) operator, which injects one modality as a gated auxiliary current into the driving neuron of the other, so the two integrate before spike firing, with a per-channel learnable injection strength that adaptively regulates the modulation magnitude. Building on CIS, we construct a bidirectional cross-modal fusion (BCMF) module and deploy it on a dual-branch architecture with asymmetric stacking depths, where the two branches develop a clear functional specialization. Extensive experiments on four IVIF benchmarks and on downstream detection and segmentation show that CIS-Fuse achieves fusion quality on par with state-of-the-art ANN-based methods while inheriting the energy efficiency of spike-based computation, with roughly an order of magnitude lower inference energy than the similarly-sized ANN-based DCEvo. Code will be released upon publication.

</details>


### [31] [MTVDiff: Multimodal Conditional Latent Diffusion for Enhanced Thermal-to-Visible Face Translation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.19886)
*Zhiyuan Xia,Haojie Li,Jingyu Lin,Yiguo Qiao,Cunjian Chen*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 提出MTVDiff，一种多模态潜在扩散框架，通过深度和文本信息融合解决热成像到可见光人脸翻译中的几何不连续、语义属性不匹配和身份退化问题，在MCXFace和SpeakingFaces数据集上显著优于现有方法，FID降低48.3%，Rank-1准确率提升8.9%。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 热成像到可见光人脸翻译面临几何不连续、语义属性不匹配和身份退化等根本性挑战，现有方法难以有效解决这些问题。

Method: 提出MTVDiff多模态潜在扩散框架，包含三个核心模块：双分支交叉注意力融合（DBCAF）模块用于多尺度热-深度特征提取与融合；门控文本到视觉特征对齐机制用于语义引导生成；空间特征变换（SFT）用于自适应多模态先验集成。

Result: 在MCXFace和SpeakingFaces数据集上，该方法在图像质量和人脸验证性能上显著优于现有GAN和扩散方法，FID降低高达48.3%，Rank-1准确率提升高达8.9%。

Conclusion: 该工作为不同光照条件下的人脸识别系统提供了鲁棒解决方案，通过有效的多模态集成推进了跨光谱人脸图像翻译的最新技术。

Abstract: Thermal-to-visible face translation presents fundamental challenges including geometric discontinuities, semantic attribute mismatches, and identity degradation. We propose MTVDiff, a novel multimodal latent diffusion framework that synergistically integrates depth and textual information to address these limitations while preserving identity characteristics. The MTVDiff framework presents three core technical contributions: (1) a Dual-Branch Cross-Attention Fusion (DBCAF) module for multi-scale thermal-depth feature extraction and fusion; (2) a Gated Text-to-Visual Feature Alignment mechanism for semantically-guided generation; and (3) Spatial Feature Transformations (SFT) for adaptive multimodal prior integration. Extensive experiments on the MCXFace and SpeakingFaces datasets demonstrate that our multimodal approach significantly outperforms existing GAN-based and diffusion-based approaches across multiple metrics, achieving substantial improvements in both image quality and face verification performance, with FID reductions of up to 48.3% and Rank-1 accuracy improvements of up to 8.9\%. Our work provides a robust solution for face recognition systems operating under varying illumination conditions and advances the state-of-the-art in cross-spectral facial image translation through effective multimodal integration.

</details>


### [32] [LAVIFT: Latent-Action-Guided Vision Fine-Tuning for Surgical Interaction Recognition](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.19889)
*Jiajun Cheng,Subarna Tripathi,Sainan Liu,Xiaofan Yu,Shan Lin*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: LAViFiT 是一种潜在动作引导的视觉-语言微调框架，旨在提升手术器械-组织交互识别与特征定位。通过逆动力学模型、前向世界模型和补丁级 SIG 正则化器，有效解决了冻结编码器噪声残留和全微调特征错位的问题，在多个编码器和数据集上实现了更优的识别与对齐性能，并增强了交互区域的空间特征一致性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 将预训练视觉-语言模型（VLM）和视觉编码器应用于细粒度手术交互时面临两难：冻结编码器完全依赖预训练表示，会保留噪声且空间定位弱；全微调虽能改善全局语义对齐，但无法保证编码器在正确的动作区域学习有意义的特征。

Method: 提出 LAViFiT，一种端到端的潜在动作引导微调框架。包含三个关键组件：1）逆动力学模型，捕捉每个动作引起的视觉变化；2）前向世界模型，驱动编码器关注动作相关区域；3）补丁级 SIG 正则化器，防止局部特征坍塌，无需边界框或伪标签等额外监督。

Result: 在多种编码器和数据集上的实验表明，该方法在识别和图像-文本对齐方面均有提升；表示分析显示，特征在完整的器械-组织交互区域上具有更强的空间定位和更一致的空间特性。

Conclusion: LAViFiT 有效克服了微调视觉-语言模型用于手术交互的局限，通过潜在动作引导实现了更好的特征学习和空间对齐，为上下文感知手术 AI 和自主机器人手术提供了有力支持。

Abstract: Understanding instrument-tissue interactions is essential for context-aware surgical AI and autonomous robotic surgery. Pretrained vision-language models (VLMs) and vision encoders offer an alternative to conventional interaction classifiers by transferring broad visual and semantic knowledge. However, adapting them to fine-grained surgical interactions remains challenging: (1) freezing the vision encoder depends entirely on pretrained representations that may retain noise and provide weak spatial localization, while (2) full fine-tuning can improve global semantic alignment without ensuring that the encoder learns meaningful features in the correct action region. We address these limitations by introducing LAViFiT, an end-to-end latent-action-guided framework for vision-language fine-tuning. An inverse dynamics model captures the visual changes induced by each action, while a forward world model drives the encoder to represent action-relevant regions. A patch-level SIG Regularizer further prevents local feature collapse without additional supervision, such as bounding boxes or pseudo-labels. Experiments across multiple encoders and datasets improve recognition and image-text alignment, while representation analyses show stronger grounding over the complete instrument-tissue interaction region and more spatially coherent features.

</details>


### [33] [OSVE: One Step Video Editing with One Step Diffusion Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.19895)
*Habin Lim,Gyeong-Moon Park*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: OSVE是首个将一步式文本到图像(T2I)扩散模型成功应用于高质量视频编辑的框架，通过可学习编码器实现单步噪声预测、结构感知编辑损失保持几何结构、统一帧编辑确保时间一致性，以及滑动窗口策略维护长视频全局一致性，在达到或超越多步方法编辑质量的同时实现约155-171倍加速，为实时视频编辑应用铺平道路。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有基于扩散模型的文本引导视频编辑由于多步采样和反演过程计算成本高昂，速度极慢，难以实用化。作者旨在通过适配一步式T2I模型来解决视频编辑中的反演效率、可编辑性和时间一致性三大核心挑战，实现实时视频编辑。

Method: 提出OSVE框架：(1) 训练可学习编码器在单次前向传播中预测每帧的初始噪声，替代迭代反演；(2) 提出结构感知编辑(SAE)损失，在结构对齐图像对上训练编码器以保留源视频几何结构；(3) 提出统一帧编辑(UFE)，通过拼接帧潜在表示实现单步生成中的跨帧注意力；(4) 对长视频采用带锚帧的滑动窗口策略维持全局一致性。

Result: OSVE在编辑质量上达到或超越当前最先进的多步方法，同时运行速度快约155-171倍，显著提升了视频编辑的效率。

Conclusion: OSVE成功将一步式T2I模型适配到视频编辑任务，解决了反演、可编辑性和时间一致性问题，为实用化实时视频编辑应用奠定了基础。

Abstract: Text-guided video editing with diffusion models is impractically slow, hindered by costly multi-step sampling and inversion. We present OSVE, the first framework to successfully adapt one-step Text-to-Image (T2I) models for high-quality video editing, addressing the core challenges of inversion, editability, and temporal consistency. To bypass slow iterative inversion, we train a learnable encoder that predicts the initial noise for each frame in a single forward pass. This encoder is trained with a novel Structure-Aware Editing (SAE) loss on a curated dataset of structurally-aligned image pairs, teaching it to preserve the source video's geometry during edits.
