For temporal coherence, we introduce Unified-Frame Editing (UFE), a technique that concatenates frame latents to facilitate cross-frame attention in a single generation step. Furthermore, for long videos, a sliding-window strategy with an anchor frame maintains global consistency. Our extensive experiments demonstrate that OSVE achieves editing quality comparable or superior to state-of-the-art multi-step methods, while operating approximately 155--171 times faster. This breakthrough paves the way for practical, real-time video editing applications. Code is available at https://github.com/KU-VGI/OSVE.

</details>


### [34] [StrokeSeg2: Stroke Lesion Segmentation in Clinical Research Workflows](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.19901)
*Youwan Mahé,Axel Plessis,Stéphanie Leplaideur,Elise Bannier,Florent Leray,Francesca Galassi*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: StrokeSeg2 是一个轻量级、跨平台的 C++/Qt 框架，通过知识蒸馏进行架构压缩，并结合 ONNX Runtime 与 Float16 量化的推理优化，将原先 102.3M 参数的 nnU-Net 教师模型压缩为 0.84M 参数的学生模型（2.1 MB 磁盘占用），在保持鲁棒病灶定位和竞争性分割性能的同时，实现平均 84% 的推理时间缩减和超过 90% 的能耗降低，并打包为 Windows/macOS/Linux 独立安装程序，无需 Docker 或外部环境依赖，便于在临床研究环境中部署。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有基于深度学习的脑病变分割框架（如 nnU-Net）虽然性能卓越，但由于软件依赖和计算资源需求高，难以在临床研究环境中部署和使用。

Method: 提出 StrokeSeg2 框架：采用 C++/Qt 实现跨平台、模块化设计；通过知识蒸馏进行架构压缩（将 102.3M 参数教师模型压缩为 0.84M 参数学生模型）；使用 ONNX Runtime 结合 Float16 量化进行推理优化；打包为 Windows、macOS、Linux 的独立安装程序，提供图形界面和命令行界面，无需 Docker 或外部依赖。

Result: 架构蒸馏是实现效率提升的主要因素，带来超过 90% 的能耗降低和平均 84% 的推理时间缩减；0.84M 参数的学生模型磁盘占用仅 2.1 MB，同时保持鲁棒的病灶定位和具有竞争力的分割性能；在 CPU、集成 GPU 和独立 GPU 等异构硬件上均表现良好。

Conclusion: StrokeSeg2 成功将高性能的 3D 卒中分割流程转化为便携、可复现的临床应用，其轻量化和无依赖特性显著降低了在常规临床研究管线中的部署门槛。

Abstract: Deep learning frameworks like nnU-Net achieve state-of-theart brain lesion segmentation performance but remain difficult to deploy in clinical research environments due to, among other reasons, software dependencies and computational requirements. We introduce StrokeSeg2, a lightweight, modular, cross-platform C++/Qt framework designed to adapt resource-intensive 3D stroke segmentation pipelines into portable and reproducible applications. To improve compatibility with standard clinical workstations, we investigate the combined effect of architectural compression through knowledge distillation and inference optimisation using ONNX Runtime with Float16 quantisation. Across heterogeneous hardware configurations (CPU, integrated GPU, and dedicated GPU) architectural distillation emerged as the primary contributor to efficiency gains, contributing to over 90% reduction in energy consumption and an average 84% reduction in inference time. Specifically, we identify a 0.84M-parameter student model as the most favourable trade-off, reducing the original 102.3M-parameter teacher architecture to a 2.1 MB disk footprint while preserving robust lesion localisation and competitive segmentation performance. This small footprint supports the development of a self-contained installer for clinical workstation targets. Finally, StrokeSeg2 packages these optimisations into standalone installers for Windows, macOS, and Linux. By providing both graphical and commandline interfaces without Docker or external environment dependencies, StrokeSeg2 facilitates deployment of high-performance segmentation workflows for routine clinical research pipelines.

</details>


### [35] [MV-Bench: Benchmarking Multimodal Large Language Models for Coordinated Multi-View Interface Construction](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.19910)
*Yue Zhao,Hongxu Liu,Feiyu Wang,Xiaoyu Yang,Tong Ge,Zhen Yang,Chao Wang,Qiong Zeng*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 提出了MV-Bench基准，用于评估多模态大语言模型在协调多视图界面构建方面的能力。实验表明，当前最强模型在视觉布局复现上达到75.45%准确率，但数据绑定正确率仅21.71%，交互完整度仅11.68%，说明模型能复现视觉外观但难以生成数据语义和交互逻辑。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有评估主要聚焦单图表生成，忽视了需要联合推理数据语义、视图协调和交互逻辑的协调多视图界面构建任务，且缺乏专门基准进行系统评估。

Method: 使用Tableau工作簿文件作为ground truth，通过多阶段流水线将其转换为可执行Web界面。基准包含92个基础界面和1048个验证实例，涵盖多种图表类型、数据集和交互模式，并提供代码、渲染界面、数据集和交互标注。

Result: 评估了五种最先进MLLM，最强模型在视觉布局复现上75.45%，数据绑定正确率21.71%，交互完整度11.68%。迭代优化能提高代码可执行性，但无法显著缩小数据绑定和交互生成的差距。

Conclusion: 当前MLLM能复现视觉外观，但在生成协调多视图界面所需的数据语义和交互逻辑方面仍十分有限。

Abstract: Multimodal large language models (MLLMs) are increasingly expected to automate visualization development by generating code directly from visual designs. However, existing evaluations mainly focus on single-chart generation and overlook coordinated multi-view interface construction, which requires joint reasoning about data semantics, view coordination, and interaction logic. Consequently, MLLM capabilities in this setting remain underexplored, and the field lacks a dedicated benchmark for systematic assessment. We introduce MV-Bench, a benchmark for evaluating MLLMs on coordinated multi-view interface construction. Instead of relying on incomplete or inconsistent open-source implementations, we use Tableau workbook files as ground truth because they explicitly encode data bindings, visual mappings, and interactions. We develop a multi-stage pipeline that converts these specifications into executable web interfaces through structured intermediate representations. The benchmark contains 92 base interfaces and 1,048 verified instances created by recombining chart types, datasets, and interaction patterns. Each instance includes executable code, a rendered interface, a dataset, and interaction annotations. We evaluate five state-of-the-art MLLMs in a single-pass setting using metrics for visual fidelity, data binding correctness, and interaction completeness. The strongest model achieves 75.45 percent accuracy in visual layout reproduction, but only 21.71 percent in data binding and 11.68 percent in interaction completeness. These results show that current MLLMs can reproduce visual appearance but remain limited in generating the data semantics and interactive logic required by coordinated multi-view interfaces. Iterative refinement improves code executability but does not substantially reduce the gap in data binding and interaction generation.

</details>


### [36] [LoRFT: Benchmarking Long-Range Vehicle Trajectory Reconstruction from Fixed Highway Cameras](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.19911)
*Yufan Zhu,Kefu Yi,Xueju Zhang,Yunyang Tian,Long Chen,Zixuan Xiao*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文提出LoRFT基准和Map-RSTNet模型，用于从固定高速公路摄像头中恢复远距离车辆轨迹。Map-RSTNet通过道路几何对齐的状态空间和动态刷新局部道路几何，在LoRFT上相比强基线提升了ADE、FDE和5秒RMSE分别达11.0%、15.4%和10.5%。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 固定摄像头拍摄远处车辆时，由于透视压缩和尺度衰减，自动跟踪轨迹容易断裂或提前终止，而实际上利用相邻帧的运动一致性仍可识别。因此需要从可靠的近场轨迹恢复远距离轨迹的延续。

Method: 提出Map-RSTNet，一种地图感知的残差序列到序列模型，在道路几何对齐的状态空间中重建远距离轨迹，并在解码过程中动态刷新局部道路几何。同时构建LoRFT基准，包含22个高速场景、366,109帧、6,601条人工验证轨迹等。

Result: 在LoRFT基准上，Map-RSTNet相比最强基线在ADE、FDE和5秒RMSE上分别降低11.0%、15.4%和10.5%，证明道路几何感知的重建能有效延长固定摄像头基础设施的可用轨迹记录。

Conclusion: LoRFT为远距离车辆轨迹重建提供了可复现的测试平台，Map-RSTNet展示了道路几何信息在该任务中的关键作用，为从现有固定摄像头基础设施中扩展可用轨迹记录提供了可行方案。

Abstract: Long-range vehicle trajectories provide important spatio-temporal evidence for traffic safety analysis, autonomous driving evaluation, and data-driven traffic management, yet continuously recovering them from fixed highway cameras remains difficult. As vehicles recede into distant road regions, perspective compression and scale decay often fragment or prematurely terminate automatic tracklets, even when their continuation remains identifiable from motion consistency across neighboring frames. We formulate this problem as recovering the far-range continuation of a vehicle trajectory from a reliable near-field tracklet. We introduce LoRFT, to our knowledge the first open benchmark dedicated to long-range vehicle trajectory reconstruction from fixed highway cameras. LoRFT comprises 22 expressway surveillance scenes, 366,109 video frames, 6,601 manually verified trajectories, 2,694,889 bounding boxes, road-geometry annotations, scene-level splits, and evaluation scripts. We further propose Map-RSTNet, a map-aware residual sequence-to-sequence model that reconstructs distant trajectories in a road-geometry-aligned state space and dynamically refreshes local road geometry during decoding. On LoRFT, Map-RSTNet reduces ADE, FDE, and 5-second RMSE by 11.0%, 15.4%, and 10.5%, respectively, relative to the strongest baseline. These results demonstrate that road-geometry-aware reconstruction can extend usable trajectory records from existing fixed-camera infrastructure. LoRFT provides a reproducible testbed for long-range vehicle trajectory reconstruction.

</details>


### [37] [WearWow: Native 2K Multi-Garment Virtual Try-On via Adaptive Token Packing and Preference Alignment](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.19923)
*Xujie Zhang,Runyan Du,Song Chang,Jiang Li,Dongliang Shao,Liping Wu,Wei Luo,Xiaochao Qu,Luoqi Liu,Xiaodan Liang*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: WearWow 是一个端到端、无遮罩的生成框架，用于超高分辨率多服装虚拟试穿。它通过自适应2D Token打包（ATP）解决内存爆炸问题，通过多维试穿奖励（MTR）系统解决纹理退化问题，并构建了WearWow-2K数据集，在原生2K多服装合成上达到SOTA。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有虚拟试穿方法在2K超高分辨率多服装合成时面临两大瓶颈：(1) 2K条件导致O(N²)内存爆炸；(2) 扩散模型的频谱偏差导致高频织物细节被过度平滑，无法生成逼真的纹理。

Method: 提出WearWow框架，包含三个核心创新：(1) 自适应2D Token打包（ATP），利用服装稀疏性将异构物品算法性地打包到统一2D画布上，并剪枝无信息的背景token，减小有效序列长度和内存开销，同时保留2D空间先验；(2) 多维试穿奖励（MTR）系统，联合语义引导奖励（显式驱动触觉恢复）和布料分布奖励（隐式锚定物理分布），有效缓解奖励黑客问题；(3) 构建WearWow-2K数据集，包含原生2K三元组，提供物理正确的空间交互，支持无遮罩生成。

Result: 大量实验表明，WearWow在原生2K多服装合成上超越了现有商业基线，建立了新的SOTA。

Conclusion: WearWow通过ATP和MTR有效解决了高分辨率多服装试穿中的内存爆炸和纹理退化问题，结合高质量的WearWow-2K数据集，实现了端到端无遮罩的超高分辨率虚拟试穿，为数字时尚领域提供了新范式。

Abstract: Synthesizing native 2K multi-garment virtual try-on is a formidable frontier in digital fashion, critically bottlenecked by two fundamental limitations: the O(N^2) memory explosion induced by 2k conditions, and the spectral bias of diffusion models that over-smooths high-frequency fabric details. We present WearWow, an end-to-end, mask-free generative framework that pioneers ultra-high-resolution multi-garment synthesis. To mitigate the memory explosion , we propose Adaptive 2D Token Packing (ATP). ATP leverages inherent garment sparsity to algorithmically pack heterogeneous items onto a unified 2D canvas and prune uninformative background tokens, minimizing the effective sequence length and subsequent memory overhead while rigorously preserving 2D spatial priors. To rectify texture degradation, we introduce the Multi-dimensional Try-on Reward (MTR) system. MTR synergizes a Semantic Guidance Reward to explicitly drive tactile restoration with a Cloth Distribution Reward to implicitly anchor the physical distribution, a joint formulation that effectively mitigates the severe reward hacking. Furthermore, we curate WearWow-2K, an extreme-quality dataset comprising native 2K triplets, providing physically correct spatial interactions that naturally empower the model's mask-free generation. Extensive experiments demonstrate that WearWow establishes a new state-of-the-art, exceeding existing commercial baselines in native 2K multi-garment synthesis.

</details>


### [38] [G-MAD: A Game-Based Data Generation Framework for Multi-View RGB-T Aerial Object Detection](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.19942)
*Yechan Kim,JongHyun Park,Dongho Yoon,Namhoon Jung,Moongu Jeon*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 提出G-MAD框架，利用Arma3生成同步多视角RGB-T航空目标检测数据，并构建了大规模多视角航空RGB-T目标检测基准AMOD，解决了真实数据集视角控制有限、RGB-T对齐不完美、标注成本高等问题。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 真实航空数据集的构建存在视角控制有限、RGB-T模态对齐不完美以及标注成本高昂等关键局限，需要一种可控、低成本且自动标注的合成数据生成方案来推动航空目标检测中视角变化、多模态融合和合成到真实迁移的研究。

Method: 基于Arma3游戏引擎开发开源框架G-MAD，支持结构化场景规范、可控多视角相机放置、同步可见光/热红外图像捕获，并利用引擎级几何元数据自动生成边界框标注。

Result: 构建并公开了AMOD——一个大规模多视角航空RGB-T目标检测基准数据集，同时开源了G-MAD框架的源代码和数据集。

Conclusion: G-MAD框架有效解决了真实航空数据集的构建瓶颈，为视角变化、多模态融合和合成到真实迁移等航空目标检测研究提供了可控的实验平台和数据支撑。

Abstract: This work introduces G-MAD, an open-source framework that uses Arma3 to generate synchronized multi-view RGB-T data for aerial object detection. G-MAD addresses key limitations of real-world aerial dataset construction, including limited viewpoint control, imperfect RGB-T alignment and high annotation cost. The framework supports structured scenario specification, controllable multi-view camera placement, simultaneous visible/thermal capture, and automatic bounding box annotation using engine-level geometric metadata. These capabilities enable controlled studies of viewpoint variation, multi-modal fusion, and synthetic-to-real transfer in aerial object detection. Besides, using G-MAD, we construct and release AMOD, a new large-scale multi-view aerial RGB-T object detection benchmark. The source code and the dataset are available at https://unique-chan.github.io/G-MAD-Project.

</details>


### [39] [SIINR: Structurally Informed Implicit Neural Representations for super-resolution with uncertainty quantification of clinical quality diffusion MRI datasets](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.19943)
*Tom Hendriks,William Consagra,Anna Vilanova,Yogesh Rathi,Maxime Chamberland*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: SIINR是一个结合监督3D U-Net先验与自监督隐式神经表示的框架，用于临床dMRI数据的超分辨率重建，同时提供不确定性量化。该方法在空间和角度域联合建模，通过数据一致性约束和近似后验分布实现不确定性估计，在多个公开数据集上优于标准插值方法，并在多发性硬化症和脑病变等临床案例中展示了其传播强度变化和标记不确定区域的能力。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 临床扩散磁共振成像(dMRI)通常受限于低面外分辨率，导致结构信息退化，限制了高级分析的应用。现有方法缺乏在超分辨率过程中量化不确定性的能力，而这对临床可靠判读至关重要。

Method: SIINR框架包含两个核心组件：(1) 一个监督训练的3D U-Net作为高分辨率先验；(2) 一个自监督隐式神经表示(INR)融合高分辨率先验和原始低分辨率数据。INR在空间和角度域进行联合建模，强制执行数据一致性，并提供解析近似后验分布用于下游不确定性量化。

Result: 在多个开放访问dMRI数据集上，SIINR在定量误差指标和定性解剖保真度方面均优于标准插值方法。在包含多发性硬化症和脑病变的临床案例中，该方法能够有效传播强度变化并在挑战性场景中标记不确定区域。

Conclusion: SIINR是一个灵活、模块化的框架，可适应不同的上采样比例和下游任务，为增强临床dMRI数据质量和支持神经影像学指标的稳健解释提供了一种原则性方法。

Abstract: Diffusion Magnetic Resonance Imaging (dMRI) is a powerful tool for probing brain microstructure, but clinical acquisitions are often limited by low out-of-plane resolution, resulting in degraded structural information and reduced utility for advanced analysis. We introduce SIINR (Structurally Informed Implicit Neural Representations), a general framework for super-resoltion of clinical dMRI datasets while quantifying uncertainty in the reconstructed outputs. SIINR utilizes a supervised 3D U-net as a prior and combines it with a self-supervised implicit neural representation (INR) that fuses the high-resolution prior and the original low-resolution data. The INR enables joint modeling across spatial and angular domains, enforces data consistency, and provides analytic approximate posterior distributions for downstream uncertainty quantification. We validate the framework on a diverse set of open-access dMRI datasets, demonstrating that SIINR outperforms standard interpolation methods in both quantitative error metrics and qualitative anatomical fidelity. Experiments on clinical cases, including subjects with multiple sclerosis and brain lesions, illustrate the framework its ability to propagate intensity changes and flag uncertain regions in challenging scenarios. SIINR is flexible, modular, and can be adapted to different upsampling ratios and downstream tasks, providing a principled approach for enhancing clinical dMRI and supporting robust interpretation of derived neuroimaging metrics.

</details>


### [40] [ETPDesigner: Multi-Agent Orchestration for Interactive Multimodal Electronic Theater Program](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.19947)
*Mengtian Li,Xinru Guo,Xiaoru Lin,Xiao Rong,Zhifeng Xie,Chaofeng Chen*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 提出ETPDesigner，一个基于多智能体协作的框架，能够从原始戏剧脚本直接合成高质量的电子戏剧节目单（ETP），包含多页异构视觉资产，并支持语音交互的虚拟角色对话。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有文本到图像范式难以处理电子戏剧节目单（ETP）这类复杂设计任务，因为它们无法理解长上下文叙事，也无法在多页之间保持视觉一致性。

Method: 采用协作式多智能体框架，包括语义脚本分析、核心海报合成、功能背景生成和角色资产分层组合等专用智能体；通过全局风格锚机制从核心海报提取视觉先验以确保审美统一；集成肖像动画、定制语音合成和基于角色的大语言模型，实现实时语音交互。

Result: 构建了领域专用基准ETP-Pro，包含专业戏剧海报和高质量角色肖像。大量评估表明该方法在生成语义忠实、审美一致且高度交互的节目单方面具有优越性。

Conclusion: ETPDesigner成功解决了现有文本到图像模型在长上下文叙事理解和多页视觉一致性方面的不足，将静态节目单升级为沉浸式交互伴侣，显著提升了电子戏剧节目单的生成质量与交互体验。

Abstract: Electronic Theater Programs (ETPs) serve as critical promotional media in the performing arts, comprising a multi-page collection of heterogeneous visual assets such as theatrical posters, performance details, and character portraits. However, existing text-to-image paradigms struggle with such complex design tasks due to their inability to comprehend long-context narratives and maintain visual consistency across multiple distinct pages. To address this, we introduce ETPDesigner, a collaborative Multi-Agent framework that directly synthesizes high-quality ETPs from raw dramatic scripts. Emulating a professional design pipeline, our framework orchestrates specialized agents for semantic script analysis, core poster synthesis, functional background generation, and the stratified composition of character assets. Central to ETPDesigner is a global style anchor mechanism that extracts visual priors from the core poster to enforce strict aesthetic uniformity across all generated components. Furthermore, we elevate the ETP from a static publication to an immersive interactive companion. By integrating portrait animation, customized speech synthesis, and persona-grounded Large Language Models (LLMs), our system enables users to engage in real-time, voice-enabled conversations with the generated virtual characters. To rigorously benchmark this task, we construct ETP-Pro, a domain-specific benchmark of professional theater posters and high-quality character portraits. Extensive evaluations demonstrate our method's superiority in producing semantically faithful, aesthetically consistent, and highly interactive program sets.

</details>


### [41] [OffNadirLoc: Benchmark and Framework for Challenging UAV-to-Satellite Geo-Localization under Large Off-Nadir Views](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.19951)
*Qian Qiao,Wenye Liu,Ting Liu,Jiuhe Shu,Peng Wang*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 提出OffNadirLoc基准和ONLoc框架，通过结构感知上下文加权和视图一致学习策略，解决大离轴无人机-卫星图像跨视图地理定位中的视角畸变和遮挡问题，在离轴和近轴数据集上均达SOTA，具备零样本泛化能力。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有无人机-卫星地理定位基准和方法主要关注近轴场景，忽视了大离轴视角下严重的透视畸变、遮挡和外观差异，且缺乏结构场景理解和域内关系约束，限制了实际部署性能。

Method: 提出ONLoc框架，包含结构感知上下文加权机制（动态增强可靠局部特征、抑制模糊/重复区域）和视图一致学习策略（将一张卫星图与多视角无人机图作为语义组进行集合级监督，学习视角不变和判别性特征，优于传统成对对比学习）。

Result: 在OffNadirLoc基准和四个近轴数据集上均持续优于现有方法，且展现出强零样本泛化能力，无需额外训练即可泛化到未见数据集。

Conclusion: 提出的大离轴基准和框架有效解决了跨视图地理定位中离轴视角的挑战，结构感知加权与多视图一致性学习是关键创新点。

Abstract: Cross-view geo-localization between UAV and satellite imagery remains a fundamental yet highly challenging task, especially under large off-nadir views where drastic perspective distortions, occlusions, and appearance gaps occur. Existing benchmarks and methods primarily focus on near-nadir scenarios and often overlook the importance of structural scene understanding and intra-domain relational constraints, limiting their performance in real-world deployments. In this work, we introduce OffNadirLoc, a new benchmark for large off-nadir UAV-to-satellite geo-localization. To tackle the unique challenges posed by off-nadir perspectives, we further propose ONLoc, a framework that incorporates a structure-aware contextual weighting mechanism to dynamically emphasize reliable local features while suppressing ambiguous or repetitive regions. Additionally, we design a view-coherent learning strategy, which treats one satellite image and the corresponding UAV images from multiple views as a cohesive semantic group. This set-level supervision enables the model to learn viewpoint-invariant and discriminative features, making it more effective at capturing multi-view consistency than conventional pairwise contrastive learning. Extensive experiments on the OffNadirLoc benchmark and four near-nadir datasets demonstrate that our method consistently outperforms state-of-the-art approaches while exhibiting strong zero-shot generalization to unseen datasets without additional training. The code will be released at https://montalario.github.io/offnadirloc/.

</details>


### [42] [Forecasting the Number of Harvest-ready Fruits of Sweet Peppers Using Multimodal Time-Series Data](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.19975)
*Enrico Pallotta,Mohamed Farag,Esra Guclu,Chris McCool,Ribana Roscher,Juergen Gall*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文提出一个包含691株甜椒植物、4837张图像的时间序列数据集，并设计了一种多模态深度学习框架（DinoV3图像特征+LSTM时序建模+数值计数），在两个生长季节中分别将RMSE降低33%和38%，同时通过深度集成和高斯负对数似然提供校准的不确定性估计。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 个体植物级别的准确产量预测对精准农业和供应链规划至关重要，但缺乏同时包含视觉生长动态和逐株测量标签的公开数据集。

Method: 构建了甜椒植物图像时间序列数据集；提出多模态框架：DinoV3提取图像特征，结合数值计数输入LSTM建模时序依赖，处理不规则采样间隔；采用深度集成和高斯NLL提供校准的不确定性估计。

Result: 多模态方法相比持久性基线在2022和2023季分别降低RMSE 33%和38%，比仅测量模型平均提升1.2%；不确定性校准误差（UCE）在0.39-0.89之间。

Conclusion: 该数据集和框架为数据驱动的园艺作物产量预测提供了基准资源和可复现方法，不确定性估计为实际农业决策提供了可信度信号。

Abstract: Accurate yield forecasting at the individual-plant level is critical for precision agriculture and supply-chain planning, yet public datasets capturing both visual growth dynamics and per-plant measurement labels are scarce. In this paper, we introduce a novel, annotated image time-series dataset of 691 sweet pepper plants monitored over two growing seasons, comprising 4837 images with per-plant fruit counts categorized by maturity. We propose a multimodal deep learning framework that fuses high-dimensional image features, extracted using the DinoV3 encoder, with numerical count measurements. Our architecture utilizes a Long Short-Term Memory (LSTM) network to model temporal dependencies and handles irregular sampling intervals common in greenhouse monitoring. Through quantitative experiments, we demonstrate that this multimodal approach reduces RMSE over a persistence baseline by 33% and 38% in the 2022 and 2023 seasons, respectively, with a further 1.2% average gain over a measurement-only model. Furthermore, we employ Deep Ensembles and Gaussian Negative Log-Likelihood (NLL) to provide calibrated uncertainty estimates, with an Uncertainty Calibration Error (UCE) ranging from 0.39 to 0.89 depending on the cross-season evaluation direction, offering a principled confidence signal for real-world agricultural decision-making. We release the dataset and code to support reproducible research and to accelerate development of data-driven yield forecasting methods for horticultural crops.

</details>


### [43] [STEREOFLOW: Progressive Stereo Matching with StereoDiT and Transition Flow Matching](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.19986)
*Hao Wang,Haoran Geng,Xiaotong Yang,Jing Tang,Songlin Wei,Linlong Lang,Yeying Jin,Zheng Zhu,Zhaoxin Fan,Biao Leng*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: StereoFlow 是一种将确定性匹配回归与生成式分布建模相结合的立体匹配框架，通过渐进级联匹配网络、频率解耦的像素扩散变换器（StereoDiT）和少步流匹配目标（Transition Flow Matching）实现高精度、细粒度的视差估计，在 Scene Flow、KITTI、ETH3D 和 Middlebury 等多个基准上取得了多项最优结果。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有的立体匹配方法通常将问题视为确定性回归任务，只能输出单点估计，无法建模多模态分布，导致在歧义区域（如纹理缺失、遮挡等）出现回归到均值的偏差。本文旨在引入一种先验引导的生成框架，融合确定性匹配与生成式分布建模，克服上述局限。

Method: StereoFlow 包含三个关键组件：(i) 两阶段渐进级联匹配网络，逐步生成多分辨率立体条件，提供互补的匹配线索；(ii) 像素扩散变换器（StereoDiT），采用频率解耦架构来建模对应歧义性；(iii) 少步流匹配目标（Transition Flow Matching），实现高效优化。

Result: 在 Scene Flow、KITTI、ETH3D 和 Middlebury 等多个基准数据集上取得了多项最先进的结果，在病态、不连续区域以及零样本泛化场景下表现出强几何一致性和丰富细节。

Conclusion: StereoFlow 提出的先验引导生成框架有效地解决了立体匹配中的回归到均值偏差问题，通过生成式建模增强了歧义区域的匹配能力，在多个标准评测中达到领先水平，验证了该范式的有效性。

Abstract: Stereo matching is a fundamental task in 3D reconstruction. Despite remarkable advances, the prevailing paradigms formulate stereo matching as a deterministic regression problem, collapsing the multimodal distribution modeling into a single-point estimation. This formulation suffers from a regression-to-mean bias, frequently struggling with ambiguous regions. In contrast, we introduce a prior-guided generative framework that integrates deterministic matching regression and generative distribution modeling within a complementary formulation. Built upon this formulation, we introduce StereoFlow through three key components: (i) a two-stage progressive cascade matching network that progressively produces multi-resolution stereo conditions with complementary matching cues; (ii) a pixel diffusion transformer (termed StereoDiT) with a frequency-decoupled architecture for modeling correspondence ambiguity; (iii) a few-step flow matching objective (termed Transition Flow Matching) for efficient optimization. In summary, \textsc{\textbf{StereoFlow}} achieves strong geometric consistency and rich fine-grained details in ill-posed, discontinuous regions and under zero-shot generalization. Extensive experiments demonstrate that the proposed StereoFlow establishes multiple state-of-the-art results across benchmarks, including Scene Flow, KITTI, ETH3D, and Middlebury.

</details>


### [44] [Toward Seasonal Guidelines for Robust Deep-Learning Sentinel-2 Building Detection in Different Area Types](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.19994)
*Michał Romaszewski,Kamil Drejer,Katarzyna Kołodziej,Anna Zawadzka,Stanisław Lewiński,Przemysław Głomb,Marek Ruciński,Michal Krupiński,Krzysztof Gryguc Przemysław Sekułaa,Szymon Sala*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 该论文系统评估了Sentinel-2 10米分辨率影像在建筑物检测中的季节性和异构性影响，通过构建华沙地区多时相数据集并基于U-Net和DeepLabV3+进行跨时间推理，为不同采集时期和建成环境下的建筑物分类提供了实用指导。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: Sentinel-2影像具有开放访问、全球覆盖和高重访率的优势，适合大规模建筑物制图，但其10米分辨率使建筑物/非建筑物分类具有挑战性，尤其是小型或亚像素建筑物，且性能受季节性和建成环境异质性影响。现有研究缺乏对这些效应的系统量化以及面向实践的模型选择指导。

Method: 构建了华沙地区的多时相Sentinel-2数据集，通过将波兰官方地形数据库（BDOT10k）建筑物足迹栅格化到Sentinel-2像素网格生成二值真值掩膜。使用U-Net和DeepLabV3+两种卷积分割骨干网络，分别针对L1C和L2A产品进行场景特定微调，选出每个月份的最佳模型，然后进行跨时间推理（将最佳月份模型应用于所有场景），评估不同月份的训练和推理条件、季节间性能迁移、处理级别影响以及不同建成类型下的差异。

Result: 确定了哪些月份提供有利的训练和推理条件，揭示了季节间性能迁移规律，量化了L1C与L2A处理级别的影响，并分析了不同建成环境类型（如密集城区、郊区等）下的效果差异。

Conclusion: 基于系统性评估，为在不同采集时期和定居特征下进行常规Sentinel-2建筑物分类提供了实用建议，有助于更形式化、面向实践的模型选择。

Abstract: Sentinel-2 imagery offers open access, global coverage, and frequent revisit times, making it attractive for practical building mapping at scale; however, its native 10m resolution makes building vs non-building classification challenging, particularly for small or sub-pixel buildings, and performance can vary with both seasonality and the heterogeneity of built-up environments. This paper introduces a Sentinel-2 building-detection framework designed to systematically quantify these effects and to support more formalised, practice-oriented model selection. We construct a dedicated multi-temporal Sentinel-2 dataset over the Warsaw region and derive binary ground-truth masks by rasterising official Polish topographic database (BDOT10k) building footprints onto the Sentinel-2 pixel grid. Using two established convolutional segmentation backbones (U-Net and DeepLabV3+), we first perform scene-specific fine-tuning to select a robust architecture and identify the best monthly models for L1C and L2A products separately. We then conduct cross-temporal inference by applying each best monthly model to all scenes, enabling an assessment of (i) which months provide favourable training and inference conditions, (ii) how performance transfers between seasons, (iii) the impact of processing level, and (iv) how these effects differ across built-up typologies. Based on these results, we provide practical guidance for routine Sentinel-2 building classification under varying acquisition periods and settlement characteristics.

</details>


### [45] [A Systematic Benchmark of Intensity Normalisation Methods for 3D Knee MRI Segmentation and Cross-Domain Generalisability](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.20028)
*Oliver Mills,Philip Conaghan,Samuel Relton*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 系统比较了七种MRI强度归一化方法对3D U-Net膝关节半月板分割模型泛化性的影响，发现内部测试性能相似，但外部测试中Z-score、Nyúl直方图匹配和CLAHE更鲁棒；然而归一化的影响远小于域转移带来的性能下降，提示需要补充策略实现稳健部署。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 强度归一化是影响MRI深度学习模型泛化性的重要但未被充分探索的因素。由于不同扫描仪和协议导致图像强度变化，临床部署需要稳健的开箱即用性能。

Method: 系统比较七种归一化方法（标准缩放、直方图方法、高斯混合模型方法）对3D U-Net半月板分割的影响。在IWOAI 2019训练，在内部测试集和外部SKM-TEA测试集评估。

Result: 内部测试性能相似；外部测试中Z-score、Nyúl直方图匹配和CLAHE更鲁棒。但归一化方法间的差异远小于数据集间域转移带来的性能下降。

Conclusion: 强度归一化对模型泛化性有可测量的影响，但相对于域转移效果有限，需要补充策略实现稳健的临床部署。

Abstract: Robust out-of-the-box performance is essential for the clinical deployment of deep learning models in medical imaging. An important but underexplored factor affecting model generalisability is intensity normalisation, particularly for magnetic resonance imaging (MRI), where image intensities vary across scanners and protocols. In this study, we systematically compared seven normalisation methods and their impact on the performance of a 3D U-Net model for meniscus segmentation from knee MRI. The methods included standard scaling approaches, histogram-based techniques, and a Gaussian Mixture Model (GMM)-based method. Models were trained on the IWOAI 2019 dataset and evaluated on both internal and external test sets (SKM-TEA) to assess generalisability. Performance was similar internally but differences were significant on external data, with Z-score, Nyúl histogram matching, and CLAHE showing greater robustness than other methods. However, these differences were small compared to the significant performance drop observed between datasets. Overall, while intensity normalisation had a measurable effect on model generalisability, its impact was limited relative to the effects of domain shift, highlighting the need for complementary strategies for robust deployment.

</details>


### [46] [Importance-Aware OBS Pruning for Diffusion Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.20048)
*Ba-Thinh Lam,Srijan Das,Hieu Le*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 提出了一种针对扩散模型的训练无关的剪枝框架，通过引入空间重要性图（来自条件信号或模型注意力）来指导剪枝目标，优先保留对语义重要区域的关键参数。在MS-COCO数据集上，该方法在高压缩比下仍能保持主体保真度和结构正确性，优于传统基于均匀重建误差的剪枝方法。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统剪枝方法基于均匀重建误差优化，忽略了图像中不同区域对感知质量的重要性差异，导致高压缩比下语义关键区域（如主体）出现严重退化。需要一种感知感知的剪枝目标来保持生成模型的视觉保真度。

Method: 提出重要性感知剪枝（importance-aware pruning），一种训练无关框架。核心是：1）从条件信号（如文本嵌入）或模型自注意力图中提取空间重要性图；2）将重要性图融入剪枝目标，使得参数排序依据感知相关性而非均匀重建误差；3）优先保留对高重要性区域贡献大的参数。

Result: 在MS-COCO数据集上，该方法在高压缩比下仍能保持主体保真度和结构正确性，而传统剪枝方法导致可见退化。

Conclusion: 内容感知的目标函数是生成模型感知忠实压缩的关键，证明了将空间重要性引入剪枝能显著改善视觉质量。

Abstract: We propose importance-aware pruning for diffusion models, a training-free framework that prioritizes preserving parameters critical to semantically salient image regions. To do so, we incorporate spatial importance maps -- derived from conditioning signals or model attention -- into the pruning objective. This produces parameter rankings aligned with perceptual relevance rather than uniform reconstruction error. On MS-COCO dataset, our proposed approach consistently retains subject fidelity and structural correctness at high compression ratios where conventional pruning causes visible degradation. These results demonstrate that content-aware objectives are key to perceptually faithful compression of generative models.

</details>


### [47] [GaussianSeed: Hierarchical Gaussian Seeding for High-Resolution 3D Occupancy Prediction](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.20071)
*Xinzhuo Li,Xianghui Pan,Jiayuan Du,Wei Wei,Liuyi Wang,Chengju Liu,Qijun Chen*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: GaussianSeed 提出渐进式多尺度高斯占用预测框架，通过粗到细层次结构实现高分辨率（0.1m）3D 占用预测，同时保持实时推理能力，并构建了 TJScenes 数据集以支持高分辨率评估。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有的视觉 3D 占用预测方法在高体素分辨率下面临巨大的计算成本，难以扩展至精细尺度，存在内存瓶颈。

Method: 提出 GaussianSeed，一种渐进式多尺度高斯占用预测框架，将原始基元组织为粗到细的层次结构，通过层级设计规避稠密表示的内存瓶颈，实现高效的高分辨率推理。

Result: 在 0.1m 空间分辨率下实现实时推理；在 Occ3D-nuScenes 和 TJScenes 数据集上达到所有评估方法中最低的延迟，同时保持极具竞争力的精度，推动了高分辨率 3D 占用预测的效率-质量前沿。

Conclusion: GaussianSeed 通过层级化高斯基元表示有效解决了高分辨率占用预测的计算与内存挑战，为自动驾驶等应用提供了高效且精确的密集场景表征方案。

Abstract: Vision-centric 3D occupancy prediction provides dense scene representations essential for autonomous driving and robotic navigation, yet existing methods struggle to scale to high voxel resolutions due to prohibitive computational costs. To address this, we introduce GaussianSeed, a progressive multi-scale Gaussian occupancy prediction framework that organizes primitives into a coarse-to-fine hierarchy. Benefiting from this hierarchical design, GaussianSeed effectively circumvents the memory bottlenecks inherent in dense representations, successfully scaling to a $0.1\text{m}$ spatial resolution while maintaining real-time inference capabilities. To comprehensively evaluate high-resolution geometric perception, we further construct TJScenes, a panoramic six-camera occupancy dataset with highly detailed $0.1\text{m}$ annotations. Extensive experiments on Occ3D-nuScenes and TJScenes demonstrate that GaussianSeed delivers the lowest latency among all evaluated methods while maintaining highly competitive accuracy, advancing the efficiency-quality frontier of high-resolution 3D occupancy prediction.

</details>


### [48] [Factor-Informed Uncertainty Distillation for Gaze Estimation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.20072)
*Mohammadreza Jamalifard,Yaxiong Lei,Javier Fumanal Idocin,Parastoo Azizinezhad,Tom Foulsham,Javier Andreu-Perez*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 提出因子引导的不确定性蒸馏（FIUD），一种教师-学生框架，通过可解释的图像质量故障模式（光照、清晰度、眼睛可见性、对称性）来对齐不确定性，在不约束场景下提升视线估计的选择性预测性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 深度视线估计在受控环境下表现良好，但在无约束场景中性能下降，系统需要拒绝不可靠的预测。现有单次不确定性方法（如异方差回归）仅从像素推断不确定性，缺乏明确的输入有效性线索；而基于采样的方法计算成本过高，难以实时使用。

Method: 提出FIUD框架：梯度提升教师模型从光照、清晰度、眼睛可见性和对称性等可解释因素预测期望视线误差；神经网络学生模型通过课程学习和排序监督蒸馏这些信号，形成轻量级单次不确定性头。

Result: 在ETH-XGaze、Gaze360和MPIIFaceGaze三个数据集（超过30万样本）上，FIUD在不确定性估计、误差秩相关性和选择性预测方面均优于确定性基线和基于采样的基线，在无约束场景下增益最大。

Conclusion: FIUD通过将不确定性估计与可解释的图像质量因素对齐，有效提升了无约束环境下视线估计的可靠性，且计算效率高，适合实时应用。

Abstract: Deep gaze estimation works well in controlled capture but degrades in unconstrained settings, where systems must reject unreliable predictions. Single-pass uncertainty (e.g., heteroscedastic regression) infers uncertainty from pixels without explicit input-validity cues, while sampling based methods are often too costly for real time use. We propose Factor-Informed Uncertainty Distillation (FIUD), a teacher-student framework that aligns uncertainty with interpretable image-quality failure modes. A gradient-boosting teacher predicts expected gaze error from factors such as illumination, sharpness, eye visibility and symmetry; a neural student distills these signals via curriculum learning and ranking supervision into a lightweight single-pass uncertainty head. Across ETH-XGaze, Gaze360, and MPIIFaceGaze (>300k samples), FIUD improves uncertainty, error rank correlation and selective prediction versus deterministic and sampling-based baselines, with the largest gains in unconstrained settings.

</details>


### [49] [Development of an automated, reliable, and clinically meaningful artificial intelligence (AI) tool for diagnosing cardiac disease from conventional cardiovascular magnetic resonance (CMR) images](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.20087)
*Sina Amirrajab,Volker Vehof,Michael Bietenbeck,Nuriye Akyol,Redouane Bouras,Khuraman Isgandarova,Alexandru Zlibut,Philipp Stalling,Ali Yilmaz*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本研究开发了基于心血管磁共振（CMR）影像的自动化数据整理流程，结合开源大型语言模型（LLMs）从CMR报告中提取诊断标签，并微调三种视觉基础模型（DINO, VST, UMedPT），在包含五种疾病类型（HCM, DCM, ICM, CA, NOR）的数据集上实现了高精度的多模态诊断分类，最终通过集成策略进一步提升性能，所有代码和模型权重已公开。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: CMR影像对于心血管疾病的非侵入性评估非常重要，但其解读需要大量经验。AI模型可以辅助解读，但受限于数据整理劳动强度大、模型性能欠佳以及实施路径不明确等问题。

Method: 研究开发了自动化数据整理流程，利用开源本地运行的LLMs从叙事性CMR报告中提取诊断标签，并预处理多模态影像数据（包括cine和LGE序列）。随后对三种视觉基础模型（DINO, VST, UMedPT）进行两阶段微调。数据集包含988例患者（HCM, DCM, ICM, CA, NOR），分742例训练和246例验证，并在1067例独立测试集上评估。

Result: 微调后的AI模型在独立测试集上取得了高判别性能，单个模型的AUC-ROC最高达0.937（HCM）和0.945（CA）。集成策略进一步提升了诊断准确性和鲁棒性，整体诊断性能最优：HCM（AUC=0.959），CA（AUC=0.966），NOR（AUC=0.872），DCM（AUC=0.848），ICM（AUC=0.840）。

Conclusion: 本研究提出的自动化数据整理与多模态视觉基础模型微调策略能够有效提升CMR影像的AI辅助诊断性能，且通过集成模型进一步增强了准确性和鲁棒性。所有代码和模型权重已公开，有助于促进AI在CMR解读中的临床应用。

Abstract: Aims: Cardiovascular magnetic resonance (CMR) imaging enables non-invasive assessment of myocardial structure, function, and pathology, but requires substantial experience in interpretation of CMR images that could be supported by artificial intelligence (AI)-based models. However, use of AI models for enhanced CMR reading is limited by labor-intensive data curation, suboptimal model performance, and unclear implementation pathways. Methods and results: We developed an automated data curation pipeline for CMR-based cardiovascular disease (CVD) diagnosis, integrating open-source locally-run large language models (LLMs) to extract diagnostic labels from narrative CMR reports and preprocessing multimodal imaging data, including cine and late-gadolinium-enhancement (LGE) CMR sequences. Three vision foundation models (DINO, VST, UMedPT) were fine-tuned across these modalities in a two-stage approach. The dataset comprised hypertrophic cardiomyopathy (HCM), dilated cardiomyopathy (DCM), ischemic cardiomyopathy (ICM), cardiac amyloidosis (CA), and normal controls (NOR). A total of 988 curated cases were randomly divided into 742 for training and 246 for validation. Fine-tuned AI-models achieved high discriminative diagnostic performance on an independent test set comprising 1067 patients , with individual AUC-ROC values of up to 0.937 for the correct diagnosis of HCM and 0.945 for cardiac amyloidosis. Ensemble strategies combining multiple models and modalities further improved AI-based diagnostic accuracy and robustness, achieving the highest overall diagnostic performance for HCM (AUC=0.959, CI [0.936-0.978]), CA (AUC=0.966, CI [0.939-0.986]), NOR (AUC=0.872, CI [0.852-0.894]), DCM (AUC=0.848, CI [0.808-0.885]) and ICM (AUC=0.840, CI [0.809-0.868]). All training and inference code, along with the trained model weights, are publicly available on https://github.com/sinaamirrajab/CMR_CVD.

</details>


### [50] [ENTRAP-VL: A Taxonomic Probe for Dual Contextual Entrainment in Vision-Language Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.20092)
*Karan Goyal,Afreen Hossain,Debojyoti Das,Vishal Bhutani*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文针对视觉-语言模型（VLM）中的上下文诱发现象（contextual entrainment），指出其研究不能简单移植纯文本基准，而需要构建基于双模态的、分类学结构化的探测工具。作者为此引入了ENTRAP-VL数据集，包含1500个条目、8个类别，按上下文与条目的关联及其与真实性的关系两个轴组织，分为文本诱发和视觉诱发两个流，旨在为社区提供系统研究该现象的工具。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 上下文诱发现象在纯语言模型中已有研究，但在视觉-语言模型中尚未被系统考察，且缺乏专门针对双模态设计的探测工具。作者认为VLM中的诱发现象本质不同：它由文本和视觉两种上下文独立驱动，并引入了一种“上下文对场景为假但世界可能为真”的真实性区分，这在纯文本的世界知识设定中不存在。因此需要新的、分类学驱动的基准。

Method: 构建了ENTRAP-VL数据集，包含1500个手工筛选的条目，按分类学组织：两个轴分别是“上下文与条目的关联”和“上下文与真实性的关系”。数据集分为文本诱发流（8种上下文条件）和视觉诱发流（3种上下文条件）。作者不直接测量具体模型的诱发现象，而是提供数据集、分类学及评估协议，供社区使用。

Result: 本文的主要产出是ENTRAP-VL数据集及其分类学框架，以及配套的评估协议。作者未给出具体模型的测量结果，而是强调该工具能支持对VLM中双模态诱发现象的严谨研究。

Conclusion: 研究VLM中的上下文诱发现象需要超越纯文本方法，考虑双模态特性。ENTRAP-VL为社区提供了系统探测该现象的工具，有助于深入理解VLM中文本和视觉上下文如何独立地影响模型输出。

Abstract: Contextual entrainment is the tendency of a model to let auxiliary context in its input pull its output, independently of whether that context is relevant, true, or even meaningful. Recently, it has been identified and given a mechanistic account in unimodal language models. Whether and how it manifests in vision-language models (VLMs) is, by contrast, largely unexamined, and the field lacks a purpose-built instrument with which to investigate it. We take the position that studying contextual entrainment in VLMs requires more than porting an existing text-only benchmark to the multimodal setting: it requires a taxonomically structured, dual-modality instrument whose conditions are constructed around the item at hand (the depicted image in the textual stream, the textual query in the visual stream). We argue that the move to VLMs is substantive rather than incremental. It makes entrainment a dual phenomenon, drivable independently by textual and by visual context, and it opens a veracity distinction (context that is false of the depicted scene yet possible in the world) that has no counterpart in the unimodal, world-knowledge-only formulation of prior work. To make this position concrete and actionable, we introduce ENTRAP-VL (ENTRainment Assessment Probe for Vision and Language), a manually curated dataset of 1,500 items across eight categories, organized by a taxonomy that spans two axes, i.e., the association of context with the item and its relationship to truth, and split into a textual-entrainment stream (eight context conditions) and a visual-entrainment stream (three context conditions). We do not claim to measure entrainment in any particular model; we provide the instrument, the taxonomy that motivates it, and the evaluation protocols it enables, so that the community can investigate the phenomenon rigorously. We will release the dataset and its documentation publicly.

</details>


### [51] [RIM: A Retrieval-In-Matching Framework for Cross-Domain Global Visual Localization of UAVs](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.20116)
*Xin Li,Siyuan Duan,Shang Wang,Zhimin Mao,Bingliang Hu,Geng Zhang*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种名为RIM（检索-匹配一体化）的无人机全球视觉定位方法，利用Google 3D Tiles采样无人机视角参考视图，通过两阶段跨域微调策略适配SALAD模型，并冻结DINOv2-B检索器同时蒸馏局部描述子解码器，实现单一前向传播同时完成检索与描述，无需第二基础模型。在EPFL Urbanscape和Chang'an Park数据集上零样本评估，RIM在Recall@1上优于10种近期检索基线，且端到端定位速度比最强稀疏匹配基线快1.8倍，比RoMa快40倍以上，为GNSS挑战环境下的无人机定位提供了高效可部署方案。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 无人机与参考遥感图像之间存在采集时间和成像平台的差异，导致显著的跨域外观和视角变化，给鲁棒的六自由度位姿估计带来挑战。现有方法难以同时高效处理检索与匹配，且依赖多个基础模型导致计算开销大。

Method: 通过Google 3D Tiles在不同位置、高度和方向采样无人机视角参考视图；提出两阶段跨域微调策略，利用位姿邻近正样本和地理距离远负样本适配SALAD，并采用局部几何一致性对Top-K候选重排序；核心创新RIM冻结已适配的DINOv2-B检索器，蒸馏一个局部描述子解码器，复用其token字段并加入浅层VGG19细节流，使单次DINOv2-B前向传播同时服务检索和局部描述。

Result: 在EPFL Urbanscape数据集上，RIM在25m/50m全3D距离度量下Recall@1比SALAD提升8.55/13.77个百分点；在Chang'an Park数据集上提升4.45/8.94个百分点。Top-K=5时完整定位查询（检索、候选匹配、鲁棒几何验证）端到端耗时67.9ms，比最强稀疏匹配基线快1.8倍，比RoMa快40倍以上。

Conclusion: RIM方法在跨域无人机视觉定位中达到先进性能，同时大幅提升效率，为GNSS挑战环境下的无人机全球视觉定位提供了高效可部署的管道。

Abstract: Global visual localization of unmanned aerial vehicles (UAVs) using remote-sensing reference maps has attracted increasing attention. However, acquisition-time and imaging-platform differences between UAV and reference imagery induce substantial cross-domain appearance and viewpoint shifts, challenging robust six-degree-of-freedom (6-DoF) pose estimation. We address these shifts by sampling UAV-viewpoint reference views from Google 3D Tiles across locations, altitudes, and orientations. A two-stage cross-domain fine-tuning recipe adapts SALAD using pose-near positives and geographically distant hard negatives, while local geometric consistency re-ranks the Top-K candidates. We further propose Retrieval-In-Matching (RIM), which freezes the adapted DINOv2-B retriever and distils a local-descriptor decoder that reuses its token field alongside a shallow VGG19 detail stream. One query-side DINOv2-B forward thus serves both SALAD retrieval and local description, eliminating a second foundation-model backbone while preserving retrieval descriptors by construction. We evaluate RIM zero-shot on the reconstructed EPFL Urbanscape and self-collected Chang'an Park datasets, both geographically disjoint from the training data. RIM outperforms ten recent retrieval baseline families. At 25/50 m under the full 3D distance metric, it improves Recall@1 over SALAD by 8.55/13.77 percentage points on EPFL and 4.45/8.94 points on Park. At Top-K=5, the complete measured localization query, including retrieval, candidate matching, and robust geometric verification, takes 67.9 ms end-to-end: 1.8 times faster than the strongest separate sparse-matching baseline and over 40 times faster than RoMa, while achieving comparable re-ranking accuracy. These results establish an efficient and deployable pipeline for UAV global visual localization in GNSS-challenged environments.

</details>


### [52] [HeadCast: Casting Attention Heads for Efficient Autoregressive Video Generation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.20125)
*Jinliang Shen,Lianghao Su,Zheming Li,Kang He,ZiLiang Lai,Yanbing Jiang,Chengru Song*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: HeadCast 是一种无需训练、即插即用的加速框架，通过在预热后一次性将注意力头分类为 Sink、Dummy、Spatial、Global 四种原型，并重构 KV 缓存为头专用路径，从而在保持长程时间一致性的前提下，显著加速自回归视频扩散模型（最高 1.95x），且质量与全注意力持平、无闪烁问题。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 自回归视频扩散模型中不断增长的 KV 缓存使注意力成为主要推理开销（尤其在高分辨率下），而现有方法要么使用粗粒度启发式策略导致帧间闪烁，要么需要重新训练模型。

Method: HeadCast 基于预训练模型的注意力头具有稳定异质性行为的观察，在短时预热后于最大噪声步执行一次性分类，将每个头归入 Sink、Dummy、Spatial、Global 四种原型，并将单一的 KV 缓存重构为头专用路径。关键点是保留 Global 头以维持长程时间一致性，而 Spatial 路径在固定大小网格上运行，其节省随分辨率增长。

Result: 在多个先进自回归模型上，HeadCast 在 720P 分辨率下实现最高 1.62x 加速，1080P 下最高 1.95x 加速，同时 VBench 质量指标与全注意力持平，且基本无闪烁。

Conclusion: HeadCast 是一种有效的无需训练加速方案，通过头分类与缓存重构在推理效率与视频质量之间取得良好平衡，代码已开源。

Abstract: Autoregressive (AR) video diffusion models have become a promising paradigm for long and streaming video synthesis, but the continuously growing Key-Value (KV) cache makes attention the dominant inference cost, especially at high resolution where each frame contributes many tokens. Existing remedies either evict the cache with coarse heuristics that cause inter-frame flickering, or require model re-training. We propose HeadCast, a training-free, plug-and-play acceleration framework built on the observation that a pre-trained AR model's attention heads exhibit stable, heterogeneous behaviors. After a short warm-up, HeadCast performs a one-time classification at the maximum-noise step that sorts every head into one of four archetypes: Sink, Dummy, Spatial, and Global, and restructures the monolithic KV cache into head-specific pathways. Crucially, it retains the Global heads that preserve the long-range temporal consistency aggressive eviction destroys. Because the Spatial pathway operates on a fixed-size grid, its savings grow with resolution: across state-of-the-art AR models, HeadCast accelerates inference by up to 1.62x at 720P and 1.95x at 1080P, while keeping VBench quality on par with full attention and largely flicker-free. Code is available at https://github.com/sjlgaga/HeadCast .

</details>


### [53] [Real-Time EEG Cap Electrode Detection for Guided Point-of-Care Placement](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.20142)
*William Lehn-Schiøler,Mads Sverker Nilsson,Nicki Skafte Detlefsen*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 提出了一种两阶段视觉系统，使用YOLO检测器实时定位EEG帽电极，并通过几何阶段利用面部 landmarks 将检测结果分配至10-20系统命名角色。在五名受试者佩戴小/中/大号临床验证帽的留一受试者交叉验证中，mAP@.5达到0.94±0.07；留一帽验证显示中号和大号性能稳定，小号因受试者熟悉度混杂而下降至0.72±0.28。几何增强（旋转、透视、混合）提升了平面内滚动鲁棒性和时间电极召回率，landmark驱动的头部裁剪将可用距离范围扩展至0.6倍表观尺度时mAP@.5从0.23提升至0.45。采用YOLOv10n轻量骨干网络，在商用CPU上实现19 FPS的实时吞吐量。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 为了在EEG记录中自动检测和验证电极帽的电极位置，确保电极正确放置在头皮上对应的10-20系统位置，从而提升脑电信号采集的可靠性和效率。

Method: 采用两阶段视觉流水线：第一阶段使用单类别YOLO检测器（YOLOv10n）从网络摄像头流中定位电极；第二阶段通过几何映射，基于面部 landmarks 将每个检测到的电极分配至10-20系统命名角色。数据增强包括几何变换（旋转、透视、混合）和基于landmark的头部裁剪，以提升鲁棒性和距离适应性。

Result: 在留一受试者交叉验证（五名受试者）中，检测器mAP@.5=0.94±0.07（合并0.96）；留一帽验证中，中号和大号帽mAP@.5分别为0.97和0.97，小号帽降至0.72±0.28（差异与受试者熟悉度相关而非帽型）。几何增强改善了平面内滚动鲁棒性和时间电极召回率，landmark头部裁剪在0.6倍表观尺度下将mAP@.5从0.23提升至0.45。系统在640像素分辨率下于商用CPU上达到19 FPS实时速度。

Conclusion: 该系统能够实时检测并验证EEG帽电极的解剖位置，在多数帽型上达到高精度，小号帽性能受受试者熟悉度影响。几何增强和头部裁剪有效提升鲁棒性和距离适应性，轻量骨干网络确保了低计算资源下的实时运行。

Abstract: We present a two-stage vision system that detects EEG cap electrodes in a live webcam stream and validates their anatomical placement in real time. A single-class YOLO detector localises electrodes; a geometric stage assigns each detection to a named 10-20 role from facial landmarks. Evaluating under subject-disjoint leave-one-subject-out (LOSO) cross-validation across five subjects wearing the clinically-validated Small/Medium/Large caps, the detector attains mAP@.5 = 0.94 +/- 0.07 across five held-out folds (0.96 pooled). A dedicated leave-one-cap-out axis, holding out every frame of a cap regardless of subject, leaves Medium and Large mAP@.5 within 0.01 of LOSO (0.97, 0.97) while Small drops to 0.72 +/- 0.28, a gap confounded with subject familiarity rather than cap style. Geometric augmentation (rotation, perspective, mixup) improves in-plane-roll robustness and temporal-electrode recall at no inference cost, and a landmark-driven head crop extends the usable distance range, lifting mAP@.5 from 0.23 to 0.45 at 0.6 x apparent scale. A compact mobile-candidate backbone (YOLOv10n) keeps the detector at real-time throughput (19 FPS) on a commodity CPU at 640 px.

</details>


### [54] [SHFormer: Dynamic Spectral Filtering Convolutional Neural Network and High-pass Kernel Generation Transformer for Adaptive MRI Reconstruction](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.20159)
*Sriprabha Ramanarayanan,Rahul G. S.,Mohammad Al Fahim,Keerthi Ram,Ramesh Venkatesan,Mohanasankar Sivaprakasam*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 提出一种基于神经调节的判别式多谱注意力机制（SHFormer），通过结合光谱滤波CNN和动态高通核生成Transformer，解决现有注意力机制在MRI重建中高频信息捕获不足及跨模态泛化能力弱的问题，在监督/自监督、扩散模型训练、闭集/开集泛化等场景下均取得约1dB PSNR和0.01 SSIM的提升。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有注意力机制（AM）在加速MRI重建中虽能捕获非局部依赖，但存在两个关键局限：（1）AM擅长低频信息而高频表征能力有限，导致重建结果过于平滑；（2）AM模型在多模态MRI数据中需要针对不同模态重新训练，因其所学特征局限于局部上下文变化，难以捕获跨异构域的可迁移特征。因此需要一种能同时提升高频细节重建质量和跨模态泛化能力的新方法。

Method: 提出神经调节（neuromodulation）驱动的判别式多谱注意力机制（SHFormer），包含两个核心模块：（1）光谱滤波CNN（spectral filtering CNN），用于捕获模态特定的可迁移特征；（2）动态高通核生成Transformer（dynamic high-pass kernel generation transformer），专注于高频细节的生成。该网络通过多谱注意力实现高频信息传播和跨域特征复用。

Result: 在监督学习、自监督学习、扩散模型训练、闭集与开集泛化等多种设定下进行评测。方法在未见过的场景中取得最佳改进：PSNR提升约1 dB，SSIM提升约0.01，表明其高质量重建与可扩展性。

Conclusion: 提出的SHFormer通过神经调制判别式多谱注意力机制，有效改善了加速MRI重建中的高频细节保持和跨模态泛化问题，在多种训练范式下均优于现有方法，代码已开源。

Abstract: Attention Mechanism (AM) selectively focuses on essential information for imaging tasks and captures relationships between distant pixel neighborhoods to compute feature representations. Accelerated MRI reconstruction benefits from AM, as the imaging process involves Fourier domain measurements that influence image representation non-locally. However, AM-based models are more adept at capturing low-frequency information with limited capacity for high-frequency representations, restricting models to smooth reconstruction. Additionally, AM-based models need mode-specific retraining for multimodal MRI data, as their knowledge is restricted to local contextual variations that may be inadequate to capture transferable features across heterogeneous domains. To address these challenges, we propose a neuromodulation-based discriminative multi-spectral AM for scalable MRI reconstruction that can (i) propagate context-aware high-frequency details for high-quality reconstruction, and (ii) capture features reusable across deviated unseen domains in multimodal MRI. The proposed network consists of a spectral filtering CNN to capture mode-specific transferable features and a dynamic high-pass kernel generation transformer focusing on high-frequency details. We evaluate our model on comparative studies in supervised and self-supervised learning, diffusion model-based training, closed-set and open-set generalization under heterogeneous MRI data, and interpretation-based analysis. Our method offers scalable, high-quality reconstruction with best improvement margins of ~1 dB in PSNR and ~0.01 in SSIM under unseen scenarios. Code: https://github.com/sriprabhar/SHFormer

</details>


### [55] [StreamHOI: Interaction-aware Temporal Memory Adaptation for Streaming HOI Video Generation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.20174)
*Zejing Rao,Haoxian Zhang,Xiaoqiang Liu,Yiping Meng,Guoxin Zhang,Pengfei Wan,Fan Tang,Tong-Yee Lee*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: StreamHOI 是一个低延迟的流式框架，用于生成长时长的交互视频，通过分析不同 Transformer 块对历史记忆的偏好，设计块特定的记忆布局，并引入记忆距离缩放模块，实现了 17.6 FPS 和 0.75s 首块延迟。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有的人-物交互（HOI）视频生成方法大多局限于离线的短视频生成，且驱动条件复杂，无法用于实时交互应用。因此需要一种低延迟、支持流式生成长视频的框架。

Method: StreamHOI 不将重条件化的 HOI 管线转为流式系统，而是研究图像到视频流式生成器如何在有限延迟下组织历史记忆以保持交互。通过离线 HOI 感知的块分析（block profiling）发现不同 Transformer 块对 HOI 区域和周围区域有不同历史记忆偏好，进而采用偏置引导的记忆专门化训练（bias-guided memory-specialized training）适配块特定记忆布局，并引入记忆距离缩放模块增强对早期交互状态的远程访问。

Result: 与长视频基线和近期 HOI 生成方法相比，StreamHOI 在交互合理性、物体保真度、人物质量和效率方面均取得更好结果，达到 17.6 FPS 和 0.75s 首块延迟。

Conclusion: StreamHOI 证明了通过 HOI 感知的块分析和记忆专门化训练，可以高效实现低延迟的流式长视频 HOI 生成，适用于实时交互应用。

Abstract: Existing human--object interaction (HOI) video generation methods are largely limited to offline short-video generation with complex driving conditions, making them unsuitable for real-time interactive applications. We present \emph{StreamHOI}, a low-latency streaming framework for long-duration HOI video generation. Instead of converting heavily conditioned HOI pipelines into streaming systems, we study how an image-to-video streaming generator should organize historical memory to preserve interactions under bounded latency. We find that the standard sink-local memory design faces a trade-off in streaming HOI generation, and different transformer blocks show different historical-memory preferences for HOI regions and surrounding regions. To match memory composition with block behavior, StreamHOI performs offline HOI-aware block profiling and applies bias-guided memory-specialized training to adapt the generator to block-specific memory layouts. We further introduce a memory distance scaling module to strengthen long-range access to early interaction states. Extensive comparisons with both long-video baselines and recent HOI generation methods demonstrate that StreamHOI achieves strong interaction plausibility, object fidelity, human quality and efficiency, reaching 17.6 FPS with 0.75s first-chunk latency.

</details>


### [56] [PerceptDrive: Perception Prior World-Action Modeling with Adaptive Expert Routing for End-to-End Autonomous Driving](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.20175)
*Yushan Liu,Tianxiong Lv,Bohua Wang,Hangqi Fan,Chenxu Zhao,He Zheng,Xuchang Zhong,Yifan Xie,Congyang Zhao,Zhihao Liao,Leigang Luo,Yang Cai,Xiao-Ping Zhang,Wenbo Ding*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: PerceptDrive是一个感知先验世界-动作建模框架，通过自适应专家路由（adaptive expert routing）和先验保留目标（prior-retention objective），从冻结的感知基础模型中提取并保留几何、语义和动态知识，在NAVSIM v1和v2上分别达到90.4 PDMS和90.2 EPDMS的最优性能，无需测试时候选选择。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 冻结的感知基础模型编码了丰富的几何、语义和动态知识，但狭窄的条件接口（narrow conditioning interfaces）会衰减任务相关线索，且静态融合（static fusion）无法根据每个场景调整专家贡献。这被定义为“先验到规划迁移问题”（prior-to-plan transfer problem）。

Method: PerceptDrive框架包含：(1) 从冻结的驾驶自适应提供者（driving-adapted provider）中提取教师蒸馏先验（teacher-distilled priors），并结合来自冻结自监督视频编码器的密集观测潜变量；(2) 专家特定查询分支（expert-specific query branches）处理这些信号，并通过先验保留目标（prior-retention objective）将每个分支锚定到其先验；(3) 路由器（router）从共享场景表示预测软门控（soft gates），在轨迹生成前组合专家条件；(4) 训练时，基于特权的规则子度量估计为分支特定轨迹草稿提供软门控蒸馏目标；(5) 预测的无动作未来潜变量条件化流匹配动作生成器（flow-matching actor）。推理时无需特权组件、测试时评分或搜索，仅需一个前视摄像头即可生成轨迹。

Result: 在NAVSIM v1上达到90.4 PDMS，在NAVSIM v2上达到90.2 EPDMS，超越现有方法。消融实验证实先验保留和场景条件路由具有互补增益，且三个先验的依赖程度不同。

Conclusion: 保留并自适应路由感知先验可以改进直接规划性能，无需测试时候选选择。

Abstract: Frozen perception foundation models encode rich geometric, semantic, and dynamic knowledge. Yet narrow conditioning interfaces may attenuate task-relevant cues, while static fusion cannot adjust expert contributions to each scene. We cast this challenge as the prior-to-plan transfer problem and introduce PerceptDrive, a perception prior world-action modeling framework with adaptive expert routing. PerceptDrive feeds teacher-distilled priors from a frozen, driving-adapted provider and dense observation latents from a frozen self-supervised video encoder into a trainable expert-routed world-action model. Expert-specific query branches process these signals, while a prior-retention objective anchors each branch to its prior. A router predicts soft gates from a shared scene representation and combines the expert conditions before trajectory generation. During training, privileged rule-based sub-metric estimates for branch-specific trajectory drafts provide soft-gate distillation targets. The predicted action-free future latent conditions a flow-matching actor. At inference, privileged components are absent; with one front-facing camera, PerceptDrive generates one trajectory per planning step without test-time scoring, reranking, or search. Experiments show that PerceptDrive achieves state-of-the-art performance with 90.4 PDMS on NAVSIM v1 and 90.2 EPDMS on NAVSIM v2, outperforming existing methods. Ablations confirm complementary gains from prior retention and scene-conditioned routing, alongside differential reliance on the three priors. These results demonstrate that preserving and adaptively routing perception priors improves direct planning without test-time candidate selection.

</details>


### [57] [RS-RIE-Bench: Benchmarking Reasoning-Guided Remote Sensing Image Editing](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.20197)
*Zihan Qin,Boao Xu,Zhao Dong,Yingping Sun,Ziheng Jiao,Junying Wang,Hongwei Wang*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: RS-RIE-Bench 是首个面向推理引导的遥感图像编辑基准，涵盖时间、因果和空间三类推理任务，评估目标区域合理性、非目标区域保持性和图像质量一致性。对8个模型的评测表明，当前最强模型仅达到24.28%的联合严格准确率，因果推理和空间推理尤为困难，揭示了现有模型在地理推理、区域控制和传感器一致性方面的显著局限。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有图像编辑基准主要针对自然图像或通用视觉场景，未能充分捕捉遥感编辑所需的地理推理、区域控制和传感器一致性能力。因此，需要构建一个专门评估遥感图像编辑中推理能力的基准。

Method: 构建RS-RIE-Bench基准，将任务分为时间推理、因果推理和空间推理三类；设计包含目标区域合理性、非目标区域保持性和图像质量一致性三个维度的评估协议；采用MLLM评估并通过跨判断一致性和分层专家评审验证可行性。

Result: 在8个开源和闭源模型上的系统评测显示：最强模型在严格联合满足标准下仅达到24.28%的总体准确率，所有模型的平均松弛联合-4成功率为32.23%；因果推理和空间推理尤为困难，部分开源模型在某些类别上接近零分。

Conclusion: RS-RIE-Bench能有效揭示当前模型在地理推理、区域控制和传感器一致性生成方面的局限，为未来遥感智能编辑模型提供标准化基准和清晰的研究方向。

Abstract: Remote sensing image editing aims to modify remote sensing images according to natural language instructions while preserving geographic rules and sensor observation characteristics. Existing benchmarks mainly target natural images or general visual scenes, and thus may not fully capture the reasoning, regional control, and sensor-consistency abilities required in remote sensing editing. To fill this gap, we introduce RS-RIE-Bench, the first benchmark for reasoning-guided remote sensing image editing. RS-RIE-Bench organizes tasks into three categories: temporal reasoning, causal reasoning, and spatial reasoning. These categories capture temporal evolution, causal consequence, and spatial imaging consistency in remote sensing scenes. The evaluation protocol covers three dimensions: target region plausibility, non-target region preservation, and image quality consistency. We further demonstrate the feasibility of MLLM-based evaluation through cross-judge consistency analysis and stratified expert review. Systematic evaluation on eight open-source and closed-source image editing models shows that current models still have clear limitations in reasoning-guided remote sensing editing. Even the strongest model achieves only 24.28\% overall accuracy under the strict joint-satisfaction criterion, while the mean relaxed joint-4 success rate across all eight models is 32.23\%. Causal reasoning and spatial reasoning remain especially challenging, and several open-source models are close to zero in some categories. These results show that RS-RIE-Bench can effectively reveal the limitations of current models in geographic reasoning, regional control, and sensor-consistent generation. It also provides a standardized benchmark and a clear research direction for future remote sensing intelligent editing models.

</details>


### [58] [Not All Patches are Equal: Sampling Matters for Visible-Infrared Pre-Training](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.20238)
*Qiwei Ma,Bin Deng,Junjie Zhu,Qiangjuan Huang,Puhong Duan,Ke Yang,Xudong Kang,Shutao Li*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文针对可见光-红外（VIS-IR）对齐预训练中均匀patch对比学习不可靠的问题，提出重要性感知采样（IAS）方法，通过基于红外结构线索的patch权重、可学习的软重要性掩码和patch课程学习策略，逐步从高可靠性区域扩展到困难patch，实现更有效的跨模态表征学习，在多个VIS-IR基准任务上取得一致提升。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有VIS-IR预训练方法采用均匀的patch-wise对比学习，但由于可见光与红外成像物理差异，空间配对的patch区域在特征层面天然存在不可比较性，统一强度对齐会损害表征学习与下游迁移效果，因此需要一种能根据patch可靠性动态调整训练重点的采样方法。

Method: 提出重要性感知采样（IAS），包含三个核心设计：(i) 从红外结构线索推导patch权重，用于重加权对比学习目标；(ii) 通过轻量级采样器学习软重要性掩码，可选择从手工先验热启动；(iii) 采用patch课程学习策略，从高可靠性区域逐步扩展至困难patch。IAS即插即用，兼容patch级/相关性级对齐（如UNIV风格）和图像级对比基线（如ImageBind风格）。

Result: 在多个VIS-IR基准任务上（红外语义分割、红外目标检测、可见光语义分割、跨模态检索），IAS相较于强基线方法取得了一致的性能提升。

Conclusion: 提出了一种即插即用的重要性感知采样方法，通过根据patch可靠性动态调整训练权重，有效解决了VIS-IR预训练中均匀对比学习的缺陷，显著提升了跨模态表征质量与下游任务性能。

Abstract: Visible-infrared (VIS-IR) alignment is a key pre-training task for robust multi-sensor perception. Most existing methods use uniform patch-wise contrastive learning, but this can be unreliable in VIS-IR data because imaging-physics differences make some spatially paired regions inherently less comparable, and aligning them with equal strength hinders representation learning and downstream transfer. In this paper, we revisit VIS-IR pre-training from a sampling perspective and propose Importance-Aware Sampling (IAS), which adjusts training emphasis based on patch reliability. Specifically, IAS (i) derives patch weights from infrared structural cues and uses them to reweight the contrastive objective; (ii) learns a soft importance mask with a lightweight sampler, optionally warm-started from the hand-crafted prior; and (iii) employs a patch curriculum learning strategy that gradually expands from high-reliability regions to harder patches. It is worth noting that IAS is plug-and-play and works with both patch-/correlation-level alignment (e.g., UNIV-style) and image-level contrastive baselines (e.g., ImageBind-style). Extensive experiments on multiple VIS-IR benchmarks demonstrate consistent improvements over strong baselines, including for IR semantic segmentation, IR object detection and VIS semantic segmentation and cross-modal retrieval task. Code will be released on https://github.com/KlayMa527/IAS.

</details>


### [59] [Vera: Identity-Faithful Human Subject-to-Video Generation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.20247)
*Yulong Xu,Xinyue Liu,Shujuan Li,huafeng shi,Yan Zhou,Jiwen Liu,Xintao Wang,Yu Shen Liu,Huaibo Huang*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: Vera是一个统一的人类中心Subject-to-Video（S2V）生成框架，通过构建百万级身份对齐数据集和两种互补设计（身份聚焦掩码监督IFMS和参考感知逐层注意力RALA），在单人和多人视频生成中显著提升身份一致性、多主体绑定和运动自然性，减少身份混淆和过度复制参考图像的问题。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有S2V生成方法在保持参考主体一致性方面取得进展，但针对人类中心生成仍存在身份关键细节漂移、多人场景下的身份-角色绑定错误、属性交换和过度复制参考外观线索等问题，亟需一种统一的人类身份一致性视频生成方法。

Method: Vera框架包含两大部分：1）构建百万级身份对齐的人类图像-视频数据集，通过跨片段检索提供显式身份对应和多样化参考；2）两种互补设计——Identity-Focal Masked Supervision (IFMS)通过空间聚焦监督增强身份感知学习并减少无关伪影干扰，Reference-Aware Layer-wise Attention (RALA)调节视频令牌与参考身份线索在DiT骨干中的交互，保持稳定身份锚点并增强逐层身份读取。

Result: 实验表明Vera在人类身份一致性、多人主体绑定和运动自然性方面均有提升，同时减少身份混淆和过度复制参考图像的问题。

Conclusion: Vera为单人和多人场景提供统一的人类中心S2V生成框架，有效解决了身份漂移和多人身份混淆等关键挑战，为未来人类身份感知视频生成研究提供了新方向。

Abstract: Subject-to-video (S2V) generation has made substantial progress in preserving reference subjects across diverse categories, yet generic subject consistency remains insufficient for human-centric generation. A video may appear globally consistent while identity-critical human details still drift across frames, poses, and interactions. This issue becomes more severe in multi-person scenarios, where incorrect identity-role binding leads to subject confusion, attribute swapping, and excessive copying of reference-specific appearance cues. We propose Vera, a unified human-centric S2V framework for single- and multi-person generation. We first construct a million-pair identity-aligned human image-video dataset through person-level cross-clip retrieval, providing explicit identity correspondence and diverse references. Built on this dataset, Vera introduces two complementary designs. Identity-Focal Masked Supervision (IFMS) strengthens identity-aware learning with spatially focused supervision while reducing interference from irrelevant artifacts. Reference-Aware Layer-wise Attention (RALA) regulates how video tokens interact with reference identity cues in the DiT backbone, preserving stable identity anchors and enhancing layer-aware identity readout. Extensive experiments demonstrate that Vera improves human identity consistency, multi-person subject binding, and motion naturalness, while reducing identity confusion and excessive reference-image copying.

</details>


### [60] [How Does Urban Context Relate to Residential Building Health? A Vision-POI Fusion Framework for Building-Level Housing Inspection](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.20263)
*Kun Zhao,Helei Ren,Guilin Tang,Tianyi Chen,Zhehui Song,Xing Liu,Lijian Zhou,Yuhong Zhao,Xiang Gao,Jinming Jiang,Qichao Ban*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 提出一种融合多视角视觉检测与POI邻域上下文信息的视觉-POI融合框架，用于住宅建筑健康评估。结果表明多视角聚合是性能提升的主要来源，POI信息作为补充性上下文先验，能带来小幅且类别依赖的改进。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有自动化建筑体检研究主要依赖单张图像，很少探究周边城市功能上下文能否为建筑级评估提供补充信息。

Method: 首先利用多种目标检测模型从单张图像提取问题位置、类别和置信度，然后通过多视角聚合构建可解释的建筑级表示；其次提取500m、1000m、1500m邻域缓冲区的POI特征，采用Pearson/Spearman相关分析及FDR校正筛选候选上下文特征；最后使用代价敏感随机森林分类器在社区隔离的空间交叉验证下融合视觉与POI特征。

Result: 多视角聚合将建筑级Macro-F1从直接检测的60.84%提升至74.95%；融入POI上下文后Macro-F1进一步提升至76.79%，但增益有限且依赖具体问题类别。

Conclusion: POI信息作为补充性上下文先验而非直接视觉证据的替代品或建筑状况的因果决定因素。

Abstract: Housing-level urban physical examination is essential for identifying residential building problems and supporting targeted urban renewal. Existing automated inspection studies primarily rely on individual images and rarely examine whether surrounding urban functional context can provide supplementary information for building-level assessment. This study proposes a vision-POI fusion framework that combines multi-view visual inspection with POI-derived neighborhood context for residential building health assessment. The empirical dataset covers 92 old residential communities, 3,237 residential buildings, and 25,608 field-acquired inspection images in Qingdao, China, encompassing seven categories of housing-related issues. First, multiple object detection models are evaluated to extract issue locations, categories, and confidence scores from individual images. The image-level outputs are subsequently aggregated across multiple views to construct interpretable building-level representations. Second, POI features are extracted within 500m, 1,000m, and 1,500m neighborhood buffers to characterize surrounding functional environments. Pearson and Spearman correlation analyses, combined with false discovery rate correction, are used to identify candidate contextual features. Finally, visual and POI features are integrated using a cost-sensitive Random Forest classifier under community-isolated spatial cross-validation. The results show that multi-view aggregation provides the main performance improvement, increasing the building-level Macro-F1 from 60.84% under Direct Detection to 74.95%. Incorporating POI context further increases Macro-F1 to 76.79%, although the additional gain is modest and category-dependent. POI information therefore functions as a supplementary contextual prior rather than a substitute for direct visual evidence or a causal determinant of building condition.

</details>


### [61] [Self-supervision drives representational convergence in medical foundation models more than clinical supervision](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.20274)
*Soroosh Tayebi Arasteh,Sebastian Ziegelmayer,Mahshad Lotfinia,Lisa Adams,Sven Nebelung,Jakob Nikolas Kather,Daniel Truhn*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文系统研究了医学图像编码器的表示收敛性，发现收敛主要由自监督目标驱动而非临床监督或规模，且收敛程度有限（自监督编码器对齐最高40.4%），但线性分类器仍可在编码器间转移（保留约85%性能），提示需通过预训练目标设计互操作性并验证共享几何最薄弱的环节。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 医学图像编码器被假设为可互换，认为规模与临床监督会使其表示收敛，但这一假设缺乏实证检验。本文旨在严谨验证编码器表示是否真正收敛、收敛的驱动因素及其临床可用性。

Method: 对18个图像编码器和7个文本编码器进行受控剖析，涵盖7M至27B参数、五种成像模态、650,982张胸部X光片（来自六个数据集）。为隔离原因，固定数据、架构和规模，仅改变训练目标（自监督、标签监督、图像-文本）训练编码器，并在合成模型中复现效果。

Result: 收敛适度但高于随机基线，主要由自监督目标驱动（胸部X光片对齐40.4%），标签监督（21.1%）和图像-文本（3.3%）远低，且不随规模（Spearman 0.302, p=0.223）或能力增长。收敛限于模态内，未达到临床语言层面，也不反映放射科医生的病例相似性判断。但线性分类器可在编码器间转移，在五个未见过医院保持约85%的编码器内性能。

Conclusion: 医学图像编码器的收敛由预训练目标决定，而非规模或临床监督。互操作性应通过目标设计来实现，并在共享几何最薄弱的环节（患者亚组、临床判断）进行验证。

Abstract: Medical image encoders from different groups are increasingly treated as interchangeable, on the assumption that scale and clinical supervision concentrate their representations onto a shared structure. Whether this convergence is real, what produces it, and whether it is clinically usable are untested, and the similarity measures behind such claims are fragile. We present a controlled dissection across 18 image and 7 text encoders, all open-weight and run locally, spanning 7M to 27B parameters and five imaging modalities, including 650,982 chest radiographs from six datasets. To isolate cause, we train encoders that vary only the objective under fixed data, architecture, and scale, and reproduce the effect in a synthetic model. Convergence is modest but above a random floor, driven by the self-supervised objective, not clinical supervision: matched self-supervised encoders aligned most (40.4% on chest radiography), with label-supervised (21.1%) and image-text (3.3%) far lower, and did not grow with size (Spearman 0.302, p=0.223) or capability. It is within-modality, does not reach clinical language, and does not reproduce how radiologists judge case similarity. Yet a linear classifier transfers across encoders and to five held-out hospitals, retaining about 85% of within-encoder performance. Convergence in medical imaging is therefore set by the pretraining objective, not inherited from scale or clinical supervision. Interoperability is accordingly something to design for through that objective, and to validate where the shared geometry is weakest, across patient subgroups and against clinical judgment.

</details>


### [62] [Multimodal Large Language Models for Remote Sensing Image Understanding: Domain-Specific or General-Purpose?](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.20284)
*Qiwei Ma,Chunping Qiu,Xinjun Cheng,Xiaoyu Zhang,Puhong Duan,Ke Yang,Xudong Kang,Shutao Li*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文对遥感图像场景理解中的多模态大语言模型进行了系统性综述与诊断性评估，比较了专用遥感MLLM与通用视觉MLLM的性能，发现通用模型在多项任务上可匹敌甚至超越专用模型，并指出了当前模型在空间推理、细粒度理解等方面的局限性及未来方向。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 遥感图像场景理解中多模态大语言模型发展迅速，但缺乏对其能力边界、跨任务泛化能力及任务特定局限性的系统性认知，亟需全面评估与比较。

Method: 采用系统性综述与诊断性评估方法，回顾了遥感MLLM在模型设计、多模态学习、训练数据和下游能力方面的技术演进，并在多种遥感场景理解任务和基准上将专用遥感MLLM与通用计算机视觉MLLM进行了对比分析。

Result: 专用遥感MLLM在领域特定设置（如遥感视觉定位和高分辨率视觉问答）中仍具竞争力；但通用CV-MLLM在多项遥感任务上无需领域微调即可匹配甚至超越专用模型，展现了强迁移能力。当前MLLM在空间与关系推理、细粒度视觉理解、指令多样性及跨异构任务格式的泛化方面存在局限。

Conclusion: 现有遥感MLLM并未在多样化遥感任务中一致优于通用CV-MLLM；未来应向可靠评估、多模态与高分辨率推理、高效部署和工具增强的遥感智能体方向发展。本文为开发鲁棒、可泛化且实用的遥感MLLM提供了系统性参考。

Abstract: The rapid development of multimodal large language models (MLLMs) has introduced a flexible paradigm for remote sensing image scene understanding (RSISU), enabling natural-language interaction with remote sensing imagery. However, a systematic understanding of the capability boundaries, cross-task generalization, and task-specific limitations of existing remote sensing MLLMs (RS-MLLMs) is still lacking. This paper presents a systematic survey and diagnostic evaluation of MLLMs for RSISU. We review the technical evolution of RS-MLLMs, focusing on model design, multimodal learning, training data, and downstream capabilities. We further compare RS-MLLMs with general-purpose computer vision MLLMs (CV-MLLMs) across diverse RSISU tasks and benchmarks. RS-MLLMs remain competitive in domain-specific settings, particularly remote sensing visual grounding and high-resolution visual question answering. More notably, general-purpose CV-MLLMs can match or even outperform these specialized models on several RSISU tasks without remote sensing-specific fine-tuning. These findings demonstrate the strong transferability of general-purpose CV-MLLMs and show that current RS-MLLMs do not consistently outperform them across diverse RSISU tasks. Current MLLMs also face limitations in spatial and relational reasoning, fine-grained visual understanding, instruction diversity, and generalization across heterogeneous task formats. Based on these findings, we outline future directions toward reliable evaluation, multimodal and high-resolution reasoning, efficient deployment, and tool-augmented remote sensing agents. This survey provides a systematic reference for developing robust, generalizable, and practical MLLMs for RSISU.

</details>


### [63] [Diverse-Intent Multi-Turn Fashion Image Retrieval](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.20291)
*Mingqiang Tang,Haokun Wen,Meng Liu,Yupeng Hu,Weili Guan,Xuemeng Song*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文针对现实世界时尚搜索中的多轮交互检索问题，提出了一个包含26K多轮会话的基准数据集DIM-Fashion，覆盖7个任务、13个数据集，以及一个多模态大语言模型-视觉语言预训练框架FashionAM，该框架直接对齐多模态对话查询与时尚图库嵌入空间，避免中间文本化过程，实验证明其有效性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有多轮检索方法假设每轮交互均遵循相同的属性编辑范式，忽略了异构意图转换（如从“红色连衣裙”到“类似款式的蓝色”）；同时现有方法依赖文本化来桥接多模态查询与视觉检索，可能丢失细粒度视觉线索。

Method: 提出FashionAM框架，结合多模态大语言模型（MLLM）和视觉语言预训练（VLP），直接对齐多模态对话查询与时尚图库嵌入空间，避免中间文本化步骤；同时构建了DIM-Fashion基准数据集，包含26K多轮会话，涵盖多样化的意图转换和回滚行为。

Result: 大量实验表明FashionAM相比现有方法具有更好的有效性。

Conclusion: 多轮时尚检索中应考虑异构意图转换，直接对齐多模态查询与视觉嵌入可避免信息丢失，所提数据集和框架为未来研究提供了基础。

Abstract: Real-world fashion search involves interactive retrieval across multiple turns. However, existing multi-turn retrieval methods are built on a restrictive assumption that every interaction follows the same attribute-editing paradigm, leaving heterogeneous intent transitions unexplored. Moreover, existing approaches often rely on textification to bridge multimodal queries and visual retrieval, which may lose fine-grained visual cues. To address these gaps, we introduce DIM-Fashion, a benchmark of 26K multi-turn sessions constructed from 13 fashion retrieval datasets across 7 tasks, featuring diverse intent transitions and rollback behaviors. We further propose FashionAM, an MLLM-VLP framework that directly aligns multimodal conversational queries with a fashion-oriented gallery embedding space, avoiding intermediate textification. Extensive experiments demonstrate the effectiveness of FashionAM over existing approaches. The dataset and code will be made publicly available upon acceptance.

</details>


### [64] [Evolving Cache Schedules for Fast Diffusion Policy Inference](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.20293)
*Siying Wang,Kangye Ji,Di Wang,Fei Cheng*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: EVO 是一个无需重新训练的加速框架，通过进化搜索为扩散策略的块-时间步网格全局调度缓存刷新，在保持近乎完整闭环性能的同时，最多实现 8.05 倍的动作生成加速，FLOPs 从 15.77G 降至 1.96G。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 扩散策略通过迭代去噪实现强视觉运动控制，但重复去噪导致实时部署计算开销大。现有基于缓存的加速方法在训练无关的调度中将计算均匀分配给所有块，忽略了不同块间的异构冗余性，导致性能-效率权衡次优。

Method: 提出 Evolving Cache Schedules (EVO)，一个无需重新训练的训练无关加速框架。将缓存刷新调度建模为块-时间步网格上的完整调度方案，通过进化搜索全局优化。引入冗余感知初始化（生成有潜力的初始调度）和目标条件早停（达到预设性能后终止验证）。优化后的调度可直接嵌入预训练扩散策略，无需微调。

Result: 在多个操作基准上，EVO 在保持近乎完整性能的同时显著降低计算量：动作生成速度提升最高 8.05 倍，FLOPs 从 15.77G 降至最低 1.96G。

Conclusion: EVO 证明了通过进化搜索全局优化缓存刷新调度，可以在无需重新训练的情况下大幅提升扩散策略的推理效率，为实时部署提供了实用方案。

Abstract: Diffusion policies achieve strong visuomotor control by iteratively denoising action chunks, but repeated denoising makes real-time deployment computationally demanding. Cache-based methods reduce inference cost by reusing intermediate activations, but existing training-free schedules typically allocate computation uniformly across blocks, ignoring heterogeneous redundancy across blocks and leading to a suboptimal performance-efficiency trade-off. To bridge this gap, we introduce Evolving Cache Schedules (EVO), a training-free acceleration framework that globally schedules cache refreshes via evolutionary search. EVO represents each candidate as a complete schedule over the block-timestep lattice. Thus, redundant transformer computations during iterative denoising can be skipped through cache reuse while preserving closed-loop rollout performance. To make the search practical, EVO introduces redundancy-aware initialization, which seeds the population with promising schedules, and target-conditioned early stopping, which verifies and terminates once a desired performance target is reached. The offline-optimized schedule can be directly plugged into pretrained diffusion policies without retraining. Extensive manipulation benchmarks show that EVO preserves near-full performance while substantially reducing computation, achieving up to 8.05x action-generation speedup and reducing FLOPs from 15.77G to as low as 1.96G. Source code is available at https://github.com/pillom/EVO.

</details>


### [65] [Toward Reliable RGB-D Semantic Segmentation: Handling Missing Modalities via Condition Dropout](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.20326)
*Xuchen Zhu,Yajuan Wei,Shuang Hao,Jiwei Jiang,Guanxiang Mao,Fang Ren*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 提出一种名为 Condition Dropout（ConD）的持续训练范式，用于解决 RGB-D 语义分割中因传感器故障或遮挡导致某一模态缺失时的性能骤降问题，通过模拟完整、RGB缺失、深度缺失三种输入情况，冻结原编码器并训练带零初始化特征注入的复制编码器，从而在保持完整模态精度的同时提升对缺失模态的鲁棒性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有 RGB-D 语义分割模型假设 RGB 和深度模态始终可用，但实际应用中传感器故障或遮挡常导致某一模态缺失。此时，仅用完整模态训练的模型无法有效利用剩余模态信息，造成严重的性能退化。

Method: ConD 采用持续训练范式：以预训练的 RGB-D 模型为基础，在第二阶段随机模拟完整、RGB缺失、深度缺失三种输入情况；冻结原始编码器，训练复制编码器并引入零初始化的特征注入，使模型学会在缺失模态时仍能利用可用信息。

Result: 在 NYU-Depth V2 和 SUN RGB-D 数据集上的实验表明，ConD 显著提升了模型在模态缺失情况下的鲁棒性，甚至在模态完整时也带来轻微的性能提升。

Conclusion: ConD 能有效缓解 RGB-D 语义分割中因单模态缺失导致的性能退化，同时保持甚至略优于完整模态下的精度，是一种简单且有效的训练策略。

Abstract: RGB-D semantic segmentation has achieved remarkable progress, yet most models assume that RGB and depth are always available. In practice, failures or occlusions of surveillance sensors often remove one modality. Although RGB or depth alone can contain sufficient cues, models trained only on full-modality inputs fail to exploit the remaining modality once one is missing, causing severe degradation. We tackle this issue with a simple continued-training paradigm, \emph{Condition Dropout (ConD)}, which mitigates degradation while preserving full-modality accuracy. Starting from a pretrained RGB-D model, ConD adds a second stage that randomly simulates complete, RGB-missing, and depth-missing inputs, freezes the original encoders, and trains copied encoders with zero-initialized feature injection. Experiments on NYU-Depth V2 and SUN RGB-D show that ConD improves robustness under missing modalities and even yields slight gains when modalities are complete. Our code will be made publicly available upon acceptance.

</details>


### [66] [Test-Time Training for Modality Order Consistency in Vision-Language Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.20351)
*Aditi Gupta,Yossi Gandelsman*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文发现视觉语言模型（VLM）对图像和问题的呈现顺序敏感，图像优先提示始终优于问题优先提示，形成模态顺序失败。作者提出一种顺序一致的测试时训练方法，有效缩小了模态顺序差距，甚至提升了更强的图像优先分支性能。激活修补定位到中层网络区域为顺序失败的关键区域，测试时训练修复了该区域的对齐问题。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 揭示并解决视觉语言模型中存在的模态顺序敏感性（即图像和问题输入顺序不同导致性能差异）问题，探究该现象的根源并开发有效的缓解方法。

Method: 1) 系统评估三种VLM在三个基准上图像优先与问题优先提示的性能差异；2) 设计顺序一致的测试时训练方法，通过双向一致性约束缩小模态顺序差距；3) 使用激活修补技术定位顺序失败的关键网络层区域；4) 分析测试时训练方法如何修复跨层的表示对齐。

Result: 1) 图像优先提示在所有评估设置中一致优于问题优先提示；2) 提出的测试时训练方法显著缩小了模态顺序差距，并在图像优先分支上获得比基线更优的性能；3) 激活修补定位到中层网络区域为表示分歧的关键区域；4) 测试时训练修复了该区域跨层的表示对齐问题。

Conclusion: 模态顺序敏感性是VLM中的电路级失败，简单的非对称测试时自适应可以有效缓解该问题，甚至提升基线性能。这表明模型存在可定位的电路级缺陷，通过针对性训练可以修复并增强模型鲁棒性。

Abstract: We find that vision-language models are sensitive to a specific semantically irrelevant change: the order in which the image and question are presented. Across three models and three benchmarks, image first prompting consistently outperforms question-first prompting, revealing a repeatable modality order failure. We use this gap to design an order-consistent test-time training method. Our method substantially closes the modality-order gap across all evaluated settings. Surprisingly, it also yields consistent improvements in the stronger image-first branch over the baseline, hence bootstrapping both orderings toward mutual consistency. Activation patching localizes the ordering failure to a narrow mid-network region where representations diverge sharply between prompt orders. We find that the test-time training method repairs this misalignment across layers. Together, our results identify modality-order sensitivity as a circuit-level failure in VLMs and demonstrate that simple, asymmetric test-time adaptation can effectively mitigate it and even improve performance over the baseline.

</details>


### [67] [Look Less, Think Faster: Joint Token-Compute Adaptation for Multimodal LLMs](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.20357)
*Pengcheng Wang,Zhiquan Wang,Jayoung Lee,Zhuoyan Xu,Ran Xu,Saurabh Bagchi,Yin Li,Somali Chaterji*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: SmartVL是一个统一的适应性推理框架，通过联合控制视觉token数量和模型计算能力，在多模态大语言模型中实现了动态的精度-效率帕累托优化。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 多模态大语言模型的高推理成本（源于大量视觉token和大语言模型的繁重计算）阻碍了实际部署。现有方法通常独立优化单个维度（如剪枝冗余token或跳过LLM层/头），忽略了不同维度间计算资源应根据输入内容动态分配的根本耦合问题。
