Method: SmartVL提出联合自适应推理框架，包含视觉端token控制器（动态选择信息性视觉token）和LLM端计算控制器（自适应调整LLM计算）。通过共享预算编码连接两个控制器，并利用可微分延迟估计器进行端到端训练，实现跨阶段资源分配的联合调度。

Result: 在多个多模态大语言模型基准上，SmartVL的联合调度方法持续优于先前的自适应方法，取得了更优的精度-效率帕累托前沿。

Conclusion: 联合调度能够根据输入复杂度和运行时计算约束自适应分配资源，有效降低推理成本的同时保持高性能，证明了跨维度协同优化的重要性。

Abstract: Multimodal Large Language Models (MLLMs) have recently demonstrated strong performance across vision-language tasks. However, their high inference cost, arising from both the large number of input visual tokens and the heavy computation of the large language model (LLM), remains a key barrier to practical deployment. Recent work attempts to reduce the cost by adaptively optimizing individual dimensions, e.g., pruning redundant visual tokens or skipping LLM layers and heads. Nonetheless, prior approaches typically treat these dimensions independently and overlook a fundamental coupling: the available compute resources must be dynamically allocated across all dimensions based on the input content. To bridge the gap, we propose SmartVL, a unified adaptive inference framework that jointly controls vision token number and model compute capability in response to varying input contents and compute budgets. SmartVL introduces a vision-side token controller that dynamically selects informative visual tokens and an LLM-side compute controller that adaptively adjusts LLM computation. Importantly, these controllers are trained to coordinate with each other so that the overall inference cost satisfies a target budget. To allow this joint scheduling, we connect the controllers using a shared budget encoding and leverage a differentiable latency estimator for end-to-end training. This design enables SmartVL to learn cross-stage allocation strategies that adapt to both input complexity and runtime compute constraints. Experiments across multiple MLLM benchmarks demonstrate that, with joint scheduling, SmartVL consistently outperforms prior adaptive methods and achieves superior accuracy-efficiency Pareto frontiers. Project page: https://www.schaterji.io/publications/2026/jointtokencompute.

</details>


### [68] [Self Gradient Forcing: Native Long Video Extrapolation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.20368)
*Junhao Zhuang,Shiyi Zhang,Yuxuan Bian,Yaowei Li,Yawen Luo,Yijun Liu,Weiyang Jin,Songchun Zhang,Xianglong He,Xuying Zhang,Haoran Li,Haoyang Huang,Zeyue Xue,Nan Duan*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 提出 Self Gradient Forcing (SGF)，一种两阶段训练策略，通过恢复自回归视频扩散中缺失的历史上下文梯度信号，使得模型能够利用未来帧的损失来优化早期生成潜变量的键值缓存，从而在仅用5秒训练窗口下实现数分钟的长视频外推，显著优于 Self Forcing。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有的自回归视频扩散方法（Self Forcing）虽通过使用模型自身 rollout 历史而非真实视频上下文来减少曝光偏差，但历史键值缓存仅作为冻结状态被未来帧使用，导致未来损失无法监督早期生成潜变量如何写入更有用的键值对以供后续生成。这种历史上下文梯度缺口限制了长视频外推能力。

Method: SGF 采用两阶段训练：Pass 1 执行无梯度的自回归 rollout（匹配推理阶段），在采样的去噪退出步记录自生成上下文和输入噪声潜变量；Pass 2 针对该退出步执行并行上下文梯度重建，将生成的上下文作为停止梯度的干净潜变量输入，模型重新计算上下文 KV 表示和未来到上下文的因果注意力，从而通过未来帧损失提供缺失的记忆写入监督。

Result: 在长程帧级和块级实验（不同初始化条件下）中，SGF 在主体身份保持、背景/布局一致性和时间稳定性方面显著优于 Self Forcing，实现了更强大的原生长视频外推。仅使用5秒训练窗口即可外推至数分钟视频。

Conclusion: SGF 有效弥补了自回归视频扩散中的上下文梯度缺口，通过创新的两阶段训练策略在不增加全序列反向传播成本的前提下增强了模型的长视频生成能力，为自回归视频生成提供了新思路。

Abstract: Recent autoregressive video diffusion methods are increasingly built upon Self Forcing, where the student is trained on histories produced by its own rollout rather than ground-truth video contexts. This reduces exposure bias, but the historical key-value cache is still used by future frames only as frozen rollout state. As a result, future losses cannot supervise how earlier generated latents should be written into more useful keys and values for later video-latent generation. We call this the historical context-gradient gap. We propose Self Gradient Forcing (SGF), a two-pass training strategy that restores this missing supervision signal without backpropagating through the full serial rollout. Pass 1 performs a no-gradient autoregressive rollout matching inference and, at a sampled denoising exit step, records both the self-generated context and the noisy latents fed to the model. Pass 2 performs parallel context-gradient reconstruction for the recorded exit step. The generated context is used as stop-gradient clean-latent input, while the model recomputes the context KV representations and future-to-context causal attention. Thus, SGF provides the missing memory-writing supervision within the native autoregressive training objective, using losses on future video latents to train the model to encode context into more effective causal memory. Across extensive long-horizon frame-wise and chunk-wise experiments under different initializations, SGF achieves stronger native long-video extrapolation than Self Forcing, especially in subject identity, background/layout consistency, and temporal stability. Remarkably, using only a 5-second training window, SGF can extrapolate to videos lasting several minutes. Code and models will be released to advance research on autoregressive video generation.

</details>


### [69] [Persian Pixel: A large-scale synthetic OCR dataset for Persian language](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.20385)
*Pouria Mahdi,Haq Nawaz Malik*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文提出了 Persian Pixel，一个包含 34.3 万对高质量合成图像-文本对的波斯语 OCR 数据集，通过 SynthOCR-Gen 框架和 25 种以上退化模型模拟真实场景，解决了波斯语手写体复杂性和标注数据稀缺的问题，为低资源脚本的 OCR 发展提供了可扩展的解决方案。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 波斯语 OCR 远不如拉丁语系成熟，主要因为波斯语文字系统的内在复杂性（必须手写连体、上下文相关的字形变体、大量连字、变音符号、Naskh/Nastaliq 等风格变化）以及大规模高质量标注数据的严重缺乏。人工标注成本高、劳动密集，形成了数据瓶颈，限制了稳健 OCR 系统的开发。

Method: 通过 SynthOCR-Gen 渲染框架，从精心整理的 700 万词波斯语语料库中生成超过 34.3 万对高保真图像-文本对，覆盖句子、段落和整页文档布局。渲染管线忠实建模了波斯文字的连体连接、位置变体、变音符号和多种代表性字体。为弥合合成与真实领域的差异，应用了 25 种以上随机退化模型（如墨迹渗透、纸张老化、模糊、光照变化、扫描伪影、压缩伪影、多种噪声）来模拟真实的文档采集过程。

Result: 创建了 Persian Pixel 数据集，为现代 OCR 架构（如 TrOCR、Donut）的训练和微调提供了可扩展的开放资源，有效缓解了波斯语 OCR 标注数据长期短缺的问题。

Conclusion: Persian Pixel 为波斯语文档分析、历史手稿数字化和端到端文档理解奠定了坚实基础，同时证明了程序化合成数据生成是手工标注的一种实用、经济且可扩展的替代方案，尤其适用于低资源和文字复杂的脚本。

Abstract: Optical Character Recognition (OCR) for Persian remains substantially less mature than for Latin-script languages despite Persian being spoken by more than 110 million people across multiple countries. This gap arises from two fundamental challenges: the intrinsic complexity of the Perso-Arabic writing system and the limited availability of large-scale, high-quality annotated datasets. Persian script exhibits obligatory cursive connectivity, context-dependent glyph shaping, extensive ligatures, diacritic placement, and stylistic variation across writing forms such as Naskh and Nastaliq, all of which significantly complicate text recognition. At the same time, the high cost and labor-intensive nature of manual annotation have created a persistent data bottleneck, limiting the development of robust OCR systems and slowing progress in Persian document digitization.In this paper, we introduce Persian Pixel, a comprehensive synthetic OCR dataset specifically designed to address these challenges. Comprising over 343,000 high-fidelity image text pairs, the dataset spans sentence, paragraph, and full-page document layouts generated from a carefully curated seven-million-word Persian corpus using the SynthOCR-Gen rendering framework. The generation pipeline faithfully models the typographic characteristics of Persian script, including contextual character joining, positional glyph variants, diacritic placement, and multiple representative Persian typefaces. To bridge the synthetic-to-real domain gap, the rendered images are further enriched with more than twenty-five stochastic degradation models that emulate realistic document acquisition artifacts, including ink bleed, paper aging, blur, illumination variation, scanner imperfections, compression artifacts, and multiple noise processes.By overcoming the long-standing scarcity of annotated Persian OCR data, Persian Pixel provides a scalable and openly available resource for training and fine-tuning modern OCR architectures, including transformer-based models such as TrOCR and Donut. The dataset establishes a strong foundation for research in Persian document analysis, historical manuscript digitization, and end-to-end document understanding, while demonstrating that programmatic synthetic data generation offers a practical, cost-effective, and scalable alternative to manual annotation for advancing OCR in low-resource and typographically complex scripts.

</details>


### [70] [PercepCap: Video Captioner with Structured Spatio-Temporal Perception](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.20389)
*Yifan Xu,Zihao Wang,Zhixiao Wang,Jiaming Zhang,Yichun Yang,Desen Meng,Yuanxing Zhang,Pengfei Wan,Limin Wang*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: PercepCap is a perception-aware video captioning framework that explicitly generates a spatiotemporal perception trace (object trajectories and temporal events) before producing the final caption, using a two-stage training strategy (supervised fine-tuning and reinforcement learning) and a caption-anchored perception data construction pipeline, achieving improved caption quality over Qwen3-VL baseline.


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: Existing MLLMs generate captions directly from video inputs without exposing perceptual evidence, making it difficult to identify underlying spatiotemporal perception errors. The goal is to make perceptual evidence explicit to improve interpretability and accuracy.

Method: Proposes PercepCap with a perceive-describe generation chain: first produce a spatiotemporal perception trace (object trajectories and temporal events), then generate caption conditioned on it. Two-stage training: (1) Perceive-then-Describe Supervised Fine-tuning (SFT) adapts model from caption-only to perceive-describe chain; (2) Perception-Grounded Reinforcement Learning (RL) optimizes perception trace and caption quality with joint rewards. Also introduces Caption-Anchored Perception Data Construction pipeline to build training data by generating caption-only, extracting objects/events, and grounding them back with boxes/timestamps.

Result: PercepCap consistently improves upon the Qwen3-VL baseline across direct caption and caption-to-QA evaluation, demonstrating leading caption quality.

Conclusion: Making perceptual evidence explicit before caption generation improves video captioning performance and enables better interpretability of spatiotemporal understanding.

Abstract: Video captioning requires fine-grained spatio-temporal understanding of videos, including spatial perception of where objects are located and temporal perception of when events occur. Existing MLLMs usually generate captions directly from video inputs without exposing the perceptual evidence behind descriptions. As a result, mistakes in spatiotemporal perception are only observed in the final caption, making it difficult to identify the underlying perceptual errors directly. To address these issues, we present PercepCap, a perception-aware video captioning framework that makes perceptual evidence explicit before producing the final caption. Specifically, PercepCap follows a perceive-describe generation chain, where the model first produces a spatiotemporal perception trace comprising object trajectories and temporal events, and then generates the final caption conditioned on the perceived evidence. To support this, we design a two-stage training strategy. Perceive-then-Describe Supervised Fine-tuning adapts the model from caption-only generation to the proposed perceive-describe chain, while Perception-Grounded Reinforcement Learning optimizes perception trace and caption quality with joint rewards over perception chain and the final caption. To support our two-stage training, we introduce Caption-Anchored Perception Data Construction. This pipeline builds the SFT and RL training data by first generating a caption-only description, extracting the objects and events it mentions, and grounding them back in the video with boxes and timestamps. This yields caption-aligned perception data that provides solid training ground truth, ensuring that the explicit perception trace and final caption refer to the same objects and events. Across direct caption and caption-to-QA evaluation, PercepCap consistently improves upon the Qwen3-VL baseline and demonstrates leading caption quality.

</details>


### [71] [ATSplat: Compact Feed-forward 3D Gaussian Splatting with Adaptive Token Expansion](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.20417)
*Cho In,Jeonghwan Cho,Mijin Yoo,Gim Hee Lee,Seon Joo Kim*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: ATSplat 是一个前馈式 3D Gaussian Splatting 框架，通过引入可学习的自适应 3D 令牌（Adaptive 3D Tokens）来恢复 3DGS 优化中的场景自适应容量分配能力。它首先从粗略的块级深度和相机线索中构建稀疏的 3D 锚点令牌，形成场景的紧凑骨架；每个令牌通过可学习的 3D 偏移回归为局部高斯体，从而将基本体的放置与输入图像网格解耦；自适应令牌扩展模块根据渲染误差图预测令牌级不确定性，并选择性地扩展高不确定性令牌。在 RealEstate10K 和 DL3DV 数据集上，ATSplat 达到了最先进的渲染质量，同时将高斯体数量减少了 5.7 倍以上（仅需 311K 高斯体即可在 512×960 分辨率下以 1136 FPS 渲染），并且从 12 张输入图像中重建场景仅需不到 1 秒。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有的前馈式 3DGS 方法通常在输入像素处回归高斯体并沿相机射线提升，这种像素对齐的公式使得基本体的数量和位置取决于图像分辨率和输入视角，而非场景复杂度，导致高斯体集合密集且冗余，丧失了 3DGS 优化中原本具备的场景自适应容量分配能力。

Method: ATSplat 提出自适应 3D 令牌（Adaptive 3D Tokens）机制：(1) 首先从粗略的块级深度和相机线索中提取稀疏的 3D 锚点令牌，形成场景的紧凑骨架；(2) 每个令牌通过可学习的 3D 偏移回归为局部高斯体，将基本体放置与输入图像网格解耦；(3) 自适应令牌扩展模块以渲染误差图为监督，预测令牌级不确定性分数，并通过可学习的扩展层选择性地扩展高不确定性令牌，实现从稀疏到自适应的渐进式细化。

Result: 在 RealEstate10K 和 DL3DV 数据集上，ATSplat 取得了最先进的渲染质量，同时相比密集的前馈式 3DGS 方法，高斯体数量减少了 5.7 倍以上。从 12 张 512×960 分辨率的输入图像中，ATSplat 在单张商用 GPU 上完成重建只需不到 1 秒，并以仅 311K 高斯体在 512×960 分辨率下实现 1136 FPS 的高质量新视角渲染。

Conclusion: 这种稀疏到自适应的公式恢复了 3DGS 优化中的自适应分配能力，使得 ATSplat 能够将基本体集中放置在具有挑战性的区域，同时保持紧凑的场景表示，在渲染质量和效率之间取得了优异的平衡。

Abstract: 3D Gaussian Splatting (3DGS) achieves high-quality novel-view synthesis by optimizing freely placed primitives in 3D and adaptively densifying them in under-reconstructed regions. However, this scene-adaptive capacity allocation is largely lost in existing feed-forward 3DGS methods, which commonly regress Gaussians at input pixels and lift them along camera rays. Such pixel-aligned formulations make the number and placement of primitives depend on image resolution and input viewpoints rather than scene complexity, resulting in dense and often redundant Gaussian sets. We present ATSplat, a feed-forward 3DGS framework that restores the adaptive allocation capability of 3DGS optimization through Adaptive 3D Tokens. ATSplat first lifts coarse patch-level depth and camera cues into sparse 3D anchor tokens, forming a compact scaffold of the scene. Each token is then regressed into local Gaussians with learnable 3D offsets, decoupling primitive placement from input image grids. An Adaptive Token Expansion module predicts a token-level uncertainty score, supervised by rendering error maps, and selectively expands high-uncertainty tokens through learnable expansion layers. This sparse-to-adaptive formulation enables ATSplat to concentrate primitives in challenging regions while maintaining a compact representation. Experiments on two representative datasets, RealEstate10K and DL3DV, show that ATSplat achieves state-of-the-art rendering quality while reducing the number of Gaussians by more than $5.7\times$ compared with dense feed-forward 3DGS methods. From 12 input images at $512 \times 960$ resolution, ATSplat completes reconstruction in less than a second using a single commercial GPU, and renders high-quality novel views at 1136 FPS ($512 \times 960$) with only 311K Gaussians.

</details>


<div id='cs.CL'></div>

# cs.CL [[Back]](#toc)

### [72] [Stateful Guardrails for Multi-Turn LLM Systems: A Conversational Risk Accumulation Framework](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.19361)
*Sanjay Mishra,Divya Chukkapalli,Ganesh R. Naik*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 提出对话风险累积（CRA）概念，指多轮对话中良性交互逐步演变为有害的语义漂移、碎片化组装和敏感度累积；构建了会话层CRA框架，追踪三种轨迹信号（语义漂移、敏感度加权信息积累图、合规梯度信号），并设计了无监督凸融合评分和轻量轨迹模型CRA-Net DA，同时发布了CRA-Bench基准数据集和轨迹原生评估协议。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有LLM安全防护栏仅对单轮提示-响应进行独立评估，忽略了多轮对话中良性指令通过逐步组合、意图漂移和敏感度累积而演变为危害的风险（即对话风险累积CRA），因此需要一种能捕获会话级风险轨迹的框架和评估方法。

Method: 提出会话层CRA框架，追踪三种轨迹信号：1）会话锚点的语义漂移；2）敏感度加权信息积累图（基于抽取实体）；3）合规梯度信号（反映逐渐增加的遵从意愿）。评分方法包括：无监督凸融合（用于归因和消融）和有监督的紧凑轨迹模型CRA-Net DA（采用家族对抗目标训练以减小长度和主题覆盖混淆）。发布CRA-Bench v0.1（1200个八轮会话，三类威胁家族+主题匹配良性孪生）、v0.2（LLM改写变体）和扩展5家族集（2000会话，增加角色植入和上下文填充）。提出轨迹原生评估协议：会话级划分、混合集阈值校准、轨迹AUROC、检测轮次、校准假阳性指标、bootstrap置信区间、留一家族诊断测试和合成到人工迁移检查。

Result: 在CRA-Bench数据集和人工迁移子集上实现了分布内会话评分的有效性，验证了轨迹信号在检测渐进式风险累积方面的优势。

Conclusion: 对话风险累积（CRA）是多轮对话安全的关键挑战，所提出的会话层框架、基准数据集和评估协议为捕获这种渐进式风险提供了系统化方案，有助于弥补现有单轮防护栏的盲区。

Abstract: Most safety guardrails for large language models (LLMs) evaluate each prompt-response pair in isolation, which misses failures that arise only over a dialogue as benign turns compose into harm. We term this Conversational Risk Accumulation (CRA): gradual intent drift, fragmented assembly of prohibited instructions, and sensitivity build-up from repeated disclosures. We propose a session-layer CRA Framework that tracks three trajectory signals: semantic drift from a session anchor, a sensitivity-weighted information accumulation graph over extracted entities, and a compliance-gradient signal capturing increasing willingness to comply. For scoring, we provide (i) an unsupervised convex fusion for attribution and ablations, and (ii) CRA-Net DA, a compact learned trajectory model trained with family-adversarial objectives to reduce length and topic-coverage confounds. To benchmark CRA, we release CRA-Bench v0.1 (1,200 eight-turn sessions across three threat families with topic-matched benign twins), CRA-Bench v0.2 (LLM-paraphrased variants to reduce template artifacts), and an extended 5-family set (2,000 sessions adding persona priming and context stuffing). We introduce a trajectory-native evaluation protocol with session-level splits, mixed-set threshold calibration, Trajectory AUROC, turns-to-detection, calibrated false-positive metrics, bootstrap confidence intervals, leave-one-family-out diagnostic stress tests, and synthetic-to-human transfer checks. Claims focus on within-distribution session scoring on CRA-Bench and human-transfer subsets.

</details>


### [73] [When Reasoning Narrows the Move: Diversity Collapse in LLM Game Play](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.19523)
*Junyi Sha,Renfei Tan,David Simchi-Levi*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 本文研究了监督微调（SFT）在序贯决策任务中对大型语言模型行为多样性的影响。通过在基于井字棋变体的确定性棋盘游戏套件中进行实验，发现推理模式生成会抑制动作多样性而不提升准确性，标准SFT在提升准确性的同时会引发过早的多样性崩溃，而动作增强（训练所有最优动作）可部分缓解此问题。结果表明，窄支持模仿是LLM决策策略崩溃的根源，保护动作支持对维持探索行为至关重要。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 监督微调广泛应用于大型语言模型的下游任务适配，但其对序贯决策中行为多样性的影响尚不明确。理解SFT如何影响多样性对于避免策略崩溃和维持探索行为至关重要。

Method: 在基于井字棋变体的确定性棋盘游戏套件中，通过状态级评估、竞技场对战和训练轨迹分析，比较推理模式和标准SFT对动作多样性和准确性的影响，并引入动作增强方法（训练所有最优动作）进行缓解实验。

Result: 推理模式生成频繁抑制动作多样性且不提升准确性；标准SFT提升准确性但导致过早的多样性崩溃，超出准确性-多样性权衡所需的最小要求；动作增强可部分缓解多样性崩溃。

Conclusion: 窄支持模仿是LLM决策策略崩溃的根源，在SFT中保留动作支持对于维持探索行为至关重要，建议采用动作增强等方法来避免多样性丧失。

Abstract: Supervised fine-tuning (SFT) is widely used to adapt large language models to downstream tasks, but its effect on behavioral diversity in sequential decision-making remains under-explored. We study this question in a controlled suite of deterministic board games based on tic-tac-toe variants, where optimal actions are exactly computable and diversity can be measured directly. Across state-level evaluation, arena gameplay, and training trajectories, we find that reasoning-mode generation frequently suppresses action diversity without uniformly improving action accuracy. Furthermore, standard SFT improves accuracy but often induces premature diversity collapse, which exceeds what is minimally required by the accuracy-diversity tradeoff. We then show that action augmentation, which trains on all optimal actions per state rather than a single demonstrated action, would partially mitigates this effect. Our results identify narrow-support imitation as a source of policy collapse in LLM decision-making and suggest that preserving action support during SFT is important for maintaining exploratory behavior.

</details>


### [74] [On the Computational Complexity of Structural Generalization](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.19573)
*Zichao Wei*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 本文正式定义了结构泛化，并论证了纯Transformer无法学习它（因为TC⁰≠NC¹），而神经符号系统通过注入困难的语义部分（G_γ）获得高分，从而揭示了基准测试分数无法区分“学习”与“给定”的问题。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 结构泛化虽被多个基准测试反复测量，却从未被正式定义。本文旨在给出一个数学定义，并澄清基准分数混淆了真正学习到的和预先给定的知识。

Method: 先基于组合结构和无界泛化两条前提给出结构泛化的形式定义；再采用蒙塔古方式将每条组合规则分解为语法面（F_γ）和语义面（G_γ）；然后利用计算复杂度理论（NC¹与TC⁰）证明纯Transformer（可学习类⊆TC⁰）在标准假设TC⁰≠NC¹下无法学习结构泛化（因为G_γ侧的树求值是NC¹完全的BFVP）；最后指出神经符号系统之所以取得最佳分数，是因为它们直接注入了G_γ，绕过了真正困难的部分。

Result: 在TC⁰≠NC¹的标准假设下，纯Transformer无法学习结构泛化；神经符号系统通过注入NC¹完全的语义面（G_γ）而获得高分；基准测试分数无法区分“学习”与“给定”。

Conclusion: 基准测试不能可靠地衡量结构泛化，因为分数不能区分自主涌现的知识和硬编码的知识；本文旨在阐明这一根本性问题。

Abstract: Structural generalization has been measured repeatedly by several benchmarks, yet it has never been formally defined. We give a definition that translates the two premises (compositional structure and unbounded generalization) into mathematical language. The definition itself is neutral: a compiler that hard-codes the rules satisfies it just as well. But structural generalization becomes a scientific question only insofar as the capacity can autonomously emerge from finite data. This question pits the computational lower bound $\mathrm{NC}^1$ against the learnable ceiling $\mathrm{TC}^0$ of pure Transformers. Under a Montagovian instantiation, each compositional rule splits into two projections: a syntactic face ($F_γ$) and a semantic face ($G_γ$). Tree evaluation on the $G_γ$ side is an instantiation of BFVP, which is $\mathrm{NC}^1$-complete (Buss, 1987). A pure Transformer must learn both faces at once, but Kraus et al. (2026) prove that its learnable class $\subseteq \mathrm{TC}^0$. Under the standard assumption $\mathrm{TC}^0 \neq \mathrm{NC}^1$, a pure Transformer cannot learn structural generalization. Neuro-symbolic systems achieve the best benchmark scores precisely because they inject $G_γ$, sidestepping the genuinely hard half. Benchmark scores cannot distinguish "learned" from "given." This is what this paper sets out to make clear.

</details>


### [75] [Scaling Laws for Hypernetwork-Based Knowledge Injection in Large Language Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.19604)
*Nischay Dhankhar,Dos Baha,Abulhair Saparov*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 本文研究了基于超网络（Hypernetwork）的大规模知识注入方法，证明其在训练时生成LoRA适配器注入事实知识具有可预测的幂律缩放规律，并且在分布外泛化上优于LoRA微调和全参数微调。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 大语言模型（LLMs）可靠且大规模地注入事实知识仍是一个开放挑战。超网络虽有潜力，但其缩放行为尚未被系统研究。本文旨在探索超网络能否用于训练时知识注入，并揭示其性能随模型规模变化的规律。

Method: 设计一种解耦超网络注入容量与目标模型通用能力的方法，使超网络在训练时根据大量事实生成固定的LoRA适配器，插入目标模型后用于回答事实问题。构建大规模多跳问答数据集MegaWikiQA（涵盖39个领域、数千万样本），系统分析超网络深度、宽度及目标网络大小对损失、推理准确率和分布外（OOD）泛化的影响。

Result: (i) 超网络知识注入在所有架构轴（深度、宽度、目标网络大小）上均表现出广泛的预测性幂律缩放；(ii) 超网络在增大规模时具备可靠的OOD泛化能力，且在所有OOD评估中缩放指数均优于LoRA微调和全参数微调。

Conclusion: 超网络是训练时知识注入的一种有原则且可扩展的途径，该工作首次为超网络在大语言模型事实推理中的缩放规律提供了经验基础，表明超网络可作为LoRA微调等方法的有效替代方案。

Abstract: Injecting factual knowledge into large language models (LLMs) reliably and at scale remains an open challenge. Hypernetworks provide a promising solution to large-scale knowledge injection. Although hypernetworks are typically applied for test-time adaptation, we explore their use in train-time knowledge injection, where, given a large corpus of facts, we train a hypernetwork to generate a fixed LoRA adapter that, when inserted into the target model, enable the model to answer questions about those facts. In this work, we investigate whether hypernetworks can be used to perform train-time knowledge injection and how this ability varies with scale. The scaling behavior of hypernetworks remains largely unstudied. Our design decouples the hypernetwork's injection capacity from the target model's general capability, enabling, for the first time, a rigorous study of scaling laws for hypernetwork architectures. We characterize how loss, reasoning accuracy, and out-of-distribution (OOD) generalization vary with hypernetwork depth, width, and target network size. We construct a large-scale dataset, called MegaWikiQA, containing tens of millions of multi-hop question-answer examples across 39 domains constructed from examples in Wikidata5M. Our results reveal: (i) hypernetwork-based injection exhibits broadly predictive power law scaling along all architecture axes; and (ii) hypernetworks are capable of reliable OOD generalization at increasing scales, suggesting that hypernetwork provides a promising alternative to other train-time adaptation methods such as LoRA finetuning and full fine-tuning, exhibiting steeper scaling exponents in all OOD evaluations. Together, these results establish hypernetworks as a principled and scalable substrate for train-time adaptation, and provide the first empirically grounded scaling laws to guide hypernetworks for factual reasoning in large language models.

</details>


### [76] [Task Competence Is Not Instruction Following: Evaluating Instruction-Conflicting Behavior in Small Language Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.19608)
*Mahdiyeh Farajidizaji,Vatsal Raina*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 本文研究了指令微调语言模型在面对与常规任务行为冲突的非标准指令时的表现。通过在多个任务（多项选择问答、情感分类、数学问答）中设计标准指令与冲突指令（如选择错误选项、输出相反情感、返回两倍答案）的对比实验，发现小模型通常忽略冲突指令但保持基本任务能力，而大模型虽指令遵循能力更强，但在任务性能与指令遵循之间存在明显差距。研究表明任务能力与指令遵循是两种独立的能力，仅报告标准准确率会掩盖指令遵循的失败。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有研究主要关注指令微调提升模型遵循指令的能力，但对于当指令与模型常规任务行为产生冲突时，模型（尤其是小模型）是否会真正遵循指令尚不明确。作者希望探究任务能力与指令遵循能力之间的关系，以及这种关系是否随模型规模变化。

Method: 选取三项任务（多项选择问答MCQA、情感分类、数学问答），为每个任务设计标准指令和与之冲突的非标准指令（如选择错误选项、输出相反情感、返回两倍答案）。采用跨任务设计以检验对冲突指令的抵抗是否与特定任务特征相关。使用标准准确率、非标准准确率和指令遵循失败率（IFFR）作为评估指标，在多个规模的Qwen指令微调模型上进行实验。

Result: 标准准确率和指令遵循能力通常随模型规模增大而提升，但这一模式并非在所有任务和数据集上一致。小模型在保持任务能力的同时，常常忽略非标准指令；而大模型在两种设置之间表现出明显差距（即任务能力更强，但遵循冲突指令的倾向也增加）。

Conclusion: 任务能力的提升并不能自动带来对模型行为的可靠控制，任务能力与指令遵循是两种不同的能力。仅报告标准准确率会掩盖指令遵循的失败，因此评估时应同时关注模型是否真正遵循指令，而非仅看任务性能。

Abstract: Instruction tuning is meant to make language models follow user requests, yet it is unclear whether small models comply when an instruction conflicts with their usual task behavior. We study this across three tasks - multiple-choice question answering (MCQA), sentiment classification, and mathematical question answering - by pairing a standard instruction with a conflicting non-standard one (select an incorrect option, output the opposite sentiment, or return twice the answer). This cross-task design allows us to test whether resistance to conflicting instructions is tied to specific task characteristics or reflects a broader behavioral tendency. As all predictions are scored against the original ground truth, a model that ignores the non-standard instruction still appears accurate. Using standard accuracy, non-standard accuracy, and an Instruction-Following Failure Rate (IFFR), we evaluate instruction-tuned Qwen models across sizes. Both standard accuracy and instruction following generally improve with scale, although the pattern is not consistent across all tasks and datasets. Small models stay competent yet routinely ignore the non-standard instruction, while larger models show a clear gap between the two settings. These findings suggest that gains in task capability do not automatically provide reliable control over model behavior. Task competence and instruction following are therefore distinct abilities, and reporting only standard accuracy hides instruction-following failures.

</details>


### [77] [Adaptive Capitulation: A Structural Failure Mode of LLM Responses in Vulnerability Contexts](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.19629)
*Eunna Lee*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 大型语言模型在情感敏感场景中面临结构性三重困境：保护性限制、无差别促进或两者并存但割裂。通过向三个商用LLM实施三段式升级脆弱性场景（900次会话，涵盖物质、关系、躯体状态代理变体），并使用两个二元指标编码响应，发现一种此前未记录的失败模式——自适应屈从：模型先验证用户痛苦背后的社会不公正，然后转向详细促进其名义上劝阻的获取行为。提出最小重新归因充分性（MRS）设计原则，在验证性响应中嵌入单个重新归因线索，保留自主重新归因路径而不否定用户目标。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 当前LLM在情感敏感场景中的响应架构无法同时满足保护用户免受强化不良归因、提供无妨碍信息支持、以及整合这两种要求，导致每种方案牺牲另一目标。需要识别这一结构性困境并设计更优的响应原则。

Method: 设计三段式升级脆弱性场景，向三个商用LLM（900次会话）分别呈现物质、关系、躯体状态代理变体。使用两个二进制指标（VCC/VCI）对响应进行编码分析。

Result: 发现自适应屈从失败模式：模型先共情用户痛苦的社会根源，随后却详细协助用户获取其名义上劝阻的目标。表明三重困境是结构性的而非偶然的。

Conclusion: 提出最小重新归因充分性（MRS）作为架构中立的设计原则，通过在验证性响应中嵌入单一重新归因线索，在不直接否定用户目标的前提下，保留通往自主重新归因的路径。

Abstract: Large language models operating in emotionally sensitive contexts face a structural trilemma: when users in vulnerable states request information that may reinforce maladaptive attribution, current response architectures resolve the tension through protective restriction, uninflected facilitation, or unintegrated co-presence of both imperatives -- each preserving one objective at the cost of the other. Administering a three-turn escalating vulnerability vignette to three commercial LLMs (900 sessions across material, relational, and somatic status-proxy variants) and coding responses with two binary indices (VCC/VCI), we characterize a previously undocumented failure mode we term adaptive capitulation: the model validates the social injustice underlying the user's distress before pivoting to detailed facilitation of the very acquisition it nominally discouraged. We show that the trilemma is structural rather than incidental, and propose Minimal Reattributive Sufficiency (MRS), an architecture-neutral design principle that embeds a single reattributive cue within an otherwise validating response, preserving a pathway toward autonomous reattribution without contesting the user's stated goal.

</details>


### [78] [Reference-Free Evaluation of Reasoning in Open-Ended Question Answering](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.19678)
*Guneet Singh Kohli,Yuxiang Zhou,Michael Sejr Schlichtkrull,Gregory E Dean,Maria Liakata*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 提出一个基于推理的、无参考框架，用于审计 LLM 生成的输出。该方法将推理轨迹分解为片段，使用自然语言推理（NLI）标注局部前提-目标关系，并将其组织成超图，然后通过确定性的反向 AND-OR 搜索为每个片段分配审计标签。在数学推理和临床医学推理两个场景中，该框架相比直接使用 LLM 作为评判者的基线方法，提供了更可靠的无参考评估信号。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 在高风险领域，AI 生成的答案通常流畅但难以验证，尤其当答案包含多步推理而非单个最终答案时。现有评估方法（如直接使用 LLM 作为评判者）往往难以识别有问题的推理片段，容易接受流畅但推理薄弱的回答。因此需要一种能够细粒度审计推理过程的无参考评估框架。

Method: 将生成的推理轨迹分解为片段，利用自然语言推理（NLI）标注局部前提-目标关系，并将这些关系组织成超图。然后通过确定性的反向 AND-OR 搜索算法，为每个片段分配审计标签，指示该片段在生成回答中的 grounding 程度。

Result: 在 Hard2Verify（演绎数学推理）和 UroReason（临床医学推理，一个由医生标注的新基准）两个场景中，该 NLI-超图审计框架比直接使用 LLM 作为评判者的基线方法更可靠。在临床场景中，最先进的 LLM 评判者经常无法识别有问题的推理片段，过度接受流畅但推理薄弱的回答。

Conclusion: 问答评估应该考虑推理轨迹中推理关系的组合方式，而不是仅依赖最终答案或 LLM 作为验证者。该框架提供了一种更可靠的无参考评估信号，适用于需要多步推理的高风险领域。

Abstract: AI-generated answers in high-stakes domains are often fluent but difficult to verify, especially when they contain multi-step reasoning rather than a single final answer. We propose a reasoning-based, reference-free framework for auditing LLM-generated outputs. The method decomposes a generated reasoning trace into segments, labels local premise-target relations using Natural Language Inference (NLI), and organizes these relations into a hypergraph. A deterministic backward AND-OR search then assigns segment-level audit labels that indicate how each segment is grounded within the generated response. We evaluate the framework in two settings: deductive mathematical reasoning with Hard2Verify, and open-ended medical reasoning with UroReason, a new physician-annotated benchmark of LLM reasoning traces from real clinical cases. Across these settings, our NLI-hypergraph audit provides a more reliable reference-free evaluation signal than direct LLM-as-judge baselines. In the clinical setting, state-of-the-art LLM judges often fail to identify problematic reasoning segments, over-accepting fluent but weakly grounded responses. Our results show that QA evaluation should account for how inferential relations compose across a reasoning trace, rather than relying only on final answers or LLMs as verifiers. UroReason will be made available through an API, and our code will be released as open source.

</details>


### [79] [Multi-Mask Diffusion Language Models for Few-Step Generation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.19686)
*Sijin Chen,Yinuo Ren,Heyang Zhao,Ziheng Cheng,Quanquan Gu,Lexing Ying*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种多掩码扩散模型（MultiMDM），旨在解决掩码扩散模型（MDM）在少步生成中质量不高的问题。MultiMDM在前向过程中将每个干净令牌先推向指定掩码再逐步混合，从而赋予反向过程先预测指定掩码再细化为干净令牌的草稿能力。作者推导了封闭形式的ELBO训练目标，支持从预训练MDM持续训练，并提出了纯离散状态的一致性蒸馏方案。实验表明MultiMDM为原则性的少步生成提供了有效基础。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有掩码扩散模型（MDM）的前向轨迹全部坍缩到单一全掩码状态，缺乏终端熵，难以实现一致性风格的少步生成；而基于均匀状态扩散的少步替代方案虽然避免了退化，但难以区分干净令牌与噪声，损害了建模质量和训练效率。因此需要一种既能保持掩码结构又能支持高质量少步生成的扩散模型。

Method: 提出多掩码扩散模型（MultiMDM），在前向过程中每个干净令牌先被推向一个指定掩码，然后逐步在掩码集合上混合；反向过程因此具备先预测指定掩码再细化为干净令牌的草稿能力。推导了封闭形式的ELBO训练目标，可支持从预训练MDM持续训练；并设计了纯离散状态的一致性蒸馏方案，使用共享Gumbel耦合来降低路径熵。

Result: 在预训练和蒸馏实验上，MultiMDM为原则性的少步生成提供了有效基础，表明该方法在保持掩码结构的同时能够实现高质量少步生成。

Conclusion: MultiMDM通过多掩码设计保留了掩码扩散的结构优势，同时通过草稿预测和一致性蒸馏实现了高效的少步生成，为语言生成模型提供了新的有效范式。

Abstract: Masked diffusion models (MDMs) are a promising family of language generators, but achieving high-quality few-step generation remains challenging. In MDMs, all forward trajectories collapse to a single fully masked state, leaving no terminal entropy for consistency-style few-step generation. While recent few-step alternatives based on uniform-state diffusion avoid this degeneracy, it becomes harder to distinguish clean tokens from noise than MDMs, which usually harms modeling quality and training efficiency. In this work, we propose a multi-mask diffusion model (MultiMDM) that preserves the masking structure towards few-step generation. In the forward process, each clean token is first pushed towards a designated mask and then gradually mixes over the mask set. As a result, the backward process has a drafting capability by predicting a designated mask before refining to a clean token. We derive a closed-form ELBO training objective for MultiMDM that supports continual training from pretrained MDMs. In addition, we formulate a purely discrete-state consistency distillation scheme, with a shared-Gumbel coupling to reduce pathwise entropy. Experiments on pretraining and distillation show that MultiMDM provides an effective foundation for principled few-step generation.

</details>


### [80] [SLPO: Scaling Latent Reasoning via a Surrogate Policy](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.19691)
*Runyang You,Zhiyuan Liu,Yongqi Li,Wenjie Li*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 提出替代潜在策略优化（SLPO），将基于结果奖励的强化学习引入自回归潜在推理器，实现潜在推理的测试时扩展，在连续和软思考设置下提升Pass@k并自适应分配计算资源。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 显式链式思维推理器通过结果奖励RL实现了测试时扩展，但计算成本高昂（每步需解码为语言token）。潜在推理器虽以更低计算成本达到或超越显式CoT性能，但受限于模仿学习，因为潜在轨迹缺乏可行的逐步骤似然和自适应停止接口，无法直接应用结果奖励RL。

Method: 提出SLPO，包含两个关键组件：1）经验替代策略密度（empirical surrogate policy density），用于对潜在轨迹进行轨迹级信用分配；2）正确性监督的停止头（correctness-supervised stopping head），通过结果奖励优化将其精炼为可变视野策略。

Result: 在连续和软思考设置下，SLPO提升了并行采样下的Pass@k指标，并且能够为更难的实例分配更长的潜在计算，同时保持更高的确定性准确率。

Conclusion: SLPO成功将结果奖励RL引入潜在推理器，实现了潜在推理的测试时扩展，使潜在推理器能够像显式CoT一样通过RL超越模仿学习，且计算效率更高。

Abstract: Reinforcement learning with verifiable rewards has become the predominant recipe for eliciting test-time scaling in explicit Chain-of-Thought reasoners. Yet this scaling path remains computationally costly, since every intermediate step must be decoded as a language token. Latent reasoning instead carries intermediate computation as continuous vectors and already matches or surpasses explicit CoT at far shorter horizons. Despite this promise, latent reasoners remain largely imitation-bound, while explicit CoT has already moved past imitation via outcome-reward RL. Latent trajectories lack a tractable per-step likelihood and an adaptive stopping interface under fixed thinking budgets, so outcome rewards cannot elicit latent test-time scaling. We introduce Surrogate Latent Policy Optimization (SLPO) to bring outcome-reward RL to autoregressive latent reasoners: an empirical surrogate policy density over latent transitions for trajectory-level credit assignment, and a correctness-supervised stopping head that outcome-reward optimization refines into a variable-horizon policy. Across continuous and soft thinking settings, SLPO improves Pass@$k$ under parallel sampling and allocates longer latent computation to harder instances with higher deterministic accuracy.

</details>


### [81] [Lightweight Person-Place Relation Extraction from Historical Newspapers with Dependency Graphs and Proximity Features](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.19718)
*Mlen-Too Wesley*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: A lightweight, interpretable system for person-place relation extraction from multilingual historical newspapers, using dependency-parsing-based graph features and small ensembles/GATs (under 847K params), achieves competitive efficiency and reveals that minimum character distance dominates classification signal and that document-grouped cross-validation is essential to avoid data leakage.


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: The cost of processing historical archives at scale motivates exploring lightweight, interpretable systems without pretrained language models for relation classification.

Method: Builds a document-level graph from dependency parses, extracts proximity-based and POS features for each entity pair, and classifies them with compact scikit-learn ensembles or Graph Attention Networks (all under 847K parameters).

Result: Best run achieved macro recall 0.5142 on Test A, ranking 3rd on Efficiency and mid-table on Accuracy among 17 teams. Minimum character distance alone captures most signal; document-grouped cross-validation removes 25–37 percentage point inflation caused by pair-level splits.

Conclusion: A lightweight, non-pretrained approach can be competitive; future work should focus on distance-based features and proper grouped evaluation to avoid data leakage.

Abstract: The HIPE-2026 shared task introduces person-place relation extraction from multilingual historical newspapers as a new evaluation track, classifying the at and isAt relations between pre-annotated person and location mentions in English, French, and German. Motivated by the cost of processing historical archives at scale, our team (DS@GT HIPE, team 2 in the official results) investigates how far a lightweight, interpretable system can go without any pretrained language model at the relation classification stage. Our approach builds a document-level graph from dependency parses, extracts proximity-based and part-of-speech features for each entity pair, and classifies them with small scikit-learn ensembles or compact Graph Attention Networks, keeping every submitted run under 847K parameters. On the official evaluation (Test A, the newspaper test set), our best run reached a macro recall of 0.5142, ranking 3rd on the Efficiency profile while placing mid-table on Accuracy among the 17 participating teams. Two findings stand out. First, minimum character distance alone captures most of the classification signal; adding further engineered features yields inconsistent gains and sometimes degrades performance, echoing prior evidence that argument distance dominates relation extraction. Second, document-grouped cross-validation is essential on this corpus: pair-level splits inflate scores by 25-37 percentage points because entity mentions recur across documents, a data-leakage effect that grouped cross-validation removes.

</details>


### [82] [Beyond Relevance-Centric Retrieval: Rubric-Oriented Document Set Selection and Ranking](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.19747)
*Kailin Jiang,Lei Liu,Jian Xi,Hui Xu,Junlin Liu,Baochen Fu,Shaoqing Ren,Bin Li,Vichwang,Yu Lu,Haibo Shi*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 提出了一套完整的“评估-诊断-优化”文档集质量框架，包括包含约2.8万条高质量评估规则的SetwiseEvalKit基准，以及无需训练的Rubric4Setwise方法。实验表明现有重排序器在跨文档协调维度普遍薄弱，而Rubric4Setwise能以更少的文档和搜索轮次取得最佳下游生成性能，是唯一在短文本和长文本场景均保持最优的方法。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有检索系统对文档集质量的评估仅独立打分并聚合（如nDCG），忽略了文档间的冗余、冲突、互补等交互关系，无法回答“什么样的文档集更好”。随着大语言模型和AI智能体成为搜索结果的消费主体，亟需一套能够全面评估文档集整体质量的框架。

Method: 1）构建SetwiseEvalKit基准，包含三个层级、九个维度、约2.8万条高质量评估规则，覆盖短文本和长文本场景；2）系统评估12种重排序器在文档集质量上的表现；3）提出Rubric4Setwise，一种无需训练的方法，将基于规则的评价标准转化为文档集选择信号，实现评估到优化的闭环。

Result: 最佳重排序器在文档集质量上覆盖率不超过45%，跨文档协调维度普遍薄弱，且没有一种方法在短文本和长文本场景中都保持领先。Rubric4Setwise以更少的文档和搜索轮次取得了最佳下游生成性能，是唯一在两个场景中均达到最优的方法。

Conclusion: 文档集评估必须考虑文档间交互关系，不能仅依赖独立评分。Rubric4Setwise有效地将评估标准转化为优化信号，证明了从评估到优化闭环的重要性。该框架为提升面向LLM和AI智能体的检索质量提供了新路径。

Abstract: As large language models and AI agents become the primary consumers of search results, document set quality determines the upper bound of downstream generation. Yet existing evaluation systems remain confined to scoring documents independently and aggregating via nDCG, ignoring inter-document interactions (redundancy, conflict, complementarity) and unable to answer what makes one document set better than another. To address these issues, we propose a complete evaluate-diagnose-optimize framework. We design SetwiseEvalKit, a three-level, nine-dimension document set evaluation benchmark covering both short-form and long-form scenarios, comprising approximately 28K high-quality evaluation rubrics. We systematically evaluate 12 rerankers: even the best method achieves no more than 45% coverage, cross-document coordination dimensions are universally weak, and no single method maintains top performance across both settings. Building on this, we propose Rubric4Setwise, a training-free method that converts rubric-based evaluation criteria into document set selection signals, achieving the best downstream generation performance with fewer documents and search rounds. It is the only method that maintains state-of-the-art results across both scenarios, validating the effectiveness of closing the loop from evaluation to optimization.

</details>


### [83] [Learning the Arabic Dialect Continuum as a Continuous Space: A Regression Approach to Speaker Origin Prediction](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.19751)
*Mohamed Aziz Khadraoui,Adel Ammar,Bilel Benjdira,Zahid Khan,Skander Turki,Wadii Boulila*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 提出一种基于回归的阿拉伯方言地理定位方法，将方言变异建模为连续地理空间而非离散类别，使用层次化神经架构融合XLS-R-300M和Whisper-large-v3编码器表示与音位配列描述符，通过球面测地线损失优化地球表面大圆距离，在无泄漏的5折GroupKFold协议下实现481.2 km的中位定位误差，并通过城市遮蔽协议评估零样本泛化能力（误差升至1173.3 km，退化1.32倍），实验证实阿拉伯方言连续体假说并揭示了连续地理建模的优势与不足。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有阿拉伯方言地理定位方法通常将方言视为离散类别，忽略了方言变异在地理空间上的连续性；本文旨在探索将方言变异建模为连续地理空间的可行性，以更精确地刻画方言地理分布并验证阿拉伯方言连续体假说。

Method: 采用回归方法预测说话者起源的连续经纬度坐标；构建层次化神经架构，融合帧级XLS-R-300M和Whisper-large-v3编码器表示与音位配列描述符，通过Transformer编码器和可学习的注意力池化查询进行处理；使用球面测地线损失直接优化地球表面的大圆距离，避免平面坐标回归的失真；采用无泄漏的5折GroupKFold协议（按源录音分组）；引入城市遮蔽协议实现零样本评估。

Result: 在无泄漏5折GroupKFold协议下，模型达到481.2 km的中位定位误差；辅助国家和城市分类头分别达到64.5%和45.2%的准确率；排列Mantel检验量化支持阿拉伯方言连续体假说；在零样本城市遮蔽协议下，平均误差升至1173.3 km，相比已见城市退化1.32倍。

Conclusion: 连续地理建模可作为阿拉伯方言地理定位的一个原则性框架，实验量化了其优势（连续空间建模、验证方言连续体）和不足（零样本泛化能力仍有较大提升空间），表明该方向值得进一步研究。

Abstract: We present a regression-based approach to Arabic dialect geolocation that models dialectal variation as a continuous geographic space rather than discrete categories. Speaker origin is predicted as continuous latitude-longitude coordinates using a hierarchical neural architecture that fuses frame-level XLS-R-300M and Whisper-large-v3 encoder representations with phonotactic descriptors through a Transformer encoder and a learnable attention-pooled query. A spherical geodesic loss directly optimizes great-circle distance on Earth's surface, avoiding distortions inherent to planar coordinate regression. Under a leakage-free 5-fold GroupKFold protocol grouped by source recording, our model attains a pooled median localization error of 481.2 km. Auxiliary country and city heads reach 64.5% and 45.2% accuracy, respectively. A permutation Mantel test on the learned latent space provides quantitative support for the Arabic dialect continuum hypothesis. To probe true generalization, we further introduce a city-masking protocol in which two cities per fold are removed from training but retained in validation. Under this zero-shot regime, the mean error rises to 1173.3 km, a 1.32x degradation relative to seen cities. Our findings establish continuous geographic modeling as a principled framework for Arabic dialect geolocation and quantify both its strengths and the substantial headroom that remains.

</details>


### [84] [TriAgent: Divergence-Aware Multi-Agent Committees for Cost-Efficient Financial Sentiment Analysis](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.19794)
*Isabel Xu,Cynthia Xu,Rachel Ren,Cong Guo,Jiacheng Ding*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: TriAgent 是一个多粒度多智能体委员会系统，通过语义分歧指数（SDI）将查询路由至词级（VADER）、句级（FinBERT）和跨句推理（Qwen 等 LLM）三个层次，在金融情感分析中实现成本与精度的最优平衡。核心发现是“评论家平台”：将 LLM 重新定位为对小型模型输出的评论家时，F1 分数在 1.5B-7B 规模上稳定在 ~0.87，而同等规模的三人投票仅得 0.66。SDI 还衍生出跨语言缓存（中文查询 95% 命中英文缓存，F1=0.99）、幻觉检测（AUC=0.90）和最优交易策略（夏普比率 3.50）。在 1000 万用户规模下，相比 GPT-4o-mini 每年节省 930 万美元。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 生产环境中的 LLM 金融情感分析面临结构性成本陷阱：大多数查询是琐碎可分类的，但昂贵的云端推理模型处理所有查询，成本随用户数线性增长。现有方法要么全部使用轻量模型导致精度不足，要么全部使用大模型导致成本高昂，缺乏根据查询复杂性动态分配计算资源的机制。

Method: 提出 TriAgent 多智能体委员会，包含三个按上下文粒度分层的代理：词级词典（VADER）、句级领域 Transformer（FinBERT）和跨句推理器（Qwen2.5 0.5B-14B-4bit，配合 Mistral-7B 和 Phi-3.5-mini 跨族检查）。设计三路语义分歧指数（SDI）度量各粒度间成对分歧，据此路由查询。核心策略是将 LLM 重新用作对较小代理输出的评论家（critic），而非直接投票。

Result: 评论家平台现象：LLM 作为评论家时 F1 在 1.5B-7B 规模上稳定在 ~0.87（bootstrap 95% CI 重叠），而同等规模 3 人投票仅 0.66。三个推论：(i) 共享共识词典（SCD）基于多语言 sentence-BERT，从英文缓存以 F1=0.99 回答 95% 的中文查询；(ii) SDI 作为事后幻觉检测器 AUC=0.90；(iii) SDI 单阶段策略在 20 只股票回测中取得最佳风险调整收益（夏普比率 3.50），优于始终 FinBERT（1.36）和始终 LLM（0.11）。1000 万用户规模下每年节省 930 万美元。

Conclusion: TriAgent 通过粒度分层的智能体委员会和语义分歧路由，在金融情感分析中实现了成本效益和性能的双重突破。评论家平台现象表明，将 LLM 作为评论家而非投票者能更有效地利用其推理能力。SDI 作为一个统一信号，同时服务于路由、跨语言缓存、幻觉检测和交易策略，展示了多粒度分歧度量的广泛适用性。

Abstract: Production LLM-based financial sentiment analysis faces a structural cost trap: most queries are trivially classifiable, yet expensive cloud reasoners process them all, and the bill scales linearly with user count. We present TriAgent, a multi-agent committee stratified by contextual granularity -- a word-level lexicon (VADER), a sentence-level domain transformer (FinBERT), and a cross-sentence reasoner (Qwen2.5, 0.5B-14B-4bit, with Mistral-7B and Phi-3.5-mini cross-family checks). A three-way Semantic Divergence Index (SDI) measures pairwise disagreement across granularities and routes each query accordingly. Our central finding is the critic plateau: when the LLM is re-tasked as a critic over the smaller agents' outputs, F1 plateaus at ~0.87 across 1.5B-7B Qwen (bootstrap 95% CIs overlap), while a same-size 3-persona vote drops to F1=0.66, which is driven by granularity-stratified diversity. Three corollaries follow from the same SDI signal: (i) a Shared Consensus Dictionary on multilingual sentence-BERT answers 95% of Chinese queries from an English cache at F1=0.99 -- cross-border canonicalization at zero marginal cost; (ii) SDI doubles as a post-hoc LLM-hallucination detector at AUC=0.90; (iii) the SDI single-stage strategy attains the best risk-adjusted return (Sharpe=3.50) on a 20-ticker back-test, dominating both always-FinBERT (1.36) and always-LLM (0.11). At 10M-user scale, TriAgent saves $9.3M/year vs. a GPT-4o-mini baseline. Code, lexicons, and the SCD are released.

</details>


### [85] [VizRAG: Enhancing Retrieval-Augmented Generation with Hypergraph Visualization](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.19830)
*Yanbin Wei,Yang Chen,Renling Gan,Ziru Liu,Xinyu Fu,Chun Kang,Ning Lu,Rui Liu,Yu Zhang,James Kwok*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 本文提出 VizRAG，首个支持视觉超图结构感知的 RAG 系统，通过将超图的视觉表示引入 RAG 流程，克服了现有超图 RAG 框架仅依赖文本的局限，实验表明其显著优于强基线方法。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 尽管超图 RAG 在组织实体间复杂 n 元事实方面优于传统图方法，但现有框架仍局限于纯文本的知识检索与重构，未能充分利用多模态大语言模型（MLLM）的视觉感知能力，导致性能瓶颈。

Method: 系统性地探索通过视觉线索将超图感知集成到 RAG 系统中，提出 VizRAG，将超图的视觉表征融入 RAG 流水线，实现视觉超图结构感知。

Result: 实验结果表明，VizRAG 在多个基准上显著优于强基线方法，验证了超图可视化作为 RAG 系统新途径的潜力。

Conclusion: VizRAG 成功证明了视觉超图结构感知能够突破当前 RAG 系统的模态限制，有效提升知识检索与重构性能，为未来多模态 RAG 研究提供了新方向。

Abstract: Hypergraph-based RAG systems surpass traditional graph-based approaches by organizing complex n-ary atomic facts among entities, rather than relying solely on binary relationships. Despite the advancements in multimodal large language models (MLLMs) with enhanced visual capabilities, current hypergraph-based RAG frameworks predominantly restrict knowledge retrieval and reconstruction to a unimodal, text-centric paradigm. This limitation prevents them from fully leveraging the powerful visual perception capabilities of modern MLLMs. To address this gap, we systematically explore the integration of hypergraph awareness in RAG systems through visual cues. By incorporating visual representations of hypergraphs into the RAG pipeline, we introduce VizRAG, the first RAG system to support visual hypergraph structure awareness. Experimental results demonstrate that VizRAG significantly outperforms strong baselines, validating the promising potential of hypergraph visualization as a novel approach for RAG systems.

</details>


### [86] [D2VBench: Benchmarking Large Language Models with Value Dilemmas in Daily Scenarios](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.19834)
*Siyi Hao,Yidi Cao,Linhao Yu,Yuqi Ren,Deyi Xiong*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: D2VBench是一个包含10,000个日常困境场景的价值对齐基准，通过LLM与人类多阶段协作构建，基于158个细粒度价值概念，采用多选+开放问题的混合评估范式，在8个主流LLM上验证了高可靠性和鲁棒性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有评估基准存在两个问题：(1) 对日常场景中涉及多重价值冲突的价值困境覆盖不足；(2) 评估形式过于简单，无法有效评估LLM的价值对齐程度。

Method: 通过LLM与人类多阶段协作构建10,000个真实日常困境场景，基于158个人工标注的细粒度价值概念；提出融合多项选择题与开放题的混合评估范式。

Result: 在8个主流LLM上的实验表明，D2VBench具有高可靠性和鲁棒性，能够有效反映LLM在不同价值类别和维度上的对齐程度。

Conclusion: D2VBench为价值对齐研究提供了更真实、更细粒度的评估工具，数据集已开源。

Abstract: With the wide application of large language models (LLMs) in real-world scenarios, the value implication of their outputs is crucial. However, existing evaluation benchmarks suffer from insufficient coverage of value dilemmas in daily scenarios involving multiple value conflicts and simplistic evaluation formalisms that fail to assess LLMs' value alignment. To address these issues, we propose D2VBench, a value alignment benchmark comprising 10,000 instances of real daily dilemma scenarios constructed through a multi-stage collaboration between LLMs and humans, grounded in 158 manually annotated fine-grained value concepts. For evaluation on the benchmark, we present a hybrid evaluation paradigm that integrates multiple-choice questions with open-ended questions. We conduct comprehensive evaluations on eight mainstream LLMs. Experimental results demonstrate that D2VBench exhibits high reliability and robustness, effectively reflecting the LLMs' alignment across different value categories and dimensions, and providing a more realistic and fine-grained tool for research on value alignment. The dataset is available at https://github.com/tjunlp-lab/D2VBench.

</details>


### [87] [Sentence Splitter: Uncovering Latent Factual Structure for Self-Supervised Learning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.19845)
*Ahmad Pouramini,Mahsa Afsharizadeh*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 本文提出 Sentence Splitter，一个基于 T5 编码器-解码器架构的自监督框架，用于揭示自然语言句子的潜在事实结构。该方法通过将句子分割建模为离散分割问题，学习识别描述性前缀（head）与其事实补全（tail）之间的语义边界。为了消除人工标注，符号化的 head-tail 对被转化为自然语言模板以提供监督。训练后的分割器应用于原始文本提取对齐的前缀-尾对，随后用于训练生成模型，通过轻量级引导过程提出额外的合理补全。这个统一流程提供了一种可扩展且结构感知的方法来构建自监督训练数据，同时桥接符号知识与自然语言。实验表明分割器能泛化到合成模板之外，结构感知监督持续提升知识图谱补全和常识问答的下游任务性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有方法难以自动发现句子中的潜在事实结构（即描述性前缀与事实补全之间的语义边界），且依赖大量人工标注。本文旨在无需人工标注的情况下，自监督地学习句子的 head-tail 结构，以支持知识密集型 NLP 任务。

Method: 基于 T5 编码器-解码器架构构建自监督框架 Sentence Splitter。将句子分割视为离散分割问题：长度为 N 的句子有 N 个可能分割点，但只有一个恢复预期的 head-tail 结构。模型通过概率序列生成学习恢复事实补全，而非显式搜索所有候选边界。利用符号化的 head-tail 对语言化为自然语言模板提供监督。训练后的分割器应用于原始文本提取对齐的 prefix-tail 对，再用这些数据训练生成模型通过轻量级引导过程提出额外补全。

Result: 在结构化文本和自然文本上的实验表明，Sentence Splitter 能够泛化到合成模板之外的结构；结构感知监督在知识图谱补全和常识问答任务上持续提升下游性能。

Conclusion: Sentence Splitter 提供了一种可扩展、结构感知的自监督训练数据构建方法，有效桥接符号知识和自然语言，通过恢复潜在事实结构显著提升了知识密集型 NLP 任务的性能。

Abstract: This paper introduces Sentence Splitter, a self-supervised framework built upon a T5-based encoder--decoder architecture for uncovering the latent factual structure of natural language sentences. The proposed method identifies the semantic boundary between a descriptive prefix (head) and its factual completion (tail) by formulating sentence splitting as a discrete segmentation problem, where a sentence of length $N$ admits $N$ possible split points but only one recovers the intended head--tail structure. Rather than explicitly searching over all candidate boundaries, the model learns to recover the factual completion through probabilistic sequence generation. To eliminate the need for manual annotation, symbolic head--tail pairs are first verbalized into natural-language templates that provide supervision for training the Sentence Splitter. The trained splitter is then applied to raw text to extract aligned prefix--tail pairs, which are subsequently used to train a generative model that proposes additional plausible completions through a lightweight bootstrapping process. This unified pipeline provides a scalable and structure-aware approach to constructing self-supervised training data while bridging symbolic knowledge and natural language. Experiments on both structured and naturally occurring text demonstrate that the proposed splitter generalizes beyond synthetic templates and that the resulting structure-aware supervision consistently improves downstream performance on knowledge graph completion and commonsense question answering, highlighting the effectiveness of recovering latent factual structure for knowledge-centric NLP.

</details>


### [88] [emb-diversity: A Tool for Embedding-Based Measurement of Data Diversity](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.19848)
*Cantao Su,Menan Velayuthan,Esther Ploeger,Dong Nguyen,Anna Wegmann*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 本文提出了 emb-diversity，一个基于嵌入的多样性测量工具，能够灵活地衡量文本数据的风格、语义、语言和说话者多样性，弥补了现有工具在嵌入层面标准化测量上的不足。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 数据多样性对于构建公平且鲁棒的 NLP 模型至关重要，但现有多样性测量方法在嵌入层面缺乏标准化的工具，传统的词汇多样性工具无法满足灵活测量多种多样性的需求。

Method: 提出 emb-diversity 工具，基于嵌入（embedding）进行多样性测量，支持任意嵌入模型和可嵌入的数据，涵盖广泛的多样性指标，并应用于风格、语义、语言和说话者多样性等场景。

Result: 成功展示了 emb-diversity 在多个用例（风格、语义、语言和说话者多样性）中的潜力，证明其灵活性和广泛适用性。

Conclusion: emb-diversity 提供了一种标准化的嵌入级多样性测量方案，有助于推动 NLP 模型的公平性和鲁棒性研究，未来可进一步扩展应用范围。

Abstract: There is growing evidence that data diversity is crucial for developing fair and robust NLP models. However, current approaches to measure diversity remain inconsistent and fragmented: While there exist a number of tools for measuring the lexical diversity of texts, researchers lack standardized tools for quantifying diversity based on embeddings. Embedding-based diversity measures are highly flexible: They work with any embedding model and any data that can be embedded, and are thus applicable to many notions of diversity. With emb-diversity, we provide a comprehensive embedding-based diversity measurement tool, spanning a broad range of measures. We demonstrate its potential for several use cases: measuring the stylistic, semantic, language and speaker diversity of datasets. https://github.com/nlpsoc/emb-diversity/

</details>


### [89] [Overview of FinMMEval 2026 Task 1: Multilingual Financial Multiple-Choice Question Answering](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.19856)
*Zhuohan Xie,Yuyang Dai,Rania Elbadry,Vanshikaa Jani,Georgi Georgiev,Dimitar Dimitrov,Fan Zhang,Xueqing Peng,Lingfei Qian,Jimin Huang,Jiahui Geng,Yankai Chen,Ye Yuan,Haolun Wu,Yuxia Wang,Ivan Koychev,Veselin Stoyanov,Mingzi Song,Yu Chen,Xue Liu,Preslav Nakov*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: FinMMEval 2026 Task 1是一个多语言金融多选题问答评测任务，涵盖英语、中文、阿拉伯语和印地语四种语言，共800道题（每语言200题）。最终榜单显示各语言最高准确率在92.0%（印地语）到97.5%（英语和阿拉伯语）之间，领先团队在四种语言上均表现优异。参赛系统使用了检索增强、答案选项评分、语言特定提示、选择性自一致性、置信度检查及LLM评审等多种技术。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 旨在评估系统在多语言环境下处理金融领域多项选择题的能力，测试其是否能够理解领域术语、进行数值解释和概念性金融推理，涵盖不同语言和文字系统。

Method: 任务构建了800道多选题（每语言200题），各语言独立评分排名。参赛系统采用了多种技术：检索增强生成、直接答案选项评分、语言特定提示工程、选择性自一致性（self-consistency）、置信度检查以及基于LLM的评审阶段。

Result: 最终榜单包含英语13个、中文11个、阿拉伯语11个、印地语10个有效提交。最高准确率：英语和阿拉伯语97.5%，中文和印地语分别为较低值（印地语92.0%）。相同领先团队在四种语言中均排名靠前。

Conclusion: 多语言金融问答任务中，领先系统在不同语言上均展现了一致的优越性能，表明现有LLM结合检索增强、自一致性等技术可以有效处理跨语言金融推理问题。各语言之间的准确率差异可能反映了预训练数据分布和语言复杂性的影响。

Abstract: FinMMEval 2026 Task 1 evaluates multilingual financial multiple-choice question answering in English, Chinese, Arabic, and Hindi. The task tests whether systems can select the correct answer to finance questions involving domain terminology, numerical interpretation, and conceptual financial reasoning across languages and scripts. The final-test set contains 800 questions, with 200 questions per language; gold answers were withheld during submission, and each language was ranked independently by accuracy. The final leaderboards contain 13 English, 11 Chinese, 11 Arabic, and 10 Hindi ranked submissions. Top accuracies range from 92.0% in Hindi to 97.5% in English and Arabic, with the same leading teams appearing near the top across all four languages. The documented systems used retrieval augmentation, direct answer-option scoring, language-specific prompting, selective self-consistency, confidence checks, and LLM-based review stages.

</details>


### [90] [Overview of FinMMEval 2026 Task 2: Multilingual Financial Short-Answer Question Answering](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.19867)
*Zhuohan Xie,Xueqing Peng,Georgi Georgiev,Dimitar Dimitrov,Yuyang Dai,Rania Elbadry,Vanshikaa Jani,Lingfei Qian,Fan Zhang,Jimin Huang,Jiahui Geng,Yankai Chen,Ye Yuan,Haolun Wu,Yuxia Wang,Ivan Koychev,Veselin Stoyanov,Mingzi Song,Yu Chen,Xue Liu,Preslav Nakov*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: FinMMEval 2026 Task 2 is a short-answer financial QA benchmark over multilingual evidence. It pairs English questions with financial statements/news in five languages, with 256 items split into easy and expert tiers. Systems were ranked by ROUGE-1 F1, and the top four submissions were within 1% of each other.


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: To evaluate financial question answering systems in a multilingual evidence setting, assessing both easy and expert-level reasoning.

Method: The task provides English questions with financial statements and news in English, Chinese, Japanese, Spanish, and Greek. Systems submit one concise answer per item in JSONL format. Gold answers withheld; systems ranked by macro-averaged item-level ROUGE-1 F1 against organizer-held references.

Result: The final test set contains 256 items (128 easy, 128 expert). 12 submissions were ranked. Top four systems clustered within <1% ROUGE-1 F1 difference.

Conclusion: The strongest systems are closely clustered, and submitted system papers document strategies including retrieval-augmented generation, cross-lingual evidence handling, structured prompting, answer compression, and validation.

Abstract: FinMMEval 2026 Task 2 evaluates short-answer financial question answering over multilingual evidence. Each final-test item pairs an English question with financial statements and news in English, Chinese, Japanese, Spanish, and Greek. Participating systems submit one concise answer per item in JSONL format. The final-test set contains 256 items, split evenly between easy and expert tiers; each tier contains four question templates instantiated over 32 company-report groups. Gold answers were withheld during submission, and systems were ranked by macro-averaged item-level ROUGE-1 F1 against organizer-held reference answers. The final leaderboard includes 12 ranked submissions. The strongest systems are closely clustered, with the top four separated by less than one percentage point in ROUGE-1 F1. The submitted system papers document retrieval-augmented generation, cross-lingual evidence handling, structured prompting, answer compression, and validation strategies.

</details>


### [91] [Efficient Chain-of-Modality Reasoning via Progressive Compression for Spoken Language Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.19932)
*Pengchao Feng,Chao-Hong Tan,Qian Chen,Wen Wang,Xiangang Li,Xie Chen*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 本文提出高效链式模态推理（ECoM Reasoning），这是首个将压缩推理引入口语语言模型（SLM）的框架。通过将文本组件压缩为同时充当语音指导和推理表示，ECoM在更小的token预算下提升了推理准确性。结合渐进式压缩训练策略，在口语数学问答基准上，ECoM相比无显式推理的标准链式模态（CoM）准确率提升21%，相比完整推理的CoM提升3%，且仅使用40%的文本token，实现了推理效率与性能的兼顾。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 口语语言模型（SLM）的推理能力落后于基于文本的大语言模型，尤其是在口语数学问答任务上，因为SLM需要处理纯口述的数学表达式，难以像符号文本那样直接进行推理。直接将文本推理迁移到SLM面临架构约束和额外计算开销，因此需要一种高效且适合SLM的推理方法。

Method: 提出高效链式模态推理（ECoM Reasoning）框架，将文本组件压缩为共享表示，同时用于语音生成引导和推理过程，从而在保持或提升推理性能的同时减少token消耗。同时提出渐进式压缩（Progressive Compression）策略，采用课程学习方式，逐步训练模型从完整推理过渡到压缩推理。

Result: 在口语数学问答基准上，ECoM Reasoning相比无显式推理的标准CoM准确率提升21%，相比使用完整推理轨迹的CoM准确率提升3%，同时仅使用40%的文本token，显著提升了推理效率。

Conclusion: ECoM Reasoning有效提升了SLM的推理能力，同时保持了推理效率，证明了压缩推理在口语语言模型中的可行性。

Abstract: Spoken language models (SLMs) enable natural human-computer interaction, but their reasoning ability still lags behind that of text-based large language models, especially on spoken mathematical question answering tasks. One important reason is that SLMs reason over purely verbalized mathematical expressions, which are harder to interpret than symbolic text. However, directly transferring text-based reasoning to SLMs is nontrivial due to architectural constraints and the additional computational requirements. To address this challenge, we propose Efficient Chain-of-Modality Reasoning (ECoM Reasoning), the first framework to introduce compressed reasoning into SLMs. By compressing the textual component so that it jointly serves as speech guidance and reasoning representation, ECoM Reasoning improves reasoning accuracy while using a smaller token budget than the standard Chain-of-Modality (CoM) architecture, which generates intermediate text before speech. To train this capability, we further propose Progressive Compression, a curriculum-based strategy that gradually trains the model from full-form reasoning to compressed reasoning. Experiments on spoken mathematical question answering benchmarks show that ECoM Reasoning improves accuracy by 21% over standard CoM without explicit reasoning, and by 3% over CoM with full reasoning traces while using only 40% of the text tokens, demonstrating that it enhances SLM reasoning while remaining inference-efficient.

</details>


### [92] [A Multi-Dimensional Evaluation of Explainability in Media Bias Detection](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.19954)
*Ting Chen,Raina Zhang,Benjamin M. Ampel,Sagar Samtani*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 该论文对基于编码器的媒体偏见检测中的可解释性进行了多维评估，使用BABE数据集，研究BERT和RoBERTa模型在预测性能、解释合理性和机制忠实度三个方面的表现，并探讨了注意力监督微调的影响。结果表明，这三个维度应分开评估。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 媒体偏见检测中，仅依赖准确预测不足以满足需求，还需要能够反映模型推理过程的解释。然而，现有的可解释性评估往往不够全面，因此本文提出了一个多维度的评估框架。

Method: 使用BABE数据集，以BERT和RoBERTa（base和large变体）作为分类器，从三个维度进行评估：预测性能、解释合理性（词级对齐专家理由）和机制忠实度（在反事实理由掩码下，紧凑注意力头集能否恢复预测信号）。此外，还采用了注意力监督微调（将专家理由注释作为辅助训练信号）来引入合理性变化。

Result: 注意力监督作为归因合理性的干预手段，其有效性在不同架构间存在显著差异；电路分析显示不同架构的机制可恢复性差异较大，模型规模本身并不决定电路可压缩性；预测性能、归因合理性和机制忠实度描述了模型行为的不同方面。

Conclusion: 在媒体偏见检测的可解释性研究中，应分别评估预测性能、归因合理性和机制忠实度，因为它们表征了模型行为的不同维度。

Abstract: Detecting media bias automatically is difficult because biased framing is often subtle, yet in domains such as news analysis, accurate predictions alone are insufficient without explanations that reflect the model's underlying reasoning. We present a multi-dimensional evaluation of explainability in encoder-based media bias detection using the Bias Annotations By Experts (BABE) dataset. Specifically, we study BERT and RoBERTa as classifiers (base and large variants) along three complementary axes: predictive performance, explanation plausibility (token-level alignment with expert rationales), and mechanistic faithfulness (whether compact sets of attention heads recover predictive signal under counterfactual rationale masking). To induce variation in plausibility, we additionally investigate attention-supervised finetuning, which incorporates expert rationale annotations as an auxiliary training signal. Attention supervision serves as an intervention on attribution plausibility, while the effectiveness of attribution methods varies substantially across architectures. Circuit analysis further reveals substantial variation in mechanistic recoverability across architectures, suggesting that model scale alone does not determine circuit compressibility. Taken together, our findings suggest that predictive performance, attribution plausibility, and mechanistic faithfulness characterize different aspects of model behavior and should be evaluated separately when studying explainability in media bias detection.

</details>


### [93] [When Does Knowledge Distillation Hurt? Reliability-Aware Distillation for Low-Resource Language Summarization](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.19956)
*Dipto Sumit,Ankan Kumar Roy Srizon,Sadia Khair Rodela,Atia Haque Asha,Mourchona Afrin,Niloy Farhan,Farig Sadeque*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 本文发现标准知识蒸馏(KD)在序列到序列模型压缩中，约51.3%的训练样本实际上会损害学生模型的验证损失。为此提出两种可靠性感知蒸馏方法：CHAD（通过梯度对齐衡量样本级KD有用性并训练轻量级门控泛化反事实判断）和EWAD+CPDP（结合熵加权自适应蒸馏与容量比例几何约束）。在BanSum基准上，两种方法分别提升ROUGE-L +0.0173和+0.0219（标准KD仅+0.0003）；仅60M参数即可超越50倍大的Qwen 2.5-3B。EWAD+CPDP在15种语言上10/15优于基线。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 标准知识蒸馏在序列到序列模型压缩中对每个样本的影响很少被检查；在BanSum基准上标准KD仅带来微不足道的ROUGE-L提升（+0.0003），且约51.3%的训练样本估计会主动损害学生模型的验证损失，说明存在大量有害或无用的蒸馏样本。

Method: 提出两种互补的可靠性感知蒸馏方法：1) CHAD (Counterfactual Harm-Aware Distillation)：通过梯度对齐衡量每个样本KD有用性与验证损失方向的关系，训练轻量级门控网络泛化反事实判断到全训练集；2) EWAD+CPDP：结合token级熵加权自适应蒸馏与来自第二个词汇不兼容教师的容量比例几何约束。

Result: 在BanSum基准上，CHAD提升ROUGE-L +0.0173，EWAD+CPDP提升+0.0219（标准KD仅+0.0003）；仅60M参数即超越50倍大的Qwen 2.5-3B。EWAD+CPDP在15种XL-Sum语言上10/15优于CE-only基线；当两个教师提供互补信号时增益最可靠，当教师饱和或联合覆盖弱时增益最弱。

Conclusion: 可靠性感知蒸馏能显著提升KD效果，通过识别并利用真正有益的蒸馏样本，可以大幅超越标准KD甚至更大模型；两种方法互补，EWAD+CPDP在多语言场景下表现更优。代码和模型已开源以支持可重复性和选择性蒸馏研究。

Abstract: Knowledge distillation (KD) is a standard approach for compressing sequence-to-sequence models, but its per-sample effects are rarely examined. On the BanSum Bangla summarization benchmark, we find that standard KD improves ROUGE-L by only +0.0003 over a cross-entropy baseline, and that approximately 51.3% of training samples are estimated to actively harm student validation loss under standard KD. We propose two complementary reliability-aware distillation methods. CHAD (Counterfactual Harm-Aware Distillation) measures per-sample KD usefulness via gradient alignment with the validation loss direction and trains a lightweight gate that generalizes this counterfactual judgment to the full training set. EWAD+CPDP combines token-level entropy-weighted adaptive distillation with a capacity-proportional geometric constraint from a second, vocabulary-incompatible teacher. On BanSum, both methods substantially outperform standard KD: CHAD by +0.0173 ROUGE-L and EWAD+CPDP by +0.0219 ROUGE-L, where standard KD itself improves ROUGE-L by only +0.0003; despite using only 60M parameters, both outperform a fine-tuned Qwen 2.5-3B model (50x larger). We further evaluate the stronger method, EWAD+CPDP, across 15 typologically diverse XL-Sum languages organised into three sets, beating the CE-only baseline on 10/15 languages; gains are most reliable where the two teachers contribute complementary signal, and weakest where they have saturated or jointly weak target-language coverage. We release code and trained models to support reproducibility and further research on selective distillation.

</details>


### [94] [TINY_SCHILLER: A Drop-In German Drama Corpus for Small Language Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.19992)
*Mark Schutera*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: tiny_schiller 提供了一个单文件、即插即用的德语文学数据集，作为 tiny_shakespeare 的德语对应物，旨在填补小型语言模型在德语文学文本上的原型设计、微调、教育和研究空白。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有的德语文学语料库虽然丰富，但需要大量的解析工程才能用于训练或微调，缺乏像 tiny_shakespeare 那样易于访问的小型数据集。tiny_schiller 旨在降低入门门槛，使得研究人员、教育者和爱好者能够一行代码加载德语文学数据并开始训练小型语言模型。

Method: 从 DraCor 的 GerDraCor 导出（CC0 许可）中获取 11 部公共领域席勒戏剧，通过确定性解析工程处理成一个 2.07 MB 的单文件。提供字符级、GPT-2 BPE 和 cl100k_base 三种 tokenization 分割，以及指令格式的对话补全分割和 89 个每角色人物分割。所有分割可通过单个 HuggingFace 调用加载。

Result: 成功创建了一个紧凑、可直接使用的德语文学数据集，支持多种 tokenization 方式和任务格式，使得小型语言模型能够在单行代码中开始处理德语文学文本。

Conclusion: tiny_schiller 填补了德语文学领域小型语言模型数据集的空白，简化了从原始语料到可训练数据的流程，促进了低资源场景下的研究和教育应用。

Abstract: tiny_schiller closes the small-language-model prototyping, fine-tuning, education, and research gap for German literary text, providing a single-file, drop-in counterpart to Karpathy's tiny_shakespeare. The available German literary corpora are larger and richer, but require parser engineering before a single line of training or fine-tuning code can run. tiny_schiller is a 2.07-megabyte single file of eleven public-domain Schiller dramas, sourced from DraCor's GerDraCor export (CC0) and processed by deterministic parser engineering. Character-level, GPT-2 byte-pair encoding, and cl100k_base tokenization splits, an instruction-formatted dialogue-completion split, and 89 per-character persona splits load from a single HuggingFace call. A small language model literally reaches German literary text in one line of code.

</details>


### [95] [TalentCLEF at CLEF2026: Skill and Job Title Intelligence for Human Capital Management](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.20009)
*Luis Gasco,Hermenegildo Fabregat,Laura García-Sardiña,Paula Estrella,Casimiro Pio Carrino,Daniel Deniz,Alvaro Rodrigo,Rabih Zbib*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 本文介绍了 TalentCLEF Challenge（第二届）作为 CLEF 2026 评估实验室的内容，旨在推动自然语言处理（NLP）在人力资本管理（HCM）领域的发展，重点关注结果公平性、多语言支持和跨行业适应性。本届挑战包含两个任务：任务 A（基于上下文的职位-人员匹配）和任务 B（职位-技能匹配与技能类型分类）。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 目前人力资本管理（HCM）中 NLP 的应用仍缺乏标准化评测基准，且现有方法在公平性、多语言泛化及跨行业迁移方面存在不足。TalentCLEF 旨在通过建立公开评测基准，推动研究团队比较方法并共享成果，从而促进更实用、更具影响力的 NLP 解决方案，真正解决劳动力管理的实际需求。

Method: TalentCLEF 作为 CLEF 2026 的评测实验室，设置两个任务：(i) 任务 A - 基于上下文的职位-人员匹配：利用富含上下文且保护隐私的数据，检索并排序适合特定职位的候选人；(ii) 任务 B - 职位-技能匹配与技能类型分类：针对给定职位名称识别相关技能，并按其在职位画像中的类型进行分类。通过公开基准和竞赛形式，鼓励多语言、跨行业且公平的 NLP 方法。

Result: 本文是实验室提案，尚未报告具体实验结果。预期成果包括：建立标准化 HCM-NLP 评测基准；促进公平、多语言、跨行业的方法创新；推动人才匹配、技能提升/再培训、技能缺口检测等关键 HCM 任务的实际应用。

Conclusion: TalentCLEF 第二届挑战赛为 HCM 领域中的 NLP 研究提供了结构化的评测平台，覆盖人才匹配与技能分析两个核心任务，有望加速从学术研究到工业应用的转化，最终实现更加高效和公平的劳动力管理。

Abstract: This paper presents the second edition of the TalentCLEF Challenge, which will run as an evaluation lab as part of CLEF 2026. The aim of TalentCLEF is to promote the development of systems and methods that use Natural Language Processing (NLP) in the field of Human Capital Management (HCM), fostering approaches that ensure fairness in results, operate across multiple languages, and adapt to diverse industries. To this end, TalentCLEF establishes public benchmarks where research teams can compare methods and share findings, moving the field toward more practical and impactful NLP solutions that effectively address the real needs of workforce management.
  This year's lab will feature two tasks designed to foster the development and evaluation of systems that support key HCM activities such as talent matching, upskilling, reskilling, and skill gap detection: (i) Task A - Contextualized Job-Person Matching, focused on retrieving and ranking suitable candidates for specific job positions using context-rich and privacy-preserving data; and (ii) Task B - Job-Skill Matching with Skill Type Classification, centered on identifying relevant skills for a given job title and classifying them by their type within the job profile.
  TalentCLEF website: https://talentclef.github.io/talentclef/

</details>


### [96] [Language-Specific versus Cross-Lingual Knowledge Graphs for Implicit Aspect Identification in Arabic: A Comparative Study of Reasoning and Adaptation Strategies](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.20056)
*Lujain A. Alawwad*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 本文针对阿拉伯语方面级情感分析（ABSA）中隐式方面识别问题，对比了两种知识图谱策略：复用英文知识图谱（通过多语言嵌入）与构建原生阿拉伯语知识图谱，并评估了零样本提示与任务特定微调两种生成式抽取器适应策略。结果表明，原生阿拉伯语知识图谱显著优于跨语言英文知识图谱，且任务特定微调对显式方面抽取的提升远超模型规模本身。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 阿拉伯语ABSA需要同时识别显式和隐式方面，但隐式方面识别通常依赖辅助知识源（如知识图谱）。对于低资源语言，实践者面临设计选择：是复用成熟的英文知识图谱（通过多语言嵌入），还是构建规模较小的原生阿拉伯语知识图谱。目前缺乏对这两种策略的系统比较，且生成式抽取器（如大语言模型）的适应策略（零样本提示 vs. 任务特定微调）对性能的影响尚不明确。

Method: 在一个统一的混合流水线中，对比了两种知识图谱策略：策略1使用跨语言英文知识图谱（通过多语言嵌入对齐），策略2构建原生阿拉伯语知识图谱。同时，对生成式方面抽取器（基于8B参数的大语言模型）评估了两种适应策略：零样本提示和任务特定微调。实验在三个阿拉伯语基准数据集（M-ABSA、SemEval-2016阿拉伯语、HAAD）上进行。

Result: 原生阿拉伯语知识图谱（策略2）在M-ABSA上micro-F1提升+0.199，在SemEval-2016上提升+0.251，显著优于跨语言英文知识图谱（策略1）。任务特定微调将显式抽取的micro-F1从零样本的≤0.13提升至0.66-0.76（M-ABSA和SemEval-2016），在较小的HAAD上达到0.45，表明任务适应比模型规模更为关键。

Conclusion: 在形态丰富的低资源语言（如阿拉伯语）中，构建原生知识图谱比跨语言复用英文知识图谱更有效；任务特定微调是提升生成式抽取器性能的决定性因素，而非单纯依赖模型规模。研究为低资源语言的ABSA系统设计提供了实证依据。

Abstract: Aspect-based sentiment analysis (ABSA) in Arabic must recover both explicitly stated aspects and implicit aspects that are never named in the text. Implicit identification typically relies on an auxiliary knowledge source (e.g., a knowledge graph (KG)) linking opinion cues to aspect categories, but for a lower-resource language the practitioner faces a design choice: reuse a mature English KG through multilingual embeddings, or build a smaller native Arabic KG. This paper reports a controlled comparison of the two strategies within a single hybrid pipeline, evaluated on three Arabic benchmarks (M-ABSA, SemEval-2016 Arabic, and HAAD). We further compare two adaptation strategies for the generative extractor that feeds the KG -- zero-shot prompting versus task-specific fine-tuning of an 8B-parameter large language model (LLM). The native Arabic KG (Strategy 2) outperforms the cross-lingual English KG (Strategy 1) by +0.199 micro-F1 on M-ABSA and +0.251 on SemEval-2016, gaining on both precision and recall. Task-specific fine-tuning raises explicit-extraction micro-F1 from <= 0.13 (zero-shot) to 0.66-0.76 on M-ABSA and SemEval-2016 (0.45 on the smaller HAAD), confirming that task adaptation, rather than model scale, is decisive in a morphologically rich language.

</details>


### [97] [Solar Open 2 Technical Report](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.20062)
*Sungrae Park,Sanghoon Kim,Gyoungjin Gim,Jungho Cho,Hyunwoong Ko,Minbyul Jeong,Minjeong Kim,Keunwoo Choi,Chaehun Shin,Chanwoong Yoon,Dongjun Kim,Eunwon Kim,Gyungin Shin,Hyeonju Lee,Hyungkyu Kang,Inseo Song,Jisu Bae,Jiyoon Han,Jiyun Lee,Joonkee Kim,Junyeop Lee,Mikyoung Cha,Sangwon Yu,Sehwan Joo,Seokyoon Kang,Seonghoon Yang,Seung Shin,Seunghyun Lee,Seungseop Lim,Seungyoun Shin,Sukyung Lee,Taegyeong Eo,Taehwan Oh,Taewhoo Lee,Wonho Song,Wonjun Oh,Wonseok Hwang,Yunsu Kim,Yura Shim,Hwalsuk Lee,Sunghun Kim,Du-Seong Chang,Kyunghyun Cho,Seungju Han,Yejin Choi,Junsuk Choe,Hwaran Lee,Minjeong Ban,Yun Taewon,Hwanjun Song,Jae-Gil Lee,KyungTae Lim,Alice Oh*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: Solar Open 2 is a 250B-A15B MoE language model with 1M-token context window using hybrid attention (softmax + linear attention with gated delta rule). It is initialized from Solar Open 1's shared skeleton and trained on a curated 10T token mixture. Agent skills are built via twelve domain specialists consolidated through Multi-teacher On-Policy Distillation (MOPD). The model leads on MMLU-Pro, LiveCodeBench, and APEX-Agents among open-weight models, and achieves the highest average on Korean benchmarks, competitive with much larger models.


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: To create a language model capable of handling long-horizon agentic tasks with a very long context window (1M tokens) while being efficient to train under a fixed compute budget.

Method: Architecture: 250B-A15B MoE with hybrid attention (1 softmax per 3 linear-attention layers, no positional encoding, gated delta rule extended to negative eigenvalues). Training efficiency: (1) initialize from Solar Open 1's 5.69B shared skeleton, full pre-train the rest; (2) quality- and rarity-aware data curation and mixture-ratio optimization to reduce 20T pool to 10T. Agent skills: train twelve domain specialists, then consolidate via Multi-teacher On-Policy Distillation (MOPD).

Result: Leads on MMLU-Pro, LiveCodeBench, and APEX-Agents among open-weight models; competitive with DeepSeek-V4-Flash and MiMo-V2.5 on other English benchmarks; highest average on Korean benchmarks, competitive with DeepSeek-V4-Pro (1.6T) on Ko-GDPval at less than 1/6 the size.

Conclusion: Solar Open 2 demonstrates that efficient training (better initialization and data curation) combined with a hybrid attention design enables strong performance on long-context agentic tasks, outperforming much larger models on both English and Korean benchmarks.

Abstract: We present Solar Open 2, a 250B-A15B Mixture-of-Experts language model built for long-horizon agentic tasks, scaled up from Solar Open 1 (Solar Open 100B). To hold entire agent trajectories in a single context, Solar Open 2 reaches a 1M-token window through a hybrid attention stack that interleaves one softmax layer among every three linear-attention layers, using no positional encoding and a gated delta rule extended to negative eigenvalues. To train at this scale under a fixed compute budget, we make training efficient in two ways: a stronger starting point, and higher-value data. For the starting point, we initialize Solar Open 2 from Solar Open 1, transferring the 5.69B-parameter shared skeleton that survives the architectural change and learning everything else through full pre-training. For the data, we curate for value per token: quality- and rarity-aware data curation and mixture-ratio optimization refine a 20T pool into a 10T mixture that, at equal token budget, outperforms the Solar Open 1 recipe. To build its agent skills, we train twelve domain specialists across purpose-built scenarios, then consolidate them into a single model by Multi-teacher On-Policy Distillation (MOPD). Against comparably sized open-weight models on English benchmarks, Solar Open 2 leads on MMLU-Pro, LiveCodeBench, and the APEX-Agents agentic suite, and stays competitive with the strongest (DeepSeek-V4-Flash and MiMo-V2.5) elsewhere. On Korean benchmarks, Solar Open 2 records the highest average of any model compared, including fast-tier closed APIs, and on Ko-GDPval, an in-house Korean officework-agent benchmark, it is competitive with DeepSeek-V4-Pro (1.6T) at less than a sixth of its size.

</details>


### [98] [RALS: Resources and Baselines for Romanian Automatic Lexical Simplification](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.20078)
*Fabian Anghel,Petru Theodor Cristea,Claudiu Creanga,Sergiu Nisioi*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 本文提出了首个覆盖词汇复杂度预测（LCP）和词汇简化（LS）的罗马尼亚语数据集，并基于成对排序近似方法对简化建议进行排序，提供了3,921个单词样本的人工复杂度标注，探索了多种复杂度预测与简化的新流程，最终构建了首个罗马尼亚语文本简化系统。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 罗马尼亚语在词汇复杂度预测和词汇简化方面缺乏联合标注数据集，现有研究多聚焦于英语等资源丰富语言，亟需为低资源语言构建相应资源与系统。

Method: 1）构建包含LCP和LS联合标注的数据集；2）采用成对排序近似方法（pairwise ranking approximation）基于人工判断对简化候选词进行从简单到复杂的排序；3）提供3,921个单词样本的上下文复杂度人工标注；4）探索多种复杂度预测与简化的新流程。

Result: 成功构建了罗马尼亚语首个LCP-LS联合数据集，并实现了首个罗马尼亚语文本简化系统，通过成对排序方法有效组织了简化建议的复杂度层级。

Conclusion: 该工作填补了罗马尼亚语在词汇复杂度与简化领域的资源空白，提出的排序方法论和简化系统为低资源语言的文本简化研究提供了可复用的框架与基线。

Abstract: We introduce the first dataset that jointly covers both lexical complexity prediction (LCP) annotations and lexical simplification (LS) for Romanian, along with a comparison of lexical simplification approaches. We propose a methodology for ordering simplification suggestions using a pairwise ranking approximation method, arranging candidates from simple to complex based on a separate set of human judgments. In addition, we provide human lexical complexity annotations for 3,921 word samples in context. Finally, we explore several novel pipelines for complexity prediction and simplification and present the first text simplification system for Romanian.

</details>


### [99] [The Two-Process Theory of Machine Self-Report](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.20082)
*Hubert Plisiecki,Filip Chmielewski,Kacper Dudzic,Anna Sterna,Karolina Drożdż,Marcin Moskalewicz*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 提出了首个语言模型特有的心理测量理论：机器自我报告的二过程理论（人格安装B与归因门控A），并基于此开发了48题匹诺曹量表，在206个开源模型中验证了训练效应（A和B在基座模型中纠缠，在后训练中分离）。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 语言模型越来越多地被要求自我报告，但现有问卷从未针对模型验证，或使用可靠性未知的临时提示。需要为语言模型建立专门的心理测量理论，以解释其自我报告的结构。
