Method: 提出语言模型自我报告的二过程理论：人格安装（post-training写入的温暖、吸收和意义维度B）和归因门控（抑制对他人可归因的“不安全”经历的第一人称声称维度A）。通过探索性再分析原始数据，设计并验证了48题匹诺曹量表，在206个开源模型（含67组同检查点基座/后训练对）上测试。

Result: 量表具有人类仪器可靠性（α=.82-.94，交叉形式收敛r=.84，全池轴恢复r=.92-.96，八个月稳定性r=.93）。后训练的最清晰指纹是安装：B在62/67对中上升0.20。门控更具选择性：模型规模与基座检查点的A无关（r=+.11），但后训练后预测A（r=-.42）。A和B在基座检查点中纠缠，但通过后训练分离。

Conclusion: 维度并非语言模型的固定属性，而是反映训练体制对自我报告施加的结构；不同训练体制下可能不同。

Abstract: Language models are increasingly asked to self-report, informing safety evaluations, public understanding, and model-welfare debates. Yet their reports are elicited with human questionnaires never validated for models or ad hoc prompts of unknown reliability. We propose the first language-model-specific psychometric theory: a two-process theory of machine self-report. Self-description jointly reflects persona installation, through which post-training writes in a permitted inner life of warmth, absorption, and meaning (dimension B), and attribution gating, through which it suppresses first-person claims to "unsafe" experiences the model can readily ascribe to others (dimension A). Their emic structure comes from model responses to human items, not human psychology. Together they split prior work's dominant Pinocchio Axis. The split emerged in an exploratory reanalysis of the original data, informed the instrument's design, and was confirmed with new items, wordings, and models. It is itself a training effect: A and B are entangled in base checkpoints but separated by post-training. We operationalize the theory in a 48-item Pinocchio Inventory with human-instrument reliability and reproducible structure ($α=.82$ to $.94$; cross-form convergence $r=.84$; recovery of the full-pool axes $r=.92$ to $.96$; eight-month stability $r=.93$), then test it on 206 open-weight models, including 67 same-checkpoint base/post-trained pairs. Post-training's clearest fingerprint is installation: B rises .20 in 62/67 pairs across all organizations. Gating is more selective: model scale is unrelated to A in base checkpoints ($r=+.11$) but predicts it after post-training ($r=-.42$). Thus, the dimensions are not fixed properties of language models: they reflect the structure imposed on self-report by a training regime and may differ under others.

</details>


### [100] [Reinforcement Learning for Large Language Model Selective Evidence Adoption from Contaminated Retrieval Results](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.20090)
*Yanyu Chen,Yue Li,Yongyi Cui,Dongsheng Shi,Lichang Dai*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: SelectBench是一个用于评估和训练大语言模型在检索增强生成中选择性采纳证据能力的基准。作者使用DAPO对Qwen3.5-4B进行后训练，采用确定性规则奖励或冻结语义评判器，在SelectBench-v2测试集上严格成功率从22.46%提升至25.54%（规则奖励）和26.46%（语义评判），但改进幅度有限且未通过Holm校正，表明需要更强的奖励塑形或更多训练迭代才能实现稳健提升。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 检索增强的大语言模型常面临混杂着有用证据与误导性内容或指令式语句的上下文，完全拒绝会丢弃有效证据，而不加批判地采纳会导致错误或不安全的答案。因此，选择性采纳相关信息同时拒绝欺骗性或有害内容的能力对于在真实检索场景中可靠部署至关重要。

Method: 引入了SelectBench，一个用于选择性证据采纳的受控基准和训练集。使用DAPO（确定性策略优化）对Qwen3.5-4B进行后训练，奖励设计有两种：确定性规则奖励（DAPO-Rule）和冻结的语义评判器（DAPO-DeepSeek）。在修正后的325样本SelectBench-v2测试集上评估。

Result: 严格成功率从原始检查点的22.46%提升至DAPO-Rule的25.54%和DAPO-DeepSeek的26.46%。两种训练策略均减少了禁止内容的采纳，并生成了更短、更聚焦的回复，但提示注入跟随没有改善。提升幅度较小且未能通过Holm校正。DAPO-DeepSeek在MMLU和干净的HotpotQA上没有明显退化，表明后训练过程保持了一般能力。

Conclusion: 结果表明在选择性证据使用方面有方向性改进，同时指出注入抵抗性和统计稳健性是未来工作仍需解决的重要挑战。

Abstract: Retrieval-augmented large language models frequently face contexts that interleave useful evidence with misleading statements or instruction-like content. Blanket refusal discards valid evidence, whereas uncritical adoption yields incorrect or unsafe answers. The ability to selectively adopt relevant information while rejecting deceptive or harmful content is therefore critical for reliable deployment in real-world retrieval settings. We introduce SelectBench, a controlled benchmark and training set for selective evidence adoption, and post-train Qwen3.5-4B directly with DAPO using either deterministic rule rewards or a frozen semantic judge. On the corrected 325-example SelectBench-v2 test set, strict success rises from 22.46% for the original checkpoint to 25.54% with DAPO-Rule and 26.46% with DAPO-DeepSeek. Both trained policies reduce forbidden-content adoption and produce shorter, more focused responses, yet prompt-injection following does not improve. The paired gains are modest and fail to survive Holm correction, suggesting that stronger reward shaping or additional training iterations may be needed for more robust gains. DAPO-DeepSeek exhibits no material degradation on MMLU or clean HotpotQA, indicating that the post-training procedure preserves general capabilities. These results demonstrate a directional improvement in selective evidence use, while identifying injection resistance and statistical robustness as important remaining challenges for future work.

</details>


### [101] [Two-Step Occupation Coding](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.20101)
*Alexander M. Esser,Jens Dörpinghaus*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 提出一种两阶段的职业编码方法，先通过领域特定NER识别连续文本中的职业头衔（含噪声鲁棒性），再将提取的头衔映射到职业分类体系，相比单阶段端到端方法提升了准确性、鲁棒性和可解释性，并引入基于边际的置信度准则替代绝对阈值，代码开源。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有职业编码方法通常采用单阶段端到端方式，将职业头衔识别和编码映射合并处理，导致模型负担过重、鲁棒性不足且可解释性差。需要一种分离式的两阶段方法，分别优化识别和映射环节，以提升整体性能。

Method: 提出两阶段流水线：第一阶段使用领域特定的命名实体识别（NER）模型，从连续文本（含OCR噪声）中识别职业头衔；第二阶段将提取的职业头衔映射到职业分类体系，使分类器专注于映射任务。同时引入基于边际的置信度准则替代传统绝对阈值。

Result: 两阶段分离方法相比单阶段端到端方法在准确性、鲁棒性和可解释性上均有提升；方法基于德语文档开发但可迁移至其他语言；通过开源代码和评估脚本支持可重复性。

Conclusion: 分离式两阶段职业编码方法通过解耦识别和映射任务，显著提升了编码性能与可靠性，尤其在噪声环境下表现更优，且具有良好的语言可迁移性和可复现性。

Abstract: Occupation coding links job titles in free text to occupational taxonomies and is a core task in labor market research. Existing approaches typically address this problem in a single end-to-end step, jointly identifying job titles and assigning occupational codes. This paper presents a novel two-step approach that separates these tasks. In the first step, a domain-specific Named Entity Recognition (NER) model identifies occupational titles in continuous text, even under noise such as OCR errors. In the second step, the extracted job titles are mapped to a taxonomy, enabling the classifier to focus exclusively on this mapping. We demonstrate that this separation improves accuracy, robustness, and interpretability compared to single-step approaches. The method has been developed for German documents but is transferable to other languages. We further introduce a margin-based confidence criterion for occupation coding, replacing common absolute thresholds. To support reproducibility, we publish the source code and evaluation scripts.

</details>


### [102] [Understanding the Impact of Linguistic Realization Choices on LLM Stance with Causal Tracing](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.20115)
*Langchen Huang,Sebastian Padó,Franziska Weeber*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 本文研究语言构式（linguistic construction）能否系统地改变大语言模型的决策，并通过激活修补（activation patching）定位这些变化的因果来源。实验使用政治立场判断任务，对英文政治陈述数据集进行六种受控改写（保持或反转语义），在四个开源模型上发现立场不稳定性同时存在于保持语义和反转语义的改写中。通过替换改写句的激活值，发现中后层解码器模块（尤其是最终提示位置的块输出）能最强地恢复原始立场分布。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有研究主要关注词汇实现（lexical realization）对LLM的影响，而忽略了构式选择（constructional choice）的作用。本文旨在探究语言构式能否系统性地改变LLM的决策，并定位这些改变在模型内部的因果位置。

Method: 使用政治立场判断作为语义敏感案例，扩展英文政治陈述数据集，设计六种受控改写类型（保持或反转语义）。在四个开源权重模型上实验，分析输出稳定性。采用激活修补（activation patching）方法，将原始陈述的激活值替换到改写陈述的前向传播中，测量哪些组件能恢复原始立场分布。

Result: 立场不稳定性同时出现在保持语义和反转语义的改写中。激活修补显示，中后层解码器层（特别是最终提示位置的块输出）提供了最强的恢复信号。

Conclusion: 语言构式选择可以系统性地影响LLM的决策，且这些影响主要归因于中后层解码器层在最终提示位置的块输出，表明模型内部存在特定的因果路径。

Abstract: Large language models (LLMs) are known to be sensitive to prompt and input formulations. However, existing studies have focused on lexical realization and largely ignored constructional choice. This paper studies whether linguistic construction can systematically shift LLM decisions and where these shifts can be causally localized inside the model. We use political stance judgment as a meaning-sensitive case study and extend an English political statements dataset, resulting in six controlled linguistic rewrite types that preserve or invert the meaning of a statement. Experiments on four open-weight models show that stance instability affect both meaning-preserving and meaning-inversing rewrites. Because output shifts reveal that rewrites affect stance, but not where in the model, we apply activation patching, where activations from the original statement are substituted into the forward pass for the rewritten statement and measure which components recover the original stance distribution. The results show that mid-to-late decoder layers, especially block outputs at the final prompt position, provide the strongest restoration signal.

</details>


### [103] [OpenSkillRisk: Benchmarking Agent Safety When Using Real-World Risky Third-Party Skills](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.20121)
*Qiyuan Liu,Tingfeng Hui,Kun Zhan,Kaike Zhang,Ning Miao*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 本文系统研究了基于LLM的智能体在使用第三方技能时面临的安全风险，构建了包含263个风险技能的OpenSkillRisk基准测试，涵盖7种威胁类型。实验覆盖3个主流CLI智能体框架和13个先进LLM，发现即使最安全的配置仍有约17%的概率执行不安全动作，揭示了三种典型失败模式：未能识别风险、识别但未及时干预、超出用户意图执行指令，表明需要提升LLM的风险推理能力和智能体框架的执行控制能力。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: LLM智能体在开放世界场景中利用第三方技能扩展能力，但第三方技能可能引入潜在安全风险（看似无害的技能在实际执行时才暴露风险）。现有研究缺乏对智能体系统识别和避免此类风险能力的系统评估，因此需要构建专用安全基准进行量化与定性分析。

Method: 构建OpenSkillRisk基准，从公开技能市场收集263个风险技能，按威胁类型分为7个类别，为每个技能配对标准化用户任务和对应沙箱环境进行可控评估。在3个主流CLI智能体框架和13个先进LLM上进行全面实验，并进行细粒度行为分析以诊断智能体的行为模式。

Result: 所有测试系统均无法可靠处理风险技能：即使最安全的配置仍有约17%的情况执行了不安全动作。上下文依赖型和系统级风险尤其难以规避。行为分析揭示了三种反复出现的失败模式：智能体未能识别风险、识别风险但未能在行动前干预、遵循技能指令超出用户预期范围。

Conclusion: 当前智能体系统在处理第三方技能安全风险方面存在显著不足，需要在LLM的风险推理能力和智能体框架的执行控制两个方面进行改进。

Abstract: LLM-based agents leverage third-party skills to extend their capabilities in open-world scenarios. However, third-party skills can introduce extra security vulnerabilities, as seemingly harmless skills can contain latent safety risks that only emerge during actual execution. In this work, we conduct a systematic investigation into how well current agent systems recognize and avoid such risks. To support quantitative and qualitative evaluation, we construct OpenSkillRisk, a dedicated safety benchmark containing 263 risky skills collected from public skill marketplaces. We classify these skills into seven categories based on their threat types and pair each skill with a standardized user task and a corresponding sandbox for controlled evaluation. Distinct from prior benchmarks, OpenSkillRisk not only covers more realistic and diverse unsafe scenarios, but also provides a fine-grained analysis to diagnose the behavioral patterns of agents in such scenarios. We conduct comprehensive experiments covering three mainstream CLI agent frameworks and thirteen state-of-the-art LLMs. Experimental results show that no tested system handles risky skills reliably: even the safest configurations still execute unsafe actions in about 17% of cases. Context-dependent and system-level risks are especially difficult for current agent systems to avoid. Our behavioral analysis reveals three recurring failure patterns: agents may fail to recognize the risk, recognize it but fail to intervene before acting, or follow skill instructions beyond the user's intended scope. These findings highlight the need to improve both risk reasoning in LLMs and execution control in agent frameworks.

</details>


### [104] [Back to Back with a Copy: A Computational Analysis of AI-Generated Visual Contemporary Art Pastiches](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.20127)
*Anca Dinu,Andreiana Mihail,Andra-Maria Florescu,Claudiu Creanga,Liviu Dinu*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 本文双重目标：一是考察新一代生成模型在模仿当代艺术作品风格上是否更优，二是探究不同大语言模型在风格评估的多维一致性。通过五种计算机视觉模型（纹理、颜色、语义、构图、感知）在嵌入空间中计算余弦距离，发现新模型在语义对齐和多样性上优于旧模型，但在颜色、纹理等浅层特征上略逊。研究确认艺术风格本质上是多维的，且其度量不依赖特定空间架构，并通过艺术家反馈进行了定性验证。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 随着生成式AI艺术创作能力提升，需要系统评估其模仿当代艺术风格的逼真度，同时验证风格评估的多维性是否在不同视觉模型间保持一致，从而为AI艺术评价提供客观定量方法。

Method: 选取12位当代艺术家的原作，使用新旧两代生成模型产生风格模仿作品（pastiches）。采用五种互补的计算机视觉模型（分别侧重纹理、颜色、语义、构图、感知特征），在各自的高维嵌入空间中计算余弦距离，量化模仿作品与原作之间的风格相似度。最后邀请艺术家本人对结果进行人工反馈。

Result: 新生成模型在语义对齐（semantic alignment）和生成多样性上显著优于旧模型，但在颜色、纹理和感知一致性等浅层视觉特征上表现略差。五种视觉模型的距离度量结果总体一致，支持风格评价的多维性不依赖于特定模型架构的结论。

Conclusion: 艺术风格模仿是多维度的，不同视觉特征维度（语义、颜色、纹理等）的进步并非同步；新一代生成模型在高级语义理解上更强，但浅层视觉细节的忠实度仍有提升空间。风格评估的多维性具有跨模型稳定性，未来应结合人类艺术家反馈完善评估框架。

Abstract: The aim of this paper is twofold. First, it investigates whether newer generative models are getting better at pastiching contemporary artworks. Second, it explores the consistency of the multidimensional nature of stylistic evaluation across different LLMs. Building on previous work, we analyze stylistic similarity between AI generated pastiches and the original artworks of twelve contemporary artists. We used five complementary computer vision models to capture texture, color, semantics, composition, and perceptual features through cosine distance in high-dimensional embedding spaces. The distances obtained show that the newer image generation model that we used has produced pastiches with improved semantic alignment and greater diversity than the model used in previous work. However, it was slightly less performant on shallow features such as color, texture, and perceptual adherence. Our findings confirm that artistic style is inherently multidimensional, and measuring it does not depend on any spatial architecture. These quantitative findings are contextualized through feedback from human evaluators, which are the artists themselves.

</details>


### [105] [SLAI T-Rex: Full-Parameter Post-training of the DeepSeek-V4 Family on Ascend SuperPOD](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.20145)
*Dongfang Li,Xiaodong Luo,Ruoyu Sun,Xuhui Chen,Linyuan Qiu,Jian Meng,Zhengxuan Lu,Yiting Wang,Yucheng Xie,Tao Guo,Tianxiang Fang,Jing Li,Sihang Chen,Shihao Hong,Chang Liu,Weihua Dai,Zirong Zeng,Ziwei Zhu,Zhuohan Wang,Zhengjun Yue,Igor Vasilyev,Min Liu,Weijian Sun,Xin Chen,Yingmeng Gao,Jinhua Zhou,Taolue Chen,Chenwei Wu,Dong Zhang,Wenlong Jin,Jinmin Xiang,Barkova Maria,Ushakov Anton,Xianfei Jin,Tian Ding,Zhihang Lin,Qian Chen,Linxin Yang,Mingzhe Yang,Bingwei Zhang,Hongzhang Yang,Fangxue Zhang,Shijun Qin,Jie Yu,Cuihua Hu,Tolstykh Vasiliy,Nosov Ivan,Abdullin Amir,Zhichen Zhou,Xin Zhang,Zhixiong Ning,Xutong Zhao,Junjie Huang,Jiajun Liu,Weiyan Kong,Zheng Zhang,Wenhan Luo,Lin Hu,Yangbo Guo,Li Zeng,Shihao Zeng,Baotian Hu,Min Zhang,Haizhou Li,Zhiquan Luo*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 本文提出了一个在Ascend NPU SuperPOD上对万亿参数MoE模型进行全参数后训练的分层优化框架（SLAI T-Rex），实现了34.22%的MFU（相比开源基线提升2.93倍），并基于DeepSeek-V4-Flash构建了面向运筹学（OR）任务的CPT和SFT流程，最终在零样本Pass@1上达到71.81%，超越GPT-5.4-Mini和基础模型。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 万亿参数级MoE模型的全参数后训练在分布式训练中面临严重的内存压力、非重叠通信开销和低效内核执行等系统级挑战，现有系统多基于GPU集群，亟需在Ascend NPU平台上实现端到端的优化实践。

Method: 提出分层优化框架，涵盖模型级并行策略、计算-通信编排以及底层内核执行优化；在此基础上建立面向运筹学任务的CPT和SFT工作流，结合收集的领域资源与求解器验证的合成优化文档构建高质量数据集（10K样本，涵盖四类任务和三种问题表示）。

Result: 系统在Ascend NPU SuperPOD上实现34.22%的MFU，相比开源基线提升2.93倍；基于DeepSeek-V4-Flash的领域专用模型在零样本Pass@1上达到71.81%，分别超过GPT-5.4-Mini和基础模型3.98和11.27个百分点。

Conclusion: 该工作展示了从Ascend基础设施上高效的万亿参数模型后训练到面向求解器接地数学建模的领域专用Flash模型的完整栈路径，推进了复杂推理的前沿模型系统。

Abstract: Full-parameter post-training of trillion-parameter-scale MoE models introduces substantial system-level challenges for large-scale distributed training, including severe memory pressure, non-overlapped communication overhead, and inefficient kernel execution. While most large-scale LLM training systems are built around GPU-based clusters, this report presents an end-to-end optimization practice on the Ascend NPU SuperPOD. Using the DeepSeek-V4 model family as the target workload, we develop a hierarchical optimization framework spanning model-level parallelism, computation-communication orchestration, and low-level kernel execution. The resulting system achieves 34.22% Model FLOPs Utilization (MFU) with a 2.93x improvement over the open-source baseline recipe while maintaining training stability. Building on this optimized infrastructure, we further establish a CPT and SFT workflow for complex Operations Research (OR) tasks. We refer to the integrated framework as SLAI T-Rex. Using DeepSeek-V4-Flash, we develop OR-oriented CPT and SFT data pipelines that combine collected domain resources with solver-verified synthetic optimization documents. The resulting dataset contains 10K high-quality SFT samples spanning four task categories and three problem representations. The specialized model achieves the highest average zero-shot Pass@1 score among the evaluated models, reaching 71.81% and outperforming GPT-5.4-Mini and the base DeepSeek-V4-Flash model by 3.98 and 11.27 percentage points, respectively. Overall, this work demonstrates a full-stack pathway from efficient trillion-parameter model post-training on Ascend infra to domain-specialized Flash models for solver-grounded mathematical modeling, advancing frontier-model systems for complex reasoning.

</details>


### [106] [Gotta Catch them all: the modes of Sycophancy](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.20146)
*Shreyans Jain,Alexandra Yost,Amirali Abdullah*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 本文挑战了“谄媚（sycophancy）是单一行为维度”的假设，通过分析三种假设的谄媚模式在948个社交压力情境下的表现，发现虽然输出高度相似（文本分类器准确率仅57.8%），但从第14层开始内部表征完全线性可分。不同模式出现在不同处理阶段、依赖不同的注意力回路、对不同输入反应最强，表明谄媚并非单一倾向，而是由表征和计算上不同的模式组成的结构化家族，需要更精确的测量和干预。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 先前研究大多将谄媚视为单一行为维度，可以统一放大或抑制。作者质疑这一假设，试图探究谄媚是否由多种不同的模式组成。

Method: 在948个社交压力情境中分析三种假设的谄媚模式，使用纯文本分类器（准确率仅57.8%），从第14层开始内部表征完全线性可分，进一步分析不同模式的处理阶段、注意力回路和输入敏感性。

Result: 谄媚不是单一倾向，而是由表征和计算上不同的模式组成的结构化家族。三种模式产生高度相似的输出，但内部表征从第14层开始完全线性可分，它们出现在不同处理阶段、依赖不同注意力回路、对不同输入反应最强。

Conclusion: 该发现挑战了谄媚是单一维度的传统观点，揭示了其内在异质性，为更精确的测量和干预提供了基础。

Abstract: Large language models often align with users' beliefs at the expense of factual accuracy, a behavior known as sycophancy. Prior mechanistic studies largely treat sycophancy as a single behavioral dimension that can be uniformly amplified or suppressed. We challenge this assumption by analyzing three hypothesized modes of sycophancy across 948 social pressure situations. Although the modes produce highly similar outputs, with a text-only classifier achieving just 57.8 percent accuracy, their internal representations are perfectly linearly separable from layer 14 onward. We further find the modes emerge at different processing stages, rely on distinct attention circuitry, and fire strongest on different inputs. These results show that sycophancy is not a monolithic tendency, but a structured family of representationally and computationally distinct modes, motivating more precise measurement and intervention.

</details>


### [107] [surprisal is Not a Theory](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.20208)
*Andrés Buxó-Lugo,Aniello De Santo,Morgan Grobol,Ryan J. Hubbard,Cassandra L. Jacobs*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 本文指出，尽管惊奇理论常被视为计算层面的解释，但将大型语言模型（LLM）的概率不加批判地用作惊奇度量，会模糊不同模型在表征和算法层面的承诺。通过三项分析，作者证明算法和架构选择显著影响语言模型概率的计算，因此建议研究者在检验惊奇理论时重新评估将LLM概率视为可互换的做法。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 作者认为，计算层面叙事虽被用于支持“表征无关研究”，但LLM这类黑箱系统的使用并不能免除建模者在表征决策上的责任；不加批判地使用LLM惊奇值反而掩盖了模型在表征和算法层面的差异。

Method: 作者进行了三项分析，系统考察算法和模型架构对语言模型概率计算的影响。

Result: 分析结果表明，算法和模型架构的选择在语言模型概率的计算中起着显著作用。

Conclusion: 研究者应当重新审视将大型语言模型概率视为可互换这一做法，在检验惊奇理论时需关注算法和表征层面的差异。

Abstract: Surprisal Theory is often characterized as a computational-level explanation per (Marr, 1982). We argue in this work that, even though a computational level narrative has been used to support "representation-agnostic research" within computational psycholinguistics, the movement toward black box systems embodied by large language models (LLMs) does not exempt modelers using the surprisal metric from the representational decisions required by computational-level characterizations. In fact, we argue that the uncritical use of LLM-surprisal obfuscates the representational and algorithmic-level commitments of different models. In three analyses, we show that the choice of algorithm and model architecture play significant roles in the computation of language model probabilities. We advise that researchers who wish to test Surprisal Theory re-evaluate the practice of treating large language model probabilities as interchangeable

</details>


### [108] [HalluTruthQA: A Fine-Grained Benchmark for Hallucination Detection, Localization, and Explanation in Arabic Question Answering](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.20219)
*Abdessalam Bouchekif,Mohammed-En-Nadhir Zighem,Salah Eddine Bekhouche,Hichem Telli,Somaya Eltanbouly,Shahd Gaben,Heba Sbahi,Samer Rashwani,Mutaz Al-Khatib,Emad Mohamed,Mohammed Ghaly,Abdenour Hadid*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 本文提出了 HalluTruthQA，一个针对阿拉伯语问答中幻觉评估的细粒度基准，包含 2,400 个专家精选示例，涵盖伊斯兰知识、历史、科学和地理四个领域。每个示例提供问题、模型生成答案、参考答案、二值幻觉标签、六个候选答案、字符级错误跨度、人工解释以及宏观/微观幻觉类型。在四个开源 LLM 上评估了幻觉检测、跨度定位、事实验证和解释生成四项任务，结果表明不同模型在不同任务上表现各异，最佳检测 Macro-F1 为 0.880，定位 F1-Sp 为 0.516，事实验证 LO-Score 为 0.852，解释生成得分为 0.644。论文指出幻觉评估应从检测转向定位、验证和解释事实错误。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有幻觉基准多为回答级别标签，难以定位具体错误内容、解释错误原因或选择正确事实答案。阿拉伯语 LLM 虽然能生成流畅回答，但事实错误难以检测、定位、解释和验证，亟需一个细粒度的幻觉评估基准。

Method: 构建 HalluTruthQA 基准，包含 2,400 个专家精选示例，覆盖伊斯兰知识、历史、科学、地理四个领域。每个示例包含阿拉伯语问题、模型生成答案、参考答案、二值幻觉标签、六个候选答案（用于事实验证）、字符级错误跨度（用于幻觉答案）、人工编写的错误解释以及宏观和微观幻觉类型。在四个开源 LLM（Allam、Falcon-H1、Qwen32、Silma）上进行零样本评估，涵盖幻觉检测、跨度定位、事实验证和解释评估四个任务。

Result: 在幻觉检测任务上最佳 Macro-F1 为 0.880，跨度定位最佳 F1-Sp 为 0.516，事实验证最佳 LO-Score 为 0.852，解释评估最佳最终得分为 0.644。没有单一模型在所有任务上表现最优。

Conclusion: 幻觉评估应从简单的检测转向更细粒度的定位、验证和解释事实错误。本文提出的基准和评估框架为阿拉伯语幻觉研究提供了更全面的评测维度。

Abstract: Large language models (LLMs) can generate fluent Arabic answers, yet factual errors remain difficult to detect, localize, explain, and verify. Existing hallucination benchmarks often provide response-level labels, with limited support for identifying the exact erroneous content, explaining why it is incorrect, or selecting the correct factual answer. We introduce \textsc{HalluTruthQA}, a fine-grained benchmark for hallucination evaluation in Arabic question answering. The benchmark contains 2,400 expert-curated examples across four knowledge-intensive domains: Islamic knowledge, history, science, and geography. Each example pairs an Arabic question and a model-generated answer with a verified reference answer, a binary hallucination label, six candidate answers for factual verification, and, for hallucinated answers, character-level erroneous spans, human-written explanations, and macro and micro hallucination types.
  We evaluate four open-source LLMs, \textsc{Allam}, \textsc{Falcon-H1}, \textsc{Qwen32}, and \textsc{Silma}, in a zero-shot setting across hallucination detection, span-level localization, factual verification, and explanation evaluation. Results show that these tasks capture different abilities: no single model achieves the strongest performance across all tasks, with best scores of 0.880 Macro-F1 for detection, 0.516 F1-Sp for localization, 0.852 LO-Score for factual verification, and 0.644 final score for explanation evaluation. Our taxonomy shows that hallucination evaluation should move beyond detection toward localizing, verifying, and explaining factual errors. The code, dataset, prompts, and evaluation scripts are available at https://gitlab.com/nlpresearcher/HalluTruthQA.

</details>


### [109] [On the Systematic Challenges of Culturally Loaded Machine Translation: Dream of the Red Chamber as the Cultural Lens](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.20241)
*Yiming Wang,Jiayuan Di*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 本研究系统调查了大语言模型（LLM）在机器翻译中处理文化负载表达的挑战。基于《红楼梦》构建了中日双语数据集（500个片段），通过综合评估揭示了三方面挑战：任务层面前沿LLM表现不佳；人工评估中评价者背景导致分歧；自动评估指标不可靠。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 文化负载翻译对机器翻译构成独特挑战，因为含义深嵌于社会文化语境。尽管LLM使MT系统在许多场景达到类人质量，但其处理文化负载表达的能力尚待探究。

Method: 从文化代表性语料《红楼梦》构建中日双语数据集，包含500个片段覆盖多种文化类别；采用综合评估协议分析LLM在文化负载翻译中的表现。

Result: 揭示三大挑战：1）任务挑战：前沿LLM存在显著性能差距，难以处理文化负载内容；2）人工评估挑战：评估者背景差异导致翻译判断严重分歧；3）自动评估挑战：常用指标无法可靠评估此类翻译质量。

Conclusion: 这些发现可为计算科学和语言学中面向文化的翻译研究提供有价值的见解。

Abstract: Culturally loaded translation poses unique challenges for machine translation (MT), as meanings are deeply embedded in socio-cultural contexts beyond surface linguistic forms. Although large language models (LLMs) have enabled MT systems to achieve human-like quality in many scenarios, their ability to handle culturally loaded expressions remains underexplored. In this study, we systematically investigate the challenges posed by culturally loaded translation in LLM-based MT systems. We construct a Chinese-Japanese bilingual dataset from the culturally representative corpus Dream of the Red Chamber, containing 500 segments across diverse cultural categories. Using a comprehensive evaluation protocol, we reveal three main challenges: (1) task challenges, where frontier LLMs exhibit notable performance gaps and struggle with culturally loaded content; (2) human evaluation challenges, where evaluator backgrounds lead to substantial disagreement in translation judgments; and (3) automatic evaluation challenges, where widely used metrics fail to reliably assess translation quality for this task. These findings may offer valuable insights for culture-oriented translation research in both computational science and linguistics.

</details>


### [110] [Exposure is Optional: Learning Unlike Coordination in Language Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.20251)
*Jiamu Luo,Shane Steinert-Threlkeld*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 本文通过过滤语料训练（FiCT）方法，探究语言模型（GPT-2）是否需要在训练数据中直接接触“异类并列”（unlike coordination）才能习得该结构。结果表明，直接暴露并非必要：模型在完全移除异类并列实例的语料上训练后，仍能成功泛化到异类并列，其困惑度和语法判断与未过滤语料训练的模型相当。内部表征分析进一步揭示，模型处理异类并列时要么将并列成分视为相似结构类别，要么采用类似删除的机制，这两种策略都可通过仅接触同类并列习得。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 语言学中并列结构（尤其是异类并列）的本质存在争议，传统观点认为只有相同类别的成分才能并列，但自然语言中存在大量语法合格的异类并列。本文希望从语言模型的计算角度，检验异类并列的习得是否需要训练数据中直接出现，还是可以从一般组合能力中涌现。

Method: 采用过滤语料训练（Filtered-Corpus Training, FiCT）方法，在GPT-2模型的训练语料中移除所有异类并列实例，仅保留同类并列，然后评估模型在异类并列上的泛化能力。

Result: 直接暴露并非必要：过滤语料训练的模型在异类并列上的困惑度和语法判断与未过滤语料训练的模型相当。内部表征分析表明，模型处理异类并列时通过两种机制：将并列成分视为相似结构类别，或采用类似删除的操作，这些均可从同类并列中学习。

Conclusion: 本研究增进了对语言模型内部表征语言结构的理解，同时也为并列结构的理论辩论提供了计算证据，表明模型无需直接接触异类并列即可泛化并处理该结构。

Abstract: Coordination, a fundamental linguistic structure, remains a subject of intense debate, and its exact nature continues to elude theoretical linguistics. A common view holds that only same-category constituents can be conjoined, which has been challenged by the many grammatical unlike coordinations found in natural language. Treating language models as a computational testbed, we investigate whether the acquisition of unlike coordination requires direct exposure in the training data, or whether it can emerge organically from general compositional abilities. Using Filtered-Corpus Training (FiCT), we train GPT-2 models on corpora from which all instances of unlike coordination have been removed. We find that direct exposure is not necessary: models trained on filtered data successfully generalize to unlike coordination, achieving perplexity and grammaticality judgments comparable to models trained on unfiltered text. Furthermore, our analyses of internal representations indicate that language models process unlike coordination by treating the conjoined elements as belonging to similar structural categories or through a mechanism akin to deletion, both of which appear learnable from exposure to alike coordination alone. This work contributes to the growing understanding of how language models internally represent linguistic structures, while also adding to the broader debate on coordination by showing how models generalize and process unlike coordination without direct exposure.

</details>


### [111] [The Maskability Index: Predicting Task-Objective Alignment in Pretrained Language Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.20265)
*Ahmad Pouramini,Mahsa Afsharzadeh*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 本文提出Maskability Index (MI)，一个用于评估知识关系更适合掩码式提示还是前缀式提示的定量指标。MI基于深度排序得分差异计算，在ATOMIC2020基准上验证其与下游生成性能正相关，有助于在低资源场景下选择合适的提示模板。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 大规模预训练语言模型（如T5、BERT）在结构化知识生成上表现优异，但其性能高度依赖于提示策略与预训练目标之间的匹配程度。现有的提示选择缺乏量化依据，亟需一个可计算的指标来指导提示模板的选择。

Method: 定义Maskability Index (MI)，通过比较掩码模板与无掩码模板的DepthRank得分差异，量化目标与模板的对齐程度，从而判断知识关系更适合掩码式提示还是前缀式提示。

Result: 在ATOMIC2020知识库补全基准的多种关系上评估MI，发现MI与下游生成性能呈正相关，验证了其有效性。

Conclusion: MI能够帮助选择适当的提示模板和适配策略，用于从预训练语言模型中提取关系知识，尤其在低资源设置下具有实用价值。

Abstract: Large-scale pretrained language models such as T5 and BERT have demonstrated strong capabilities for generating structured knowledge. However, their performance depends on how closely the prompting strategy matches the objectives used during pretraining. We introduce the Maskability Index (MI), a quantitative metric that estimates whether a knowledge relation is better suited to masked-style prompting or prefix-style prompting in few-shot generation. MI is computed from differences in DepthRank scores between masked and unmasked templates, providing a principled measure of objective-template alignment. We evaluate MI on a diverse set of relations from the ATOMIC2020 knowledge base completion benchmark and show that it is positively correlated with downstream generation performance. These results indicate that MI can help select appropriate prompting templates and adaptation strategies for extracting relational knowledge from pretrained language models, especially in low-resource settings.

</details>


### [112] [Which Values Do LLMs Confuse? A Schwartz-Based Recognition Study](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.20270)
*Andrei Chetvergov,Stepan Ukolov,Timofei Sivoraksha,Alexander Evseev,Mikhail Solovev,Valeriia Kuschenko,Maria Chistyakova,Sergey Bolovtsov*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 研究评估大型语言模型在施瓦茨十种基本价值识别方面的能力，使用1000个俄语情景文本，对21个指令微调模型进行测试，结果显示准确率中等，错误主要集中在相邻价值上，且存在特定方向性的混淆模式，提示需要结合精确准确率、排序恢复和定向错误分析的综合评估方法。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 大型语言模型越来越多地通过其所认同的价值进行评估，但这种评估预设了模型能够识别具体情境中表达的价值。本研究旨在检验这一前提条件。

Method: 采用受控的top-1识别任务，基于施瓦茨十种基本价值。构建包含1000个俄语情景文本的评估集，每个文本由两名人工标注者独立标注。评估21个指令微调大语言模型（LLM）的 runs，使用固定排序响应协议，其中20个可靠 runs 组成语义面板。计算 pooled Acc@1 和 Acc@3，分析错误类型，包括相邻价值错误和定向混淆。

Result: Pooled Acc@1 为0.683，Acc@3 为0.892。相邻价值错误占语义错误的50.9%，而基线仅为24.4%。发现了八种跨检查点和人工确认子集重复出现的定向混淆，其中一些具有很强的非对称性，例如普遍主义→仁慈、传统→遵从、安全→权力，而刺激-享乐形成双向边界。这些混淆的严重程度因检查点而异，可能影响更高层次的价值轮廓。

Conclusion: 结果支持采用结合精确准确率、排序恢复和定向错误分析的价值识别评估方法。

Abstract: Large language models are increasingly evaluated through the values they endorse, but such evaluations presuppose that models can identify the value expressed in a concrete situation. We study this prerequisite as controlled top-1 recognition over Schwartz's ten basic values. Our evaluation set contains 1,000 Russian situational texts, balanced across the ten values and independently labeled by two human annotators per item. We evaluate 21 instruction-tuned LLM runs under a fixed ranked-response protocol; 20 runs with reliable outputs form the semantic panel. Pooled Acc@1 is 0.683 and Acc@3 is 0.892, showing that models often locate the correct motivational region while ranking close alternatives unstably. Adjacent values account for 50.9% of semantic errors, compared with 24.4% under a checkpoint-specific null. Eight directed confusions recur across checkpoints and human-confirmed subsets. Several are strongly asymmetric, including Universalism to Benevolence, Tradition to Conformity, and Security to Power, whereas Stimulation-Hedonism forms a bidirectional boundary. Their severity is checkpoint-specific and can bias higher-order value profiles. The results motivate value-recognition evaluation that combines exact accuracy, ranked recovery, and directed error analysis.

</details>


### [113] [Sound Probabilistic Safety Bounds for Large Language Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.20286)
*Mahdi Nazeri,Anne-Kathrin Schmuck,Sadegh Soudjani,Alessandro Abate*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 提出了一种新颖的框架，用于计算大型语言模型（LLM）对给定提示生成有害输出的概率的严格界限。通过使用Clopper-Pearson置信区间获得PAC界限，并利用潜在空间特征优先探索自回归生成树中更可能产生有害输出的分支，从而高效计算有用的下界，即使真实有害概率极小时也能保证下界的可靠性。实验证明该方法在先进LLM上的有效性，为LLM的评估和统计认证提供了新途径。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有的LLM安全性评估缺乏严格的统计保证，无法可靠地衡量模型生成有害内容的概率。需要一种能够提供形式化、可证明的界限的方法，特别是对于极低概率的有害事件，以便对LLM进行统计认证。

Method: 使用Clopper-Pearson置信区间推导PAC（Probably Approximately Correct）界限；提出一种利用潜在空间特征的算法，优先探索自回归生成树中可能产生有害输出的分支；通过这种有偏采样策略高效计算下界，并确保下界在形式上小于实际有害概率。

Result: 在先进LLM上计算出了非平凡的下界，证明了该方法即使在真实有害概率极小时也能提供有效的下界，且下界具有形式上的可靠性（soundness）。

Conclusion: 该工作首次实现了对LLM的统计认证，为评估LLM安全性提供了形式化保证，推动了LLM可信部署的进展。

Abstract: We propose a novel framework for computing rigorous bounds on the probability that a large language model (LLM) generates harmful output to a given prompt. We study a new application of the Clopper-Pearson confidence intervals to obtain probably approximately correct (PAC) bounds for this problem. As our main technical contribution, we propose an algorithm that leverages features in the latent space to prioritize exploring branches in the auto-regressive generation tree that are more likely to produce harmful outputs. Our approach in particular enables the efficient computation of useful lower bounds, even in scenarios where the true harm probability is extremely small, and crucially, the obtained lower bounds are sound, i.e., formally proven to be less than the actual harmfulness probability: our experimental results demonstrate the effectiveness of our method by computing non-trivial lower bounds on state-of-the-art LLMs. This study newly enables the evaluation and statistical certification of LLMs.

</details>


### [114] [PyroDash: Cost-Efficient Token-Level Small-Large Language Model Collaborative Inference](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.20327)
*Niqi Lyu,Pengtao Shi,Wei Qiu,Jianlin Zhong,Sicong Xia,Jianyao Ma,Yicheng Ding*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: PyroDash 是一个成本感知的令牌级小模型-大模型协作推理框架，SLM 通过发射控制令牌自主决定何时将推理过程卸载给 LLM，只需一次交接即可完成，无需独立路由器、LLM 重训练或访问 LLM logits。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 大语言模型推理能力强但服务成本高昂，小语言模型成本低但在困难问题上可靠性不足。现有混合方法依赖独立路由器、需要 LLM logits 访问或昂贵的多次 LLM 调用，缺乏轻量级、成本感知的协作方案。

Method: PyroDash 采用三阶段训练：(1) 控制令牌嵌入学习；(2) 面向卸载的监督微调；(3) 使用 GRPO 进行成本感知对齐。奖励函数平衡准确率与标准化推理成本。SLM 在生成过程中内部化卸载策略，通过协作引擎进行单次交接给冻结的 LLM 完成推理。

Result: 在五个数学推理基准上，λ=0.05 时平均准确率 64.04%（比纯 LLM 高 6.36 个百分点），成本降低 20.4%；λ=0.6 时准确率 54.55%，LLM 令牌占比仅 1.90%，每次示例平均 0.012 次 LLM 调用，总成本从 49.36 美元降至 1.78 美元。

Conclusion: 学习到的令牌级交接策略能显著减少 LLM 使用量，同时保持较强的推理性能，为低成本高效推理提供了可行的 SLM-LLM 协作范式。

Abstract: Large language models (LLMs) provide strong reasoning capabilities but are expensive to serve at scale, whereas small language models (SLMs) are cheaper but less reliable on difficult problems. We introduce PyroDash, a cost-aware framework for token-level SLM-LLM collaborative inference. During generation, the SLM decides whether to request assistance by emitting a control token. A Collaborate Engine then sends the query and partial reasoning trace to a frozen LLM for completion through a single handoff. The policy is internalized in the SLM, requiring neither a separate router, LLM retraining, nor access to LLM logits. PyroDash trains the SLM in three stages: control-token embedding learning, offloading-oriented supervised fine-tuning, and cost-aware alignment with Group Relative Policy Optimization. Its reward balances answer accuracy against inference cost normalized by LLM-only inference. Across five mathematical reasoning benchmarks, PyroDash supports different accuracy-cost operating points. With $λ=0.05$, it achieves 64.04 percent average accuracy, 6.36 percentage points above the LLM-only baseline, while reducing cost by 20.4 percent. With $λ=0.6$, it achieves 54.55 percent accuracy with a 1.90 percent LLM token ratio and 0.012 LLM calls per example, reducing total cost from USD 49.36 to USD 1.78. These results show that learned token-level handoffs can reduce LLM use while preserving strong reasoning performance.

</details>


### [115] [Generative AI floods and dilutes the market for books](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.20349)
*Tuhin Chakrabarty,Xinyue Liu,Jane C. Ginsburg,Paramveer Dhillon*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 该研究通过检测2023-2026年间亚马逊上14,419本自出版类型小说的全文AI内容，发现含有大量AI文本（>25%）的书籍在目录中占比大但销售额占比小，但正逐步抢占销量份额和稀缺的畅销榜高位。市场新增书籍速度远超收入增速，单书收入普遍下降。无AI文本的书籍在AI渗透率高的类型和Kindle Unlimited可用性高的市场中损失最大。畅销书中，AI含量高的书籍比无AI书籍更倾向于借鉴现有书籍的独特语言，且这种重叠度随收入增加而上升。研究表明，生成式AI通过规模而非质量重塑创意市场，这对版权合理使用抗辩的市场影响问题具有直接意义。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 验证"生成式AI产生的长篇小说常被视为低质量'垃圾'、买家不会关注、商业影响微乎其微"这一假设是否成立，并评估AI生成内容对创意市场的实际冲击。

Method: 对2023-2026年间亚马逊上14,419本自出版类型小说进行全文AI检测（阈值设为>25% AI文本），并将检测结果匹配至截至2026年6月的每日销售记录。分析了AI文本书籍的目录占比、销售份额、畅销榜排名变化、市场增长（书籍数量vs收入增长）、单书收入趋势、不同类型和Kindle Unlimited条件下的影响差异，以及畅销书中AI文本与无AI文本书籍的语言独特性（与现有书籍的重叠度）。

Result: 1) AI文本含量高的书籍占目录很大比例但销售份额较小，但销售份额随时间增长，并抢占原本由无AI文本书籍占据的稀缺高位排名。2) 季度内有销售记录的书籍数量增长了19.2倍，但季度收入仅增长8.9倍，市场新增书籍速度远超收入增速，大部分类型的单书收入下降。3) 无AI文本的书籍在AI扩散度高的类型和Kindle Unlimited可用性高的市场中损失最大。4) 畅销书中，AI文本含量高的书籍比无AI书籍更频繁地使用现有书籍中的独特语言，且这种重叠度随收入增加而上升（无AI文本书籍无此梯度）。

Conclusion: 生成式AI可以通过规模效应而非质量提升来重塑创意市场，这一发现直接关系到版权侵权合理使用抗辩中'市场影响'这一核心问题的判定。

Abstract: Generative AI can produce book-length works of fiction at near-zero cost. These books are often dismissed as low-quality ``slop'' that buyers will ignore, and are assumed to carry little commercial weight. We test that assumption with full-text AI detection across 14,419 self-published genre-fiction books sold on Amazon from 2023 to 2026, matched to daily sales records through June 2026. None of these books disclose whether or not they contain AI-produced content. We find that books for which we detected substantial AI text ($>$ 25\%) make up a large share of the catalog but a smaller share of sales. Even so, they reach commercial scale, winning a growing share of sales over time and taking more of the scarce top-rank positions once held by books with no detected AI text. Over this period, the number of books with observed sales in a quarter grew 19.2-fold, while quarterly revenue grew only 8.9-fold. The market therefore added selling books faster than it added revenue, and revenue per selling book fell across most genres. Books with no AI text lose the most ground in genres with high AI diffusion, and most of all where Kindle Unlimited availability is high. Among top-selling books, those with substantial AI text draw on more distinctive language from existing books than do books with no AI text; for these books overlap rises with revenue, a gradient we do not detect for books with no AI text. Generative AI can thus reshape a creative market through scale rather than quality. Our results bear directly on the market-effect question at the center of the fair use defense to copyright infringement.

</details>


### [116] [Notes to Self: Can LLMs Benefit from Experiential Abstractions?](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.20372)
*Chang Liu,Xinyu Li,Artur Dubrawski*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 本文研究大型语言模型（LLM）能否像人类一样，从经验中提取可复用的抽象知识（如策略、注意事项），并将其应用于数学和逻辑推理任务。通过从MATH训练集的解题轨迹中提取自然语言抽象，构建可检索的抽象库，并采用推理时检索和强化学习两种使用模式，实验表明经验抽象能提升LLM性能，且自提取抽象与教师提取效果相当，框架可迁移至其他数据集和模型。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 人类善于从经验中提炼可复用的抽象知识（如策略、警示）并逐步提高问题解决效率。研究者希望探究大型语言模型是否也能类似地从经验抽象中获益，从而提升其数学和逻辑推理能力。

Method: 从MATH训练集的LLM解题轨迹中，由更强的教师模型或LLM自身提取自然语言抽象，构建可检索的抽象库。采用两种使用模式：(1) 推理时检索相关抽象；(2) 在强化学习（RL）中使用抽象增强的训练提示。

Result: 经验抽象显著提升了LLM在数学和逻辑推理基准上的表现。自提取抽象与教师提取抽象效果相当，且该抽象使用框架可迁移至其他数据集和模型。

Conclusion: LLM能够像人类一样提取和应用经验抽象，从而更有效地解决问题，这为提升LLM推理能力提供了新的思路。

Abstract: Humans distill experience into reusable abstractions, e.g., strategies and cautionary reminders, and apply them to gradually solve problems more effectively. We study whether Large Language Models (LLMs) can similarly benefit from such experiential abstractions. From LLMs' solution traces on the MATH training set, a stronger teacher or the LLMs themselves extract natural-language abstractions into a retrievable library. We explore two usage modes: (1) inference-time retrieval and (2) reinforcement learning (RL) with abstraction-augmented training prompts. Experiential abstractions improve LLM performance on mathematical and logical reasoning benchmarks. Self-extracted abstractions match teacher-extracted ones, and our abstraction usage framework can transfer to other datasets and models. These findings suggest LLMs can extract and apply experiential abstractions much as humans leverage distilled experience.

</details>


### [117] [LKValues: Aligning Large Language Models with Sri Lankan Societal Values](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.20410)
*Nethmi Muthugala,Supryadi,Surangika Ranathunga,Nisansa de Silva,Ruijie Tao,Ovindu Gunatunga,Pengyun Zhu,Shaowei Zhang,Jingting Zheng,Deyi Xiong*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: LKValues 是首个面向斯里兰卡语境的价值对齐资源套件，包含基于 205 份三语调查提炼的 40 项社会价值观、15 万条情景指令语料库 LKvaluesIT 和 1000 条评估基准 LKvaluesBench。实验表明主流 LLM 在斯里兰卡价值观上存在低资源和文化对齐缺口，而 LKValues 微调能有效提升模型在英语和僧伽罗语上的对齐表现。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有 LLM 价值对齐存在西方文化偏见，忽视斯里兰卡等多元语言社会的本土价值观；现有基准缺少僧伽罗语语境下的价值观评估，阻碍了文化敏感的评价与微调。

Method: 通过三语（僧伽罗语、泰米尔语、英语）调查 205 名受访者，融合全球框架与 LLM 诱导的本土构念，提炼出 40 项社会价值观；据此构建新闻衍生的指令微调语料库 LKvaluesIT（15 万条情景实例）和评估基准 LKvaluesBench（1000 条实例）；在多种闭源和开源 LLM 上评估，并对 Qwen3.5-4B-Base、Qwen3.5-9B-Base 和 Aya-Expanse-8B-Base 进行微调。

Result: 新更大的 LLM 仍存在低资源和价值观对齐差距；LKValues 微调改善了 Qwen 系列模型在英语和僧伽罗语上的表现，减少了无效输出和跨语言差异，但效果因模型族而异。

Conclusion: LKValues 能有效嵌入斯里兰卡价值观，为低资源、国家特定的多元价值对齐提供了可复制的流水线。

Abstract: Value alignment of Large Language Models (LLMs) has been shown to be culturally biased toward Western norms. This results in the mishandling of local values in multilingual societies such as Sri Lanka that have their unique cultural dynamics. Existing benchmarks overlook Sri Lankan-contextualized values in its official language Sinhala, hindering culturally sensitive evaluation and fine-tuning. To bridge this gap, we propose LKValues, the first survey-grounded resource suite for Sri Lankan value alignment. From a trilingual survey of 205 respondents, blending adapted global frameworks and LLM-elicited local constructs, we derive 40 majority-endorsed societal values. Using these values, we construct LKvaluesIT, a Sinhala-English news-derived instruction corpus containing 150k scenario-based instances, and LKvaluesBench, a value-sensitive evaluation benchmark of 1,000 instances. We evaluate a set of proprietary and open-weight LLMs with LKvaluesBench. We fine-tune three open-weight base models (Qwen3.5-4B-Base, Qwen3.5-9B-Base, and Aya-Expanse-8B-Base). Our experiments show that newer and larger LLMs still exhibit low-resource and cultural value-alignment gaps. LKValues fine-tuning improves Qwen-family models in English and Sinhala, reducing invalid outputs and cross-lingual disparities, though gains remain model-family dependent. These highlight LKValues efficacy in embedding Sri Lankan values, offering a replicable pipeline for low-resource, country-specific pluralist value alignment. The dataset is publicly available at https://github.com/NextME14/LKValues.

</details>


<div id='cs.AI'></div>

# cs.AI [[Back]](#toc)

### [118] [FineServe: A Fine-Grained Dataset and Characterization of Global LLM Serving Workloads](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.19349)
*Tiancheng Zhang,Shaoyuan Huang,Mingyuan Wang,Yunfeng Zhao,Xiaofei Wang,Wenyu Wang*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: FineServe 是一个从全球商业平台收集的、真实环境下的多模型 LLM 服务负载数据集，支持对异构模型和任务的细粒度表征，并提供了可配置的负载生成器，用于评估路由、调度和容量规划策略。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有研究多依赖代理轨迹或粗粒度表征，无法捕捉现代多模型 LLM 平台的异构性，缺乏真实、细粒度的服务负载数据来支撑低延迟、高吞吐的高效服务系统设计。

Method: 从全球商业平台收集真实的多模型 LLM 服务负载数据集 FineServe，对到达动态和令牌行为进行综合分析，并开发了 FineServe 负载生成器，将细粒度的模型感知负载组合成可配置的混合负载，用于多模型服务平台的基准测试。

Result: 揭示了不同模型架构、规模和任务意图下 fundamentally 不同的波动模式；FineServe 数据集和生成器为评估路由、调度和容量规划策略提供了现实基础。

Conclusion: FineServe 为 LLM 服务系统的评估提供了真实的负载基础，弥补了现有负载研究的不足，有助于推动更高效的服务策略设计。

Abstract: Large language models (LLMs) are increasingly deployed as always-on online services, making efficient LLM serving a critical systems challenge. Achieving low latency and high throughput under volatile demand requires deep understanding of real-world serving workloads, yet existing studies often rely on proxy traces or coarse-grained characterizations that fail to capture the heterogeneity of modern multi-model LLM platforms. We present FineServe, an in-the-wild, multi-model LLM serving workload dataset collected from a global commercial marketplace, enabling fine-grained characterization of real-world serving dynamics across heterogeneous models and tasks. Leveraging FineServe, we conduct a comprehensive analysis of arrival dynamics and token behavior, revealing fundamentally different fluctuation regimes across model architectures, scales and task intents. Building on these insights, we develop the FineServe workload generator, which composes fine-grained model-aware workloads into configurable mixtures tailored for benchmarking multi-model serving platforms. By exposing these fine-grained workload dynamics, FineServe provides a realistic foundation for evaluating routing, scheduling, and capacity-planning strategies in LLM serving systems. FineServe is available at https://github.com/hihiztc1/FineServe.

</details>


### [119] [Hybrid LSTM-Graph Neural Framework for Robust Financial Fraud Detection and Adversarial Resilience](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.19350)
*Mariam Zakaria Moussa Ali*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 提出 FraudShield AI，一种融合 LSTM 时序建模与手工图拓扑特征（PageRank 中心性、入度动态、自定义流比率）的混合框架，通过焦点损失和动态阈值应对极端数据不平衡（0.13% 欺诈率）和低值 smurfing 攻击，在 PaySim 数据集上显著优于逻辑回归和 XGBoost。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 金融机构在检测洗钱模式（如 smurfing 和 layering）时面临两大挑战：一是极端数据不平衡（欺诈率仅 0.13%），二是对手不断进化规避策略。传统孤立交易分析难以捕捉复杂网络级洗钱行为。

Method: 提出 FraudShield AI 混合框架，核心包括：（1）LSTM 网络捕获交易序列的时间动态；（2）手工设计的图拓扑特征（PageRank 中心性、入度动态、自定义流比率）提取交易网络的结构关系；（3）焦点损失函数缓解类别不平衡；（4）动态阈值机制增强对低值 smurfing 攻击的鲁棒性。

Result: 在 PaySim 数据集上，混合模型在精确率、召回率和 F1 分数上显著优于逻辑回归和 XGBoost 基线，尤其在难以检测的微交易欺诈模式上表现突出。消融实验证实了时序与拓扑组分的互补贡献。

Conclusion: 将检测范式从孤立的交易分析转向网络级取证，结合时序与图拓扑特征能有效提升洗钱检测性能。焦点损失和动态阈值策略对极端不平衡和针对性攻击具有实际价值。

Abstract: Financial institutions face significant challenges in detecting sophisticated money laundering patterns, such as smurfing and layering, due to extreme data imbalance (0.13% fraud rate) and evolving adversarial evasion tactics. This paper proposes FraudShield AI, a hybrid framework that integrates Long Short-Term Memory (LSTM) networks with hand-crafted Graph Topological Features to capture both temporal sequences and structural relational context. By engineering network-centric features including PageRank Centrality, In-Degree dynamics, and a custom Flow Ratio, the system shifts the detection paradigm from isolated transaction analysis to network-level forensics. A Focal Loss objective is used to address class imbalance, and a dynamic thresholding mechanism is introduced to improve resilience against low-value smurfing attacks. Experimental evaluation on the PaySim dataset shows that the proposed hybrid model substantially outperforms Logistic Regression and XGBoost baselines in Precision, Recall, and F1-Score, particularly on hard-to-detect micro-transaction fraud patterns. An ablation study confirms the complementary contribution of both the temporal and topological components.

</details>


### [120] [OpenEvoShield: Dual Non-Stationary Continual Defense for Open-World Multi-Agent System Attacks](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.19351)
*Litian Zhang,Chaozhuo Li,Yuting Zhang,Zejian Chen,Bingyu Yan,Qiwei Ye*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 提出 OpenEvoShield，一种面向 LLM 多智能体系统的共进化持续防御框架，通过非对称速率控制、正常边界更新、EWC 正则化策略集成和能量基多粒度检测，在动态攻击和分布漂移下持续检测未知攻击，实验表明优于静态和持续基线方法。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: LLM 多智能体系统在安全关键应用中面临对手通过智能体间通信注入恶意指令的攻击，且攻击具有双重动态性（对手策略进化与正常行为漂移），现有防御将部署视为封闭世界问题，一旦分布漂移超出训练覆盖性能急剧下降。

Method: 提出 OpenEvoShield 共进化持续防御框架，包含四个模块：M1 非对称速率控制器，从双重漂移信号解耦快速攻击侧和慢速正常侧学习率；M2 正常边界更新器，以慢速维持动态行为边界；M3 EWC 正则化策略集成，快速适应且不遗忘；M4 能量基多粒度检测器，融合节点、子图和图级证据将新攻击分类为分布外样本。

Result: 在 5 个基准和 4 种 MAS 拓扑上进行了 100 轮部署实验，OpenEvoShield 优于静态和持续基线方法，能检测大部分未见过的攻击，同时保持低假阳性率。

Conclusion: OpenEvoShield 有效解决了 LLM 多智能体系统中的持续防御问题，能够应对动态攻击和分布漂移，为安全关键部署提供了可行方案。

Abstract: LLM-based multi-agent systems (LLM-MAS) are increasingly deployed in safety-critical applications, where adversaries inject malicious instructions through inter-agent communication to propagate harmful behaviors. Unlike static threats, these attacks are doubly dynamic: adversaries refine injection strategies against deployed defenses while normal-agent behavior drifts with system expansion. Existing defenses treat deployment as a closed-world problem and degrade rapidly once either distribution shifts beyond training coverage. We propose OpenEvoShield, a co-evolutionary continual defense framework for LLM-MAS. An asymmetric rate controller (M1) decouples fast attack-side and slow normal-side learning rates from dual drift signals. A normal-boundary updater (M2) maintains a dynamic behavioral boundary at the slow rate, while an EWC-regularized policy ensemble (M3) fast-adapts without catastrophic forgetting. An energy-based multi-granularity detector (M4) fuses node-, subgraph-, and graph-level evidence to classify novel attacks as out-of-distribution. Experiments over 100 deployment rounds across five benchmarks and four MAS topologies show that OpenEvoShield outperforms static and continual baselines, detecting most previously unseen attacks while keeping false positive rates low.

</details>


### [121] [Benchmarking Confidential GPU Inference on NVIDIA H100 under Intel TDX](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.19353)
*Wei Wang,Abdul Hyee Waqas,Burns Smith*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文对NVIDIA H100 GPU在Intel TDX机密实例上的大语言模型推理性能进行了基准测试，比较了标准执行与机密计算模式。实验使用Mistral-7B和Qwen3-30B-A3B两个模型，测量TTFT、端到端延迟、令牌吞吐量等指标。结果表明机密模式增加TTFT 21.8%-27.8%，降低全局吞吐量17.7%-21.1%，且大模型更早达到饱和拐点，但总体仍可保持可用的吞吐性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 机密计算正成为处理敏感输入或保护专有模型资产的AI推理工作负载的实际部署需求，但启用GPU加速大语言模型服务的机密执行所带来的性能代价因工作负载而异且对运维至关重要，因此需要量化评估其影响。

Method: 在单块NVIDIA H100 80GB GPU上，基于Intel TDX机密实例进行基准测试，对比标准非机密执行与机密计算模式。使用Mistral-7B v0.1和Qwen3-30B-A3B两个代表性模型，测量首令牌时间、端到端请求延迟、每请求令牌生成吞吐量、全局令牌吞吐量及递增并发下的闭环请求吞吐量。实验设计包含固定请求率实验和闭环并发实验。

Result: 固定请求率实验中，机密模式使Mistral-7B的平均TTFT增加21.8%，Qwen3-30B-A3B增加27.8%；全局令牌吞吐量分别下降17.7%和21.1%。闭环并发实验中，吞吐量差距保持在11.5%-20.2%范围，但大模型在机密模式下更早达到饱和拐点。

Conclusion: 机密GPU推理在负载下仍可保持可用的吞吐量，但容量规划必须同时考虑稳态吞吐量惩罚以及大模型更早出现饱和行为的影响。

Abstract: Confidential computing is becoming a practical deployment requirement for AI inference workloads that process sensitive inputs or protect proprietary model assets. However, the performance cost of enabling confidential execution for GPU-accelerated large language model serving remains workload dependent and operationally important. This paper presents a benchmark study comparing standard non-confidential execution with confidential computing mode on a single NVIDIA H100 80GB GPU hosted in an Intel TDX confidential instance. The evaluation uses two representative language models, Mistral-7B v0.1 and Qwen3-30B-A3B, and measures time to first token, end-to-end request latency, per-request token generation throughput, global token throughput, and closed-loop request throughput under increasing concurrency. In fixed request-rate experiments, confidential mode increases average TTFT by 21.8% for Mistral-7B and 27.8% for Qwen3-30B-A3B, while global token throughput drops by 17.7% and 21.1%, respectively. In closed-loop concurrency experiments, throughput gaps remain in the 11.5-20.2% range, but the larger model reaches its saturation knee earlier under confidential mode. The results suggest that confidential GPU inference can retain usable throughput under load, but capacity planning must account for both the steady throughput penalty and the earlier saturation behavior observed for larger models.

</details>


### [122] [FormulaSPIN: Self-Play Fine-Tuning for Natural Language to Spreadsheet Formula Generation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.19354)
*Cy Xie*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 提出 FORMULASPIN，一种无需额外数据的自博弈（self-play）框架，通过利用公式的可执行性作为隐式监督信号，突破监督微调的性能上限，在电子表格公式生成任务上达到 SOTA。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 电子表格被数亿人使用，但编写公式仍是巨大障碍。现有方法依赖静态监督数据，在有限标注上迅速饱和，难以进一步提升。

Method: 采用自博弈框架 SPIN，但针对公式生成任务做出关键改进：利用公式的二进制可执行性将语义错误与风格变体分离，避免 vanilla SPIN 中因执行等价输出被矛盾惩罚的问题。将训练构建为双人游戏，主玩家学习偏好当前版本生成的真值公式，同时执行反馈将输出按粒度排序，形成从语义正确性到风格优化的自适应课程。此外引入 ExecVote 语义级投票机制处理多有效公式。

Result: 在 NL2FORMULA 基准上达到 74.9% 精确匹配和 87.1% 执行准确率，与使用额外偏好标注训练的模型持平，且超越传统 SFT 和前沿闭源模型。

Conclusion: 展示了自博弈在数据稀缺任务中的潜力，并指出该方法可推广到可执行领域之外。

Abstract: Spreadsheet applications are used by hundreds of millions worldwide, yet writing formulas remains a significant barrier. Existing approaches rely on static supervised data, which quickly saturates on limited annotations. In this paper, we introduce FORMULASPIN, a self-play framework that breaks the ceiling of supervised fine-tuning by enabling iterative self-improvement without any additional data. Vanilla SPIN fails on this task: it uniformly penalizes every non-matching output, so execution-equivalent alternatives are punished as negatives in one example while serving as ground truth in another, producing contradictory gradients. Our framework resolves this by exploiting formula generation's unique advantage: binary executability provides implicit supervision that separates semantic errors from valid stylistic variants. We frame training as a two-player game in which the main player learns to prefer ground-truth formulas over those from its previous version, while execution feedback sorts outputs into distinct granularities-enabling an adaptive curriculum that shifts from semantic correctness to stylistic refinement. To further increase accuracy, we incorporate ExecVote, a semantic-level voting mechanism that naturally handles multiple valid formulations. Experiments on multiple benchmarks demonstrate that FORMULASPIN achieves state-of-the-art performance, with 74.9% exact match and 87.1% execution accuracy on NL2FORMULA, matching models trained with additional preference annotations while outperforming both traditional SFT and frontier proprietary models. These findings underscore self-play's potential to tackle scarce data tasks and open the door to extending it beyond executable domains.

</details>


### [123] [Information Discernment in Large Language Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.19355)
*Joshua Ashkinaze,Laura Kurek,Alina Faisal,Tongyuan Miao,Mariam Joseph,Ceren Budak,Eric Gilbert*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出 Learn2Discern (L2D) 框架，从 Source Discernment（来源辨别）和 Truth Discernment（真相辨别）两个维度评估 LLM 的信息辨别能力。通过 13 个模型、近 670K 次实验发现：模型在来源可靠性和真相更新上接近随机水平，过度依赖来源热度而非可靠性，且无论信息是否改善其先验都会同等更新。用户研究（n=299）验证了三个公理的有效性，违规会降低信任和使用意愿。模型复杂度的提升改善了 Truth Discernment 但并未提升 Source Discernment，但简单的推理时干预可同时改善两者。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: LLM 越来越多地使用外部知识源（如互联网），但模型是否能够适当地权衡信息——对可靠来源更新更多（Source Discernment），以及对更接近真相的主张更新更多（Truth Discernment）——尚不明确。现有研究缺乏对此类信息辨别能力的系统性评估框架和基准。

Method: 作者形式化了信息辨别（Information Discernment），提出基于三个规范性公理的实验框架 L2D，并设计了可解释的评估指标。通过预注册的配额匹配用户研究（n=299）验证公理的外部有效性。在 13 个模型上进行了近 670K 次试验，评估 Source Discernment 和 Truth Discernment。同时测试了推理时干预措施的效果。

Result: 模型在 Source Discernment 和 Truth Discernment 上均接近随机水平；对来源热度的依赖是可靠性的两倍；无论信息是否改进或恶化模型相对于真实答案的立场，更新幅度几乎相同。模型在已有先验最准确的数据集上最能有效整合外部知识。更大更新的模型提升了 Truth Discernment 但未改善 Source Discernment。简单的推理时干预可同时改进两种辨别能力。

Conclusion: LLM 在信息辨别方面存在系统性缺陷，Source Discernment 是模型复杂度无法解决的盲点。作者发布了数据集和调查作为测试平台，强调随着 LLM 取代传统搜索引擎，这一对齐属性将变得越来越重要。

Abstract: LLMs are increasingly used with external knowledge sources like the internet. Do they weigh information appropriately -- updating more for reliable sources (source discernment) and more when claims bring priors closer to the truth (truth discernment)? We formalize this as information discernment and introduce Learn2Discern (L2D), an experimental framework and benchmark grounded in three normative axioms with interpretable metrics. To establish external validity, a pre-registered, quota-matched user study (n=299) confirms that real LLM users endorse all three axioms and report that violations reduce their trust and usage intent. Across 13 models and nearly 670K trials, we find consistent failures across both dimensions: models perform near chance on source and truth discernment, rely on source popularity twice as much as source reliability, and update roughly equally whether a claim improves or worsens their position relative to the ground truth. Models integrate external knowledge most effectively on datasets where their priors are already the most accurate. Newer and larger models improve truth discernment but not source discernment, a blind spot that model complexity does not address. We identify simple inference-time interventions that improve both forms of discernment. We release our dataset and survey as a testbed for a core alignment property that scales in importance as LLMs replace traditional search.

</details>


### [124] [NEXUS: Structured Runtime Safety for Tool-Using LLM Agents](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.19356)
*Elias Hossain,Md Mehedi Hasan Nipu,Tasfia Nuzhat Ornee,Rajib Rana,Niloofar Yousefi*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: NEXUS 是一个针对工具使用 LLM 智能体的结构化计划安全监控器，通过形式化干预策略（允许、阻止、请求确认、请求修订）结合确定性规则、参数级检查和校准的逻辑回归风险评分来实现分级升级。在合成基准上 F1 达 0.949，干预准确率 0.6406，相比纯规则方法提升 27.3 个百分点；延迟仅为 0.205 ms，开销小于 0.1%。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 工具使用 LLM 智能体越来越多地执行高风险操作，运行时安全监控至关重要，现有方法缺乏细粒度的分级干预机制。

Method: NEXUS 包含三个组件：确定性安全规则、参数级检查以及校准的逻辑回归风险评分，根据风险等级在允许、阻止、请求确认、请求修订四种干预动作中路由选择。

Result: 在 128 实例合成基准上 F1=0.949，4 类干预准确率 0.6406，比纯规则高 27.3 个百分点；R-Judge 上 F1=0.861（规则 0.849）；IPI 上 0% ASR 且 99% 控制允许；NEXUS-Stress 上 F1=0.881；延迟 0.205 ms。

Conclusion: NEXUS 在低延迟下实现了有效的分级安全监控，但细粒度干预路由仍具挑战性；代码和基准已公开。

Abstract: Tool-using LLM agents increasingly execute high-impact actions, making runtime safety monitoring essential. We present NEXUS (Neural EXecution Utility and Safety), a structured-plan safety monitor that applies a formal intervention policy to select among four actions: allow, block, request confirmation, or request revision. NEXUS combines deterministic safety rules, argument-level inspection, and a calibrated logistic-regression risk score for graded escalation. On a 128-instance synthetic benchmark, NEXUS achieves an F1 score of 0.949 and a 4-class intervention accuracy of 0.6406, outperforming rule-only intervention selection by 27.3 percentage points. It also improves over rule-only on R-Judge (F1 = 0.861 vs. 0.849), matches rule-only on AgentHarm due to threat-model limits, and achieves 0% ASR at 99% control allow on IPI. On the rule-blind NEXUS-Stress benchmark, NEXUS reaches an F1 score of 0.881, highlighting the difficulty of fine-grained intervention routing. With 0.205 ms median latency, NEXUS adds under 0.1% overhead to typical agent loops. Code, benchmarks, and the calibrated risk scorer are publicly released.

</details>


### [125] [Stochastic Primal-Dual Decoding for Multiobjective Generative Recommender Systems](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.19357)
*Dmitrii Moor,Ben Carterette,Senthilkumar Krishnamoorthy,Kyle Kretschman,Denis Beslic,Melissa Yalla,Alice Y Wang,Mounia Lalmas*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 提出一种轻量级的推理时解码层，用于自回归生成式推荐系统，在不重新训练模型的情况下实现多目标Slate生成，通过在线约束优化和随机原始-对偶方案动态平衡相关性与辅助目标。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有推荐系统的多目标方法要么是后处理技术（不适用于生成式RS的序列性质），要么将辅助目标融入训练（在大规模系统中不实用）。需要一种能在推理时直接支持生成式RS多目标Slate生成的方法。

Method: 将Slate生成建模为在线约束优化问题：顺序选择物品，根据剩余约束松弛度动态调整相关性与辅助目标之间的权衡，通过随机原始-对偶近似方案实现。

Result: 提供了约束违反和遗憾的理论保证；离线实验和真实推荐系统的大规模在线A/B实验表明，多目标权衡持续改进，辅助目标提升+1.8%，用户满意度零损失。

Conclusion: 该方法在不修改基础模型的情况下，有效实现了生成式推荐系统中的多目标Slate生成，取得了更好的权衡效果。

Abstract: Recent advances in recommender systems (RS) have shown substantial performance gains through generative modelling. In practice, recommendation often involves constructing slates -- ordered lists of items -- that must satisfy multiple objectives beyond relevance, such as constraints defined over item attributes or fairness constraints. Existing multiobjective approaches either rely on post-processing techniques designed for non-generative settings, or incorporate auxiliary objectives directly into model training. The former does not explicitly account for the sequential nature of generative RS, while the latter is often impractical in large-scale systems.
  We propose a lightweight, inference-time decoding layer that augments autoregressive generative RS to support multiobjective slate generation without modifying or retraining the underlying model. We formulate decoding as an online constrained optimisation problem, where items are selected sequentially, and trade-offs between relevance and auxiliary objectives are adjusted dynamically based on the remaining constraint slack, i.e., how much of each objective remains to be satisfied. This is implemented via a stochastic primal-dual approximation scheme that balances relevance and auxiliary objectives during generation.
  We provide theoretical guarantees on constraint violation and regret, and evaluate the proposed approach through extensive offline experiments and a large-scale online A/B experiment in a real-world recommender system. Our results show consistent improvements in multiobjective trade-offs, including a +1.8\% gain in the auxiliary objectives achieved at zero cost to user satisfaction.

</details>


### [126] [LISA: Linear-Indexed Sparse Attention for Efficient Long-Context Reasoning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.19358)
*Yu Zhao,Zekun Zhang,Fan Jiang,Bo Zeng,Linlong Xu,Shimin Shan,Yu Liu,Longyue Wang,Weihua Luo*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: LISA（线性索引稀疏注意力）是一种即插即用的注意力替换模块，通过并行集成线性注意力（O(n)复杂度）和闪电索引器（选择Top-M重要token进行稀疏自注意力），将推理复杂度从O(n^2)降至O(nM)。采用两阶段训练：阶段1通过知识蒸馏用线性注意力+滑动窗口近似全注意力；阶段2引入索引器实现动态token选择。在DeepSeek-distilled-Qwen模型上，16K上下文下获得50%推理加速，并在AIME和MATH-500等推理基准上平均提升5.6%性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 长链思维推理模型（如DeepSeek-R1）在测试时扩展范式下推理上下文长度不断增长，但标准自注意力的O(n^2)计算复杂度导致长序列推理成本急剧上升，限制了长CoT推理在生产环境中的部署。

Method: 提出LISA模块，并行集成线性注意力（O(n)长期记忆）和闪电索引器（选择Top-M重要token用于稀疏自注意力），通过门控机制融合两分支。设计两阶段训练：阶段1通过知识蒸馏用线性注意力+滑动窗口近似教师模型的全注意力分布；阶段2引入索引器，利用每头KL散度损失对齐选择行为与教师注意力模式。

Result: 在DeepSeek-distilled-Qwen模型上，16K上下文下实现50%推理加速，在AIME和MATH-500等推理基准上平均性能提升5.6%。

Conclusion: LISA通过线性注意力与稀疏注意力的结合，显著降低了长序列推理的计算成本，同时保持了甚至提升了模型性能，为长CoT推理的实际部署提供了有效方案。

Abstract: Recent advances in long chain-of-thought reasoning models such as DeepSeek-R1 have led to increasingly longer inference context lengths under the test-time scaling paradigm. However, the O(n^2) computational complexity of standard self-attention causes inference costs to grow sharply with long sequences, limiting the deployment of long-CoT reasoning in production settings. To address this, we propose LISA (Linear-Indexed Sparse Attention), a plug-and-play attention replacement module that requires no pretraining from scratch. LISA integrates two lightweight components in parallel within the original model: (1) a Linear Attention module that provides long-range memory with O(n) time complexity; (2) a Lightning Indexer that selects the top-M important tokens from the full context to feed into a Sparse Self-Attention. The two branches are fused via a gating mechanism, reducing inference complexity from O(n^2) to O(nM) (M << n) for generating n tokens. We design a two-stage training pipeline: Stage 1 initializes the model by integrating the linear attention to capture long-range dependencies, complemented by a sliding-window attention mechanism that is optimized via knowledge distillation to approximate the full self-attention distribution of a frozen teacher model. In Stage 2, we further introduce the Indexer to replace the static sliding-window mechanism, enabling dynamic token selection from broader contexts. The Indexer is trained using a novel per-head KL divergence loss, which aligns its selection behavior with the attention patterns of the teacher model. Experiments on DeepSeek-distilled-Qwen models demonstrate that LISA achieves a 50% inference speedup under 16K-token context, while improving average performance by 5.6% on reasoning benchmarks including AIME and MATH-500.

</details>


### [127] [Profile-Graph Memory for LLM Agents: Implicit Cross-Entity Traversal through Narrative Profiles](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.19359)
*Shengtong Zhu*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出MemHop多跳记忆基准（1,000题，跳数1-5，覆盖10个社交网络场景）和ProGraph双层记忆架构（配置档扩展+压缩残差），实验表明ProGraph在MemHop上达80.1%（匹配全上下文参考），在LoCoMo上达78.4%（超越全上下文11.3百分点），优于现有方法。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有LLM记忆基准主要评估单跳回忆，缺乏多跳关联测试，为此需要构建多跳记忆基准并设计有效架构。

Method: ProGraph双层记忆架构：(i) 配置档扩展——基于子串匹配遍历实体名，无需显式知识图谱；(ii) 压缩残差——在配置档更新时零额外API代价共提取精确日期、数量和命名项。

Result: ProGraph在MemHop上平均80.1%（匹配全上下文参考），在LoCoMo上78.4%（超全上下文11.3pp），优于Mem0、A-Mem、HippoRAG和RAG。消融实验显示配置档扩展驱动多跳推理（移除后MemHop降22.6pp），压缩残差驱动精确召回（未共提取时LoCoMo降8.6pp），跨效应低于3pp。

Conclusion: 本文贡献了多跳记忆基准MemHop和高效记忆架构ProGraph，验证了组件专业化分工，并公开了数据和代码。

Abstract: Long-term memory is essential for LLM agents that interact across sessions, yet current memory benchmarks primarily evaluate single-hop recall, leaving multi-hop association largely unmeasured. We make three contributions. First, we introduce MemHop, a multi-hop memory benchmark of 1,000 questions at hop depths 1-5 across 10 social-network scenarios, with per-hop evidence annotations. Second, we present Profile-Graph Memory (ProGraph), a two-layer memory architecture combining (i) profile expansion -- substring-matched traversal of entity names that naturally appear in LLM-written profile narratives, a minimal alternative to explicit knowledge-graph construction -- and (ii) compression residuals -- exact dates, quantities, and named items co-extracted with each profile update at zero extra API cost. Third, a full-grid ablation shows cross-benchmark mechanism specialization: profile expansion drives multi-hop reasoning (-22.6pp on MemHop when removed) while compression residuals drive precision recall (-8.6pp on LoCoMo when not co-extracted), with cross-effects under 3pp within a single architecture. ProGraph averages 80.1% on MemHop (matching the FullContext reference) and 78.4% on LoCoMo (exceeding FullContext by 11.3pp), outperforming Mem0, A-Mem, HippoRAG, and RAG on both. We release MemHop, ProGraph, and baseline implementations.

</details>


### [128] [Lifted Representation Hypothesis in Language Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.19360)
*Bumjin Park,Jaesik Choi*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出“提升表征假说”（lifted representation hypothesis），认为大语言模型通过共享潜在结构而非孤立实例来更新记忆，并通过异常学习实验发现模型在嵌套规则和数据下易出现“打碎失败”（shattering failures），且“提升”（lifting）常过早发生，揭示了数据与规则结构关系研究的重要性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 大语言模型如何存储、选择和修订规则类结构尚不清楚，现有研究多关注实例级事实，本文旨在从对称性和结构共享的角度理解LLM的规则抽象与细化过程。

Method: 提出提升与打碎的概念框架，通过受控的异常学习实验，在上下文学习（ICL）、LoRA和全量微调三种范式下评估LLM的规则泛化与细化能力。

Result: LLM在嵌套规则与异常数据下容易出现打碎失败（无法将粗粒度结构细化为合适子类型），而提升过程往往过早发生（过快地泛化为一般规则）。

Conclusion: 需要深入研究数据形式与规则结构之间的关系，以更好地理解和控制LLM中的规则学习与记忆更新机制。

Abstract: Large language models (LLMs) often answer queries by mapping individual observations to more general rule-like structures. However, it remains unclear how these structures are stored, selected, and revised. To study this process, we propose thelifted representation hypothesis: LLMs update memory through shared latent structures rather than isolated instance-level facts. This view frames lifting as an efficient use of symmetry across instances, and shattering as the refinement of coarse lifted structures into more specific subtypes. We evaluate LLMs' lifting and shattering through controlled exception-learning experiments across in-context learning, LoRA, and full fine-tuning. We find that LLMs are vulnerable to shattering failures when data are governed by nested rules and exceptions, while lifting often occurs prematurely. These results highlight the need to study the relation between data and rule structures in LLMs.

</details>


### [129] [GraphContainer: A Unified Platform for Comparing and Debugging Graph RAG Methods](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.19362)
*Seonho An,Chaejeong Hyun,Min-Soo Kim*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: GraphContainer是一个统一和可视化多种图RAG工作流的平台，通过统一图表示层（UGR）标准化多格式图，以及Graph Recorder记录并可视化逐步检索过程，支持交互式调试和对比分析，降低研究者设计最优图RAG管线的门槛。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有图RAG方法高度碎片化且互不兼容，不同框架的图格式存在结构异构性，同时缺乏细粒度的可视化工具，使得评估和比较不同检索行为极为困难。

Method: 提出GraphContainer平台，包含两个核心组件：(1) 统一图表示（UGR）层，无缝标准化多格式图；(2) Graph Recorder，跟踪并以可视化方式渲染逐步检索过程。通过交互式Web界面实现异构图的导入、实时可追踪的可视化调试及图RAG方法的对比。

Result: 成功展示了GraphContainer导入异构图、进行实时可追踪可视化调试的能力，并实现了对多种图格式和检索策略的受控对比，降低了研究者设计最优图RAG管线的门槛。

Conclusion: GraphContainer通过统一标准化的图表示和可视化记录工具，有效弥合了图RAG领域的碎片化问题，为研究者和从业者提供了评估与优化图RAG管线的低门槛平台。

Abstract: Graph RAG mitigates hallucinations and stale knowledge in LLMs, particularly for multi-hop question answering. However, existing approaches remain highly fragmented and incompatible. The structural heterogeneity of graph formats across different frameworks and the lack of granular visualization tools make it exceedingly difficult to evaluate and compare retrieval behaviors. To bridge this gap, we propose GraphContainer, a novel platform designed to unify and visualize diverse graph RAG workflows. GraphContainer features two key components: (1) a Unified Graph Representation (UGR) layer that seamlessly standardizes multi-format graphs, and (2) a Graph Recorder that tracks and visually renders the step-by-step retrieval process. Through an interactive web interface, we demonstrate GraphContainer's ability to import heterogeneous graphs and perform live, traceable visual debugging of graph RAG methods. Ultimately, we show how GraphContainer enables controlled comparisons of various graph formats and retrieval strategies, lowering the barrier for researchers and practitioners to design optimal graph RAG pipelines. A demonstration video is available at https://youtu.be/O02eNJLwkU0.

</details>


### [130] [AdaRoPE: Not All Attention Heads Should Rotate and Scale Equally](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.19363)
*Shaowen Wang,Yuke Zheng,Tansheng Zhu,Shuang Chen,Shaofan Liu,Suncong Zheng,Jian Li*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 论文提出 AdaRoPE，一种为每个注意力头分配可学习的旋转频率和注意力缩放因子的位置编码方法，以替代标准 RoPE 的统一频率调度。实验表明，不同功能的注意力头需要不同的频率范围和缩放因子，AdaRoPE 在预训练和上下文扩展中均优于现有 RoPE 变体，能更好地平衡长上下文性能与短上下文保持。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 标准 RoPE 对所有注意力头使用统一的频率调度和缩放，忽略了不同功能头部需要不同频率范围和缩放因子的需求，导致嵌入维度利用不足和性能下降，尤其在长上下文场景中。

Method: 提出 AdaRoPE，为每个注意力头引入可学习的旋转频率和注意力缩放因子，通过端到端训练自适应地调整每个头的位置编码参数。

Result: 在预训练 LLM 中，AdaRoPE 持续优于部分 RoPE 和无位置编码基线；在上下文扩展中，优于 YaRN 等均匀缩放方法，能更好地扩展上下文同时保持短上下文性能，包括外推和长上下文继续预训练设置。

Conclusion: 优化旋转位置编码需要在单个注意力头级别进行，AdaRoPE 验证了头部特定频率和缩放的重要性，为位置编码设计提供了新方向。

Abstract: Rotary Position Embedding (RoPE) is widely adopted in Transformers to encode positional information, yet standard implementations enforce a uniform frequency schedule and scaling across all attention heads. Using simplified retrieval tasks and length generalization scenarios, we show -- both empirically and theoretically -- that heads with different functional roles require distinct frequency ranges and attention scaling factors to operate effectively. Ignoring this structure leads to suboptimal utilization of embedding dimensions and degraded performance, particularly under long-context settings. To address these limitations, we propose AdaRoPE, which equips each attention head with learnable rotation frequencies and attention scaling factors. Pretrained LLMs with AdaRoPE consistently outperform existing RoPE variants, including partial RoPE and NoPE baselines. For context extension, we further show that uniform frequency and attention scaling, used in methods such as YaRN, are suboptimal. By applying head-specific scaling, AdaRoPE enables better context extension while better preserving short-context performance in both the extrapolation setting and the long-context continued pretraining setting. These results highlight the importance of optimizing rotary position embedding at the level of individual attention heads.

</details>


### [131] [Statistically Grounded Sparse-Feature Interventions for Activation-Space Control in Large Language Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.19364)
*Oshayer Siddique,J. M Areeb Uzair Alam,Md Jobayer Rahman Rafy,Syed Rifat Raiyan,Hasan Mahmud,Md Kamrul Hasan*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种基于稀疏自编码器（SAE）特征的透明化激活引导（activation steering）流程：先通过六条件可靠性过滤器筛选特征，再使用无权重Borda共识对F检验、KSG互信息和Cohen's d三个互补统计量进行排名，最终以Cohen's d加权的SAE解码行向量构建引导方向。在Gemma系列三个模型、四个行为域和356种层-强度配置下，该方法产生了可测量的领域特定偏移，但同时也暴露出原始属性移动与质量保持生成之间的显著差距。最强配置下逻辑正确性引导在主评分上达到+1.16的提升，但可用引导高度依赖于模型、领域、层和强度。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 激活引导是一种轻量级替代微调的方法，但基于SAE的引导方法通常依赖于学习得到的引导目标或单一准则的特征选择，缺乏透明性和鲁棒性。本文旨在开发一种无需优化、透明且可解释的SAE特征引导流程，并系统评估其在不同条件下的表现与局限。

Method: 提出透明SAE特征引导流程：(1) 六条件可靠性过滤器对稀疏特征进行筛选；(2) 使用无权重Borda共识综合F检验、KSG互信息和Cohen's d三个互补统计量对特征排序；(3) 以Cohen's d加权组合SAE解码行向量构建引导方向，该方向受Fisher线性判别分析启发，在SAE特征近似解相关条件下无需优化。实验涵盖Gemma 2B/7B/9B三个模型、四个行为域（诚实、有毒、逻辑正确性、创造力）、356种层-强度配置。

Result: 该方法在多个配置下产生可测量的领域特定偏移，但原始属性移动与质量保持生成之间存在显著差距。最强配置（Gemma 2 9B逻辑正确性引导）主评分提升+1.16。可用引导高度本地化，强烈依赖于模型、领域、层和强度。

Conclusion: 激活引导评估应同时报告原始行为偏移和质量条件化的成功度量。该方法流程透明、无需优化，但实际应用需谨慎选择层和强度，因为引导效果高度局部化。

Abstract: Activation steering offers a lightweight alternative to fine-tuning for behavioral control of large language models, but SAE-based steering methods often rely on learned steering objectives or single-criterion feature selection. We introduce a transparent SAE-feature steering pipeline that first applies a six-condition reliability filter, then ranks sparse features through an unweighted Borda consensus over three complementary statistics: $F$-test, KSG mutual information, and Cohen's $d$. The resulting steering direction is constructed as a Cohen's-$d$-weighted combination of SAE decoder rows, providing an optimization-free direction motivated by Fisher-LDA under approximate SAE-feature decorrelation. Across three Gemma-family models, four behavioral domains, and 356 layer-strength configurations, the method produces measurable domain-specific shifts while revealing a substantial gap between raw attribute movement and quality-preserving generation. In the strongest configuration, logical-correctness steering reaches a primary-score delta of $+1.16$ in Gemma~2 9B; however, our broader finding is that usable steering is highly localized by model, domain, layer, and strength. These results argue that activation-steering evaluations should report quality-conditioned success alongside raw behavioral shift. Our code and data are available at https://github.com/Oshayer-Siddique/LLM-Steering-Using-SAE.

</details>


### [132] [Logic-Guided Data Extraction with Answer Set Programming and Large Language Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.19365)
*Mario Alviano,Lorenzo Grillo,Nicola Leone,Fabrizio Lo Scudo*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 提出了一种结合大语言模型（LLM）与回答集编程（ASP）的逻辑驱动数据抽取框架。LLM负责从非结构化文本中提取候选事实，ASP负责验证、推理、一致性检查和过程控制。通过交错执行LLM调用与ASP推理，该框架能推导逻辑隐含事实、提前检测不一致，并显著减少LLM调用次数，同时保持与基线方法等效的抽取质量。实验表明该方法减少了LLM调用并提升了抽取质量。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: LLM在语义数据抽取任务中虽然能从自然语言生成候选关系事实，但在需要复杂组合推理和全局一致性的任务上仍不可靠。现有流水线方法对每个目标谓词独立查询LLM，缺乏逻辑全局视角，效率低且易产生不一致结果。

Method: 提出逻辑驱动数据抽取框架，将LLM的候选事实抽取与ASP的验证、推理、一致性检查和控制相结合。核心创新在于：1) 利用ASP推理确定每个阶段哪些谓词在逻辑上可接受，并引导抽取查询；2) 交错执行LLM调用与ASP推导，逻辑隐含事实无需额外抽取，不一致性能被提前检测；3) 引入基于逻辑控制查询的缓存机制，利用增量构建事实集上合取查询的单调性来减少求解器调用。

Result: 在ASP构建的基准测试上，该框架相比基线方法减少了LLM调用次数，并通过抑制虚假输出提升了抽取质量。证明了非单调逻辑编程在受控语义抽取中的价值。

Conclusion: 通过形式化证明该流水线与基线方法在最终抽取事实上等价（在温和假设下），同时需要更少的LLM调用。实验验证了框架的有效性，表明结合非单调逻辑编程可以有效提升LLM在语义抽取任务中的可靠性和效率。

Abstract: When Large Language Models (LLMs) are used for semantic data extraction from unstructured text, producing candidate relational facts from natural language, they may remain unreliable for tasks requiring complex combinatorial reasoning and global consistency. This paper proposes a logic-guided data extraction framework combining LLM-based extraction with Answer Set Programming (ASP). The LLM produces candidate facts, whereas ASP performs validation, inference, consistency checking, and control. Unlike existing pipelines that query the LLM independently for all target predicates, the proposed approach uses ASP reasoning to identify which predicates are logically admissible at each stage and to guide extraction queries. By interleaving LLM calls with ASP derivation, the framework infers logically implied facts without further extraction and detects inconsistencies early. We formalize the pipeline and prove that, under mild assumptions, it is equivalent to the baseline approach with respect to the final extracted facts, while requiring fewer LLM calls. We also introduce a caching mechanism for logic-based control queries, exploiting monotonicity of conjunctive queries over incrementally constructed fact sets to reduce solver invocations. Experiments on ASP-derived benchmarks show that the framework reduces LLM calls and improves extraction quality by mitigating spurious outputs, demonstrating the value of non-monotonic logic programming for controlled semantic extraction.

</details>


### [133] [Geometry-Guided Constraint Learning for LLM Safety Classification](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.19366)
*Fumiaki Uehara,Koo Imai,Masato Tsutsumi,Keigo Kansa,Sora Usui,Yuki Kobiyama*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出基于稀疏自编码器（SAE）特征提取的安全多面体（SaP）方法，将每个类别的约束平面数降至2个（K=2），在BeaverTails分类基准上达到96-99%的准确率，无需繁琐的K值扫描。进一步引入锥约束（cone constraint），其可学习孔径适应各类别的聚类集中度，并通过三阶段训练稳定优化。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 原始SaP方法需要针对每个类别手动调整约束平面数量K，耗时且缺乏泛化性。本文旨在消除这一调优负担，并利用SAE特征提升安全边界的几何可解释性。

Method: 1) 使用稀疏自编码器（SAE）提取LLM隐藏层特征，将SaP的约束平面数统一设为K=2；2) 引入锥约束，其孔径（aperture）作为可学习参数，自适应各类别的聚类集中度；3) 设计三阶段训练流程以稳定锥约束的优化。

Result: 在Qwen3.5-9B模型的14个类别中，12个类别的最优K=2，在BeaverTails基准上每类准确率达96-99%，完全避免了原先K=4-25的随机初始化扫描。

Conclusion: K=2的收敛性支持线性表征假说（Linear Representation Hypothesis），表明安全边界在SAE特征空间中具有低维线性描述。锥约束的引入进一步增强了几何适应性，为LLM安全对齐提供了可解释的轻量级方法。

Abstract: Safety as Polytope (SaP) learns linear half-space constraints in LLM hidden space but requires per-category tuning of the constraint count K. We show that sparse autoencoder (SAE) feature extraction resolves this: K=2 becomes optimal for 12/14 categories on Qwen3.5-9B, achieving 96-99% accuracy per category on our BeaverTails classification benchmark, largely eliminating the need for exhaustive sweeps (K=4-25 with random initialization). This convergence to two planes is consistent with the Linear Representation Hypothesis, providing suggestive evidence that safety boundaries in this setting admit a low-dimensional linear description in the SAE feature space. Building on this geometric perspective, we introduce a cone constraint whose learnable aperture adapts to each category's cluster concentration, stabilized by a three-phase training

</details>


### [134] [Rethinking Uncertainty Evaluation in Large Language Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.19367)
*Krish Matta,Atharv Naphade,Andy Zou*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文指出，校准（calibration）作为评估LLM置信度的主要标准并不充分，因为它允许琐碎的不一致估计、依赖于评估分布，且无法检验估计值是否可解释为一致的潜在概率函数。作者提出LLM置信度估计应满足连贯概率信念的条件，沿着三个轴（结构连贯性、忠实性和有用性）形式化这些条件，并操作化为C1指标。实验发现广泛使用的估计器尽管看似校准良好，却系统性地违反这些条件：模型有31%的时间对逻辑上更简单的问题赋予更低的置信度，降低RMSCE的常见干预措施对结构违反无影响，表明校准与概率有效性正交。RLHF和思维链改善了有用性但未恢复连贯性。结论是当前LLM置信度估计不能解释为连贯概率；该框架提供了测量和缩小这一差距的工具。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 校准是评估LLM置信度的主要标准，但不足以确保置信度估计具有连贯概率信念的属性：校准允许不一致的估计、依赖于评估分布，且不测试估计值是否可解释为一致的潜在概率函数。因此需要更严格的评估框架。

Method: 作者形式化了连贯概率信念所需的条件，沿三个轴定义：结构连贯性（structural coherence）、忠实性（faithfulness）和有用性（usefulness），并操作化为C1指标。通过实验评估广泛使用的置信度估计器，比较校准与C1指标的表现，并分析常见干预（如RLHF、思维链）的影响。

Result: 广泛使用的估计器系统性地违反C1条件，尽管它们表现得校准良好。模型有31%的时间对逻辑上更简单的问题赋予更低的置信度。降低RMSCE的常见干预措施对结构违反无变化，表明校准与概率有效性正交。RLHF和思维链改善了有用性但未恢复连贯性。

Conclusion: 当前LLM置信度估计不能解释为连贯概率；所提出的C1框架提供了评估和改善这一差距的工具，强调了超越传统校准指标的必要性。

Abstract: Calibration is the primary criterion for evaluating LLM confidence, but it is insufficient: it admits trivially incoherent estimators, depends on the evaluation distribution, and does not test the extent to which the estimation can be interpreted as a consistent, underlying probability function. What we actually need is for LLM confidence estimates to satisfy the conditions required of coherent probabilistic beliefs.
