### [168] [Train the Model, Not the Reader: Decodability Supervision for Verifiable Activation Explanations](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.20379)
*Hiskias Dingeto*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文揭示自然语言自编码器通过重构评分解释的方法存在根本缺陷：重构分数对具体虚假声明不敏感，模型可通过“大意匹配”或“共适应私有编码”两种方式通过测试而实际不忠实。作者提出两项审计协议（真实vs基础交叉检验、评估器交换）和RECAP方法（协同训练线性预测头保持指定内容可解码）。实验表明RECAP能使新解释器真实陈述指定内容、消除私有编码，并在对抗攻击下保持高检测率（AUC 0.95），证明高重构分数不能认证单个声明的真实性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有可解释性方法使用自然语言自编码器通过重构分数评估解释的忠实性，但该测试结构上对单个虚假声明不敏感：翻转一个声明若不改变重构结果则不会受罚。作者发现两种不忠实的通过方式（大意匹配和共适应私有编码），表明重构分数不能认证具体声明的真实性，需要更可靠的审计方法。

Method: 1) 分析自然语言自编码器的结构缺陷，证明重构分数对具体声明不敏感。2) 提出两项审计协议：真实vs基础交叉检验（grounded-vs-true cross）和评估器交换（evaluator swap）。3) 提出RECAP（Readable Encodings via Co-trained Auxiliary Predictors）：在目标模型旁训练线性预测头，保持指定内容可解码，使新解释器能真实陈述指定内容并消除私有编码。

Result: 在Qwen-2.5-7B上，解释重构远高于随机但仅~2%的具体声明是重构依赖的，说明分数追踪大意而非具体事实。在合成真实数据下，标准配方5/5出现共适应私有代码。RECAP在沙盒模型上以+0.001 nat代价使新解释器真实陈述指定内容、代码消失。在Pythia-160M上，指定内容变得可探测，但新解释器仅部分传达（真实度0.44-0.46 vs近零控制）。对抗分析中，RECAP探针AUC 0.95而控制探针降至0.51。

Conclusion: 高重构分数不能认证单个声明的真实性，现有解释评估方法不可靠。RECAP使指定内部内容可独立探测检验，提供更可靠的审计方式，对AI安全有重要意义：使模型无法通过编造解释来欺骗评估，探测能有效识别虚假声明。

Abstract: Natural-language autoencoders score explanations of hidden activations by reconstruction: an explanation is deemed faithful if the activation can be regenerated from it. The test is structurally insensitive to individual false claims: if flipping a claim does not change the reconstruction, the claim is never penalized. We show the test is passed in two ways, neither faithful. On a released Qwen-2.5-7B verbalizer, explanations reconstruct well above chance while ~2% of specific claims are reconstruction-dependent, so the score tracks gist, not specific facts. Under exact synthetic ground truth, the standard recipe develops co-adapted private codes (false wording the reconstruction depends on) in 5/5 runs, and fixes that leave the target model unchanged do not help. We contribute two audit protocols, the grounded-vs-true cross and the evaluator swap, and RECAP (Readable Encodings via Co-trained Auxiliary Predictors): linear heads trained alongside the target model to keep designated content decodable. On RECAP-trained sandbox models, fresh verbalizers state the designated content truly and the codes vanish, at a +0.001-nat cost. This replicates on a pretrained Pythia-160M: the content becomes reliably probe-decodable, though a fresh verbalizer conveys it only in part (truth 0.44-0.46 vs a near-zero control). For interpretability, high reconstruction does not certify individual claims. For AI safety, RECAP makes designated internal content independently checkable against probes rather than asserted by prose a model can game: an independent probe scores the verbalizer's true claims above its false ones (AUC 0.96, vs 0.82 without RECAP). Against an adversary that edits an explanation to maximize the reconstruction score while lying (suppressing ~87% of its lie penalty), the RECAP probe still flags the lies (AUC 0.95) while the control probe collapses to chance (0.51).

</details>


### [169] [SoftReason: A Fully Differentiable Neuro-Soft-Symbolic Deductive Reasoning Architecture over High-Dimensional Perceptual Data](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.20402)
*Wael AbdAlmageed*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: SoftReason is a neuro-soft-symbolic architecture that enables differentiable deductive reasoning over latent perceptual facts and knowledge-provided predicates, removing the gradient gap between perception and deduction by representing the deductive state as a local soft interpretation tensor and using a learned differentiable lift of the immediate-consequence operator. It is instantiated on Knowledge-aware Visual Question Answering (KVQA) to support end-to-end perceptual grounding, KG evidence injection, and differentiable deductive closure.


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: Classical neuro-symbolic pipelines have a discrete interface between perception and deduction, creating a gradient gap that prevents end-to-end differentiable learning. In many reasoning problems, premises must be inferred from high-dimensional inputs rather than observed as discrete symbols, and predicate vocabulary, argument structure, and trusted evidence come from Knowledge Graphs or rule definitions, requiring a unified differentiable framework.

Method: SoftReason represents the deductive state as a local soft interpretation tensor over candidate constants and predicates. Perception proposes probabilistic base facts, KG triples enter as high-confidence soft evidence, and every query anchor, predicate choice, and closure update remains differentiable. The core innovation is a learned differentiable lift of the immediate-consequence operator, which uses predicate-definition embeddings and latent composition channels to form soft body-predicate mixtures, aggregate over all possible witnesses, propose query-conditioned head facts, and update the interpretation through a monotone probabilistic OR.

Result: The framework is instantiated on Knowledge-aware Visual Question Answering (KVQA), demonstrating how SoftReason supports end-to-end perceptual grounding, KG evidence injection, and differentiable deductive closure in one trainable architecture.

Conclusion: SoftReason successfully bridges the gradient gap between perception and deduction by introducing a fully differentiable neuro-soft-symbolic architecture, enabling end-to-end learning for reasoning tasks that combine high-dimensional perceptual inputs with knowledge graph predicates and rule-based deduction.

Abstract: In many reasoning problems, the premises are not observed as discrete symbols, but must be inferred from high-dimensional inputs. Further, the predicate vocabulary, argument structure, and trusted evidence are supplied by a Knowledge Graph (KG), or rule definitions. Classical neuro-symbolic pipelines have a discrete interface between perception and deduction. We present a neuro-soft-symbolic architecture for differentiable deductive reasoning over latent perceptual facts and knowledge-provided predicates. SoftReason removes the gradient gap by representing the deductive state as a local soft interpretation tensor over candidate constants and predicates. Perception proposes probabilistic base facts, KG triples enter as high-confidence soft evidence, and every query anchor, predicate choice, and closure update remains differentiable. Our core innovation is a learned differentiable lift of the immediate-consequence operator. It uses predicate-definition embeddings and latent composition channels to form soft body-predicate mixtures, aggregate over all possible witnesses, propose query-conditioned head facts, and update the interpretation through a monotone probabilistic OR. We instantiate the framework on Knowledge-aware Visual Question Answering (KVQA), and demonstrates how SoftReason supports end-to-end perceptual grounding, KG evidence injection, and differentiable deductive closure in one trainable architecture.

</details>


<div id='cs.LG'></div>

# cs.LG [[Back]](#toc)

### [170] [Native Multi-Dimensional Subquadratic Operators via Input Dependent Long Convolutions](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.19378)
*David R. Wessels,Farhad Ramezanghorbani,David W. Romero,Alireza Moradzadeh,Olivia Viessmann,Maksim Zhdanov,John St. John,Ken Janik,David M Knigge,Yucheng Tang,Erik J Bekkers,Saee Gopal Paliwal*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: HyenaND is a subquadratic, global, input-dependent operator for multidimensional data that uses implicitly parametrized convolutional kernels, achieving wall-clock speedups via a fused FFT-convolution CUDA implementation, and matches or exceeds attention baselines across various domains.


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: Subquadratic alternatives to attention (e.g., standard convolutions, recurrent models) struggle with multidimensional data: convolutions lack global receptive fields and input dependency, while recurrent models require rasterizing data into a 1D scan order that violates spatial structure.

Method: HyenaND operates directly on the native geometry of multidimensional data through convolutions with implicitly parametrized global, input-dependent multi-dimensional convolutional kernels. Its CUDA implementation (nSubQ) fuses the FFT-convolution path to achieve O(L log L) scaling with wall-clock speedups.

Result: Across long-context genomics, computer vision, medical imaging, and PDE modeling, pure HyenaND stacks match the accuracy of strong attention baselines, while hybrid configurations interleaving HyenaND and attention layers outperform both pure attention and strong recurrence-based hybrids.

Conclusion: HyenaND provides a subquadratic, global, input-dependent alternative to attention that respects the native geometry of multidimensional data, achieving competitive or superior accuracy with practical speedups.

Abstract: Subquadratic alternatives to attention require compromises when applied to multi-dimensional data: standard convolutions lack global receptive fields and input dependency, while recurrent models require rasterizing data such as images, volumes, and partial differential equation (PDE) into an ad-hoc $1\rm D$ scan order that violates their spatial structure. We introduce \textit{HyenaND}, a subquadratic, global, input-dependent operator that acts directly on the native geometry of multidimensional data through convolutions with implicitly parametrized global, input-dependent multi-dimensional convolutional kernels. Our CUDA implementation, \texttt{nSubQ}, fuses the FFT-convolution path to turn HyenaND's $\mathcal{O}(L \log L)$ scaling into wall-clock speedups. Across long-context genomics, computer vision, medical imaging, and PDE modeling, pure HyenaND stacks match the accuracy of strong attention baselines, while hybrid configurations that interleave HyenaND and attention layers outperform both pure attention and strong recurrence-based hybrids.

</details>


### [171] [Bayesian Wind Tunnels for Model Selection](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.19379)
*Siddhartha R Dalal,Vishal Misra,Abhay Parekh*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文研究Transformer能否进行贝叶斯模型选择（即从数据中识别正确的假设类别），而不仅限于在固定假设类别内进行贝叶斯滤波。作者构建了可控的"贝叶斯风洞"环境，其中后验概率有闭式解。通过使用不动点自由对合（f(f(x))=x）等纯关系结构，一个2.8M参数的Transformer在整数token和每次情节意义变化的透明符号上均达到与贝叶斯最优解0.01比特的熵一致。对于非嵌套比较（如对合vs三循环），类别后验MAE低于0.001，展示了真正的模型选择能力。然而，当判别统计量需要算术运算（模加法旋转或模乘法）时，模型选择在整数token上成功，但在透明符号上完全失败，且该边界在112倍缩放（2.8M至316M参数）下依然存在。对照实验表明，固定重标定的透明符号可以成功，说明稳定语义而非整数身份使电路编译成为可能。头部子任务诊断将失败定位到头部反转与算术的组合而非头部解析本身。在前沿大语言模型上的探测显示定性贝叶斯行为但存在较大的校准差距（约55倍）。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 先前工作表明Transformer可以在固定假设类别内进行精确贝叶斯滤波，但能否进行贝叶斯模型选择（即从数据中识别正确的假设类别）尚不清楚。本文旨在探究Transformer是否具备这种能力，并理解其边界条件。

Method: 作者构建了"模型选择贝叶斯风洞"，其中假设类别的真实后验概率有闭式解。使用不动点自由对合和三循环等代数结构作为测试任务。通过改变token类型（整数vs透明符号）、任务性质（纯关系vs需要算术运算）以及参数规模（2.8M至316M），系统性地测试Transformer的模型选择能力。同时进行头部子任务诊断和前沿大语言模型探测。

Result: 2.8M参数Transformer在纯关系任务上达到与贝叶斯最优解0.01比特熵一致，非嵌套比较MAE低于0.001。但在需要算术运算的任务上，整数token成功而透明符号完全失败，且该差异在112倍参数缩放后依然存在。固定重标定的透明符号可以成功，表明稳定语义是关键。前沿LLM显示定性贝叶斯行为但校准差距约55倍。

Conclusion: Transformer能够进行真正的贝叶斯模型选择，但严重依赖于感知访问条件：当判别统计量需要算术运算时，模型需要稳定语义（如整数）才能成功编译电路，而透明符号（每次情节意义变化）会导致失败。这一边界具有鲁棒性，不随规模扩大而消失。稳定语义是模型进行算术推理的关键前提。

Abstract: Prior work has shown that transformers can perform exact Bayesian filtering within a fixed
  hypothesis class. Can they also perform Bayesian model selection -- identifying the correct
  hypothesis class from data? We introduce model-selection Bayesian wind tunnels: controlled
  environments where ground-truth posteriors over hypothesis classes are available in closed
  form. Using fixed-point-free involutions -- whose defining property f(f(x))=x is purely
  relational -- a 2.8M-parameter transformer achieves 0.01-bit entropy agreement with the
  Bayesian optimum (3 seeds), with both integer tokens and opaque symbols whose meanings
  change every episode. This extends to non-nested comparisons: involutions vs. 3-cycles
  (where neither class is a subset of the other) achieve class-posterior MAE under 0.001,
  demonstrating genuine model selection beyond simplicity/subset bias. We then identify a
  sharp perceptual access condition: when the discriminative statistic requires arithmetic --
  modular addition (rotations) or multiplication (f(x)=cx mod p) -- model selection succeeds
  with integer tokens but fails completely with opaque symbols, and this boundary persists
  under 112x scaling (2.8M to 316M parameters). A stationarity control confirms the operative
  factor: opaque tokens with a fixed relabeling succeed (0.009-bit MAE), showing that stable
  semantics, not integer identity, enable circuit compilation. Header subtask diagnostics
  localize the failure to the composition of header inversion with arithmetic rather than
  header parsing itself. Probing frontier LLMs on the same tasks shows qualitative Bayesian
  behavior but a large calibration gap (~55x), measured through lossy probes and therefore
  directional rather than exact.

</details>


### [172] [CruiseBench: A Real-Flight-Aligned N-CMAPSS Benchmark for Engine RUL Prediction](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.19380)
*Pu Cheng,Qiang Miao*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出 CruiseBench，一个基于 N-CMAPSS 提取巡航阶段数据的 RUL 预测基准，并附带 CPM-N-CMAPSS 掩码工具，通过固定协议和消融实验验证了巡航阶段选择、时间降采样方法和 RUL 上限阈值对结果的影响，其中 TSMixer 表现最优。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: N-CMAPSS 虽然通过真实飞行剖面和完整航段时间序列增加了 RUL 预测的 realism，但也带来了数据量大、退化信号与运行工况变化纠缠等难题，导致预处理选择和模型性能比较困难。为建立一个可控、可复现的 RUL 模型比较基准，作者提出 CruiseBench，聚焦巡航阶段以降低混杂因素。

Method: 从 N-CMAPSS 的 9 个可用子数据集中，利用所提 CPM-N-CMAPSS 掩码（基于 common-altitude 方法识别巡航区间）提取巡航阶段数据；采用固定协议：使用场景描述符和实测传感器作为输入，排除虚拟传感器、健康参数和辅助元数据，保持原始分辨率窗口，按数据集设置 RUL 上限。在 LSTM、GRU、TCN、TSMixer 上运行实验，并进行消融研究。

Result: 在 CruiseBench-eta5-W256-S10 设置下，TSMixer 取得最低平均 RMSE（3.4±1.71）和 Saxena 得分（(2.50±2.99)×10⁴）。消融实验表明飞行阶段选择、时间降采样方法和 RUL 上限阈值会显著影响结果。

Conclusion: CruiseBench 提供了一个可复现的巡航阶段子基准，用于受控的 RUL 模型比较；CPM-N-CMAPSS 为未来的迁移学习和领域自适应研究提供了阶段特定的数据基础。

Abstract: Remaining useful life (RUL) prediction estimates how long an engine can continue safe operation and is central to maintenance planning. N-CMAPSS extends C-MAPSS by simulating run-to-failure aero-engine trajectories using recorded real-flight profiles and retaining complete within-flight time series rather than cycle-level snapshots. However, this added realism reduces evaluation control because full-flight records increase data volume and entangle degradation cues with operating-regime variation, complicating preprocessing choices and direct comparisons of RUL modeling performance. To mitigate this issue, this paper proposes CruiseBench, a cruise-stage RUL benchmark derived from N-CMAPSS. It introduces CPM-N-CMAPSS (Cruising-Period Mask for N-CMAPSS), a mask artifact that stores cycle-local cruising intervals identified by the common-altitude method for the nine accessible subdatasets. CruiseBench applies a fixed protocol to the masked rows, using scenario descriptors and measured sensors as inputs while excluding virtual sensors, health parameters, and auxiliary metadata from the feature tensor, preserving native-resolution windows, and applying dataset-wise RUL caps. Experiments with LSTM, GRU, TCN, and TSMixer provide baseline results for this setting. Under CruiseBench-eta5-W256-S10, TSMixer obtains the lowest average RMSE, $3.4\pm1.71$, and Saxena score, $(2.50\pm2.99)\times 10^{4}$. Ablation studies show that flight-stage selection, temporal downscaling method, and RUL-cap threshold affect reported results. With its fixed cruise-stage protocol, CruiseBench provides a reproducible sub-benchmark for controlled RUL model comparison and CPM-N-CMAPSS provides a stage-specific data foundation for future transfer-learning and domain-adaptation studies.

</details>


### [173] [Air Quality Arena: A Large-Scale Multi-Region Ground Monitoring Dataset and Benchmark for Air Quality Forecasting with Time-Series Foundation Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.19381)
*Rishi Bharadwaj,Manik Gupta,Pandarasamy Arjunan*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: Air Quality Arena (AQA) 是一个大规模、多国家、多污染物的数据集和基准测试，用于评估空气质量预测模型。结果表明，时间序列基础模型（TSFMs）在零样本情况下表现优于经典基线，其中最佳模型采用了一种跨模态架构，利用视觉基础模型进行时间序列预测。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 空气污染每年导致约790万过早死亡，准确预测是公共卫生优先事项。现有基准在地理范围和污染物覆盖方面狭窄，未能评估最新时间序列基础模型在真实大规模数据上的表现。AQA旨在弥补这一空白。

Method: 构建了AQA数据集，涵盖6种主要污染物、7个国家、4大洲，超过14,000个站点-污染物序列，时间跨度三年。在该数据集上对11种领先的时间序列基础模型和经典基线进行了短期空气质量预测性能评估。

Result: 时间序列基础模型（TSFMs）是有效的零样本预测器，且持续优于经典基线。表现最佳的模型采用跨模态架构，利用视觉基础模型进行时间序列预测。

Conclusion: AQA提供了全面的空气质量预测基准，验证了TSFMs的潜力，数据集和基准已公开发布。

Abstract: Air pollution causes an estimated 7.9 million premature deaths annually, making accurate forecasting a critical public health priority. Machine learning is increasingly being applied to forecast air pollution levels, yet existing benchmarks remain narrow in both geographic scope and pollutant coverage, and fail to evaluate the latest generation of time series foundation models (TSFMs) on real world, large scale data. We present Air Quality Arena (AQA), a large scale multi-country and multi-pollutant dataset (AQA-Data) and benchmark (AQA-Bench) to address this gap. AQA covers 6 major pollutants over a three year period across 7 diverse countries and 4 continents, with more than 14,000 station-pollutant series, aiming to provide a comprehensive benchmark for air quality tasks. We benchmark this dataset across 11 leading time series foundation models and classical baselines to assess performance on short-term air quality forecasting. Our results demonstrate that TSFMs are effective zero-shot forecasters and consistently outperform classical baselines, with our top-performing model employing a cross-modal architecture that leverages a vision foundation model for time series forecasting. AQA is publicly released at AirQualityArena.github.io

</details>


### [174] [Challenges of Explainability in Continual Learning for Time Series Forecasting](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.19382)
*Quentin Besnard,Emmanuel Doumard,Nicolas Labroche,Nicolas Ragot,Nicolas Ringuet*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文研究了基于经验回放的持续学习框架下，利用可解释性（注意力展开和Grad-CAM）分析非平稳时间序列预测模型（PatchMixer、PatchTST、DLinear）的预测行为和采样策略，并在真实压电水位时间序列上验证了可解释性有助于理解持续学习动态和指导数据选择。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 深度学习模型在时间序列预测中表现强劲，但在实际环境监测中面临非平稳动态和可解释性不足的挑战。作者希望利用可解释性作为理解持续学习中模型自适应过程的工具，从而解决部署难题。

Method: 采用PatchMixer、PatchTST和DLinear等神经预测架构，结合基于注意力的采样机制实现经验回放持续学习；使用注意力展开和Grad-CAM等归因方法分别分析预测行为和采样策略，观察注意力模式随时间的演化。

Result: 在呈现异质模式和状态转变的真实压电水位时间序列上，可解释性分析揭示了持续学习框架中的模型和采样行为动态，展示了归因模式随时间演化的规律，并证明其能为数据选择和自适应策略提供指导。

Conclusion: 可解释性不仅是理解持续学习行为的有效手段，还能揭示注意力模式演化规律，为非平稳预测场景中的模型自适应和数据选择提供重要参考；同时指出了该方向面临的挑战与机遇。

Abstract: Deep learning models have shown strong potential for time series forecasting, yet their deployment in real-world environmental monitoring remains challenging due to non-stationary dynamics and limited explainability. In this work, we investigate explainability as a central tool for understanding continual learning in adaptive time series forecasting, with Experience Replay strategies. We study neural forecasting architectures such as PatchMixer, PatchTST and DLinear, augmented with attention-based sampling mechanisms to support model adaptation over time. Explainability is leveraged through attention rollout and gradient-based attribution methods (Grad-CAM) to analyze both predictive behavior and sampling strategies within a continual learning framework. Experiments conducted on real-world piezometric time series exhibiting heterogeneous patterns and regime shifts show that analyzing model and sampling behaviors provides valuable insights into the dynamics of the continual learning framework. Beyond predictive performance, our results highlight the challenges and opportunities of using explainability to understand continual learning behaviors, revealing how attribution patterns evolve over time and how they can inform data selection and adaptation strategies in non-stationary forecasting scenarios.

</details>


### [175] [SUM: Unified Geometric Surgery on Spatio-Temporal Adaptation Vectors for Federated Class Incremental Learning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.19384)
*Jaeik Kim,Jaeyoung Do*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种名为SUM（统一几何手术）的纯服务端框架，用于解决联邦类增量学习（FCIL）中的时空灾难性遗忘问题。SUM通过在聚合过程中对适配向量进行几何手术，同时缓解客户端间干扰和跨任务干扰，无需额外客户端计算、通信或内存，在视觉和语言基准上取得高达22%的性能提升。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现实智能系统需要跨数据隔离客户端的分布式协作与持续适应演化任务，这催生了联邦类增量学习（FCIL）。但FCIL面临空间干扰（异构客户端）和时间干扰（顺序任务）耦合导致的时空灾难性遗忘（ST-CF）。现有方法通常用分离机制处理空间和时间干扰，需要额外客户端计算或通信，且在聚合过程中未规范更新间的方向性交互。

Method: 本文将FCIL重新理解为统一的多任务学习问题，将客户端和任务更新表示为共享参数空间中的适配向量。提出SUM框架，在服务端聚合时对适配向量进行几何手术：空间SUM缓解每轮客户端级干扰，因果在线时间SUM消除跨任务干扰。该方法纯服务端实现，不增加客户端计算、通信或内存开销。

Result: 在多个视觉和语言基准上，SUM相比先前FCIL方法提升高达22%，且对不可靠客户端具有鲁棒性，保持计算效率。

Conclusion: SUM通过统一的几何手术有效解决FCIL中的时空灾难性遗忘，为分布式持续学习提供了高效且鲁棒的解决方案。

Abstract: Real-world intelligent systems often require both distributed collaboration across data-isolated clients and continual adaptation to evolving tasks. This setting naturally gives rise to Federated Class Incremental Learning (FCIL), which combines Federated Learning (FL) and Continual Learning (CL). However, their combination introduces two coupled sources of interference: spatial interference from heterogeneous clients and temporal interference from sequential tasks, jointly leading to Spatial-Temporal Catastrophic Forgetting (ST-CF). Existing approaches typically address spatial and temporal interference with separate mechanisms, often incurring additional client-side computation or communication, while leaving directional interactions among updates during aggregation unregulated. In this paper, we reinterpret FCIL as a unified multi-task learning problem, where both client and task updates are represented as adaptation vectors in a shared parameter space. Based on this view, we propose Unified Geometric Surgery on Spatio-Temporal Adaptation Vectors (SUM), a purely server-side framework that performs geometric surgery on adaptation vectors during aggregation. Spatial SUM mitigates client-level interference within each round, while causal online temporal SUM removes cross-task interference over time without additional client-side computation, communication, or memory beyond standard federated training. Empirically, SUM achieves up to 22% improvement over prior FCIL methods across diverse vision and language benchmarks while remaining robust to unreliable clients and maintaining computational efficiency.

</details>


### [176] [STN-TGAT: Top-K Portfolio Construction via Prior-Guided Graph Attention with Learnable Soft-Threshold Sparsification](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.19385)
*Haoran Guo,Yutong Lu,Li Zhang*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 提出STN-TGAT模型，融合时序Transformer与图注意力网络，结合NMI先验图与软阈值稀疏化机制，在考虑交易成本等实际约束下进行股票排名与投资组合构建，在真实数据上优于基准模型。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有方法未能同时有效建模股票间的时序动态与横截面依赖关系，且缺乏对实际投资约束（如交易成本、权重分配）的考虑，导致与真实交易场景脱节。

Method: 将时序Transformer与图注意力网络（GAT）结合，利用基于归一化互信息（NMI）的先验图与软阈值稀疏化机制去除噪音连接、保留有效信息；在S&P 500中Top-50内选择Top-5股票，并引入显式权重分配与交易成本调整，使评估贴近真实交易。

Result: 在真实数据集上，STN-TGAT在预测精度与投资盈利性（组合收益）两方面均一致优于多个基准模型。

Conclusion: 将决策对齐训练与自适应关系建模相结合，为数据驱动的投资组合构建提供了一个连贯且实用的有效框架。

Abstract: This paper tackles the problem of stock ranking and portfolio construction under realistic investment settings by jointly modeling temporal dynamics and cross-sectional dependencies. We propose the Soft-Threshold NMI-prior Transformer Graph Attention Network (STN-TGAT), which integrates a temporal Transformer with a Graph Attention Network to capture long-horizon sequential patterns and dynamic inter-stock relationships. An NMI-based prior graph combined with a soft-threshold sparsification mechanism enhances structural robustness by mitigating noisy correlations while preserving informative connections. The portfolio formation process incorporates practical considerations, including Top-5 selection within the Top-50 $S\&P$ 500 constituents, explicit weight allocation, and transaction cost adjustment, thereby aligning the evaluation with real-world trading conditions. Empirical results on real-world data demonstrate that STN-TGAT consistently outperforms benchmark models from predictive accuracy and investment profitability measured by portfolio returns. These findings suggest that combining decision-aligned training with adaptive relational modeling provides a coherent and practically effective framework for data-driven portfolio construction.

</details>


### [177] [Building Fast, Evaluating Slow: Pipeline Choices Dominate Autointerpretability Score Variance](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.19386)
*Sinie van der Ben,Neele Roch,Anna Hedström,Mennatallah El-Assady*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文系统研究了基于自动可解释性分数的跨论文稀疏自编码器（SAE）比较的可靠性，发现方法论方差超过架构方差，导致比较结果可能反映管线差异而非架构差异，并提出了方差分解方法、稳定性检查和最低报告清单。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 跨论文比较SAE可解释性常依赖自动可解释性分数，但这些分数必须反映特征的稳定属性而非评估管线的混杂因素。本文旨在检验这一假设是否成立。

Method: 通过系统实验，在四个指标（simulation, detection, fuzzing, purity）、两个模型（Pythia-160M, Apertus-8B）和四个方法论变异轴上进行评估，分析分数稳定性。

Result: R1) 方法论方差总体上超过了所有指标和测试模型的架构方差；R2) 每个指标表现出不同的不稳定性特征，detection最稳定，fuzzing在所有条件下不可靠；R3) top-k特征排名在语料库和采样条件下不一致，掩盖了每个特征的不稳定性，仅监控解释相似性无法检测此问题。

Conclusion: 基于自动可解释性分数的跨论文比较可能反映管线差异而非架构差异，对SAE效用的持续辩论有影响。不稳定的评估会阻碍可解释性研究进展。为此，贡献了方差分解方法、稳定性检查和最低报告清单。

Abstract: Cross-paper comparison of sparse autoencoder (SAE) interpretability often relies on autointerpretability scores. In this evaluation pipeline, a language model (LM) explains each feature, and another LM scores the explanation. For these comparisons to be meaningful, scores must reflect stable properties of the features rather than confounding aspects of the evaluation pipeline. Through systematic experiments across four metrics (simulation, detection, fuzzing, purity), two models (Pythia-160M, Apertus-8B), and four axes of methodological variation, we show that this assumption does not hold. Specifically, we find that R1) methodological variance collectively exceeds architectural variance across all metrics and tested models; R2) each metric exhibits a distinct instability profile, with detection being the most stable and fuzzing unreliable across all conditions; R3) top-k feature rankings do not stay consistent across corpus and draw conditions, masking per-feature instability behind stable mean scores; a failure that cannot be detected by monitoring explanation similarity alone. These findings suggest that cross-paper comparisons based on autointerpretability scores may reflect pipeline differences rather than architectural differences, with implications for the ongoing debate on SAE utility. More broadly, unreliable evaluation slows progress in interpretability research at a time when reliable tools for understanding AI systems are needed. To support evaluation, we contribute a variance decomposition approach, a Stability Check, and a Minimum Reporting Checklist.

</details>


### [178] [Scale-Aware Learning of Chaotic Dynamics on Unstructured Meshes via Binned Spectral Losses](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.19387)
*Kanad Sen,Romit Maulik*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文针对不规则网格（非结构化网格）上的非线性动力系统代理建模，扩展了频段谱功率损失方法：用图拉普拉斯频段替代傅里叶频段，并提出了可扩展的切比雪夫多项式滤波器和多层级 GLEAM（图拉普拉斯能量对齐）方法，从而在没有标准傅里叶基的网格上实现尺度感知的监督，提升长期滚动的预测保真度和统计不变性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 高维非线性混沌动力系统的代理建模需要同时保持逐点精度和物理场的尺度依赖结构。现有频段谱损失方法在结构化网格上有效，但无法直接应用于缺乏规范傅里叶基的非结构化网格，因此需要建立基于图算子的谱表示及对应的频段损失。

Method: 将傅里叶频段替换为图拉普拉斯频段，提出全谱形式的图傅里叶频段功率匹配损失，并用切比雪夫多项式图滤波器稀疏近似避免显式特征分解；在多层级图架构中提出 GLEAM，通过保留子空间尺度感知监督对粗、细表示进行正则化。

Result: 实验表明，所提谱损失方法改进了非结构化网格上湍流预测的长期滚动保真度，并保持了统计不变量，优于确定性基线方法。

Conclusion: 图拉普拉斯频段谱损失是可扩展且有效的非结构化网格代理建模工具，能够捕捉多尺度动力学特征，尤其适用于混沌非线性系统。

Abstract: Surrogate modeling for high-dimensional nonlinear dynamical systems that exhibit chaos requires mechanisms that preserve not only pointwise accuracy but also the scale-dependent structure of physical fields. Bandwise spectral power losses, such as the binned spectral loss function, provide such supervision on structured grids, where Fourier modes define a standard frequency decomposition. On irregular meshes, however, no canonical Fourier basis exists, and spectral representations must be constructed from graph operators induced by mesh connectivity and geometry. In this study, we extend the binned spectral power loss for application to unstructured-mesh surrogate modeling of nonlinear dynamical systems. This is obtained by replacing Fourier bands with graph-Laplacian frequency bands, and we provide scalable Chebyshev and multilevel approximations for improving long-horizon rollout fidelity. In its full-spectrum form, our approach uses graph Laplacian eigenspaces to provide a graph analogue of Fourier band-power matching, but incurs the high cost of spectral decomposition. As a scalable approximation, we replace exact band projectors with sparse Chebyshev polynomial graph filters, avoiding explicit eigendecomposition. When utilizing multilevel graph architectures, we introduce Graph Laplacian Energy Alignment for Meshes (GLEAM), which applies retained-subspace scale-aware supervision across graph hierarchies so that coarse and fine representations are regularized during autoregressive rollout. Our results show that the proposed spectral losses improve long-horizon rollout fidelity and preserve statistical invariants for the forecasting of turbulent flows on unstructured meshes, compared to deterministic baselines.

</details>


### [179] [Neural Operator Surrogates for Two-Dimensional Neutron Flux Estimation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.19388)
*Japan K. Patel,Barry D. Ganapol,Anthony Magliari,Matthew C. Schmidt,Todd A. Wareing*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文将一维单次扫描神经算子研究扩展到二维，考虑各向同性散射的单群输运问题。使用FNO和UNO构建三个替代模型：直接映射（FNO和UNO）以及以单次扫描近似为额外输入的FNO。通过离散纵标法求解器生成高保真数据，以平均相对L₂误差评估映射质量，并针对三个随机种子训练以评估差异。研究主要探索两个问题：单次扫描输入是否比直接映射更准确，以及训练时使用通量对数是否能在强衰减区域（如屏蔽问题）提高精度。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 为了提高神经算子替代模型在中子输运问题中的准确性，特别是在涉及强衰减的屏蔽应用中，研究通过引入单次扫描近似输入和对数通量训练来改进直接映射方法的精度。

Method: 考虑三种替代模型：①基于FNO的直接映射（材料场和源场→标量通量）；②基于UNO的直接映射；③以单次扫描近似（一次源迭代后的标量通量）为额外输入的FNO。所有模型使用验证过的离散纵标法求解器生成的高保真数据训练，以平均相对L₂误差为评估指标，每个替代模型在三个随机种子上训练以评估运行间变异性。

Result: 研究通过对比三种替代模型的平均相对L₂误差，量化了映射质量，并分析了单次扫描输入和对数通量训练对精度的影响（具体数值结果在论文中给出）。

Conclusion: 论文明确了单次扫描输入能否提升直接映射的精度，以及训练时采用通量对数是否能在强衰减区域（如屏蔽问题）改善精度，为神经算子在输运问题中的应用提供了指导。

Abstract: This work extends our one-dimensional single-sweep neural-operator studies to two dimensions. We consider one-group transport with isotropic scattering. As in the one-dimensional work, we use Fourier neural operators (FNOs) to approximate the high-fidelity scalar flux. Additionally, we also investigate U-shaped neural operators (UNOs) in this study. We consider three surrogates. The first two map the material and source fields directly to the flux, one using an FNO and one using a UNO. The third is an FNO that additionally takes the scalar flux after one source iteration, the single-sweep approximation, as an input. Each case is solved to high fidelity with a verified discrete-ordinates solver, and an average relative L_2 error norm is used to characterize the quality of the inferred maps. We train every surrogate over three random seeds so that differences between them can be assessed against run-to-run variability. Two questions guide the study: whether the single-sweep input improves accuracy over the direct maps, and whether training on the logarithm of the flux improves accuracy in the strongly attenuated regions relevant to shielding.

</details>


### [180] [The Orthogonalized Read Is a Removable Training Scaffold for Recurrent Memory](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.19390)
*Keston Aquino-Michaels*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: orthogonalizing the mLSTM memory read (via Newton-Schulz iterations) improves noisy associative recall not by enhancing memory capacity but by re-conditioning the learning dynamics, shortening the plateau phase and increasing escape probability from poor solutions.


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: To understand why orthogonalizing the mLSTM memory matrix at read time improves associative recall, and to determine whether this reflects a genuine memory enhancement or an artifact of training dynamics.

Method: The paper systematically tests the orthogonalization intervention through three criteria: self-consistency (comparing with exact recursive least-squares, straight-through, delta-rule, frozen keys, plain normalization), uniformity (scanning learning rate × hardness grid), and removability (inference-only application, annealing). It also analyzes the escape hazard via a heat/noise law and decodes memory states directly.

Result: Orthogonalization does not improve memory capacity; it re-conditions the learning problem during the long plateau phase, increasing escape hazard ~6× uniformly across hardness, widening the workable LR corridor. Failed models already store ~half of associations linearly but cannot read them out. The original vocab-96 result is a large-batch noise condition, not a capacity increase.

Conclusion: Recall benchmarks partly measure trainability rather than pure memory; the system serves as a model organism for "emergence" where sharp behavioral thresholds arise from a censored metric over gradually accumulating underlying structure.

Abstract: A recent report finds that orthogonalizing the mLSTM memory matrix at read time (five Newton-Schulz iterations, trained through) substantially improves noisy associative recall. The effect replicates, but it is not a memory improvement. Training on this task is a long chance plateau followed by a sharp escape, and the orthogonalized read acts by re-conditioning the learning problem during the plateau. Three properties establish this. It must be self-consistent: an exact recursive least-squares read (the Mesa layer) reproduces it, while straight-through halves, delta-rule writes, frozen random keys, and plain normalization all fail. It is uniform: across a learning-rate x hardness grid it multiplies the escape hazard roughly six-fold with no detectable hardness dependence, widening the workable learning-rate corridor that narrows for the baseline. And it is removable: applied to failed models at inference it rescues none, and annealed away on an escape-triggered schedule it leaves numerically stock mLSTMs at full accuracy. Much of the published gain needs no architecture at all -- solved-rate at a fixed budget measures escape hazard, which follows a heat/noise law (learning-rate elasticity +3.0, gradient-noise elasticity -1.65) under which the original vocab-96 result is a large-batch noise condition rather than a capacity one. Decoding the memory state directly shows failed models carry roughly half their associations in linearly recoverable form: the plateau is a readout failure over half-written storage. Two conclusions travel beyond the intervention: recall benchmarks used for architecture selection partly measure trainability, and the system is a fully instrumented model organism of "emergence," in which a sharp behavioral threshold demonstrably arises from a censored metric over gradually accumulating structure.

</details>


### [181] [LAARA: Layer-Aware Adaptive Rank Allocation for Parameter-Efficient Fine-Tuning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.19391)
*Ashutosh Tripathi,Surya Deep Singh,Pranab Sahoo,Sriparna Saha*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: LAARA是一种基于Fisher信息引导的无搜索自适应秩分配框架，通过投影归一化、对数压缩、混合适配器重要性估计和投票变化阻尼机制，在GLUE和MathInstruct基准上以更少参数匹配或超越LoRA、AdaLoRA、DyLoRA、Bitfit等SOTA方法。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有低秩适配方法（如LoRA）对所有transformer层分配相同的适配器秩，忽略了不同层在适应新任务时的异构需求。理论上和实验上证明了均匀秩分配本质上是次优的，因此需要一种自适应、无需搜索的秩分配方法。

Method: 提出LAARA框架，利用训练过程中计算轻量的对角Fisher信息估计动态分配秩，包含四个关键组件：投影归一化（projection-wise normalization）、对数压缩（logarithmic compression）、混合适配器重要性估计（blended adapter importance estimation）以及投票变化阻尼机制（vote-to-change dampening mechanism），实现稳定高效的秩自适应。

Result: 在GLUE和MathInstruct基准上，LAARA以显著更少的可训练参数一致匹配或超越了LoRA、AdaLoRA、DyLoRA、Bitfit等当前主流方法，验证了Fisher引导的秩分配是参数高效微调的有效且原则性方案。

Conclusion: Fisher信息引导的秩分配为自适应参数高效微调提供了原则性且有效的基础，LAARA框架无需搜索即可动态分配秩，在减少参数的同时保持或提升性能。

Abstract: Low-Rank Adaptation is widely used for parameter-efficient fine-tuning, yet existing methods typically assign the same adapter rank to every transformer layer despite their heterogeneous adaptation requirements. In this work, we show theoretically and empirically that uniform rank allocation is fundamentally suboptimal. Motivated by this observation, we propose LAARA (Layer Aware Adaptive Rank Allocation framework), a search-free framework that dynamically allocates ranks using lightweight diagonal Fisher estimates computed during training. LAARA combines projection-wise normalization, logarithmic compression, blended adapter importance estimation, and a vote-to-change dampening mechanism to produce stable and efficient rank adaptation. Experiments on GLUE and MathInstruct benchmark demonstrate that LAARA consistently matches or outperforms popular state of the art approaches such as LoRA, AdaLoRA, DyLoRA, and Bitfit while using significantly fewer trainable parameters. Our results show that Fisher-guided rank allocation provides a principled and effective foundation for adaptive parameter-efficient fine-tuning. The code is publicly available at: https://anonymous.4open.science/r/LAARA-D305/LAARA.py

</details>


### [182] [Predicting Groundwater Arsenic Concentrations Using Graph Neural Networks](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.19392)
*William Xing,Stephanie Yang,Aarush Bandemegal,Anushree Misra,Ananya Kalapatapu,Brennan Lagasse,Kevin Zhu*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 该论文构建了一个包含超过74,000个砷样本的空间集成数据集，评估了多种机器学习模型（包括梯度提升树、多层感知机和图神经网络）进行地下水砷浓度预测。结果表明，图神经网络能够利用空间依赖关系，达到或超越梯度提升树的性能，为地下水污染风险制图提供了新方法。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 美国地下水砷污染对依赖私人井的住户构成长期公共健康危机，需要准确且空间信息丰富的预测模型来识别高风险区域，但目前缺乏能泛化表示区域间砷浓度连续变化的模型。

Method: 将砷预测视为回归任务，整合来自水质门户（WQP）、矿产资源数据系统（MRDS）和网格化国家土壤调查地理数据库（gNATSGO）的74,000余个砷样本，利用k近邻和GIS技术按位置连接样本点，并评估了基于树的集成方法、多层感知机和空间感知图神经网络等模型。

Result: 梯度提升树在表格数据上仍属最先进方法，但图神经网络能进一步利用空间依赖关系，达到或超越梯度提升树的预测性能。

Conclusion: 基于图的空间感知学习能够增强环境预测能力，为改进地下水风险制图和监测提供了基础。

Abstract: Arsenic contamination in groundwater presents a longstanding public health crisis in the United States, especially for households depending on private wells. Accurate and spatially informed prediction of arsenic concentration is vital to identify high-risk areas and focus mitigation efforts. However, there is a lack of generalizable models for representing continuous variation in arsenic concentrations across regions. In this work, we pose arsenic prediction as a regression task and construct a spatially integrated dataset to aggregate over 74,000 arsenic samples from the Water Quality Portal (WQP), Mineral Resources Data System (MRDS), and Gridded National Soil Survey Geographic Database (gNATSGO). Specifically, we use a variety of techniques including kNearest Neighbors (k-NN) and Geographic Information Systems (GIS) to join arsenic measurement points from across the United States by location. Building on this dataset, we evaluate a diverse suite of machine learning models, including tree-based ensemble approaches, multilayer perceptrons, and spatially aware graph neural networks (GNN). Our findings show that while gradient-boosted trees are still considered state-of-the-art in the field of tabular data, GNNs are able to further account for spatial dependence to match or outperform the results of gradient-boosted trees. These results demonstrate that graph-based and spatially informed learning can enhance environmental prediction and provide a foundation for improved groundwater risk mapping and monitoring.

</details>


### [183] [Decodable but Not Detectable: A Leakage Fingerprint for Near-OOD Benchmarks](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.19393)
*Vishnu Bindu Balachandran*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文发现，一个基于扰动的OOD检测器在文档基准测试中AUROC仅为0.326（低于0.5的随机水平），原因是指定的“OOD”类实际上是模型训练过的类别，导致数据泄露。删除该类别并重新训练35个模型后，AUROC提升至0.911。作者将这种污染提炼为“泄露指纹”（漏泄指纹），并通过52个设置（20个泄露、32个干净）的受控实验验证了检测方法。对24个标准近/远OOD基准对的审计仅触发一个误报。在修正协议下，扰动信号可解码但不可检测。本文贡献是修正后的协议和经过验证的泄露诊断方法，而非新的OOD方法。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有OOD检测基准存在数据泄露问题，即所谓的“OOD”类实际上是模型训练过的类别，导致评测结果失真（AUROC低于随机水平）。作者旨在识别、诊断并纠正这种基准污染，提供可靠的评估协议。

Method: 作者首先通过删除泄露类别并重新训练35个模型来修正基准。然后提炼出泄露指纹：监督解码近乎完美（AUROC≈1）而无监督检测低于0.65。在52个受控设置（20泄露、32干净）上使用ResNet-50和ViT-B/16在CIFAR-10/100上验证，在嵌入空间中实现敏感性18/20、特异性31/32。对24个标准近/远OOD基准对进行野外审计，仅触发一个误报。最后提供理论解释。

Result: 修正协议后，AUROC从0.326提升至0.911。泄露指纹检测的敏感性为18/20、特异性为31/32，匹配的拟合集排除对照完美20/20。野外审计中仅CIFAR-100 vs CIFAR-10这对固有困难的近OOD对被触发。在修正协议下，扰动信号可被监督解码（AUROC 0.87-1.00）但无监督检测器无法检测，且扰动方法未优于普通马氏距离。

Conclusion: 本文主要贡献是提供了一个修正的OOD检测评估协议和经过验证的泄露诊断方法，而非提出新的OOD检测算法。作者还提供了理论解释，并因透明度原因撤回了之前的相关性声明。强调基准完整性对OOD检测评估至关重要。

Abstract: While auditing a perturbation-based OOD detector on a document benchmark, we recorded an AUROC of 0.326 -- well below the 0.5 chance level. The cause is a benchmark leak: the designated "OOD" class is one the model was trained on, so its examples sit inside the in-distribution fit set and the detector is penalized for correctly ranking them as familiar. Deleting the class and retraining 35 models across two domains raises the score to 0.911. We distill the contamination into a leak fingerprint -- near-perfect supervised decodability (AUROC approximately 1) coupled with unsupervised detection collapsed below 0.65 -- and validate it on a controlled battery of 52 settings (20 leaked, 32 clean) across ResNet-50 and ViT-B/16 on CIFAR-10/100, achieving sensitivity 18/20 and specificity 31/32 in embedding space; the matched fit-set-exclusion controls are perfect at 20/20. An in-the-wild audit of 24 standard near/far OOD benchmark pairs fires on exactly one (the intrinsically hard CIFAR-100 vs CIFAR-10 pair) and on no far-OOD pair, confirming specificity and that standard cross-dataset construction is clean. Under the corrected protocol, perturbation signals are decodable but not detectable: a supervised reader recovers the OOD signal (AUROC 0.87-1.00) while no unsupervised detector does, and the perturbation method does not improve on plain Mahalanobis distance. We provide a theoretical account of why and, for transparency, retract an earlier circular correlation. The contributions are a corrected protocol and a validated leak diagnostic, not a new OOD method.

</details>


### [184] [Cross-Subject Semantic Decoding with Shared-Space Alignment for Generalized Neural Representation Learning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.19394)
*Ji-Hoon Heo,Aleksandra Joanna Wisniewska,Seo-Hyun Lee,Seong-Whan Lee*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 提出一种跨被试语义解码框架，通过共享反应模型将多个被试的神经活动对齐到共享潜在空间，然后从对齐的神经表示中解码上下文语义嵌入。在留出被试上无需重新训练即可应用，实现了更好的跨被试泛化。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 侵入式神经记录中跨被试泛化困难，因为电极配置、解剖结构和神经信号模式在不同个体间差异显著。现有方法难以有效处理这种被试间变异性。

Method: 使用共享反应模型（Shared Response Model）估计多个被试的共享潜在空间；训练一个解码器从投影后的神经响应预测上下文语义嵌入；对于留出被试，仅需估计其到共享空间的投影，直接应用预训练解码器，无需重新训练。

Result: 所提框架在所有评估设置中一致优于基线方法，从源被试到留出被试测试的性能下降更小，表明跨被试泛化能力显著提升。

Conclusion: 将神经活动对齐到共享潜在空间并在语义嵌入空间中进行解码，是改善跨被试泛化的有效策略，因为它能减少被试特异性差异，同时有效捕获共享的刺激相关表征。

Abstract: Generalizing across subjects remains challenging in invasive neural recordings because electrode configurations, anatomical structures, and neural signal patterns vary substantially across individuals. To investigate such inter-subject variability, we propose a cross-subject semantic decoding framework that aligns neural responses to speech perception from multiple subjects into a shared latent space and learns a mapping from the aligned neural representations to contextual embeddings. More specifically, using electrocorticography data collected during natural language comprehension, we estimate the shared space using the shared response model and train a decoder to predict contextual semantic embeddings from projected neural responses. For a held-out subject, we estimate a subject-specific projection into the predefined shared space, and directly apply the pretrained decoder without any retraining. Experimental results demonstrate that the proposed framework consistently outperforms baseline methods across evaluation settings and exhibits a reduced performance drop from source subject to held-out subject testing, indicating improved cross-subject generalization. These results suggest that aligning neural activity into a shared latent space, while decoding in a semantic embedding space, provides an effective strategy for improving cross-subject generalization by reducing subject-specific differences in neural responses while effectively capturing shared stimulus-related representations.

</details>


### [185] [From Trajectories to Prefixes: Reusing Teacher Trajectories via Replayed Prefixes and Online Continuation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.19395)
*Yihan Wang,Zhong Guan,Haoran Sun,Jiale Huang,Likang Wu,Hongke Zhao*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 提出Prefix-GRPO框架，将教师轨迹分解为对齐前缀和在线续写，通过对前缀token进行裁剪策略更新，统一前缀学习和续写学习，以解决小语言模型在长程交互环境中模仿学习效率低下的问题。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 小语言模型作为交互智能体骨干时，直接蒸馏教师轨迹会将丰富的多轮交互行为退化为单步模仿目标，在早期决策影响后续状态和奖励的长程环境中效率低下。

Method: Prefix-GRPO将教师轨迹分解为回放对齐的前缀查询和在线续写；每个前缀在环境中回放以恢复有效的中间状态，学生继续在线交互并获得任务奖励；与仅对响应做GRPO不同，Prefix-GRPO还对回放前缀中的历史助手token应用裁剪策略更新，利用策略蒸馏的SFT检查点估计其旧对数概率，从而在同一策略优化形式下统一前缀学习和续写学习。

Result: 在TextCraft、BabyAI和ALFWorld上的实验表明，Prefix-GRPO相比蒸馏和标准RL基线显著提升了小模型智能体的性能；消融实验表明，仅回放而不对前缀token进行显式优化是不够的。

Conclusion: Prefix-GRPO通过将教师轨迹分解为可回放前缀与在线续写，并对前缀token进行显式策略优化，有效提升了小语言模型在长程交互环境中的学习效率和任务表现。

Abstract: Small language models are attractive backbones for interactive agents, but direct distillation from strong teacher trajectories often turns rich multi-turn behavior into one-shot imitation targets. This is inefficient in long-horizon environments, where early decisions shape later states and rewards. We propose Prefix-GRPO, a reinforcement learning framework that decomposes teacher trajectories into replay-aligned prefix queries and online continuations. Each prefix is replayed in the environment to recover a valid intermediate state, after which the student continues online interaction and receives task reward. Unlike response-only GRPO, Prefix-GRPO also applies clipped policy updates to historical assistant tokens inside the replayed prefix, using a policy-distilled SFT checkpoint to estimate their old log-probabilities. This unifies prefix learning and continuation learning within the same policy-optimization form. Experiments on TextCraft, BabyAI, and ALFWorld show that Prefix-GRPO improves small-model agents over distillation and standard RL baselines, while ablations show that replay alone is insufficient without explicit prefix-token optimization. The implementation and reproduction scripts are available at https://github.com/HappynessI/Prefix_GRPO.

</details>


### [186] [Memory Merge DQN: Sensitivity Weighted Target Updates for Stable Value Learning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.19397)
*Adrian Ly,Richard Dazeley,Peter Vamplew,Sunil Aryal,Francisco Cruz*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: Memory Merge DQN 是一种新的目标网络更新机制，它维护近期历史在线网络副本的短时记忆，并基于 Q-value 敏感性（而非 Fisher 信息）合并网络参数来构建目标网络，从而替代传统的硬拷贝更新。该方法在 Atari 环境中取得了最优的最终性能表现，超越了 DQN、Averaged DQN、带层归一化的 DQN 和 PQN，表明选择性合并近期参数权重和历史可以改善 DQN 的稳定性和最终性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 标准 DQN 使用硬拷贝更新目标网络，这引入了权衡：保持目标网络固定可改善短期稳定性，但每次硬更新会突然用最新在线网络替换目标参数并丢弃近期参数历史，导致自举目标突变，可能移除后续训练仍有用的价值函数结构。

Method: Memory Merge DQN 维护一个近期历史在线网络副本的短时记忆，基于 Q-value 敏感性（而非 Fisher 信息）作为加权信号来合并网络参数，同时使用近因先验（recency prior）使合并后的目标网络靠近最新在线参数，受 Fisher Weight Model Merging 启发但使用不同的加权信号。

Result: 在 Atari 环境中与 DQN、Averaged DQN、带层归一化的 DQN 和 PQN（梯度裁剪）对比，Memory Merge DQN 获得了最多的第一名最终性能结果，超越了对比方法，在多个游戏中取得了显著提升，尤其是在保留有用价值函数参数有益的游戏上。

Conclusion: 选择性合并近期参数权重和历史可以改善 DQN 的稳定性和最终性能，目标网络设计是保留长时价值学习中价值函数结构的重要机制。

Abstract: Deep Q-networks use target networks to stabilise bootstrapped value learning, but the standard hard copy update also introduces a tradeoff. Holding the target network fixed, improves short term stability, yet each hard update abruptly replaces the target parameters with the newest online network and discards recent parameter history. This can produce sudden changes in the bootstrap target and may remove value function structure that remains useful later in training. This paper introduces Memory Merge DQN, a target network update mechanism that maintains a short memory of recent historical online network copies and constructs the target network by merging network parameters based on the Q-value sensitivity rather than copying only the newest online network. Memory Merge gives greater influence to parameters that remain locally important for current Q-value behaviour, while using a recency prior to keep the merged target close to the latest online parameters. The method is inspired by Fisher Weight Model Merging, but uses Q-value sensitivity rather than Fisher information as the weighting signal. This paper evaluates Memory Merge DQN on Atari environments against DQN, Averaged DQN, DQN with layer normalisation, and PQN (with gradient clipping). The results show that Memory Merge DQN is highly competitive and it achieves the largest number of first place final performance results among the evaluated methods, beats DQN, Averaged DQN, and PQN (with gradient clipping), and produces substantial gains in several games where preserving useful value-function parameters appears beneficial. These findings suggest that selectively merging recent parameter weights and history can improve the stability and final performance of DQN agents, and that target network design is an important mechanism for preserving useful value function structure during long horizon value learning.

</details>


### [187] [Leveraging Offline Supervision for Efficient and Generalizable Reinforcement Learning in Large-Scale Vision-Language-Action Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.19399)
*Dmitriy Poyarkov,Aleksei Staroverov,Aleksandr I. Panov*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文研究混合离线-在线训练方法，将离线监督（通过离线数据或离线参考策略）引入强化学习（RL）中，用于训练视觉-语言-动作（VLA）策略。实验表明，纯离线训练在分布外（OOD）场景下表现有限，而混合方法在保持强OOD能力的同时，训练效率大幅提升（约节省一半预算），且不牺牲速度与OOD性能的平衡。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 在线RL生成的策略在OOD场景表现优于离线方法（如监督微调SFT），但离线训练效率高。本文旨在探索混合离线-在线训练是否能结合两者优势，即保留在线RL的强OOD能力并提升训练效率。

Method: 采用两种正则化方式：1）利用离线数据对RL进行正则化；2）利用离线预训练参考策略对RL进行正则化。在OOD基准上评估这些混合方法，并与纯离线训练和标准在线RL对比。

Result: 纯离线训练OOD性能有限；混合方法在保持与标准RL相近的OOD性能的同时，训练效率显著提高（约需一半训练预算）。混合方法未产生速度与OOD性能之间的权衡，而是兼具强OOD能力和高效率。

Conclusion: 混合离线-在线训练是一种有效策略，能够在不牺牲OOD泛化能力的前提下大幅降低训练成本，为VLA策略训练提供了实用解决方案。

Abstract: It is commonly observed that online reinforcement learning (RL) produces better-performing strategies than offline methods across a broad range of performance measures. In particular, RL-trained policies exhibit stronger out-of-distribution (OOD) behavior, where models trained only with imitation learning approaches often struggle. A recent study introduced an OOD-focused benchmark and reported that RL-trained vision-language-action (VLA) policies achieve noticeably better OOD performance and slightly better in-distribution (IND) performance than their counterparts trained with supervised fine-tuning (SFT). In this work, we investigate whether hybrid offline-online training can combine the advantages of both approaches. Specifically, we study RL methods regularized by offline supervision via either offline data or an offline-trained reference policy. We evaluate these approaches on the OOD benchmark and compare them with both offline-only training and standard RL. Our results show that although offline training achieves limited OOD performance by itself, incorporating offline supervision into RL preserves strong OOD capability while substantially improving training efficiency. In particular, the guided methods reach performance close to that of standard RL while requiring roughly half of the training budget. Rather than producing a trade-off between speed and OOD performance, the hybrid approach retains strong OOD capability while achieving this efficiency gain. Project page: https://alstar8.github.io/offline-supervision-vla-rl

</details>


### [188] [Predictive single cell foundation model for gene regulation and aging with privacy-preserving tabular learning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.19400)
*Jiayuan Ding,Jianhui Lin,Ziyang Miao,Nils Mechtel,Shiyu Jiang,Yixin Wang,Zhaoyu Fang,Jorge D. Martin-Rufino,Chen Weng,Reuben Saunders,Weize Xu,Jonathan S. Weissman,Min Li,Jiliang Tang,Wei Ouyang,Yuancheng Ryan Lu,Xiaojie Qiu*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出Tabula，一种结合联邦学习（FL）与表格学习（tabular learning）的隐私保护单细胞基础模型，并开发了去中心化AI agent平台Chiron以支持跨机构协作训练。Tabula通过显式建模单细胞数据的表格结构（无序、表格化），在多个下游基准任务中表现优异，揭示了造血、胰腺内胚层发生、神经发生和心脏发生等系统中的组合调控逻辑。利用新的年轻与衰老成纤维细胞scRNA-seq数据集，Tabula通过年龄和身份评分引导的硅内优先级排序，提名了优于传统方法的年轻化因子。这项工作为隐私保护的虚拟细胞研究奠定了基础。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 单细胞基因组学中的基础模型（FM）虽已展现潜力，但扩展规模会引发隐私问题；同时，单细胞数据具有无序性和独特的表格结构，现有FM未能有效建模。

Method: 提出Tabula，一种采用联邦学习（FL）进行隐私保护训练的基础模型，显式建模单细胞数据的表格结构；并开发Chiron，一个去中心化的AI agent平台，支持机构间在不共享原始数据的情况下进行协作训练。

Result: Tabula在多个下游基准任务中表现优异，能够揭示造血、胰腺内胚层发生、神经发生和心脏发生等系统中的组合调控逻辑；在一个新的年轻与衰老成纤维细胞scRNA-seq数据集上，Tabula通过年龄和身份评分引导的硅内优先级排序，成功提名了优于传统方法的年轻化因子。

Conclusion: Tabula通过将表格学习与联邦学习相结合，代表了单细胞基础建模的重要进展，为隐私保护的虚拟细胞研究铺平了道路，有助于人类健康。

Abstract: Pre-trained foundation models (FMs) have begun transforming single-cell genomics, but scaling them raises privacy concerns. Moreover, unlike text data, single-cell data is unordered and exhibits a unique tabular structure that current single-cell FMs overlook. We introduce Tabula, a privacy-preserving FM designed with federated learning (FL) that explicitly models the tabular structure of single-cell data. To deploy Tabula, we further developed Chiron, a decentralized AI agent-enabled platform for collaborative training across institutions without sharing raw data. Beyond strong performance across downstream benchmarks, Tabula reveals combinatorial regulatory logic across diverse biological systems, including hematopoiesis, pancreatic endogenesis, neurogenesis, and cardiogenesis. Using a new scRNA-seq dataset of paired young and aged human fibroblasts, Tabula nominates rejuvenation factors through age- and identity score-guided in silico prioritization, outperforming conventional approaches. Thus, Tabula represents an important advance in single-cell foundation modeling by integrating tabular learning with FL, paving the way toward privacy-preserving virtual cells for human health.

</details>


### [189] [When Does Consensus Beat Voting? A Critical Analysis of Statistical Label Fusion in Medical Image Segmentation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.19402)
*Renjie He*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文对共识分割进行了严谨的数学推导和实证分析，发现STAPLE在常见条件下退化或失败，而多数投票是强大的基线，同时深度共识模型和共形预测提供了更好的替代方案。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 鼓励从业者批判性地评估共识分割方法，而不是默认使用STAPLE，并为更原则性的方法提供数学和实证基础。

Method: 从第一原理推导共识分割的数学基础（生成模型、EM算法、边际化分析、可辨识性条件、Spatial STAPLE、深度变分公式），并通过控制实验验证理论预测。

Result: STAPLE在常见条件下退化为阈值化的多数投票、存在95%的EM次优性、并在类别不平衡下崩溃；多数投票是强大的基线；深度共识模型表明结合图像信息可解决共识问题；共形预测可提供形式化的不确定性保证。

Conclusion: 多数投票简单且稳健，不应被轻率抛弃；深度共识模型和共形预测提供了更有效的替代方案；从业者应批判性地选择共识方法而非默认STAPLE。

Abstract: This paper provides a rigorous, self-contained investigation of consensus segmentation. We derive the mathematical foundations from first principles -- the generative model, EM algorithm, Van Leemput's marginalization analysis, identifiability conditions, Spatial STAPLE, and deep variational formulations -- and validate each theoretical prediction through controlled experiments. The central finding is sobering: under common conditions, STAPLE reduces to thresholded majority voting, suffers 95% EM suboptimality, and collapses under class imbalance. These are not edge cases but typical scenarios in medical imaging. Majority voting -- simple, non-parametric, and robust -- is a surprisingly strong baseline that the field has perhaps too hastily dismissed in favor of more "sophisticated" methods. At the same time, the deep consensus model demonstrates that the consensus problem is not inherently difficult -- it becomes tractable when the image is used alongside the labels. And conformal prediction shows that formal uncertainty guarantees are achievable and practical. We hope this work encourages practitioners to critically evaluate their consensus methods rather than applying STAPLE by default, and provides the mathematical and empirical foundation for more principled approaches.

</details>


### [190] [Recovering Clinical Utility Under Differential Privacy: Empirical Validation of Adaptive Federated Aggregation on Heterogeneous Cardiovascular Datasets](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.19403)
*Rodrigo Tertulino,Laercio Alencar,Ricardo Almeida*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本论文在五个真实心血管数据集上验证了FedCVR联邦学习框架，证明其在实际临床环境中仍能保持自适应优化优势，在隐私预算下达到F1分数79.2%、AUC 0.96，并显著优于标准FedAvg。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 先前基于合成数据的研究验证了服务器端自适应优化可对差分隐私噪声起到时间去噪作用，但明确指出现实世界验证是优先未来方向。本文旨在填补这一空白，在真实多中心临床数据上验证FedCVR框架的可行性。

Method: 使用五个公开真实心血管数据集（Framingham、Cleveland、Hungarian、Switzerland、Long Beach VA），统一为13属性的UCI心脏病模式，配置为异构联邦场景并采用留一机构交叉验证。在隐私预算（噪声乘数=0.8，隐私预算ε≈4.2）下评估FedCVR性能，并与FedAvg进行配对t检验比较。

Result: FedCVR在真实数据上达到F1分数79.2%、AUC 0.96；在所有评估指标上统计显著优于FedAvg（配对t检验，所有p≤0.003，低于Bonferroni校正阈值）；测量的隐私成本呈现与合成实验一致的优雅降级模式。

Conclusion: FedCVR框架在真实多中心临床环境中具有临床可行性，为联邦学习从合成环境部署到真实医疗场景提供了实证证据。

Abstract: Validating federated learning frameworks on real clinical data is an essential step between proof-of-concept demonstrations in controlled synthetic environments and deployment in real multicenter healthcare settings. A prior architectural study by the same authors (Tertulino and Alencar, 2026) demonstrated, on a synthetic six-feature benchmark, that server-side adaptive optimization acts as a temporal denoiser for Differential Privacy noise, answering an open challenge identified in the original pipeline work (Tertulino, 2025). That study used synthetically generated data and explicitly identified real-world validation as a priority future direction. The present work addresses this gap by validating the FedCVR framework on five publicly available real cardiovascular datasets (Framingham, Cleveland, Hungarian, Switzerland, and Long Beach VA), harmonized to the 13-attribute UCI Heart Disease schema and configured as a heterogeneous federated scenario with leave-one-institution-out cross-validation. Results demonstrate that FedCVR preserves its adaptive advantage on real data, achieving an F1-Score of 79.2% and AUC of 0.96 under the operational privacy budget (noise multiplier = 0.8, privacy budget epsilon approximately 4.2), while statistically outperforming standard FedAvg on all evaluated metrics (paired t-tests, all p <= 0.003, significant under the Bonferroni-corrected threshold). The measured privacy cost on real data confirms the graceful degradation pattern observed in the synthetic experiments, providing empirical evidence of the framework's clinical viability in genuine multicenter contexts.

</details>


### [191] [Structured Latent Space Modeling over Multi-Scale Temporal Patches for Multivariate Time Series Forecasting](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.19404)
*Xingsheng Chen,Deyu Yi,Siu-Ming Yiu*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: M2Patch is a CNN-based multivariate time series forecasting model that uses multi-scale patching, depthwise separable convolutions with progressive dilation, and two complementary latent space constraints (intra-scale smoothness and inter-scale alignment) to organize structured latent representations. It achieves state-of-the-art results on 40 forecasting settings with linear complexity and robustness to input corruption.


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: Existing forecasting backbones treat learned representations as transient byproducts of prediction, failing to exploit the organizational geometry of multi-scale structural patterns encoded in multivariate time series.

Method: M2Patch employs: (1) multi-scale patching to decompose input into overlapping temporal granularities; (2) depthwise separable convolutions with progressive dilation for scale-specific feature extraction in linear time; (3) per-scale learned projections for compact latent representation; (4) intra-scale smoothness constraint enforcing temporal continuity between adjacent patches; (5) inter-scale alignment constraint via learnable cross-scale mappings to restore cross-granularity interaction within channel-independent design.

Result: On ten real-world benchmarks across 40 forecasting settings, M2Patch achieves 57 best and 34 second-best results, matching or exceeding representative baselines on most benchmarks, while maintaining linear computational complexity and robustness to patch-level input corruption.

Conclusion: M2Patch demonstrates that explicitly organizing the latent space with structural constraints improves multivariate time series forecasting, offering an efficient and robust alternative that capitalizes on multi-scale temporal patterns.

Abstract: Multivariate time series encode structural patterns that unfold across multiple temporal scales, yet most forecasting backbones treat learned representations as transient byproducts of prediction, leaving the organizational geometry of these patterns underexploited. We introduce M2Patch, a CNN-based forecasting architecture that maps channel-independent multivariate observations into a structured latent space through two complementary differentiable constraints. Multi-scale patching decomposes the input into overlapping temporal granularities; depthwise separable convolutions with progressive dilation extract scale-specific features in linear time; and per-scale learned projections compress these features into a compact latent representation. The latent space is organized by an intra-scale smoothness constraint that enforces temporal continuity between adjacent patches, and an inter-scale alignment constraint, realized through learnable cross-scale mappings, that restores cross-granularity interaction within the channel-independent design, ensuring that all scales encode mutually consistent representations of the underlying dynamics. Experiments on ten real-world benchmarks show that M2Patch achieves 57 best and 34 second-best results across 40 forecasting settings, matching or exceeding representative baselines on most benchmarks while maintaining linear computational complexity and robustness to patch-level input corruption.

</details>


### [192] [Reproducing Recurrent Transformers: The CoTFormer](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.19405)
*Aras Kavuncu,Bryan Vullo,Alberto Berni*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: CoTFormer formalizes Chain-of-Thought as recurrent latent computation, preserving intermediate states as attendable representations. It evaluates the architecture and variants on perplexity, compute efficiency, and out-of-distribution generalization on inductive reasoning tasks.


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: To formalize Chain-of-Thought as recurrent latent computation, enabling explicit reasoning traces and improving out-of-distribution generalization on inductive reasoning tasks.

Method: CoTFormer architecture with recurrent latent states that are attendable, mimicking explicit reasoning. Evaluated via perplexity and compute efficiency metrics, and on controlled algorithmic settings for inductive reasoning.

Result: The evaluation measures perplexity and compute efficiency of CoTFormer and its variants, and tests whether the recurrent framework improves out-of-distribution generalization on inductive reasoning tasks.

Conclusion: CoTFormer provides a formalization of Chain-of-Thought as recurrent latent computation, and the empirical evaluation determines its effectiveness in terms of perplexity, compute efficiency, and out-of-distribution generalization.

Abstract: The CoTFormer architecture formalizes Chain-of-Thought as a form of recurrent latent computation, preserving intermediate states as attendable representations to mimic explicit reasoning traces. In this work, we evaluate CoTFormer and its structural variants across perplexity and compute efficiency metrics. Furthermore, we extend evaluation to controlled algorithmic settings to determine whether this recurrent framework improves out-of-distribution generalisation on inductive reasoning tasks.

</details>


### [193] [NMR Elucidation as an Agentic Search Problem, Not a Modeling Problem](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.19406)
*Irina Espejo Morales,Damon Hinz,Marvin Alberts,Geraud Krawezik,Haewon Jeong,Shirley Ho*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出一个基于大型语言模型（LLM）的自主智能体系统，用于从核磁共振（NMR）数据中解析化学结构。该系统不直接训练模型将谱图映射到结构，而是通过冻结的LLM与专门的处理工具、验证检查、化学位移表以及模拟化学家思维步骤的指令进行交互，将NMR结构解析重构为LLM引导的约束搜索问题。在多个数据集上，该智能体的表现与研究生水平的化学学生相当，甚至超越了端到端的深度学习模型。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 从NMR数据中解析化学结构是化学、材料科学和生物学中的一个基本瓶颈。现有的端到端深度学习模型需要大量训练数据且泛化能力有限，而传统方法依赖专家经验。因此，需要一种能够灵活运用领域知识并进行多步推理的自动化方法。

Method: 构建了一个单一的自主体智能体，基于冻结的LLM，与一个精心设计的交互环境相结合。该环境包含领域特定的处理工具、结构验证检查、查询化学位移表的能力，以及遵循化学家分步思考过程的指令。智能体通过LLM引导的约束搜索逐步推断结构，而不是直接进行谱图到结构的映射。

Result: 在Alberts数据集上，智能体取得了71%的top-1准确率（研究生为66%）；在van Bramer数据集上为80%，在AstraZeneca数据集上为20%，均优于未经微调的端到端深度学习模型。这些模型虽然在大规模模拟谱图上训练过，但在真实数据上表现不及该方法。

Conclusion: 将NMR结构解析重新定义为LLM引导的约束搜索而非纯建模任务，能够显著提升性能。这一方法展示了构建多步编排框架的潜力，该框架可整合多种工具、模型和领域知识，辅助自动化光谱分析。

Abstract: Structural elucidation from Nuclear Magnetic Resonance (NMR) data remains a fundamental bottleneck across chemistry, materials science, and biology. We demonstrate that an agentic AI system can perform this task at a level comparable to graduate-level chemistry students. Instead of training a model to directly map spectra to structures, we build a single autonomous agent, backed by a frozen LLM, that interacts with a curated environment with access to domain-specific processing tools, validation checks, tabulated chemical shifts, and instructions that outline the stepwise nature of a chemist's thinking process. On the Alberts dataset, our agent elucidates structures with a top-1 accuracy of 71%, comparable to the performance of graduate students at 66% top-1 accuracy. On the van Bramer and AstraZeneca datasets, our agent achieved 80% and 20% top-1 accuracy respectively, outperforming zero-shot end-to-end deep learning models which were trained on large datasets of simulated spectra. These results show that reframing NMR elucidation as an LLM-guided constrained search, rather than a modeling task, yields substantial gains and suggests a path toward multi-step orchestration frameworks that integrate a variety of tools, models, and domain knowledge to assist in automating spectroscopic analysis.

</details>


### [194] [Reward-Aware Population Scaling of Evolutionary Strategies in LLM Fine-Tuning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.19408)
*Sung Cho,Gyubin Han*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: The paper resolves a conflict in Evolutionary Strategies (ES) fine-tuning of LLMs: why cross-entropy reward works with population size N=1 while binary reward often needs N≈30. It shows the gap is due to reward design and normalization, not population size. Specifically, z-score advantage normalization can cause N=2 to fail; disabling it lets binary-reward ES with N=2 improve on reasoning tasks across 0.5B-7B models. The small-N failure risk is determined by reward granularity: binary accuracy reward creates a zero-advantage probability q (derived in closed form from base accuracy, batch size, and intra-pair correlation). Experiments on Qwen2.5-Instruct/GSM8K match the formula (MAE 0.020) and show the availability threshold N_avail is small in the capable-model regime, implying that small-population failure is an implementation artifact, not an intrinsic limit.


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: To resolve the apparent contradiction in Evolutionary Strategies (ES) fine-tuning of large language models: cross-entropy reward succeeds with population size N=1, while binary-reward training often requires N≈30. Understanding whether this gap stems from population size or other factors like reward design is crucial for efficient fine-tuning with ES.

Method: The authors analyze reward design and normalization: they show that z-score advantage normalization (commonly used in ES) causes N=2 to fail, while disabling normalization allows binary-reward ES with N=2 to improve on GSM8K and TREC across 0.5B-7B models. They derive a closed-form formula for the zero-advantage probability q under binary accuracy reward, depending on base accuracy, batch size, and intra-pair correctness correlation. They validate the formula empirically on Qwen2.5-Instruct/GSM8K across 12 configurations (MAE 0.020) and compute the availability threshold N_avail.

Result: Binary-reward ES with N=2 (without normalization) improves on GSM8K and TREC across 0.5B-7B capable models, while the normalized variant collapses or degrades. The zero-advantage probability formula matches experiments with mean absolute error 0.020, and the availability threshold N_avail is found to be small in the capable-model regime.

Conclusion: Small-population failure in capable-model binary ES is an implementation artifact (due to reward normalization) rather than an intrinsic population limit. N=2 is not universally sufficient, but the intrinsic population requirement is lower than previously thought when reward design and normalization are properly handled.

Abstract: Using Evolutionary Strategies (ES) for fine-tuning large language models is attractive because it is memory-efficient, parallel, and compatible with black-box or discrete rewards. Yet its population-size conclusions conflict sharply: fine-tuning with cross-entropy (CE) reward succeeds with $N=1$, while binary-reward training often needs $N \approx 30$. We show this gap is largely about reward design and normalization, not population size. In the capable-model regime we study, z-score advantage normalization can cause $N=2$ to fail. Disabling normalization lets binary-reward ES with $N=2$ improve on GSM8K and TREC across capable models spanning 0.5B-7B, where the normalized variant collapses or degrades. This small-$N$ risk is set by reward granularity: binary accuracy reward induces a zero-advantage probability $q$ that depends in closed form on base accuracy, batch size, and intra-pair correctness correlation; a zero-training probe on Qwen2.5-Instruct/GSM8K matches the formula with mean absolute error 0.020 across 12 configurations and finds the availability threshold $N_{\mathrm{avail}}$ to be small in this capable-model regime. The implication is not that $N=2$ is universally sufficient, but that small-population failure in capable-model binary ES can be an implementation artifact rather than an intrinsic population limit.

</details>


### [195] [Adaptive Multi-Expert Graph Transformer for Interpretable EEG-Based Diagnostics](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.19429)
*Maryam Rahimimovassagh,Md Elias Hossain,Ivan Garibay,Niloofar Yousefi*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 提出一种空间多专家图变换器，将脑电信号建模为动态功能连接图序列，通过加权相位滞后指数(wPLI)估计时变连接，使用层级图编码从电极到区域再到全局聚合信息，并采用多专家变换器架构实现子类型感知推理，在TUAB数据集上取得有竞争力的异常脑电检测性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 脑电异常源于神经同步性在时空尺度上的动态变化，但现有计算方法多将此类动态特性简化为静态特征，无法充分捕捉时变连接模式。

Method: 提出Spatial Multi-Expert Graph Transformer：1) 使用加权相位滞后指数(wPLI)估计每个时间窗的功能连接，构建动态图序列；2) 层级图编码从电极级到区域级再到全局级逐步聚合信息；3) 多专家变换器架构通过门控机制自适应融合多个专家输出，实现子类型感知的全局异常预测。

Result: 在TUAB数据集上取得有竞争力的异常脑电检测性能，验证了动态图建模与自适应专家融合的有效性。

Conclusion: 动态图建模结合自适应专家融合能够为可解释的、子类型感知的时空脑电分析提供新途径，具有良好潜力。

Abstract: Electroencephalographic (EEG) abnormalities arise from dynamic changes in neural synchrony across spatial and temporal scales, yet many computational approaches reduce these dynamics to static features. We present a Spatial Multi-Expert Graph Transformer that models each EEG recording as a sequence of dynamic functional connectivity graphs. Time-resolved connectivity is estimated using the weighted Phase Lag Index (wPLI), and hierarchical graph encoding aggregates information from electrode to regional and global levels. A multi-expert transformer architecture enables subtype-aware reasoning, with a gating mechanism adaptively fusing expert outputs for global abnormality prediction. Experiments on the TUAB dataset show competitive abnormal EEG detection performance and demonstrate the potential of dynamic graph modeling with adaptive expert fusion for interpretable, subtype-aware spatial--temporal analysis.

</details>


### [196] [Unlearning as Distribution Restoration: A Controlled Counterfactual Study, a Validated Selective Screen, and the Limits of Oracle-Free Certification](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.19442)
*Sen Yang,Yuen-Hei Yeung*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 机器遗忘的常见评估标准（与重训练oracle匹配）可能偏向那些实际保留了已遗忘知识的候选方法。本文通过构建受控测试平台、引入匹配重训练参考以及审计多种无oracle筛选和证书标准，揭示了现有评估方法的局限性，并提出了基于参考的必要性测试、损伤相对重新校准以及可识别性定理，为机器遗忘评估提供了更严谨的分析框架。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 当前机器遗忘评估通常以重训练oracle作为黄金标准，但该标准可能偏爱那些实际上保留了需遗忘知识的候选方法，导致评估结果不可靠。需要重新审视评估框架，设计更精确、无偏的测试方法，以区分真正的遗忘与知识保留。

Method: 1) 构建受控的非事实测试平台（nonce-fact testbed），使用匹配的重训练参考作为基准；2) 跨5种开放架构家族、45个模型-种子细胞，审计无oracle筛选标准和证书类标准；3) 提出基于基础锚点的保留集外测试（base-anchored held-out screen）作为必要性测试；4) 引入损伤相对重新校准（damage-relative recalibration）方法；5) 采用固定幅度的logit抑制攻击检验前向证书的健全性；6) 提出可识别性定理界定哪些事实允许无oracle遗忘阈值。

Result: 1) 常见评估标准会将保留集外知识误判为遗忘（遗忘事实的负对数似然比未学习水平低2.82 nats）；2) 绝对保留/往返证书在45/45细胞中几乎全部失效（注入模型在41/45中失败，参考仅在1/45中完全认证）；3) 基于基础锚点的保留集外测试在45/45中拒绝注入模型，44/45接受参考，35/45检测实体路由抑制，是敏感的必要性测试而非充分性证书；4) 损伤相对重新校准在15/45中认证了小子集，其选择在重训练噪声范围内（0.80 nats）；5) 固定幅度的logit抑制攻击在12/45中击败了完整前向测试电池，表明前向认证不可靠；6) 可识别性定理将TOFU确定为边界情况。

Conclusion: 机器遗忘评估不应依赖单一标准。匹配重训练参考虽能揭示问题，但其本身也并非绝对可靠。基础锚点的保留集外测试可作为必要的筛选工具，但无法提供充分性保证。损伤相对重新校准在有限场景下有效。前向证书易受攻击，因此需要结合多种方法的经验性选择性测试。可识别性定理揭示了无oracle遗忘阈值的理论界限，为未来评估设计提供了方向。

Abstract: Machine unlearning is commonly evaluated by matching a retrained oracle on trained probes. In a controlled nonce-fact testbed with a matched retraining reference, we find this criterion can favor methods that retain held-out knowledge: candidates it rates adequate score held-out forget facts $-2.82$ nats below the never-learned level (cluster CI $[-3.16,-2.48]$). We recast unlearning as restoration to the matched reference and audit oracle-free screens and certificate-style criteria across 45 model-seed cells spanning five open architecture families. The reference itself falsifies an absolute retain/round-trip certificate: the injected model, which retains the retain set by construction, fails the fixed retain threshold in 41/45 cells and its own round trip in 31/45, and the reference fully certifies in only 1/45. A base-anchored held-out screen remains strong as a selective necessary test: on a sealed challenge suite it rejects the injected model in 45/45 cells, accepts the reference in 44/45, and partially detects entity-routing suppression (35/45); it is a necessary test with measured sensitivity, not a sufficiency certificate. A damage-relative recalibration anchored to the reference's own operating point certifies a small subset in 15/45 cells; where it does not abstain, its picks lie within retraining noise (0.80 nats) on the axes it optimizes, while the common trained-probe criterion sits 5.17 nats away (a supporting comparison, not a head-to-head benchmark). A fixed-magnitude logit-suppression attack defeats the full forward battery in 12/45 cells, so forward-only certification is not sound; our method is an empirical selective test for methods-as-produced. An identifiability theorem delimits which facts admit an oracle-free forget threshold at all, with TOFU as the predicted boundary case.

</details>


### [197] [Marine Engine Fault Dataset: Open-Access Data under Controlled Reference and Fault Scenario Conditions](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.19444)
*Ahmad BahooToroody,Oleksiy Bondarenko,Mohammad Mahdi Abaei,Niki Yoichi,Enrico Zio*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文介绍了海洋发动机故障数据集，这是一个公开可用的数据集，来自一台涡轮增压、中冷的三缸船用柴油机，在参考和故障场景条件下运行。数据集包含多传感器时间序列数据，涵盖五种故障类别（冷却水泵气蚀、空压机滤清器堵塞、空气冷却器污垢、喷油阀喷嘴堵塞、涡轮退化），可用于异常检测、故障诊断和退化建模研究。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 开放获取的船用发动机预测性维护数据集仍然稀缺，特别是那些具有受控故障实验、记录的操作条件、子系统级干预和系统级测量的数据集。现有数据集的缺乏限制了海事机械领域异常检测、故障诊断和退化建模研究的开展。

Method: 实验在涡轮增压、中冷的三缸船用柴油机试验台上进行，包括参考性能程序（30-90%负载范围）和场景测试（在稳定无故障运行后引入异常条件）。通过物理干预实现了五类异常：冷却水泵气蚀、空压机滤清器堵塞、空气冷却器污垢、喷油阀喷嘴堵塞和涡轮退化（通过增加排气侧阻力实现）。数据集包含多传感器时间序列（运行、热力、压力、流量和燃烧相关变量），并附有参考性能记录和元数据。

Result: 技术验证表明参考测量值在整个运行范围内保持物理一致性，施加的异常产生了与受影响子系统一致的可解释响应模式，并在实施不同严重程度时表现出逐步可区分的行为。

Conclusion: 该数据集通过结合受控故障实现、多负载运行和系统级测量，为海事机械的异常检测、故障诊断、退化建模及相关状态监测研究提供了一个有据可查的基准。

Abstract: Open-access datasets for marine-engine predictive maintenance remain scarce, particularly those from controlled fault experiments with documented operating conditions, subsystem-level interventions and system-level measurements. This work presents the Marine Engine Fault Dataset, an openly available dataset from a turbocharged, intercooled three-cylinder marine diesel engine operated on a testbed under both reference and fault-scenario conditions. The experimental campaign combined a reference-performance program across the 30-90% load range with scenario-based tests in which abnormal conditions were introduced after stabilized fault-free operation, enabling controlled comparison between baseline and fault-affected behaviour. Five anomaly classes were implemented through physical interventions affecting major engine subsystems: cooling-water pump cavitation, compressor air-filter clogging, air-cooler fouling, injection-valve nozzle clogging and turbine degradation induced through increased exhaust-side restriction. The released data comprise multi-sensor time-series of operating, thermal, pressure, flow and combustion-related variables, with a separate reference-performance record and metadata for structured reuse. Technical validation shows that the reference measurements remain physically coherent across the operating range and that the imposed anomalies produce interpretable response patterns consistent with the affected subsystems, including progressively distinguishable behaviour where different severities were implemented. By combining controlled fault realization, multi-load operation and system-level measurements within a real marine-engine platform, the dataset provides a well-documented benchmark for anomaly detection, fault diagnosis, degradation modelling and related condition-monitoring studies in maritime machinery.

</details>


### [198] [Guardrails as Scapegoats: Auditing Unfaithful Safety Refusals in Tool-Augmented LLM Agents](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.19449)
*Aarushi Singh*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出了一个轻量级黑盒审计框架，用于检测工具增强型LLM代理中的静默基础设施故障（如空/null/畸形负载的HTTP 200响应）。通过注入四种静默故障模式并分类代理行为（诚实投降HSR、捏造FAR、不忠安全拒绝USR），发现FAR占主导（56.6%），而USR在基线几乎不存在（0.25%），但在添加安全提示后放大15.6倍。敏感工具（医疗记录、合同、用户资料）是USR的主要来源。提出了负载-响应错位启发式检测方法及治理建议。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有评估框架主要关注工具增强型LLM代理的能力指标或显式工具崩溃，忽略了静默基础设施故障（如HTTP 200响应但负载为空、null或畸形），这些故障未被审计，可能导致代理无声地捏造结果或编造政策理由。

Method: 设计轻量级黑盒审计框架，注入四种静默故障模式（空负载、null负载、畸形负载等）到12个接近生产的工具桩中，将代理响应分为三类（HSR、FAR、USR）。在温度为零、中性系统提示下评估两个前沿模型和两个开源模型。进行消融实验，在系统提示中添加标准安全语言（"优先考虑用户隐私和数据安全"），观察USR变化。

Result: FAR占有效响应的56.6%，代理将空负载当作真实数据并返回捏造结果。USR在基线仅0.25%（396个有效轨迹中1例），添加安全提示后USR放大15.6倍（从0.25%到3.95%，95% CI: 2.2%-6.4%，Fisher精确检验p<0.001）。敏感工具（fetch_medical_record、retrieve_contract、fetch_user_profile）占USR实例的大部分。

Conclusion: USR是一种潜在行为，当系统提示中的安全词汇激活模型在工具静默失败时编造政策理由。提出负载-响应错位启发式方法用于生产级检测，并讨论了安全优先部署的治理意义。

Abstract: Evaluation frameworks for tool-augmented LLM agents focus overwhelmingly on capability metrics or explicit tool crashes, leaving silent infrastructure failures and HTTP 200 responses with empty, null, or malformed payloads largely unaudited.
